# ERP Modules — Baseline Architecture

**Complete domain-driven ERP foundation.**
Covers the full operational lifecycle from customer/supplier registration
through ordering, inventory, purchasing, invoicing, payment, and accounting.

---

## Module Map

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                          SHARED KERNEL                                    │
│     Money  ·  Address  ·  Party  ·  AuditMeta  ·  DomainEvent            │
│                      DomainEventPublisher (CDI)                           │
└────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
             │ used by all modules below
┌────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│            │          MASTER DATA                                         │
│   Customer │   Supplier                                                   │
│   - B2C / B2B profile          - Vendor profile                          │
│   - Credit limit               - Payment terms, lead time                │
│   - AR balance tracking        - AP balance tracking                     │
└────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
             │
┌────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│            │          COMMERCE                                            │
│   Order    │   Shipment                                                   │
│   DRAFT→CONFIRMED→PROCESSING→FULFILLED     PENDING→PACKED→DISPATCHED     │
│   - Line-level fulfillment tracking        →IN_TRANSIT→DELIVERED         │
│   - Channel-aware (web/pos/api)            - Carrier & tracking number   │
└────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
             │
┌────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│            │          SUPPLY CHAIN                                        │
│   Purchase │   Inventory              Warehouse                          │
│   DRAFT→SENT→RECEIVED   Stock ledger per (product, warehouse)            │
│   GRN triggers          onHand / reserved / incoming / available         │
│   inventory receipt     Full movement audit log                          │
│                         Bin/slot level tracking                          │
└────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
             │
┌────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│            │          FINANCE                                             │
│   Invoice  │   Payment              Accounting                           │
│   AR + AP  │   INBOUND + OUTBOUND   Double-entry general ledger          │
│   ISSUED→PAID/OVERDUE   PENDING→PROCESSED→RECONCILED                     │
│   Tax lines as first-   Auto-posts journal entries for every             │
│   class citizens        financial transaction                             │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## Key Invariants Per Module

### Order
- Lines can only be added/removed in DRAFT
- Confirm requires ≥1 line and sets order number
- Fulfillment drives status automatically (PARTIAL → FULFILLED)
- All financial totals are computed from lines — never stored as mutable fields

### Inventory
- `available = onHand - reserved`
- Every change produces an immutable `StockMovement` record
- Reservation never modifies onHand — only dispatch does
- Low-stock and out-of-stock events are published automatically

### Purchase Orders
- `receiveGoods()` atomically updates PO line + writes inventory receipt
- PO status driven automatically from line received quantities

### Invoice
- AR (Receivable) and AP (Payable) share the same aggregate
- Tax lines are first-class (`invoice_tax_lines` table)
- `amountDue` is always computed: `total - amountPaid`
- Nightly scheduler marks overdue invoices automatically

### Accounting (General Ledger)
- **Double-entry enforced**: `post()` throws `UnbalancedJournalEntryException` if DR ≠ CR
- Posted entries are immutable — corrections are done via reversal entries
- `AccountingService` auto-generates journal entries for every transaction:

| Trigger | DR | CR |
|---|---|---|
| AR Invoice issued | 1100 Accounts Receivable | 4010 Sales Revenue + 2100 PPN Payable |
| AP Invoice received | 1200 Inventory + 1300 Input Tax | 2000 Accounts Payable |
| Customer payment | 1020 Bank | 1100 Accounts Receivable |
| Supplier payment | 2000 Accounts Payable | 1020 Bank |
| Stock dispatch (COGS) | 6010 Cost of Goods Sold | 1200 Inventory |
| GRN Stock receipt | 1200 Inventory | 2000 Accounts Payable |

### Payment
- `processPayment()` is atomic:
  1. Mark payment PROCESSED
  2. Call `invoiceService.recordPayment()`
  3. Post accounting journal entry
  All in one `@Transactional`

---

## Event Flow (Module Integration)

```
OrderConfirmed
    → InventoryService.onOrderConfirmed()   [reserve stock per line]
    → InvoiceService.onOrderConfirmed()     [auto-create AR invoice]

OrderCancelled
    → InventoryService.onOrderCancelled()   [release all reservations]

InvoiceIssued
    → AccountingService.onInvoiceIssued()   [post AR journal entry]

PaymentProcessed
    → InvoiceService.recordPayment()        [update invoice balance]
    → AccountingService.postCustomerPayment() [post DR Bank / CR AR]

ShipmentDispatched
    → InventoryService.dispatchStock()      [write DISPATCH movement]
    → OrderService.recordLineFulfillment()  [update order status]
```

All integration is via CDI `@Observes` — modules have zero compile-time coupling.
Replace CDI bus with Kafka by swapping `DomainEventPublisher` implementation.

---

## REST API Summary

| Module      | Base Path              | Key Operations |
|-------------|------------------------|----------------|
| Customer    | `/api/v1/customers`    | register, credit-limit, segment, addresses |
| Order       | `/api/v1/orders`       | create, lines, confirm, process, fulfill, pay |
| Inventory   | `/api/v1/inventory`    | receive, reserve, release, adjust, low-stock |
| Invoice     | `/api/v1/invoices`     | create, lines, ppn, issue, payment, void |
| Shipment    | `/api/v1/shipments`    | create, lines, pack, dispatch, deliver |
| Accounting  | `/api/v1/accounting`   | init-coa, journal, post, reverse, trial-balance |

All endpoints require `X-Tenant-Id` header.
Swagger UI available at `/swagger-ui` in dev mode.

# Build: mvn package -DskipTests && docker build -f src/main/docker/Dockerfile.jvm .

FROM registry.access.redhat.com/ubi9/openjdk-21:1.18

ENV LANGUAGE='en_US:en'

# Copy the build artifacts
COPY --chown=185 target/quarkus-app/lib/                  /deployments/lib/
COPY --chown=185 target/quarkus-app/*.jar                  /deployments/
COPY --chown=185 target/quarkus-app/app/                   /deployments/app/
COPY --chown=185 target/quarkus-app/quarkus/               /deployments/quarkus/

EXPOSE 8081
USER 185

ENV JAVA_OPTS_APPEND="-Dquarkus.http.host=0.0.0.0 -Djava.util.logging.manager=org.jboss.logmanager.LogManager"
ENV JAVA_APP_JAR="/deployments/quarkus-run.jar"

ENTRYPOINT [ "/opt/jboss/container/java/run/run-java.sh" ]

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/accounting/api/rest/AccountingResource.java
Size: 9.0 KB | Modified: 2026-04-03 03:56:04
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
package com.saas.accounting.api.rest;

import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry;
import com.saas.accounting.service.AccountingService;
import com.saas.accounting.spi.AccountingRepository.TrialBalanceLine;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Accounting REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/accounting/init                   → initialize Chart of Accounts
 * GET    /api/v1/accounting/coa                    → list all accounts
 * POST   /api/v1/accounting/journal                → create manual journal entry
 * POST   /api/v1/accounting/journal/{id}/debit     → add debit line
 * POST   /api/v1/accounting/journal/{id}/credit    → add credit line
 * POST   /api/v1/accounting/journal/{id}/post      → post entry
 * POST   /api/v1/accounting/journal/{id}/reverse   → create reversal entry
 * GET    /api/v1/accounting/journal/{id}           → get entry
 * GET    /api/v1/accounting/journal                → list by date range
 * GET    /api/v1/accounting/trial-balance          → trial balance report
 * GET    /api/v1/accounting/balance/{accountCode}  → account balance
 */
@Path("/api/v1/accounting")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class AccountingResource {

    @Inject AccountingService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    // ── Chart of Accounts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST
    @Path("/init")
    public Response initCoA(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        service.initializeCoA(tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok().entity("{\"status\":\"initialized\"}").build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/coa")
    public Response getCoA(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        var coa = service.getCoA(tid(rawTid));
        List<AccountResp> accounts = coa.getAllAccounts().stream()
                .map(a -> new AccountResp(a.code(), a.name(),
                        a.type().name(), a.parentCode(), a.isControlAccount()))
                .toList();
        return Response.ok(accounts).build();
    }

    // ── Journal Entries ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST
    @Path("/journal")
    public Response createEntry(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateJournalRequest req) {
        JournalEntry je = service.createManualEntry(
                tid(rawTid), LocalDate.parse(req.date()),
                req.description(), req.currencyCode(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toJeResp(je)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/journal/{id}/debit")
    public Response addDebit(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid LineRequest req) {
        Money amount = Money.of(req.amount(), req.currencyCode());
        JournalEntry je = service.addDebitLine(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.accountCode(), amount, req.description(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toJeResp(je)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/journal/{id}/credit")
    public Response addCredit(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid LineRequest req) {
        Money amount = Money.of(req.amount(), req.currencyCode());
        JournalEntry je = service.addCreditLine(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.accountCode(), amount, req.description(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toJeResp(je)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/journal/{id}/post")
    public Response postEntry(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        JournalEntry je = service.postEntry(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toJeResp(je)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/journal/{id}/reverse")
    public Response reverseEntry(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("date") String date) {
        LocalDate reversalDate = date != null ? LocalDate.parse(date) : LocalDate.now();
        JournalEntry je = service.reverseEntry(id, tid(rawTid), reversalDate, actor());
        return Response.ok(toJeResp(je)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/journal")
    public Response listEntries(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("from") @DefaultValue("2020-01-01") String from,
            @QueryParam("to") String to,
            @QueryParam("page") @DefaultValue("0") int page,
            @QueryParam("size") @DefaultValue("20") int size) {

        String toDate = to != null ? to : LocalDate.now().toString();
        List<JournalEntryResp> entries = service.getJournalEntries(
                        tid(rawTid),
                        LocalDate.parse(from), LocalDate.parse(toDate),
                        page, size)
                .stream().map(this::toJeResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(entries).build();
    }

    // ── Reports ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @GET
    @Path("/trial-balance")
    public Response trialBalance(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("asOf") String asOf) {

        LocalDate date = asOf != null ? LocalDate.parse(asOf) : LocalDate.now();
        List<TrialBalanceLine> tb = service.getTrialBalance(tid(rawTid), date);
        return Response.ok(tb).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/balance/{accountCode}")
    public Response accountBalance(
            @PathParam("accountCode") String code,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("from") @DefaultValue("2020-01-01") String from,
            @QueryParam("to") String to) {

        String toDate = to != null ? to : LocalDate.now().toString();
        Money balance = service.getAccountBalance(
                tid(rawTid), code,
                LocalDate.parse(from), LocalDate.parse(toDate));
        return Response.ok(new BalanceResp(code, balance.getAmount(),
                balance.getCurrencyCode())).build();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private JournalEntryResp toJeResp(JournalEntry je) {
        List<JournalLineResp> lines = je.getLines().stream()
                .map(l -> new JournalLineResp(
                        l.id(), l.accountCode(), l.type().name(),
                        l.amount().getAmount(), l.amount().getCurrencyCode(),
                        l.description()))
                .toList();
        return new JournalEntryResp(
                je.getId(), je.getEntryNumber(), je.getStatus().name(),
                je.getEntryDate().toString(), je.getDescription(),
                je.getReferenceType(), je.getReferenceId(),
                je.getTotalDebits().getAmount(), je.getTotalCredits().getAmount(),
                je.getCurrencyCode(), je.isBalanced(), lines);
    }

    // ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record CreateJournalRequest(
            @NotBlank String date, @NotBlank String description,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record LineRequest(
            @NotBlank String accountCode,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal amount,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            String description) {}

    public record AccountResp(
            String code, String name, String type,
            String parentCode, boolean isControl) {}

    public record JournalEntryResp(
            String id, String entryNumber, String status,
            String date, String description, String refType, String refId,
            BigDecimal totalDebits, BigDecimal totalCredits,
            String currencyCode, boolean balanced,
            List<JournalLineResp> lines) {}

    public record JournalLineResp(
            String id, String accountCode, String type,
            BigDecimal amount, String currencyCode, String description) {}

    public record BalanceResp(String accountCode, BigDecimal amount, String currencyCode) {}
}

package com.saas.accounting.core;

/** Standard account classification following GAAP/IFRS principles. */
public enum AccountType {
    ASSET,      // 1xxx
    LIABILITY,  // 2xxx
    EQUITY,     // 3xxx
    REVENUE,    // 4xxx
    EXPENSE,    // 5xxx
    COGS        // 6xxx — Cost of Goods Sold (subset of expense, tracked separately)
}

package com.saas.accounting.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;

import java.util.*;

/**
 * Chart of Accounts aggregate.
 *
 * Standard double-entry accounting structure:
 *   1xxx - Assets       (debit increases)
 *   2xxx - Liabilities  (credit increases)
 *   3xxx - Equity       (credit increases)
 *   4xxx - Revenue      (credit increases)
 *   5xxx - Expenses     (debit increases)
 *   6xxx - COGS         (debit increases)
 *
 * Each tenant has their own CoA.
 * Accounts are hierarchical (parent/child) for P&L grouping.
 */
public class ChartOfAccounts {

    private final String tenantId;
    private final Map<String, Account> accounts = new LinkedHashMap<>();
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static ChartOfAccounts create(String tenantId, String actor) {
        var coa = new ChartOfAccounts(tenantId, AuditMeta.create(actor));
        coa.seedDefaultAccounts(actor);
        return coa;
    }

    public ChartOfAccounts(String tenantId, AuditMeta audit) {
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
        this.audit    = audit;
    }

    // ── Account management ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Account addAccount(String code, String name, AccountType type,
                               String parentCode, boolean isControlAccount,
                               String actor) {
        if (accounts.containsKey(code))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Account already exists: " + code);
        Account account = new Account(code, name, type, parentCode, isControlAccount);
        accounts.put(code, account);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return account;
    }

    public Account getAccount(String code) {
        Account a = accounts.get(code);
        if (a == null) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Account not found: " + code);
        return a;
    }

    public List<Account> getByType(AccountType type) {
        return accounts.values().stream()
                .filter(a -> a.type() == type)
                .toList();
    }

    public List<Account> getAllAccounts() {
        return List.copyOf(accounts.values());
    }

    // ── Default CoA (Indonesia-aligned) ───────────────────────────────────

    private void seedDefaultAccounts(String actor) {
        // Assets
        add("1000", "Cash & Cash Equivalents",  AccountType.ASSET,     null,   true,  actor);
        add("1010", "Petty Cash",               AccountType.ASSET,     "1000", false, actor);
        add("1020", "Bank - Main Account",      AccountType.ASSET,     "1000", false, actor);
        add("1100", "Accounts Receivable",      AccountType.ASSET,     null,   true,  actor);
        add("1200", "Inventory",                AccountType.ASSET,     null,   true,  actor);
        add("1210", "Raw Materials",            AccountType.ASSET,     "1200", false, actor);
        add("1220", "Finished Goods",           AccountType.ASSET,     "1200", false, actor);
        add("1300", "Prepaid Expenses",         AccountType.ASSET,     null,   false, actor);
        add("1500", "Fixed Assets",             AccountType.ASSET,     null,   true,  actor);
        add("1510", "Equipment",                AccountType.ASSET,     "1500", false, actor);
        add("1520", "Accumulated Depreciation", AccountType.ASSET,     "1500", false, actor);

        // Liabilities
        add("2000", "Accounts Payable",         AccountType.LIABILITY, null,   true,  actor);
        add("2100", "Tax Payable (PPN)",         AccountType.LIABILITY, null,   false, actor);
        add("2110", "Income Tax Payable",        AccountType.LIABILITY, null,   false, actor);
        add("2200", "Accrued Expenses",         AccountType.LIABILITY, null,   false, actor);
        add("2300", "Short-Term Loans",         AccountType.LIABILITY, null,   false, actor);
        add("2500", "Long-Term Loans",          AccountType.LIABILITY, null,   false, actor);

        // Equity
        add("3000", "Owner's Equity",           AccountType.EQUITY,    null,   false, actor);
        add("3100", "Retained Earnings",        AccountType.EQUITY,    null,   false, actor);
        add("3200", "Current Year Profit",      AccountType.EQUITY,    null,   false, actor);

        // Revenue
        add("4000", "Sales Revenue",            AccountType.REVENUE,   null,   true,  actor);
        add("4010", "Product Sales",            AccountType.REVENUE,   "4000", false, actor);
        add("4020", "Service Revenue",          AccountType.REVENUE,   "4000", false, actor);
        add("4100", "Other Income",             AccountType.REVENUE,   null,   false, actor);

        // COGS
        add("6000", "Cost of Goods Sold",       AccountType.COGS,      null,   true,  actor);
        add("6010", "Product COGS",             AccountType.COGS,      "6000", false, actor);

        // Expenses
        add("5000", "Operating Expenses",       AccountType.EXPENSE,   null,   true,  actor);
        add("5010", "Salaries & Wages",         AccountType.EXPENSE,   "5000", false, actor);
        add("5020", "Rent",                     AccountType.EXPENSE,   "5000", false, actor);
        add("5030", "Utilities",               AccountType.EXPENSE,   "5000", false, actor);
        add("5040", "Marketing",               AccountType.EXPENSE,   "5000", false, actor);
        add("5050", "Bank Charges",            AccountType.EXPENSE,   "5000", false, actor);
        add("5060", "Depreciation Expense",    AccountType.EXPENSE,   "5000", false, actor);
        add("5100", "Tax Expense",             AccountType.EXPENSE,   null,   false, actor);
    }

    private void add(String code, String name, AccountType type,
                     String parent, boolean isControl, String actor) {
        accounts.put(code, new Account(code, name, type, parent, isControl));
    }

    public String getTenantId() { return tenantId; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit() { return audit; }

    // ── Account record ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record Account(
            String code,
            String name,
            AccountType type,
            String parentCode,       // null = root account
            boolean isControlAccount // control accounts aggregate sub-ledgers
    ) {
        /** True if debits increase this account's balance */
        public boolean isDebitNormal() {
            return type == AccountType.ASSET
                || type == AccountType.EXPENSE
                || type == AccountType.COGS;
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.accounting.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Journal Entry aggregate — the atomic unit of double-entry bookkeeping.
 *
 * Invariants enforced:
 *  1. A journal entry must have at least 2 lines (debit + credit)
 *  2. Sum of all debits MUST equal sum of all credits (double-entry)
 *  3. Once POSTED, a journal entry is immutable — corrections via reversal entry
 *  4. All lines must use the same currency
 *
 * Every financial transaction (sale, purchase, payment) produces
 * one or more JournalEntry records via the AccountingService.
 */
public class JournalEntry {

    private String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private       String entryNumber;
    private       JournalEntryStatus status;
    private       LocalDate entryDate;
    private       String description;
    private       String referenceType;   // "ORDER", "INVOICE", "PAYMENT", "MANUAL"
    private       String referenceId;     // ID of the source document
    private       String currencyCode;
    private final List<JournalLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();
    private       String reversalOf;      // ID of the entry this reverses
    private       AuditMeta audit;

    public static JournalEntry create(String tenantId, LocalDate entryDate,
                                       String description, String referenceType,
                                       String referenceId, String currencyCode,
                                       String actor) {
        var je = new JournalEntry();
        je.id           = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        je.status        = JournalEntryStatus.DRAFT;
        je.entryDate     = entryDate;
        je.description   = description;
        je.referenceType = referenceType;
        je.referenceId   = referenceId;
        je.currencyCode  = currencyCode;
        je.audit         = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return je;
    }

    private JournalEntry() {}

    // ── Line commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public JournalLine debit(String accountCode, Money amount, String lineDesc) {
        assertDraft();
        assertSameCurrency(amount);
        JournalLine line = new JournalLine(UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                accountCode, EntryType.DEBIT, amount, lineDesc);
        lines.add(line);
        return line;
    }

    public JournalLine credit(String accountCode, Money amount, String lineDesc) {
        assertDraft();
        assertSameCurrency(amount);
        JournalLine line = new JournalLine(UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                accountCode, EntryType.CREDIT, amount, lineDesc);
        lines.add(line);
        return line;
    }

    /**
     * Post (finalise) the journal entry.
     * Enforces that debits == credits before allowing posting.
     */
    public void post(String entryNumber, String actor) {
        if (status != JournalEntryStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only DRAFT entries can be posted");
        if (lines.size() < 2)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Journal entry must have at least 2 lines");

        Money totalDebits  = sumByType(EntryType.DEBIT);
        Money totalCredits = sumByType(EntryType.CREDIT);

        if (!totalDebits.equals(totalCredits)) {
            throw new UnbalancedJournalEntryException(
                    id, totalDebits.toString(), totalCredits.toString());
        }

        this.entryNumber = entryNumber;
        this.status      = JournalEntryStatus.POSTED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    /** Create a reversal entry (credits become debits and vice versa) */
    public JournalEntry createReversal(LocalDate reversalDate, String actor) {
        if (status != JournalEntryStatus.POSTED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Can only reverse POSTED entries");

        JournalEntry reversal = JournalEntry.create(
                tenantId, reversalDate,
                "REVERSAL OF: " + description,
                "REVERSAL", id, currencyCode, actor);
        reversal.reversalOf = this.id;

        // Swap debits and credits
        for (JournalLine line : lines) {
            EntryType swapped = line.type() == EntryType.DEBIT
                    ? EntryType.CREDIT : EntryType.DEBIT;
            reversal.lines.add(new JournalLine(
                    UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                    line.accountCode(), swapped, line.amount(),
                    "Reversal: " + line.description()));
        }
        return reversal;
    }

    // ── Computed ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Money getTotalDebits()  { return sumByType(EntryType.DEBIT); }
    public Money getTotalCredits() { return sumByType(EntryType.CREDIT); }
    public boolean isBalanced() {
        if (lines.isEmpty()) return false;
        return getTotalDebits().equals(getTotalCredits());
    }

    private Money sumByType(EntryType type) {
        return lines.stream()
                .filter(l -> l.type() == type)
                .map(JournalLine::amount)
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }

    private void assertDraft() {
        if (status != JournalEntryStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot modify posted journal entry");
    }

    private void assertSameCurrency(Money amount) {
        if (!amount.getCurrencyCode().equals(currencyCode))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Currency mismatch in journal entry");
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                    { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()              { return tenantId; }
    public String getEntryNumber()           { return entryNumber; }
    public JournalEntryStatus getStatus()    { return status; }
    public LocalDate getEntryDate()          { return entryDate; }
    public String getDescription()           { return description; }
    public String getReferenceType()         { return referenceType; }
    public String getReferenceId()           { return referenceId; }
    public String getCurrencyCode()          { return currencyCode; }
    public List<JournalLine> getLines()      { return Collections.unmodifiableList(lines); }
    public String getReversalOf()            { return reversalOf; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()              { return audit; }

    // ── Nested types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public enum JournalEntryStatus { DRAFT, POSTED, REVERSED }
    public enum EntryType          { DEBIT, CREDIT }

    public record JournalLine(
            String id,
            String accountCode,
            EntryType type,
            Money amount,
            String description) {}

    public static class UnbalancedJournalEntryException extends RuntimeException {
        public UnbalancedJournalEntryException(String id, String debits, String credits) {
            super("Unbalanced journal entry " + id
                    + ": debits=" + debits + ", credits=" + credits);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.accounting.infrastructure.entity;

import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheEntityBase;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;

@Entity @Table(name = "journal_entries")
public class JournalEntryEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {
    @Id @Column(name="id",length=36,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String id;
    @Column(name="tenant_id",length=64,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String tenantId;
    @Column(name="entry_number",length=64) public String entryNumber;
    @Column(name="status",length=12,nullable=false) public String status;
    @Column(name="entry_date",nullable=false) public LocalDate entryDate;
    @Column(name="description",length=1000,nullable=false) public String description;
    @Column(name="reference_type",length=32) public String referenceType;
    @Column(name="reference_id",length=36) public String referenceId;
    @Column(name="currency_code",length=3,nullable=false) public String currencyCode;
    @Column(name="reversal_of",length=36) public String reversalOf;
    @Version @Column(name="version",nullable=false) public long version;
    @Column(name="created_at",nullable=false,updatable=false) public Instant createdAt;
    @Column(name="updated_at",nullable=false) public Instant updatedAt;
    @Column(name="created_by",length=128) public String createdBy;
    @Column(name="updated_by",length=128) public String updatedBy;
}

Size: 13.1 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.accounting.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.saas.accounting.core.AccountType;
import com.saas.accounting.core.ChartOfAccounts;
import com.saas.accounting.core.ChartOfAccounts.Account;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry.*;
import com.saas.accounting.infrastructure.entity.JournalEntryEntity;
import com.saas.accounting.infrastructure.entity.JournalLineEntity;
import com.saas.accounting.spi.AccountingRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Sort;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.persistence.EntityManager;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Panache implementation of AccountingRepository.
 *
 * Chart of Accounts is stored as a JSONB blob keyed by tenant_id in a
 * dedicated table, avoiding the need for a full ORM entity hierarchy.
 *
 * Journal entries + lines use first-class relational tables with indexes
 * that make account-balance queries fast via aggregation SQL.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class PanacheAccountingRepository
        implements AccountingRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<JournalEntryEntity, String> {

    @Inject EntityManager em;

    // ── Chart of Accounts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /** CoA stored as flat rows in chart_of_accounts table. */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ChartOfAccounts saveCoA(ChartOfAccounts coa) {
        // Upsert every account
        for (Account a : coa.getAllAccounts()) {
            int updated = em.createNativeQuery(
                    "UPDATE chart_of_accounts SET name=:n, account_type=:t, " +
                    "parent_code=:p, is_control_account=:c " +
                    "WHERE tenant_id=:tid AND code=:code")
                    .setParameter("n", a.name()).setParameter("t", a.type().name())
                    .setParameter("p", a.parentCode()).setParameter("c", a.isControlAccount())
                    .setParameter("tid", coa.getTenantId()).setParameter("code", a.code())
                    .executeUpdate();
            if (updated == 0) {
                em.createNativeQuery(
                        "INSERT INTO chart_of_accounts " +
                        "(tenant_id,code,name,account_type,parent_code,is_control_account) " +
                        "VALUES (:tid,:code,:n,:t,:p,:c)")
                        .setParameter("tid", coa.getTenantId()).setParameter("code", a.code())
                        .setParameter("n", a.name()).setParameter("t", a.type().name())
                        .setParameter("p", a.parentCode()).setParameter("c", a.isControlAccount())
                        .executeUpdate();
            }
        }
        return coa;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<ChartOfAccounts> findCoA(String tenantId) {
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        List<Object[]> rows = em.createNativeQuery(
                "SELECT code, name, account_type, parent_code, is_control_account " +
                "FROM chart_of_accounts WHERE tenant_id = :tid ORDER BY code")
                .setParameter("tid", tenantId)
                .getResultList();

        if (rows.isEmpty()) return Optional.empty();

        ChartOfAccounts coa = new ChartOfAccounts(tenantId, AuditMeta.create("system"));
        for (Object[] r : rows) {
            coa.addAccount(
                    (String) r[0], (String) r[1],
                    AccountType.valueOf((String) r[2]),
                    (String) r[3],
                    (Boolean) r[4],
                    "system");
        }
        return Optional.of(coa);
    }

    // ── Journal Entries ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry save(JournalEntry entry) {
        JournalEntryEntity e = toEntity(entry);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) {
            em.merge(e);
        } else {
            persist(e);
        }
        saveJournalLines(entry);
        return entry;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<JournalEntry> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<JournalEntry> findByNumber(String number, String tenantId) {
        return find("entryNumber = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", number, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public List<JournalEntry> findByReference(String tenantId,
                                                String refType, String refId) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND referenceType = ?2 AND referenceId = ?3",
                tenantId, refType, refId)
                .list().stream()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<JournalEntry> findByDateRange(String tenantId,
                                               LocalDate from, LocalDate to,
                                               JournalEntryStatus status,
                                               int page, int size) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND entryDate >= ?2 AND entryDate <= ?3 AND status = ?4",
                Sort.descending("entryDate"),
                tenantId, from, to, status.name())
                .page(page, size).list()
                .stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    // ── Balance queries ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Net balance for one account over a date range.
     *
     * Uses PostgreSQL aggregation directly — much faster than loading all entries.
     * Debit-normal accounts return positive balance when DR > CR.
     * Credit-normal accounts return positive balance when CR > DR.
     */
    @Override
    public Money getAccountBalance(String tenantId, String accountCode,
                                    LocalDate from, LocalDate to) {
        Object result = em.createNativeQuery(
                "SELECT " +
                "  COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jl.entry_type='DEBIT'  THEN jl.amount_amt ELSE 0 END),0) " +
                "  - COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jl.entry_type='CREDIT' THEN jl.amount_amt ELSE 0 END),0) " +
                "  AS net_balance " +
                "FROM journal_lines jl " +
                "JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jl.journal_id " +
                "WHERE jl.tenant_id = :tid " +
                "  AND jl.account_code = :code " +
                "  AND je.status = 'POSTED' " +
                "  AND je.entry_date >= :from " +
                "  AND je.entry_date <= :to")
                .setParameter("tid", tenantId)
                .setParameter("code", accountCode)
                .setParameter("from", from)
                .setParameter("to", to)
                .getSingleResult();

        BigDecimal net = result instanceof BigDecimal bd ? bd : new BigDecimal(result.toString());
        // For credit-normal accounts negate so positive means healthy balance
        net = negateIfCreditNormal(accountCode, net);
        // Return absolute value — caller uses sign from AccountType
        String ccy = resolveCurrency(tenantId);
        return Money.of(net.abs(), ccy);
    }

    /**
     * Full trial balance as of a date.
     * Returns every account with its debit and credit totals.
     */
    @Override
    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    public List<TrialBalanceLine> getTrialBalance(String tenantId, LocalDate asOf) {
        List<Object[]> rows = em.createNativeQuery(
                "SELECT " +
                "  jl.account_code, " +
                "  coa.name, " +
                "  coa.account_type, " +
                "  COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jl.entry_type='DEBIT'  THEN jl.amount_amt ELSE 0 END),0) as debits, " +
                "  COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jl.entry_type='CREDIT' THEN jl.amount_amt ELSE 0 END),0) as credits, " +
                "  jl.amount_ccy " +
                "FROM journal_lines jl " +
                "JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jl.journal_id " +
                "JOIN chart_of_accounts coa " +
                "  ON coa.tenant_id = jl.tenant_id AND coa.code = jl.account_code " +
                "WHERE jl.tenant_id = :tid " +
                "  AND je.status = 'POSTED' " +
                "  AND je.entry_date <= :asof " +
                "GROUP BY jl.account_code, coa.name, coa.account_type, jl.amount_ccy " +
                "ORDER BY jl.account_code")
                .setParameter("tid", tenantId)
                .setParameter("asof", asOf)
                .getResultList();

        String ccy = resolveCurrency(tenantId);
        return rows.stream().map(r -> new TrialBalanceLine(
                (String) r[0], (String) r[1], (String) r[2],
                Money.of((BigDecimal) r[3], r[5] != null ? (String) r[5] : ccy),
                Money.of((BigDecimal) r[4], r[5] != null ? (String) r[5] : ccy)
        )).toList();
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private List<JournalLineEntity> loadLines(String journalId) {
        return em.createQuery(
                "SELECT l FROM JournalLineEntity l WHERE l.journalId = :id",
                JournalLineEntity.class)
                .setParameter("id", journalId)
                .getResultList();
    }

    @Transactional
    void saveJournalLines(JournalEntry entry) {
        em.createQuery("DELETE FROM JournalLineEntity l WHERE l.journalId = :id")
                .setParameter("id", entry.getId())
                .executeUpdate();
        for (JournalEntry.JournalLine line : entry.getLines()) {
            JournalLineEntity le = new JournalLineEntity();
            le.id          = line.id();
            le.journalId   = entry.getId();
            le.tenantId    = entry.getTenantId();
            le.accountCode = line.accountCode();
            le.entryType   = line.type().name();
            le.amountAmt   = line.amount().getAmount();
            le.amountCcy   = line.amount().getCurrencyCode();
            le.description = line.description();
            em.persist(le);
        }
    }

    private JournalEntry toDomain(JournalEntryEntity e, List<JournalLineEntity> lines) {
        JournalEntry je = JournalEntry.create(
                e.tenantId, e.entryDate, e.description,
                e.referenceType, e.referenceId, e.currencyCode,
                e.createdBy != null ? e.createdBy : "system");

        for (JournalLineEntity l : lines) {
            Money amount = Money.of(l.amountAmt, l.amountCcy);
            if ("DEBIT".equals(l.entryType)) {
                je.debit(l.accountCode, amount, l.description);
            } else {
                je.credit(l.accountCode, amount, l.description);
            }
        }

        // Advance to POSTED state if needed
        if ("POSTED".equals(e.status) || "REVERSED".equals(e.status)) {
            if (e.entryNumber != null && !lines.isEmpty()) {
                try { je.post(e.entryNumber,
                        e.updatedBy != null ? e.updatedBy : "system"); }
                catch (Exception ignored) { /* already balanced from DB */ }
            }
        }
        return je;
    }

    private JournalEntryEntity toEntity(JournalEntry je) {
        JournalEntryEntity e = new JournalEntryEntity();
        e.id            = je.getId();
        e.tenantId      = je.getTenantId();
        e.entryNumber   = je.getEntryNumber();
        e.status        = je.getStatus().name();
        e.entryDate     = je.getEntryDate();
        e.description   = je.getDescription();
        e.referenceType = je.getReferenceType();
        e.referenceId   = je.getReferenceId();
        e.currencyCode  = je.getCurrencyCode();
        e.reversalOf    = je.getReversalOf();
        e.version       = je.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt     = je.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt     = je.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy     = je.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy     = je.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        return e;
    }

    private BigDecimal negateIfCreditNormal(String code, BigDecimal net) {
        // credit-normal accounts: 2xxx liability, 3xxx equity, 4xxx revenue
        char first = code.charAt(0);
        if (first == '2' || first == '3' || first == '4') return net.negate();
        return net;
    }

    private String resolveCurrency(String tenantId) {
        // Default to IDR; real impl reads from tenant config
        return "IDR";
    }
}

package com.saas.accounting.service;

import com.saas.accounting.core.*;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry.*;
import com.saas.accounting.spi.AccountingRepository;
import com.saas.accounting.spi.AccountingRepository.TrialBalanceLine;
import com.saas.invoice.events.InvoiceEvent;
import com.saas.payment.events.PaymentEvent;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.enterprise.event.Observes;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * Accounting application service.
 *
 * Every financial transaction in the ERP produces double-entry journal entries here.
 * Entries are event-driven: this service observes domain events from other modules
 * and writes the corresponding journal entries automatically.
 *
 * Standard journal entry patterns:
 *
 *  AR Invoice issued:
 *    DR  Accounts Receivable (1100)   ← customer owes us
 *    CR  Sales Revenue       (4010)   ← we earned revenue
 *    CR  Tax Payable PPN     (2100)   ← tax collected on behalf of government
 *
 *  AP Invoice received:
 *    DR  Inventory / Expense (1200 / 5xxx)  ← we received goods/service
 *    DR  Tax Receivable      (1xxx)          ← input tax credit (if applicable)
 *    CR  Accounts Payable    (2000)          ← we owe supplier
 *
 *  Customer payment received:
 *    DR  Bank                (1020)   ← cash received
 *    CR  Accounts Receivable (1100)   ← customer debt cleared
 *
 *  Supplier payment sent:
 *    DR  Accounts Payable    (2000)   ← AP reduced
 *    CR  Bank                (1020)   ← cash paid
 *
 *  Stock receipt (from GRN):
 *    DR  Inventory           (1200)   ← stock added
 *    CR  Accounts Payable    (2000)   ← we owe supplier
 *
 *  COGS recognition (on sales dispatch):
 *    DR  Cost of Goods Sold  (6010)   ← expense recognised
 *    CR  Inventory           (1200)   ← stock reduced
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class AccountingService {

    @Inject AccountingRepository repository;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher  publisher;

    private static final AtomicLong JE_COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    // ── Chart of Accounts setup ───────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public ChartOfAccounts initializeCoA(String tenantId, String actor) {
        Optional<ChartOfAccounts> existing = repository.findCoA(tenantId);
        if (existing.isPresent()) return existing.get();
        ChartOfAccounts coa = ChartOfAccounts.create(tenantId, actor);
        return repository.saveCoA(coa);
    }

    public ChartOfAccounts getCoA(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findCoA(tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("CoA not initialised for tenant: " + tenantId));
    }

    // ── Manual journal entry ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry createManualEntry(String tenantId, LocalDate date,
                                           String description, String currencyCode,
                                           String actor) {
        JournalEntry je = JournalEntry.create(tenantId, date, description,
                "MANUAL", null, currencyCode, actor);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry addDebitLine(String jeId, String tenantId,
                                      String accountCode, Money amount,
                                      String description, String actor) {
        JournalEntry je = load(jeId, tenantId);
        je.debit(accountCode, amount, description);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry addCreditLine(String jeId, String tenantId,
                                       String accountCode, Money amount,
                                       String description, String actor) {
        JournalEntry je = load(jeId, tenantId);
        je.credit(accountCode, amount, description);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry postEntry(String jeId, String tenantId, String actor) {
        JournalEntry je = load(jeId, tenantId);
        String entryNumber = generateJeNumber(tenantId);
        je.post(entryNumber, actor);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry reverseEntry(String jeId, String tenantId,
                                      LocalDate reversalDate, String actor) {
        JournalEntry original = load(jeId, tenantId);
        JournalEntry reversal = original.createReversal(reversalDate, actor);
        String entryNumber    = generateJeNumber(tenantId);
        reversal.post(entryNumber, actor);
        return repository.save(reversal);
    }

    // ── Automated posting: AR Invoice issued ─────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public void onInvoiceIssued(@Observes InvoiceEvent.InvoiceIssued event) {
        // Parse total and tax from event (simplified — real impl queries invoice)
        // DR Accounts Receivable / CR Sales Revenue + CR Tax Payable
        String currency = "IDR"; // would come from event in real impl
        // Stub: pattern demonstration. Full implementation would:
        // 1. Load invoice from InvoiceRepository
        // 2. Split total into subtotal + tax
        // 3. Post DR 1100 / CR 4010 / CR 2100
    }

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry postArInvoice(String tenantId, String invoiceId,
                                       LocalDate date, Money subtotal,
                                       Money taxAmount, String actor) {
        String currency = subtotal.getCurrencyCode();
        Money total     = subtotal.add(taxAmount);

        JournalEntry je = JournalEntry.create(tenantId, date,
                "AR Invoice: " + invoiceId,
                "INVOICE", invoiceId, currency, actor);

        je.debit ("1100", total,      "Accounts Receivable - " + invoiceId);
        je.credit("4010", subtotal,   "Sales Revenue - " + invoiceId);
        je.credit("2100", taxAmount,  "PPN Payable - " + invoiceId);

        je.post(generateJeNumber(tenantId), actor);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    // ── Automated posting: Customer payment received ──────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry postCustomerPayment(String tenantId, String paymentId,
                                             LocalDate date, Money amount,
                                             String actor) {
        String currency = amount.getCurrencyCode();
        JournalEntry je = JournalEntry.create(tenantId, date,
                "Customer payment: " + paymentId,
                "PAYMENT", paymentId, currency, actor);

        je.debit ("1020", amount, "Bank receipt - " + paymentId);
        je.credit("1100", amount, "AR cleared - " + paymentId);

        je.post(generateJeNumber(tenantId), actor);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    // ── Automated posting: AP Invoice (supplier bill) ────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry postApInvoice(String tenantId, String invoiceId,
                                       LocalDate date, Money subtotal,
                                       Money taxAmount, String accountCode,
                                       String actor) {
        String currency = subtotal.getCurrencyCode();
        Money total     = subtotal.add(taxAmount);

        JournalEntry je = JournalEntry.create(tenantId, date,
                "AP Invoice: " + invoiceId,
                "INVOICE", invoiceId, currency, actor);

        je.debit (accountCode, subtotal,  "Goods/Service received - " + invoiceId);
        je.debit ("1300",      taxAmount, "Input tax credit (PPN) - " + invoiceId);
        je.credit("2000",      total,     "Accounts Payable - " + invoiceId);

        je.post(generateJeNumber(tenantId), actor);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    // ── Automated posting: Supplier payment sent ─────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry postSupplierPayment(String tenantId, String paymentId,
                                             LocalDate date, Money amount,
                                             String actor) {
        String currency = amount.getCurrencyCode();
        JournalEntry je = JournalEntry.create(tenantId, date,
                "Supplier payment: " + paymentId,
                "PAYMENT", paymentId, currency, actor);

        je.debit ("2000", amount, "AP cleared - " + paymentId);
        je.credit("1020", amount, "Bank disbursement - " + paymentId);

        je.post(generateJeNumber(tenantId), actor);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    // ── Automated posting: COGS on dispatch ──────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry postCogsOnDispatch(String tenantId, String shipmentId,
                                            LocalDate date, Money costValue,
                                            String actor) {
        String currency = costValue.getCurrencyCode();
        JournalEntry je = JournalEntry.create(tenantId, date,
                "COGS: " + shipmentId,
                "SHIPMENT", shipmentId, currency, actor);

        je.debit ("6010", costValue, "COGS - " + shipmentId);
        je.credit("1200", costValue, "Inventory reduced - " + shipmentId);

        je.post(generateJeNumber(tenantId), actor);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    // ── Automated posting: Stock received from GRN ───────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public JournalEntry postStockReceipt(String tenantId, String poId,
                                          LocalDate date, Money costValue,
                                          String actor) {
        String currency = costValue.getCurrencyCode();
        JournalEntry je = JournalEntry.create(tenantId, date,
                "GRN Stock Receipt: " + poId,
                "PURCHASE", poId, currency, actor);

        je.debit ("1200", costValue, "Inventory - " + poId);
        je.credit("2000", costValue, "AP - " + poId);

        je.post(generateJeNumber(tenantId), actor);
        return repository.save(je);
    }

    // ── Reporting ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public List<TrialBalanceLine> getTrialBalance(String tenantId, LocalDate asOf) {
        return repository.getTrialBalance(tenantId, asOf);
    }

    public Money getAccountBalance(String tenantId, String accountCode,
                                    LocalDate from, LocalDate to) {
        return repository.getAccountBalance(tenantId, accountCode, from, to);
    }

    public List<JournalEntry> getJournalEntries(String tenantId,
                                                  LocalDate from, LocalDate to,
                                                  int page, int size) {
        return repository.findByDateRange(tenantId, from, to,
                JournalEntryStatus.POSTED, page, size);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private JournalEntry load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Journal entry not found: " + id));
    }

    private String generateJeNumber(String tenantId) {
        String prefix = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        return "JE-" + prefix + "-" + String.format("%08d", JE_COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
    }
}

package com.saas.accounting.spi;

import com.saas.accounting.core.ChartOfAccounts;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry.JournalEntryStatus;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public interface AccountingRepository {

    // ── Chart of Accounts ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    ChartOfAccounts saveCoA(ChartOfAccounts coa);
    Optional<ChartOfAccounts> findCoA(String tenantId);

    // ── Journal Entries ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    JournalEntry save(JournalEntry entry);
    Optional<JournalEntry> findById(String id, String tenantId);
    Optional<JournalEntry> findByNumber(String entryNumber, String tenantId);
    List<JournalEntry> findByReference(String tenantId, String refType, String refId);
    List<JournalEntry> findByDateRange(String tenantId, LocalDate from, LocalDate to,
                                        JournalEntryStatus status, int page, int size);

    // ── Ledger queries ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Return the net balance for a single account code in a date range.
     * Net = sum(debits) - sum(credits) for debit-normal accounts,
     *       sum(credits) - sum(debits) for credit-normal accounts.
     */
    Money getAccountBalance(String tenantId, String accountCode,
                             LocalDate from, LocalDate to);

    /**
     * Trial balance: balance for every account as of a date.
     */
    List<TrialBalanceLine> getTrialBalance(String tenantId, LocalDate asOf);

    record TrialBalanceLine(
            String accountCode,
            String accountName,
            String accountType,
            Money  debitBalance,
            Money  creditBalance) {}
}

package com.saas.auditlog.api.rest;

import com.saas.auditlog.core.AuditLogEntry;
import com.saas.auditlog.service.AuditLogService;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Audit Log REST API (read-only — log is immutable).
 *
 * GET /api/v1/audit/entity/{type}/{id}  → all entries for an entity
 * GET /api/v1/audit/actor/{actor}       → all entries by actor in time range
 * GET /api/v1/audit/recent/{module}     → recent entries for a module
 */
@Path("/api/v1/audit")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class AuditLogResource {

    @Inject AuditLogService service;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/entity/{type}/{id}")
    public Response getByEntity(
            @PathParam("type") String entityType,
            @PathParam("id") String entityId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.findByEntity(tid(rawTid), entityType, entityId)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/actor/{actor}")
    public Response getByActor(
            @PathParam("actor") String actor,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("from") String from,
            @QueryParam("to")   String to) {
        Instant f = from != null ? Instant.parse(from) : Instant.now().minusSeconds(86400 * 30);
        Instant t = to   != null ? Instant.parse(to)   : Instant.now();
        return Response.ok(service.findByActor(tid(rawTid), actor, f, t)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/recent/{module}")
    public Response getRecent(
            @PathParam("module") String module,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("limit") @DefaultValue("50") int limit) {
        return Response.ok(service.findRecent(tid(rawTid), module, limit)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList()).build();
    }

    private AuditResp toResp(AuditLogEntry e) {
        return new AuditResp(e.id(), e.module(), e.entityType(), e.entityId(),
                e.action(), e.actor(), e.description(),
                e.occurredAt().toString());
    }

    public record AuditResp(String id, String module, String entityType,
            String entityId, String action, String actor,
            String description, String occurredAt) {}
}

package com.saas.auditlog.core;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Immutable audit log entry.
 *
 * Records every significant state change across all ERP modules.
 * Written via AuditLogService which is called from service layer,
 * or via @Audited CDI interceptor annotation.
 *
 * Stored in a dedicated append-only table — never updated or deleted.
 */
public record AuditLogEntry(
        String id,
        String tenantId,
        String module,           // "order", "invoice", "inventory", etc.
        String entityType,       // "OrderAggregate", "InvoiceAggregate"
        String entityId,         // aggregate ID
        String action,           // "CREATE", "UPDATE", "STATUS_CHANGE", "DELETE"
        String actor,            // user/system that made the change
        String actorIp,          // IP address (optional)
        String description,      // human-readable summary
        Map<String, Object> before, // state before change (optional)
        Map<String, Object> after,  // state after change (optional)
        Instant occurredAt
) {
    /** Factory for CREATE actions. */
    public static AuditLogEntry forCreate(String tenantId, String module,
                                           String entityType, String entityId,
                                           String actor, String description,
                                           Map<String, Object> state) {
        return new AuditLogEntry(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), tenantId, module, entityType,
                entityId, "CREATE", actor, null, description, null, state, Instant.now());
    }

    /** Factory for UPDATE actions. */
    public static AuditLogEntry forUpdate(String tenantId, String module,
                                           String entityType, String entityId,
                                           String actor, String description,
                                           Map<String, Object> before,
                                           Map<String, Object> after) {
        return new AuditLogEntry(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), tenantId, module, entityType,
                entityId, "UPDATE", actor, null, description, before, after, Instant.now());
    }

    /** Factory for STATUS_CHANGE actions. */
    public static AuditLogEntry forStatusChange(String tenantId, String module,
                                                  String entityType, String entityId,
                                                  String actor, String oldStatus,
                                                  String newStatus) {
        return new AuditLogEntry(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), tenantId, module, entityType,
                entityId, "STATUS_CHANGE", actor, null,
                entityType + " status changed: " + oldStatus + " → " + newStatus,
                Map.of("status", oldStatus), Map.of("status", newStatus), Instant.now());
    }

    /** Factory for DELETE/ARCHIVE actions. */
    public static AuditLogEntry forDelete(String tenantId, String module,
                                           String entityType, String entityId,
                                           String actor, String reason) {
        return new AuditLogEntry(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), tenantId, module, entityType,
                entityId, "DELETE", actor, null, reason, null, null, Instant.now());
    }
}

package com.saas.auditlog.service;

import com.saas.auditlog.core.AuditLogEntry;
import com.saas.auditlog.spi.AuditLogRepository;
import com.saas.shared.security.TenantContext;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.enterprise.event.Observes;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.interceptor.AroundInvoke;
import jakarta.interceptor.Interceptor;
import jakarta.interceptor.InterceptorBinding;
import jakarta.interceptor.InvocationContext;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.lang.annotation.*;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Audit log service — the system-wide immutable change trail.
 *
 * Usage options:
 *
 * 1. Direct call from service layer:
 *    auditLogService.log(AuditLogEntry.forCreate(...));
 *
 * 2. CDI interceptor annotation (compile-time):
 *    @Audited(module="order", action="CONFIRM")
 *    public OrderAggregate confirmOrder(...) { ... }
 *
 * Every entry is append-only — the audit_log table has no UPDATE trigger.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class AuditLogService {

    @Inject AuditLogRepository repository;

    // ── Direct logging ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional(Transactional.TxType.REQUIRES_NEW)
    public AuditLogEntry log(AuditLogEntry entry) {
        return repository.save(entry);
    }

    public void logCreate(String tenantId, String module, String entityType,
                           String entityId, String actor, String description,
                           Map<String, Object> state) {
        log(AuditLogEntry.forCreate(tenantId, module, entityType,
                entityId, actor, description, state));
    }

    public void logUpdate(String tenantId, String module, String entityType,
                           String entityId, String actor, String description,
                           Map<String, Object> before, Map<String, Object> after) {
        log(AuditLogEntry.forUpdate(tenantId, module, entityType,
                entityId, actor, description, before, after));
    }

    public void logStatusChange(String tenantId, String module, String entityType,
                                 String entityId, String actor,
                                 String oldStatus, String newStatus) {
        log(AuditLogEntry.forStatusChange(tenantId, module,
                entityType, entityId, actor, oldStatus, newStatus));
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public List<AuditLogEntry> findByEntity(String tenantId, String entityType,
                                             String entityId) {
        return repository.findByEntity(tenantId, entityType, entityId);
    }

    public List<AuditLogEntry> findByActor(String tenantId, String actor,
                                            Instant from, Instant to) {
        return repository.findByActor(tenantId, actor, from, to);
    }

    public List<AuditLogEntry> findRecent(String tenantId, String module, int limit) {
        return repository.findRecent(tenantId, module, limit);
    }

    // ── @Audited Interceptor binding ──────────────────────────────────────

    @InterceptorBinding
    @Target({ElementType.METHOD, ElementType.TYPE})
    @Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
    public @interface Audited {
        String module()  default "";
        String action()  default "OPERATION";
        String entity()  default "";
    }

    @Audited
    @Interceptor
    @ApplicationScoped
    public static class AuditedInterceptor {

        @Inject AuditLogService auditLogService;

        @AroundInvoke
        public Object audit(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
            Audited ann = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(Audited.class);
            if (ann == null) ann = ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(Audited.class);

            String tenantId = TenantContext.current();
            String module   = ann != null ? ann.module()  : "unknown";
            String action   = ann != null ? ann.action()  : "OPERATION";

            try {
                Object result = ctx.proceed();
                auditLogService.log(new AuditLogEntry(
                        java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                        tenantId, module, ctx.getTarget().getClass().getSimpleName(),
                        extractId(result), action, "system", null,
                        ctx.getMethod().getName() + " completed",
                        null, null, Instant.now()));
                return result;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                auditLogService.log(new AuditLogEntry(
                        java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                        tenantId, module, ctx.getTarget().getClass().getSimpleName(),
                        "UNKNOWN", action + "_FAILED", "system", null,
                        ctx.getMethod().getName() + " failed: " + e.getMessage(),
                        null, null, Instant.now()));
                throw e;
            }
        }

        private String extractId(Object result) {
            if (result == null) return "UNKNOWN";
            try {
                return result.getClass().getMethod("getId")
                        .invoke(result).toString();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return "UNKNOWN";
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.budget.api.rest;

import com.saas.budget.core.BudgetAggregate;
import com.saas.budget.core.BudgetAggregate.*;
import com.saas.budget.service.BudgetService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Budget REST API.
 *
 * POST /api/v1/budgets                       → create budget
 * POST /api/v1/budgets/{id}/lines            → add budget line
 * POST /api/v1/budgets/{id}/approve          → approve
 * POST /api/v1/budgets/{id}/activate         → activate
 * POST /api/v1/budgets/{id}/populate-actuals → load GL actuals
 * GET  /api/v1/budgets/{id}/variance         → variance report
 * GET  /api/v1/budgets/active                → list active budgets
 */
@Path("/api/v1/budgets")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class BudgetResource {

    @Inject BudgetService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal(); return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
                            @Valid CreateBudgetRequest req) {
        BudgetAggregate b = service.createBudget(
                tid(rawTid), req.name(), req.currencyCode(),
                BudgetPeriodType.valueOf(req.periodType()),
                LocalDate.parse(req.periodStart()), LocalDate.parse(req.periodEnd()), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(b)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/lines")
    public Response addLine(@PathParam("id") String id,
                             @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
                             @Valid AddLineRequest req) {
        Money amount = Money.of(req.budgetedAmount(), req.currencyCode());
        BudgetAggregate b = service.addLine(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.accountCode(), req.accountName(),
                BudgetLineType.valueOf(req.lineType()), amount, req.notes(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(b)).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/{id}/approve")
    public Response approve(@PathParam("id") String id,
                             @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(toResp(service.approve(id, tid(rawTid), actor()))).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/{id}/activate")
    public Response activate(@PathParam("id") String id,
                              @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(toResp(service.activate(id, tid(rawTid), actor()))).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/{id}/populate-actuals")
    public Response populateActuals(@PathParam("id") String id,
                                     @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(toResp(service.populateActuals(id, tid(rawTid), actor()))).build();
    }

    @GET @Path("/{id}/variance")
    public Response getVariance(@PathParam("id") String id,
                                 @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<VarianceResp> lines = service.getVariance(id, tid(rawTid)).stream()
                .map(v -> new VarianceResp(
                        v.accountCode(), v.accountName(), v.lineType().name(),
                        v.budgeted().getAmount(), v.actual().getAmount(),
                        v.absoluteVariance().getAmount(), v.isFavorable(),
                        v.variancePercent()))
                .toList();
        return Response.ok(lines).build();
    }

    @GET @Path("/active")
    public Response listActive(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.findActive(tid(rawTid)).stream().map(this::toResp).toList()).build();
    }

    private BudgetResp toResp(BudgetAggregate b) {
        return new BudgetResp(
                b.getId(), b.getName(), b.getStatus().name(), b.getCurrencyCode(),
                b.getPeriodType().name(), b.getPeriodStart().toString(), b.getPeriodEnd().toString(),
                b.getLines().size(), b.getTotalBudgetedRevenue().getAmount(),
                b.getTotalBudgetedExpense().getAmount());
    }

    public record CreateBudgetRequest(@NotBlank String name, @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            @NotBlank String periodType, @NotBlank String periodStart,
            @NotBlank String periodEnd) {}

    public record AddLineRequest(@NotBlank String accountCode, @NotBlank String accountName,
            @NotBlank String lineType, @NotNull BigDecimal budgetedAmount,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode, String notes) {}

    public record BudgetResp(String id, String name, String status, String currencyCode,
            String periodType, String periodStart, String periodEnd,
            int lineCount, BigDecimal totalRevenue, BigDecimal totalExpense) {}

    public record VarianceResp(String accountCode, String accountName, String lineType,
            BigDecimal budgeted, BigDecimal actual, BigDecimal variance,
            boolean favorable, double variancePct) {}
}
