package com.saas.budget.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Budget aggregate root.
 *
 * A budget represents planned financial performance for a period.
 * Budget lines map to GL account codes from ChartOfAccounts.
 *
 * Variance analysis:
 *   variance = actual - budget
 *   For expenses: negative variance = favorable (spent less than planned)
 *   For revenue:  positive variance = favorable (earned more than planned)
 *
 * Lifecycle: DRAFT → APPROVED → ACTIVE → CLOSED
 */
public class BudgetAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String name;
    private String currencyCode;
    private BudgetPeriodType periodType;
    private LocalDate periodStart;
    private LocalDate periodEnd;
    private BudgetStatus status;
    private List<BudgetLine> lines;
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static BudgetAggregate create(String tenantId, String name,
                                          String currencyCode, BudgetPeriodType periodType,
                                          LocalDate periodStart, LocalDate periodEnd,
                                          String actor) {
        BudgetAggregate b = new BudgetAggregate();
        b.id           = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        b.tenantId     = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        b.name         = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        b.currencyCode = Objects.requireNonNull(currencyCode);
        b.periodType   = Objects.requireNonNull(periodType);
        b.periodStart  = Objects.requireNonNull(periodStart);
        b.periodEnd    = Objects.requireNonNull(periodEnd);
        b.status       = BudgetStatus.DRAFT;
        b.lines        = new ArrayList<>();
        b.audit        = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return b;
    }

    public static BudgetAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String name, String currencyCode,
            BudgetPeriodType periodType, LocalDate start, LocalDate end,
            BudgetStatus status, List<BudgetLine> lines, AuditMeta audit) {
        BudgetAggregate b = new BudgetAggregate();
        b.id = id; b.tenantId = tenantId; b.name = name; b.currencyCode = currencyCode;
        b.periodType = periodType; b.periodStart = start; b.periodEnd = end;
        b.status = status; b.lines = new ArrayList<>(lines); b.audit = audit;
        return b;
    }

    private BudgetAggregate() {}

    // ── Line management ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public BudgetLine addLine(String accountCode, String accountName,
                               BudgetLineType lineType, Money budgetedAmount,
                               String notes, String actor) {
        assertEditable();
        // Update if account already exists
        Optional<BudgetLine> existing = lines.stream()
                .filter(l -> l.accountCode().equals(accountCode)).findFirst();
        if (existing.isPresent()) {
            lines.remove(existing.get());
        }
        BudgetLine line = new BudgetLine(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), accountCode, accountName,
                lineType, budgetedAmount, Money.zero(currencyCode), notes);
        lines.add(line);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return line;
    }

    public void updateActual(String accountCode, Money actual, String actor) {
        int idx = findLineIndex(accountCode);
        BudgetLine line = lines.get(idx);
        lines.set(idx, line.withActual(actual));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void approve(String actor) {
        if (status != BudgetStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only DRAFT budgets can be approved");
        if (lines.isEmpty())
            throw new IllegalStateException("Budget has no lines");
        this.status = BudgetStatus.APPROVED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void activate(String actor) {
        if (status != BudgetStatus.APPROVED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only APPROVED budgets can be activated");
        this.status = BudgetStatus.ACTIVE;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void close(String actor) {
        if (status != BudgetStatus.ACTIVE)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only ACTIVE budgets can be closed");
        this.status = BudgetStatus.CLOSED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Variance analysis ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public List<BudgetVarianceLine> getVarianceReport() {
        return lines.stream().map(line -> {
            Money variance = line.actual().subtract(
                    line.actual().isGreaterThan(line.budgeted())
                            ? line.budgeted() : line.actual());
            // Recalculate properly: actual - budget (can be negative)
            boolean actualGtBudget = line.actual().isGreaterThan(line.budgeted());
            Money abs = actualGtBudget
                    ? line.actual().subtract(line.budgeted())
                    : line.budgeted().subtract(line.actual());

            // Favorable if: revenue actual > budget OR expense actual < budget
            boolean favorable = switch (line.lineType()) {
                case REVENUE -> actualGtBudget;
                case EXPENSE, COGS -> !actualGtBudget;
            };

            double pct = line.budgeted().isZero() ? 0.0
                    : abs.getAmount().doubleValue()
                        / line.budgeted().getAmount().doubleValue() * 100;

            return new BudgetVarianceLine(
                    line.accountCode(), line.accountName(), line.lineType(),
                    line.budgeted(), line.actual(), abs, favorable, pct);
        }).toList();
    }

    public Money getTotalBudgetedRevenue() {
        return sumByType(BudgetLineType.REVENUE, true);
    }

    public Money getTotalBudgetedExpense() {
        return sumByType(BudgetLineType.EXPENSE, true)
                .add(sumByType(BudgetLineType.COGS, true));
    }

    public Money getBudgetedNetIncome() {
        return getTotalBudgetedRevenue().subtract(
                getTotalBudgetedRevenue().isGreaterThan(getTotalBudgetedExpense())
                        ? getTotalBudgetedExpense()
                        : getTotalBudgetedRevenue());
    }

    private Money sumByType(BudgetLineType type, boolean useBudget) {
        return lines.stream()
                .filter(l -> l.lineType() == type)
                .map(l -> useBudget ? l.budgeted() : l.actual())
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }

    private void assertEditable() {
        if (status == BudgetStatus.CLOSED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot modify a CLOSED budget");
    }

    private int findLineIndex(String accountCode) {
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++)
            if (lines.get(i).accountCode().equals(accountCode)) return i;
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Budget line not found: " + accountCode);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String          getId()           { return id; }
    public String          getTenantId()     { return tenantId; }
    public String          getName()         { return name; }
    public String          getCurrencyCode() { return currencyCode; }
    public BudgetPeriodType getPeriodType()  { return periodType; }
    public LocalDate        getPeriodStart() { return periodStart; }
    public LocalDate        getPeriodEnd()   { return periodEnd; }
    public BudgetStatus    getStatus()       { return status; }
    public List<BudgetLine> getLines()       { return Collections.unmodifiableList(lines); }
    public AuditMeta        getAudit()       { return audit; }

    // ── Nested types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public enum BudgetPeriodType { MONTHLY, QUARTERLY, ANNUAL, CUSTOM }
    public enum BudgetStatus     { DRAFT, APPROVED, ACTIVE, CLOSED }
    public enum BudgetLineType   { REVENUE, EXPENSE, COGS }

    public record BudgetLine(
            String id, String accountCode, String accountName,
            BudgetLineType lineType, Money budgeted, Money actual, String notes) {
        public BudgetLine withActual(Money newActual) {
            return new BudgetLine(id, accountCode, accountName,
                    lineType, budgeted, newActual, notes);
        }
    }

    public record BudgetVarianceLine(
            String accountCode, String accountName, BudgetLineType lineType,
            Money budgeted, Money actual, Money absoluteVariance,
            boolean isFavorable, double variancePercent) {}
}

Size: 3.8 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.budget.service;

import com.saas.accounting.service.AccountingService;
import com.saas.budget.core.BudgetAggregate;
import com.saas.budget.core.BudgetAggregate.*;
import com.saas.budget.spi.BudgetRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Budget service.
 *
 * Key feature: populateActuals() loads account balances from
 * AccountingService (GL) and writes them into budget lines automatically,
 * enabling real-time variance analysis without manual data entry.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class BudgetService {

    @Inject BudgetRepository   repository;
    @Inject AccountingService  accountingService;

    @Transactional
    public BudgetAggregate createBudget(String tenantId, String name,
                                         String currencyCode, BudgetPeriodType periodType,
                                         LocalDate start, LocalDate end, String actor) {
        return repository.save(BudgetAggregate.create(
                tenantId, name, currencyCode, periodType, start, end, actor));
    }

    @Transactional
    public BudgetAggregate addLine(String id, String tenantId,
                                    String accountCode, String accountName,
                                    BudgetLineType lineType, Money budgetedAmount,
                                    String notes, String actor) {
        BudgetAggregate budget = load(id, tenantId);
        budget.addLine(accountCode, accountName, lineType, budgetedAmount, notes, actor);
        return repository.save(budget);
    }

    @Transactional
    public BudgetAggregate approve(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        BudgetAggregate budget = load(id, tenantId);
        budget.approve(actor);
        return repository.save(budget);
    }

    @Transactional
    public BudgetAggregate activate(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        BudgetAggregate budget = load(id, tenantId);
        budget.activate(actor);
        return repository.save(budget);
    }

    /**
     * Populate budget lines with actual amounts from the GL (AccountingService).
     * Called at period-end for variance reporting.
     */
    @Transactional
    public BudgetAggregate populateActuals(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        BudgetAggregate budget = load(id, tenantId);

        for (BudgetLine line : budget.getLines()) {
            try {
                Money actual = accountingService.getAccountBalance(
                        tenantId, line.accountCode(),
                        budget.getPeriodStart(), budget.getPeriodEnd());
                budget.updateActual(line.accountCode(), actual, actor);
            } catch (Exception e) {
                // Account has no transactions — actual stays zero
            }
        }
        return repository.save(budget);
    }

    public Optional<BudgetAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public List<BudgetAggregate> findActive(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findActiveBudgets(tenantId, LocalDate.now());
    }

    public List<BudgetVarianceLine> getVariance(String id, String tenantId) {
        return load(id, tenantId).getVarianceReport();
    }

    private BudgetAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new BudgetNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    public static class BudgetNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public BudgetNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Budget not found: " + id + " / " + t);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.catalog.api.rest;

import com.saas.catalog.core.BrandAggregate;
import com.saas.catalog.core.CategoryAggregate;
import com.saas.catalog.service.CatalogService;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Catalog REST API.
 *
 * Categories:
 *   POST /api/v1/catalog/categories               → create root category
 *   POST /api/v1/catalog/categories/{id}/children → create child category
 *   GET  /api/v1/catalog/categories               → list root categories
 *   GET  /api/v1/catalog/categories/{id}/children → list children
 *   GET  /api/v1/catalog/categories/slug/{slug}   → find by slug
 *   PATCH /api/v1/catalog/categories/{id}         → rename
 *
 * Brands:
 *   POST /api/v1/catalog/brands                   → create brand
 *   GET  /api/v1/catalog/brands                   → list all brands
 *   GET  /api/v1/catalog/brands/slug/{slug}       → find by slug
 *   PATCH /api/v1/catalog/brands/{id}             → update
 */
@Path("/api/v1/catalog")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CatalogResource {

    @Inject CatalogService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    // ── Categories ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST @Path("/categories")
    public Response createRoot(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CategoryRequest req) {
        CategoryAggregate c = service.createRootCategory(
                tid(rawTid), req.slug(), req.name(), req.description(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toCatResp(c)).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/categories/{id}/children")
    public Response createChild(
            @PathParam("id") String parentId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ChildCategoryRequest req) {
        CategoryAggregate c = service.createChildCategory(
                tid(rawTid), req.slug(), req.name(), req.description(),
                parentId, req.displayOrder(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toCatResp(c)).build();
    }

    @GET @Path("/categories")
    public Response listRoots(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.getRootCategories(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toCatResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @GET @Path("/categories/{id}/children")
    public Response listChildren(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.getChildren(tid(rawTid), id)
                .stream().map(this::toCatResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @GET @Path("/categories/slug/{slug}")
    public Response getBySlug(
            @PathParam("slug") String slug,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findBySlug(slug, tid(rawTid))
                .map(c -> Response.ok(toCatResp(c)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @PATCH @Path("/categories/{id}")
    public Response renameCategory(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CategoryRequest req) {
        CategoryAggregate c = service.renameCategory(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.name(), req.description(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toCatResp(c)).build();
    }

    @PUT @Path("/categories/{id}/schema")
    public Response setSchema(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            Map<String, Object> schema) {
        CategoryAggregate c = service.setAttributeSchema(id, tid(rawTid), schema, actor());
        return Response.ok(toCatResp(c)).build();
    }

    @DELETE @Path("/categories/{id}")
    public Response deactivate(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        service.deactivateCategory(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.noContent().build();
    }

    // ── Brands ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST @Path("/brands")
    public Response createBrand(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid BrandRequest req) {
        BrandAggregate b = service.createBrand(
                tid(rawTid), req.slug(), req.name(),
                req.description(), req.countryOfOrigin(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toBrandResp(b)).build();
    }

    @GET @Path("/brands")
    public Response listBrands(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.getAllBrands(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toBrandResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @GET @Path("/brands/slug/{slug}")
    public Response getBrandBySlug(
            @PathParam("slug") String slug,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findBrandBySlug(slug, tid(rawTid))
                .map(b -> Response.ok(toBrandResp(b)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @PATCH @Path("/brands/{id}")
    public Response updateBrand(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid UpdateBrandRequest req) {
        BrandAggregate b = service.updateBrand(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.name(), req.description(),
                req.logoUrl(), req.websiteUrl(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toBrandResp(b)).build();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private CatResp toCatResp(CategoryAggregate c) {
        return new CatResp(c.getId(), c.getSlug(), c.getName(), c.getDescription(),
                c.getParentId(), c.getDisplayOrder(), c.isActive(),
                c.isRoot(), c.getImageUrl());
    }

    private BrandResp toBrandResp(BrandAggregate b) {
        return new BrandResp(b.getId(), b.getSlug(), b.getName(), b.getDescription(),
                b.getLogoUrl(), b.getWebsiteUrl(), b.getCountryOfOrigin(), b.isActive());
    }

    // ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record CategoryRequest(@NotBlank String slug, @NotBlank String name, String description) {}
    public record ChildCategoryRequest(@NotBlank String slug, @NotBlank String name,
            String description, int displayOrder) {}
    public record BrandRequest(@NotBlank String slug, @NotBlank String name,
            String description, String countryOfOrigin) {}
    public record UpdateBrandRequest(@NotBlank String name, String description,
            String logoUrl, String websiteUrl) {}

    public record CatResp(String id, String slug, String name, String description,
            String parentId, int displayOrder, boolean isActive, boolean isRoot, String imageUrl) {}
    public record BrandResp(String id, String slug, String name, String description,
            String logoUrl, String websiteUrl, String countryOfOrigin, boolean isActive) {}
}

package com.saas.catalog.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import java.util.*;

/** Brand registry — manufacturer/supplier brand associated with products. */
public class BrandAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String slug;
    private String name;
    private String description;
    private String logoUrl;
    private String websiteUrl;
    private String countryOfOrigin;
    private boolean isActive;
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static BrandAggregate create(String tenantId, String slug, String name,
                                         String description, String countryOfOrigin,
                                         String actor) {
        BrandAggregate b = new BrandAggregate();
        b.id              = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        b.tenantId        = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        b.slug            = Objects.requireNonNull(slug).toLowerCase().replaceAll("[^a-z0-9-]", "-");
        b.name            = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        b.description     = description;
        b.countryOfOrigin = countryOfOrigin;
        b.isActive        = true;
        b.audit           = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return b;
    }

    public static BrandAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String slug, String name, String description,
            String logoUrl, String websiteUrl, String countryOfOrigin,
            boolean isActive, AuditMeta audit) {
        BrandAggregate b = new BrandAggregate();
        b.id = id; b.tenantId = tenantId; b.slug = slug; b.name = name;
        b.description = description; b.logoUrl = logoUrl; b.websiteUrl = websiteUrl;
        b.countryOfOrigin = countryOfOrigin; b.isActive = isActive; b.audit = audit;
        return b;
    }

    private BrandAggregate() {}

    public void update(String name, String description, String logoUrl,
                        String websiteUrl, String actor) {
        this.name        = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        this.description = description;
        this.logoUrl     = logoUrl;
        this.websiteUrl  = websiteUrl;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void deactivate(String actor) { this.isActive = false; audit = audit.touch(actor); }

    public String getId()             { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()       { return tenantId; }
    public String getSlug()           { return slug; }
    public String getName()           { return name; }
    public String getDescription()    { return description; }
    public String getLogoUrl()        { return logoUrl; }
    public String getWebsiteUrl()     { return websiteUrl; }
    public String getCountryOfOrigin(){ return countryOfOrigin; }
    public boolean isActive()         { return isActive; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()       { return audit; }
}

package com.saas.catalog.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;

import java.util.*;

/**
 * Category aggregate — hierarchical product category tree.
 *
 * Supports unlimited nesting (root → ... → leaf).
 * Each category has:
 *  - A unique slug (URL-safe, immutable after creation)
 *  - Display order within its parent
 *  - Active/inactive status
 *  - Custom attributes schema (JSONB) for products in this category
 */
public class CategoryAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String slug;              // e.g. "electronics", "laptops"
    private String name;
    private String description;
    private String parentId;          // null = root category
    private String parentSlug;
    private int    displayOrder;
    private boolean isActive;
    private Map<String, Object> customAttributeSchema;  // JSON Schema for product attributes
    private String imageUrl;
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static CategoryAggregate createRoot(String tenantId, String slug, String name,
                                                String description, String actor) {
        CategoryAggregate c = new CategoryAggregate();
        c.id          = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        c.tenantId    = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        c.slug        = Objects.requireNonNull(slug).toLowerCase().replaceAll("[^a-z0-9-]", "-");
        c.name        = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        c.description = description;
        c.parentId    = null;
        c.displayOrder = 0;
        c.isActive    = true;
        c.customAttributeSchema = new HashMap<>();
        c.audit       = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return c;
    }

    public static CategoryAggregate createChild(String tenantId, String slug, String name,
                                                  String description, String parentId,
                                                  String parentSlug, int displayOrder,
                                                  String actor) {
        CategoryAggregate c = new CategoryAggregate();
        c.id           = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        c.tenantId     = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        c.slug         = Objects.requireNonNull(slug).toLowerCase().replaceAll("[^a-z0-9-]", "-");
        c.name         = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        c.description  = description;
        c.parentId     = Objects.requireNonNull(parentId);
        c.parentSlug   = parentSlug;
        c.displayOrder = displayOrder;
        c.isActive     = true;
        c.customAttributeSchema = new HashMap<>();
        c.audit        = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return c;
    }

    public static CategoryAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String slug, String name, String description,
            String parentId, String parentSlug, int displayOrder, boolean isActive,
            Map<String, Object> schema, String imageUrl, AuditMeta audit) {
        CategoryAggregate c = new CategoryAggregate();
        c.id = id; c.tenantId = tenantId; c.slug = slug; c.name = name;
        c.description = description; c.parentId = parentId; c.parentSlug = parentSlug;
        c.displayOrder = displayOrder; c.isActive = isActive;
        c.customAttributeSchema = schema != null ? schema : new HashMap<>();
        c.imageUrl = imageUrl; c.audit = audit;
        return c;
    }

    private CategoryAggregate() {}

    public void rename(String newName, String description, String actor) {
        this.name        = Objects.requireNonNull(newName);
        this.description = description;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void reorder(int displayOrder, String actor) {
        this.displayOrder = displayOrder;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setAttributeSchema(Map<String, Object> schema, String actor) {
        this.customAttributeSchema = schema != null ? schema : new HashMap<>();
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setImageUrl(String url, String actor) {
        this.imageUrl = url;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void deactivate(String actor) { this.isActive = false; audit = audit.touch(actor); }
    public void activate(String actor)   { this.isActive = true;  audit = audit.touch(actor); }

    public boolean isRoot()    { return parentId == null; }

    public String   getId()           { return id; }
    public String   getTenantId()     { return tenantId; }
    public String   getSlug()         { return slug; }
    public String   getName()         { return name; }
    public String   getDescription()  { return description; }
    public String   getParentId()     { return parentId; }
    public String   getParentSlug()   { return parentSlug; }
    public int      getDisplayOrder() { return displayOrder; }
    public boolean  isActive()        { return isActive; }
    public Map<String, Object> getCustomAttributeSchema() { return Collections.unmodifiableMap(customAttributeSchema); }
    public String   getImageUrl()     { return imageUrl; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()       { return audit; }
}

package com.saas.catalog.service;

import com.saas.catalog.core.BrandAggregate;
import com.saas.catalog.core.CategoryAggregate;
import com.saas.catalog.spi.CatalogRepository;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Catalog service — manages the product category tree and brand registry.
 *
 * Category tree structure:
 *   Electronics
 *     └── Laptops
 *           └── Gaming Laptops
 *     └── Smartphones
 *   Furniture
 *     └── Chairs
 *     └── Desks
 *
 * Products reference categoryId + brandId.
 * PricingService uses categoryId for bulk discount rules.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class CatalogService {

    @Inject CatalogRepository repository;

    // ── Categories ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public CategoryAggregate createRootCategory(String tenantId, String slug,
                                                 String name, String description,
                                                 String actor) {
        assertUniqueSlug(slug, tenantId);
        return repository.saveCategory(
                CategoryAggregate.createRoot(tenantId, slug, name, description, actor));
    }

    @Transactional
    public CategoryAggregate createChildCategory(String tenantId, String slug,
                                                   String name, String description,
                                                   String parentId, int displayOrder,
                                                   String actor) {
        assertUniqueSlug(slug, tenantId);
        CategoryAggregate parent = repository.findCategoryById(parentId, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CategoryNotFoundException(parentId));
        return repository.saveCategory(
                CategoryAggregate.createChild(tenantId, slug, name, description,
                        parentId, parent.getSlug(), displayOrder, actor));
    }

    @Transactional
    public CategoryAggregate renameCategory(String id, String tenantId,
                                             String name, String description,
                                             String actor) {
        CategoryAggregate cat = loadCategory(id, tenantId);
        cat.rename(name, description, actor);
        return repository.saveCategory(cat);
    }

    @Transactional
    public CategoryAggregate setAttributeSchema(String id, String tenantId,
                                                 Map<String, Object> schema,
                                                 String actor) {
        CategoryAggregate cat = loadCategory(id, tenantId);
        cat.setAttributeSchema(schema, actor);
        return repository.saveCategory(cat);
    }

    @Transactional
    public CategoryAggregate deactivateCategory(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        CategoryAggregate cat = loadCategory(id, tenantId);
        cat.deactivate(actor);
        return repository.saveCategory(cat);
    }

    public List<CategoryAggregate> getRootCategories(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findRootCategories(tenantId);
    }

    public List<CategoryAggregate> getChildren(String tenantId, String parentId) {
        return repository.findChildCategories(tenantId, parentId);
    }

    public Optional<CategoryAggregate> findBySlug(String slug, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findCategoryBySlug(slug, tenantId);
    }

    /** Returns the full category tree as a flat list (sorted by display order). */
    public List<CategoryAggregate> getAllCategories(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findAllCategories(tenantId);
    }

    // ── Brands ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public BrandAggregate createBrand(String tenantId, String slug, String name,
                                       String description, String countryOfOrigin,
                                       String actor) {
        if (repository.findBrandBySlug(slug.toLowerCase(), tenantId).isPresent())
            throw new DuplicateSlugException("Brand slug already exists: " + slug);
        return repository.saveBrand(
                BrandAggregate.create(tenantId, slug, name, description, countryOfOrigin, actor));
    }

    @Transactional
    public BrandAggregate updateBrand(String id, String tenantId, String name,
                                       String description, String logoUrl,
                                       String websiteUrl, String actor) {
        BrandAggregate brand = loadBrand(id, tenantId);
        brand.update(name, description, logoUrl, websiteUrl, actor);
        return repository.saveBrand(brand);
    }

    public List<BrandAggregate> getAllBrands(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findAllBrands(tenantId);
    }

    public Optional<BrandAggregate> findBrandBySlug(String slug, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findBrandBySlug(slug.toLowerCase(), tenantId);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private CategoryAggregate loadCategory(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findCategoryById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CategoryNotFoundException(id));
    }

    private BrandAggregate loadBrand(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findBrandById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new BrandNotFoundException(id));
    }

    private void assertUniqueSlug(String slug, String tenantId) {
        if (repository.findCategoryBySlug(slug.toLowerCase(), tenantId).isPresent())
            throw new DuplicateSlugException("Category slug already exists: " + slug);
    }

    public static class CategoryNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public CategoryNotFoundException(String id) { super("Category not found: " + id); }
    }

    public static class BrandNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public BrandNotFoundException(String id) { super("Brand not found: " + id); }
    }

    public static class DuplicateSlugException extends RuntimeException {
        public DuplicateSlugException(String msg) { super(msg); }
    }
}

package com.saas.currency.api.rest;

import com.saas.currency.core.ExchangeRateAggregate;
import com.saas.currency.core.ExchangeRateAggregate.RateType;
import com.saas.currency.service.CurrencyService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Currency REST API.
 *
 * POST /api/v1/currency/rates              → upsert exchange rate
 * GET  /api/v1/currency/rates              → list all rates for tenant
 * POST /api/v1/currency/convert            → convert amount between currencies
 * POST /api/v1/currency/revalue            → FX gain/loss revaluation
 */
@Path("/api/v1/currency")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CurrencyResource {

    @Inject CurrencyService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal(); return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/rates")
    public Response upsertRate(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid UpsertRateRequest req) {
        LocalDate date = req.effectiveDate() != null
                ? LocalDate.parse(req.effectiveDate()) : LocalDate.now();
        ExchangeRateAggregate agg = service.upsertRate(
                tid(rawTid), req.baseCurrency(), req.targetCurrency(),
                req.rate(), RateType.valueOf(req.rateType()),
                date, req.source() != null ? req.source() : "MANUAL", actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(agg)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/rates")
    public Response listAll(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<RateResponse> rates = service.findAll(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(rates).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/convert")
    public Response convert(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ConvertRequest req) {
        Money amount = Money.of(req.amount(), req.fromCurrency());
        LocalDate date = req.date() != null ? LocalDate.parse(req.date()) : LocalDate.now();
        Money converted = service.convert(tid(rawTid), amount, req.toCurrency(), date);
        return Response.ok(new ConvertResponse(
                req.amount(), req.fromCurrency(),
                converted.getAmount(), converted.getCurrencyCode(), date.toString())).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/revalue")
    public Response revalue(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid RevalueRequest req) {
        Money foreignAmt = Money.of(req.foreignAmount(), req.foreignCurrency());
        Money baseAmt    = Money.of(req.originalBaseAmount(), req.baseCurrency());
        LocalDate date   = req.revalDate() != null
                ? LocalDate.parse(req.revalDate()) : LocalDate.now();
        var result = service.revalue(tid(rawTid), foreignAmt, baseAmt, date);
        return Response.ok(new RevalResponse(
                result.foreignAmount().getAmount(), result.foreignAmount().getCurrencyCode(),
                result.originalBaseAmount().getAmount(), result.revaluedBaseAmount().getAmount(),
                result.fxGainLoss().getAmount(), result.isGain() ? "GAIN" : "LOSS",
                req.baseCurrency(), date.toString())).build();
    }

    private RateResponse toResp(ExchangeRateAggregate a) {
        return new RateResponse(a.getId(), a.getBaseCurrency(), a.getTargetCurrency(),
                a.getRates().stream().map(r -> new EntryResp(
                        r.rate(), r.type().name(), r.effectiveDate().toString(), r.source()))
                        .toList());
    }

    public record UpsertRateRequest(
            @NotBlank String baseCurrency, @NotBlank String targetCurrency,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal rate,
            @NotBlank String rateType, String effectiveDate, String source) {}

    public record ConvertRequest(
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal amount,
            @NotBlank String fromCurrency, @NotBlank String toCurrency, String date) {}

    public record RevalueRequest(
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal foreignAmount, @NotBlank String foreignCurrency,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal originalBaseAmount, @NotBlank String baseCurrency,
            String revalDate) {}

    public record RateResponse(String id, String base, String target, List<EntryResp> rates) {}
    public record EntryResp(BigDecimal rate, String type, String effectiveDate, String source) {}
    public record ConvertResponse(BigDecimal from, String fromCcy, BigDecimal to, String toCcy, String date) {}
    public record RevalResponse(BigDecimal foreign, String foreignCcy, BigDecimal original,
            BigDecimal revalued, BigDecimal fxGainLoss, String direction, String baseCcy, String date) {}
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/currency/core/ExchangeRateAggregate.java
Size: 6.0 KB | Modified: 2026-04-05 14:24:53

package com.saas.currency.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Currency exchange rate aggregate.
 *
 * Manages exchange rates between a base currency and target currencies.
 * Each tenant configures their base currency (e.g. IDR for Indonesian companies).
 *
 * Rate types:
 *  - SPOT    : current market rate
 *  - BUY     : bank buying rate (when you sell foreign currency to bank)
 *  - SELL    : bank selling rate (when you buy foreign currency from bank)
 *  - AVERAGE : mid-market average (used for accounting revaluation)
 */
public class ExchangeRateAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String baseCurrency;        // e.g. "IDR"
    private String targetCurrency;      // e.g. "USD"
    private List<RateEntry> rates;
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static ExchangeRateAggregate create(String tenantId,
                                                String baseCurrency,
                                                String targetCurrency,
                                                String actor) {
        var e = new ExchangeRateAggregate();
        e.id             = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        e.tenantId       = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        e.baseCurrency   = Objects.requireNonNull(baseCurrency).toUpperCase();
        e.targetCurrency = Objects.requireNonNull(targetCurrency).toUpperCase();
        e.rates          = new ArrayList<>();
        e.audit          = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return e;
    }

    public static ExchangeRateAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String base, String target,
            List<RateEntry> rates, AuditMeta audit) {
        var e = new ExchangeRateAggregate();
        e.id = id; e.tenantId = tenantId; e.baseCurrency = base;
        e.targetCurrency = target; e.rates = new ArrayList<>(rates); e.audit = audit;
        return e;
    }

    private ExchangeRateAggregate() {}

    // ── Rate management ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void addRate(BigDecimal rate, RateType type,
                         LocalDate effectiveDate, String source, String actor) {
        if (rate.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Exchange rate must be positive: " + rate);
        rates.add(new RateEntry(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), rate, type,
                effectiveDate, source));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Conversion ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Get the best matching rate for a date and type.
     * Falls back to AVERAGE if the specific type is not found.
     */
    public Optional<RateEntry> rateFor(LocalDate date, RateType type) {
        // Most recent rate on or before the date, matching type
        Optional<RateEntry> exact = rates.stream()
                .filter(r -> r.type() == type && !r.effectiveDate().isAfter(date))
                .max(Comparator.comparing(RateEntry::effectiveDate));

        if (exact.isPresent()) return exact;

        // Fallback: most recent AVERAGE rate
        return rates.stream()
                .filter(r -> r.type() == RateType.AVERAGE && !r.effectiveDate().isAfter(date))
                .max(Comparator.comparing(RateEntry::effectiveDate));
    }

    /**
     * Convert an amount from the target currency to the base currency.
     * e.g. 1 USD × 15500 = 15500 IDR (when base=IDR, target=USD)
     */
    public Money toBase(Money targetAmount, LocalDate date, RateType type) {
        assertTargetCurrency(targetAmount);
        RateEntry entry = rateFor(date, type)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new NoRateException(targetCurrency, baseCurrency, date));
        BigDecimal converted = targetAmount.getAmount()
                .multiply(entry.rate())
                .setScale(java.util.Currency.getInstance(baseCurrency).getDefaultFractionDigits(),
                        RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        return Money.of(converted, baseCurrency);
    }

    /**
     * Convert an amount from the base currency to the target currency.
     * e.g. 15500 IDR / 15500 = 1 USD
     */
    public Money fromBase(Money baseAmount, LocalDate date, RateType type) {
        if (!baseAmount.getCurrencyCode().equals(baseCurrency))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Amount currency must be " + baseCurrency);
        RateEntry entry = rateFor(date, type)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new NoRateException(baseCurrency, targetCurrency, date));
        BigDecimal converted = baseAmount.getAmount()
                .divide(entry.rate(), 4, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        return Money.of(converted, targetCurrency);
    }

    private void assertTargetCurrency(Money m) {
        if (!m.getCurrencyCode().equals(targetCurrency))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    "Amount currency must be " + targetCurrency + ", got " + m.getCurrencyCode());
    }

    public String getId()             { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()       { return tenantId; }
    public String getBaseCurrency()   { return baseCurrency; }
    public String getTargetCurrency() { return targetCurrency; }
    public List<RateEntry> getRates() { return Collections.unmodifiableList(rates); }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()       { return audit; }

    public enum RateType { SPOT, BUY, SELL, AVERAGE }

    public record RateEntry(
            String id, BigDecimal rate, RateType type,
            LocalDate effectiveDate, String source) {}

    public static class NoRateException extends RuntimeException {
        public NoRateException(String from, String to, LocalDate date) {
            super("No exchange rate from " + from + " to " + to + " for date " + date);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.currency.service;

import com.saas.currency.core.ExchangeRateAggregate;
import com.saas.currency.core.ExchangeRateAggregate.*;
import com.saas.currency.spi.ExchangeRateRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Currency exchange rate service.
 *
 * Key operations:
 *  - Upsert exchange rates (manual or API-sourced)
 *  - Convert amounts between currencies
 *  - Revaluation: calculate FX gain/loss on open foreign-currency balances
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class CurrencyService {

    @Inject ExchangeRateRepository repository;

    @Transactional
    public ExchangeRateAggregate upsertRate(String tenantId, String baseCurrency,
                                             String targetCurrency, BigDecimal rate,
                                             RateType type, LocalDate effectiveDate,
                                             String source, String actor) {
        ExchangeRateAggregate aggregate =
                repository.findByPair(tenantId, baseCurrency, targetCurrency)
                        .orElseGet(() -> ExchangeRateAggregate.create(
                                tenantId, baseCurrency, targetCurrency, actor));
        aggregate.addRate(rate, type, effectiveDate, source, actor);
        return repository.save(aggregate);
    }

    /**
     * Convert amount from one currency to another via base currency.
     * e.g. USD → EUR via IDR: USD→IDR then IDR→EUR
     */
    public Money convert(String tenantId, Money amount,
                          String toCurrency, LocalDate date) {
        String from = amount.getCurrencyCode();
        if (from.equals(toCurrency)) return amount;

        // Try direct pair
        Optional<ExchangeRateAggregate> direct =
                repository.findByPair(tenantId, toCurrency, from);
        if (direct.isPresent()) {
            return direct.get().toBase(amount, date, RateType.AVERAGE);
        }

        // Try inverse pair
        Optional<ExchangeRateAggregate> inverse =
                repository.findByPair(tenantId, from, toCurrency);
        if (inverse.isPresent()) {
            return inverse.get().fromBase(amount, date, RateType.AVERAGE);
        }

        throw new ExchangeRateAggregate.NoRateException(from, toCurrency, date);
    }

    /**
     * Revaluation: calculate FX gain/loss.
     * Used at period-end to restate foreign-currency balances at current rates.
     *
     * gain/loss = (current rate - original rate) × original amount in foreign CCY
     */
    public RevaluationResult revalue(String tenantId, Money originalForeignAmount,
                                      Money originalBaseAmount, LocalDate revalDate) {
        String foreignCcy = originalForeignAmount.getCurrencyCode();
        String baseCcy    = originalBaseAmount.getCurrencyCode();

        Money currentBase = convert(tenantId, originalForeignAmount, baseCcy, revalDate);

        boolean isGain = currentBase.isGreaterThan(originalBaseAmount);
        Money difference = isGain
                ? currentBase.subtract(originalBaseAmount)
                : originalBaseAmount.subtract(currentBase);

        return new RevaluationResult(
                originalForeignAmount, originalBaseAmount,
                currentBase, difference, isGain, revalDate);
    }

    public Optional<ExchangeRateAggregate> findByPair(String tenantId,
                                                        String base, String target) {
        return repository.findByPair(tenantId, base, target);
    }

    public List<ExchangeRateAggregate> findAll(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findAll(tenantId);
    }

    public record RevaluationResult(
            Money foreignAmount,
            Money originalBaseAmount,
            Money revaluedBaseAmount,
            Money fxGainLoss,
            boolean isGain,
            LocalDate revalDate) {}
}

package com.saas.customer.api.rest;

import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate;
import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate.CustomerType;
import com.saas.customer.service.CustomerService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

/**
 * Customer REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/customers                          → register
 * GET    /api/v1/customers/{id}                     → get
 * PATCH  /api/v1/customers/{id}/credit-limit        → set credit limit
 * PATCH  /api/v1/customers/{id}/segment             → set segment
 * PUT    /api/v1/customers/{id}/billing-address     → set billing address
 * DELETE /api/v1/customers/{id}                     → deactivate
 */
@Path("/api/v1/customers")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class CustomerResource {

    @Inject CustomerService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "anonymous";
    }

    @POST
    public Response register(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid RegisterRequest req) {
        CustomerAggregate c = service.register(
                tid(rawTid), req.name(), req.email(),
                CustomerType.valueOf(req.type()), req.currencyCode(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(c)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(c -> Response.ok(toResp(c)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @PATCH
    @Path("/{id}/credit-limit")
    public Response setCreditLimit(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreditLimitRequest req) {
        Money limit = Money.of(req.limitAmount(), req.currencyCode());
        CustomerAggregate c = service.setCreditLimit(
                id, tid(rawTid), limit, req.paymentTermsDays(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(c)).build();
    }

    @PATCH
    @Path("/{id}/segment")
    public Response setSegment(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            SegmentRequest req) {
        CustomerAggregate c = service.setSegment(id, tid(rawTid), req.segment(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(c)).build();
    }

    @PUT
    @Path("/{id}/billing-address")
    public Response setBillingAddress(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid AddressRequest req) {
        Address addr = Address.builder()
                .line1(req.line1()).line2(req.line2()).city(req.city())
                .stateProvince(req.state()).postalCode(req.postalCode())
                .countryCode(req.countryCode()).phone(req.phone())
                .recipientName(req.recipientName()).build();
        CustomerAggregate c = service.setBillingAddress(id, tid(rawTid), addr, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(c)).build();
    }

    @DELETE
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response deactivate(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        service.deactivate(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.noContent().build();
    }

    private CustomerResponse toResp(CustomerAggregate c) {
        return new CustomerResponse(
                c.getId(), c.getTenantId(), c.getName(),
                c.getEmail(), c.getPhone(), c.getType().name(),
                c.getTaxId(), c.getStatus().name(), c.getSegment(),
                c.getCreditLimit() != null ? c.getCreditLimit().getAmount() : null,
                c.getCurrentBalance().getAmount(), c.getCurrencyCode(),
                c.getPaymentTermsDays());
    }

    public record RegisterRequest(
            @NotBlank String name, @NotBlank String email,
            @NotBlank String type, @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record CreditLimitRequest(
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal limitAmount,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            @PositiveOrZero int paymentTermsDays) {}

    public record SegmentRequest(@NotBlank String segment) {}

    public record AddressRequest(
            @NotBlank String line1, String line2,
            @NotBlank String city, String state,
            String postalCode, @NotBlank String countryCode,
            String phone, String recipientName) {}

    public record CustomerResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String name,
            String email, String phone, String type,
            String taxId, String status, String segment,
            BigDecimal creditLimit, BigDecimal currentBalance,
            String currencyCode, int paymentTermsDays) {}
}

package com.saas.customer.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.util.*;

/**
 * Customer aggregate root.
 *
 * Covers B2C (individual) and B2B (company) customers.
 * Credit limit, payment terms, and segment drive order/invoice behavior.
 */
public class CustomerAggregate {

    // ── Identity ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private final String id;
    private final String tenantId;

    // ── Profile ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private String name;
    private String email;
    private String phone;
    private CustomerType type;           // INDIVIDUAL, COMPANY
    private String taxId;               // NPWP / VAT number
    private CustomerStatus status;

    // ── Addresses ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private Address billingAddress;
    private Address shippingAddress;     // null = same as billing

    // ── Commercial terms ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private String segment;             // "retail", "wholesale", "vip", "government"
    private Money  creditLimit;         // null = no credit (cash only)
    private Money  currentBalance;      // outstanding receivable balance
    private int    paymentTermsDays;    // net-30, net-60, etc.
    private String currencyCode;        // preferred transaction currency

    // ── Metadata ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    private Map<String, String> tags;
    private AuditMeta audit;

    // ── Factory ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static CustomerAggregate create(String tenantId, String name,
                                           String email, CustomerType type,
                                           String currencyCode, String actor) {
        var c = new CustomerAggregate();
        c.id              = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        c.tenantId        = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        c.name            = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        c.email           = email;
        c.type            = Objects.requireNonNull(type);
        c.status          = CustomerStatus.ACTIVE;
        c.segment         = "retail";
        c.currencyCode    = currencyCode != null ? currencyCode : "IDR";
        c.currentBalance  = Money.zero(c.currencyCode);
        c.paymentTermsDays = 0;
        c.tags            = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        c.audit           = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return c;
    }

    public static CustomerAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String name, String email, String phone,
            CustomerType type, String taxId, CustomerStatus status,
            Address billing, Address shipping,
            String segment, Money creditLimit, Money currentBalance,
            int paymentTermsDays, String currencyCode,
            Map<String, String> tags, AuditMeta audit) {
        var c = new CustomerAggregate();
        c.id = id; c.tenantId = tenantId; c.name = name; c.email = email;
        c.phone = phone; c.type = type; c.taxId = taxId; c.status = status;
        c.billingAddress = billing; c.shippingAddress = shipping;
        c.segment = segment; c.creditLimit = creditLimit;
        c.currentBalance = currentBalance; c.paymentTermsDays = paymentTermsDays;
        c.currencyCode = currencyCode; c.tags = tags; c.audit = audit;
        return c;
    }

    private CustomerAggregate() {}

    // ── Commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void updateProfile(String name, String email, String phone, String taxId, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        this.name  = name;
        this.email = email;
        this.phone = phone;
        this.taxId = taxId;
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setBillingAddress(Address addr, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        this.billingAddress = addr;
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setShippingAddress(Address addr, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        this.shippingAddress = addr;
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setCreditLimit(Money limit, int paymentTermsDays, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        this.creditLimit       = limit;
        this.paymentTermsDays  = paymentTermsDays;
        this.audit             = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setSegment(String segment, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        this.segment = segment;
        this.audit   = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    /** Increase outstanding balance when an invoice is issued */
    public void debitBalance(Money amount, String actor) {
        this.currentBalance = currentBalance.add(amount);
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    /** Decrease outstanding balance when payment is received */
    public void creditBalance(Money amount, String actor) {
        this.currentBalance = currentBalance.subtract(amount);
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void deactivate(String actor) {
        this.status = CustomerStatus.INACTIVE;
        this.audit  = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Queries ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public boolean hasAvailableCredit(Money orderTotal) {
        if (creditLimit == null) return false; // cash only
        Money used = currentBalance.add(orderTotal);
        return creditLimit.isGreaterOrEqual(used);
    }

    public boolean isCashOnly() { return creditLimit == null; }

    private void assertActive() {
        if (status != CustomerStatus.ACTIVE)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Customer is not active: " + id);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()              { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()        { return tenantId; }
    public String getName()            { return name; }
    public String getEmail()           { return email; }
    public String getPhone()           { return phone; }
    public CustomerType getType()      { return type; }
    public String getTaxId()           { return taxId; }
    public CustomerStatus getStatus()  { return status; }
    public Address getBillingAddress() { return billingAddress; }
    public Address getShippingAddress(){ return shippingAddress != null ? shippingAddress : billingAddress; }
    public String getSegment()         { return segment; }
    public Money getCreditLimit()      { return creditLimit; }
    public Money getCurrentBalance()   { return currentBalance; }
    public int getPaymentTermsDays()   { return paymentTermsDays; }
    public String getCurrencyCode()    { return currencyCode; }
    public Map<String, String> getTags(){ return Collections.unmodifiableMap(tags); }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()        { return audit; }

    public enum CustomerType   { INDIVIDUAL, COMPANY }
    public enum CustomerStatus { ACTIVE, INACTIVE, BLOCKED }
}

package com.saas.customer.infrastructure.entity;

import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheEntityBase;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

@Entity
@Table(name = "customers")
public class CustomerEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {

    @Id
    @Column(name = "id", length = 36, nullable = false, updatable = false)
    public String id;

    @Column(name = "tenant_id", length = 64, nullable = false, updatable = false)
    public String tenantId;

    @Column(name = "name", length = 512, nullable = false)
    public String name;

    @Column(name = "email", length = 320)
    public String email;

    @Column(name = "phone", length = 32)
    public String phone;

    @Column(name = "type", length = 20, nullable = false)
    public String type;

    @Column(name = "tax_id", length = 64)
    public String taxId;

    @Column(name = "status", length = 20, nullable = false)
    public String status;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "billing_address", columnDefinition = "jsonb")
    public Map<String, Object> billingAddress;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "shipping_address", columnDefinition = "jsonb")
    public Map<String, Object> shippingAddress;

    @Column(name = "segment", length = 64)
    public String segment;

    @Column(name = "credit_limit_amount", precision = 19, scale = 4)
    public BigDecimal creditLimitAmount;

    @Column(name = "credit_limit_ccy", length = 3)
    public String creditLimitCcy;

    @Column(name = "current_balance_amt", precision = 19, scale = 4, nullable = false)
    public BigDecimal currentBalanceAmt;

    @Column(name = "current_balance_ccy", length = 3, nullable = false)
    public String currentBalanceCcy;

    @Column(name = "payment_terms_days", nullable = false)
    public int paymentTermsDays;

    @Column(name = "currency_code", length = 3, nullable = false)
    public String currencyCode;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "tags", columnDefinition = "jsonb")
    public Map<String, String> tags;

    @Version
    @Column(name = "version", nullable = false)
    public long version;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    public Instant createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false)
    public Instant updatedAt;

    @Column(name = "created_by", length = 128)
    public String createdBy;

    @Column(name = "updated_by", length = 128)
    public String updatedBy;
}

package com.saas.customer.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate;
import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate.CustomerStatus;
import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate.CustomerType;
import com.saas.customer.infrastructure.entity.CustomerEntity;
import com.saas.customer.spi.CustomerRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Page;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Sort;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanacheCustomerRepository
        implements CustomerRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<CustomerEntity, String> {

    @Inject ObjectMapper mapper;

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CustomerAggregate save(CustomerAggregate c) {
        CustomerEntity e = toEntity(c);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) {
            getEntityManager().merge(e);
        } else {
            persist(e);
        }
        return c;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<CustomerAggregate> findByEmail(String email, String tenantId) {
        return find("email = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", email, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean existsByEmail(String email, String tenantId) {
        return count("email = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", email, tenantId) > 0;
    }

    @Override
    public List<CustomerAggregate> findBySegment(String tenantId, String segment,
                                                   int page, int size) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND segment = ?2",
                Sort.ascending("name"), tenantId, segment)
                .page(Page.of(page, size)).list()
                .stream().map(this::toDomain).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public long count(String tenantId) {
        return count("tenantId = ?1", tenantId);
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private CustomerAggregate toDomain(CustomerEntity e) {
        Address billing  = e.billingAddress  != null ? addrFromMap(e.billingAddress)  : null;
        Address shipping = e.shippingAddress != null ? addrFromMap(e.shippingAddress) : null;

        Money creditLimit = e.creditLimitAmount != null
                ? Money.of(e.creditLimitAmount, e.creditLimitCcy) : null;
        Money balance = Money.of(
                e.currentBalanceAmt != null ? e.currentBalanceAmt : BigDecimal.ZERO,
                e.currentBalanceCcy);

        AuditMeta audit = rebuildAudit(e);

        return CustomerAggregate.reconstitute(
                e.id, e.tenantId, e.name, e.email, e.phone,
                CustomerType.valueOf(e.type), e.taxId,
                CustomerStatus.valueOf(e.status),
                billing, shipping, e.segment,
                creditLimit, balance, e.paymentTermsDays,
                e.currencyCode,
                e.tags != null ? e.tags : Map.of(),
                audit);
    }

    private CustomerEntity toEntity(CustomerAggregate c) {
        CustomerEntity e = new CustomerEntity();
        e.id              = c.getId();
        e.tenantId        = c.getTenantId();
        e.name            = c.getName();
        e.email           = c.getEmail();
        e.phone           = c.getPhone();
        e.type            = c.getType().name();
        e.taxId           = c.getTaxId();
        e.status          = c.getStatus().name();
        e.segment         = c.getSegment();
        e.currencyCode    = c.getCurrencyCode();
        e.paymentTermsDays = c.getPaymentTermsDays();
        e.currentBalanceAmt = c.getCurrentBalance().getAmount();
        e.currentBalanceCcy = c.getCurrentBalance().getCurrencyCode();
        e.tags            = c.getTags();
        e.version         = c.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt       = c.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt       = c.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy       = c.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy       = c.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();

        if (c.getCreditLimit() != null) {
            e.creditLimitAmount = c.getCreditLimit().getAmount();
            e.creditLimitCcy    = c.getCreditLimit().getCurrencyCode();
        }
        if (c.getBillingAddress() != null)
            e.billingAddress  = addrToMap(c.getBillingAddress());
        if (c.getShippingAddress() != null)
            e.shippingAddress = addrToMap(c.getShippingAddress());
        return e;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private Address addrFromMap(Map<String, Object> m) {
        return Address.builder()
                .line1((String) m.get("line1"))
                .line2((String) m.get("line2"))
                .city((String) m.get("city"))
                .stateProvince((String) m.get("stateProvince"))
                .postalCode((String) m.get("postalCode"))
                .countryCode((String) m.getOrDefault("countryCode", "ID"))
                .phone((String) m.get("phone"))
                .recipientName((String) m.get("recipientName"))
                .build();
    }

    private Map<String, Object> addrToMap(Address a) {
        return Map.of(
                "line1",         a.getLine1() != null ? a.getLine1() : "",
                "line2",         a.getLine2() != null ? a.getLine2() : "",
                "city",          a.getCity() != null ? a.getCity() : "",
                "stateProvince", a.getStateProvince() != null ? a.getStateProvince() : "",
                "postalCode",    a.getPostalCode() != null ? a.getPostalCode() : "",
                "countryCode",   a.getCountryCode() != null ? a.getCountryCode() : "ID",
                "phone",         a.getPhone() != null ? a.getPhone() : "",
                "recipientName", a.getRecipientName() != null ? a.getRecipientName() : "");
    }

    private AuditMeta rebuildAudit(CustomerEntity e) {
        // Reconstitute AuditMeta from entity fields using reflection-free factory
        // We use create() and then touch() to set all fields correctly
        return AuditMeta.reconstitute(
                e.createdAt, e.updatedAt, e.createdBy, e.updatedBy, e.version);
    }
}

package com.saas.customer.service;

import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate;
import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate.CustomerType;
import com.saas.customer.events.CustomerEvent;
import com.saas.customer.spi.CustomerRepository;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class CustomerService {

    @Inject CustomerRepository repository;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    @Transactional
    public CustomerAggregate register(String tenantId, String name, String email,
                                      CustomerType type, String currencyCode, String actor) {
        if (repository.existsByEmail(email, tenantId))
            throw new DuplicateEmailException(email, tenantId);

        CustomerAggregate customer = CustomerAggregate.create(
                tenantId, name, email, type, currencyCode, actor);
        repository.save(customer);

        publisher.publish(new CustomerEvent.CustomerRegistered(
                customer.getId(), tenantId, name, email,
                type.name(), actor, Instant.now()));
        return customer;
    }

    @Transactional
    public CustomerAggregate setCreditLimit(String id, String tenantId,
                                            Money limit, int termsDays, String actor) {
        CustomerAggregate c = load(id, tenantId);
        c.setCreditLimit(limit, termsDays, actor);
        repository.save(c);
        publisher.publish(new CustomerEvent.CreditLimitSet(
                id, tenantId, limit.toString(), termsDays, actor, Instant.now()));
        return c;
    }

    @Transactional
    public CustomerAggregate setBillingAddress(String id, String tenantId,
                                               Address addr, String actor) {
        CustomerAggregate c = load(id, tenantId);
        c.setBillingAddress(addr, actor);
        return repository.save(c);
    }

    @Transactional
    public CustomerAggregate setShippingAddress(String id, String tenantId,
                                                Address addr, String actor) {
        CustomerAggregate c = load(id, tenantId);
        c.setShippingAddress(addr, actor);
        return repository.save(c);
    }

    @Transactional
    public CustomerAggregate setSegment(String id, String tenantId,
                                        String segment, String actor) {
        CustomerAggregate c = load(id, tenantId);
        c.setSegment(segment, actor);
        return repository.save(c);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void deactivate(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        CustomerAggregate c = load(id, tenantId);
        c.deactivate(actor);
        repository.save(c);
        publisher.publish(new CustomerEvent.CustomerDeactivated(id, tenantId, actor, Instant.now()));
    }

    public Optional<CustomerAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public Optional<CustomerAggregate> findByEmail(String email, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findByEmail(email, tenantId);
    }

    private CustomerAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new CustomerNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    // ── Domain exceptions ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class CustomerNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public CustomerNotFoundException(String id, String tenantId) {
            super("Customer not found: id=" + id + ", tenant=" + tenantId);
        }
    }

    public static class DuplicateEmailException extends RuntimeException {
        public DuplicateEmailException(String email, String tenantId) {
            super("Email already registered: " + email + " in tenant " + tenantId);
        }
    }
}
