package com.saas.employee.api.rest;

import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate;
import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate.*;
import com.saas.employee.service.EmployeeService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Employee REST API.
 *
 * POST /api/v1/employees               → hire employee
 * GET  /api/v1/employees/{id}          → get employee
 * GET  /api/v1/employees/active        → list active employees (paginated)
 * GET  /api/v1/employees/dept/{deptId} → employees in department
 * PATCH /api/v1/employees/{id}/salary  → revise salary
 * POST /api/v1/employees/{id}/transfer → transfer to new department/position
 * POST /api/v1/employees/{id}/terminate → resign or terminate
 * PATCH /api/v1/employees/{id}/bank   → set bank details
 */
@Path("/api/v1/employees")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class EmployeeResource {

    @Inject EmployeeService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "hr-system";
    }

    @POST
    public Response hire(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid HireRequest req) {
        Money salary = Money.of(req.basicSalary(), req.currencyCode());
        EmployeeAggregate emp = service.hire(
                tid(rawTid), req.firstName(), req.lastName(), req.email(),
                req.departmentId(), req.departmentName(), req.positionTitle(),
                EmploymentType.valueOf(req.employmentType()),
                LocalDate.parse(req.hireDate()), salary, actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(emp)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(e -> Response.ok(toResp(e)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/active")
    public Response listActive(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("page") @DefaultValue("0") int page,
            @QueryParam("size") @DefaultValue("20") int size) {
        List<EmpResponse> employees = service.findActive(tid(rawTid), page, size)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(employees).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/dept/{deptId}")
    public Response listByDepartment(
            @PathParam("deptId") String deptId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.findByDepartment(tid(rawTid), deptId)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @PATCH
    @Path("/{id}/salary")
    public Response reviseSalary(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid SalaryRequest req) {
        Money newSalary = Money.of(req.basicSalary(), req.currencyCode());
        EmployeeAggregate emp = service.reviseSalary(id, tid(rawTid), newSalary, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(emp)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/transfer")
    public Response transfer(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid TransferRequest req) {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = service.transfer(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.departmentId(), req.departmentName(),
                req.positionId(), req.positionTitle(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(emp)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/terminate")
    public Response terminate(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid TerminateRequest req) {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = service.terminate(
                id, tid(rawTid), LocalDate.parse(req.effectiveDate()),
                req.reason(), req.isResignation(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(emp)).build();
    }

    @PATCH
    @Path("/{id}/bank")
    public Response setBankDetails(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid BankRequest req) {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = service.setBankDetails(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.bankAccount(), req.bankName(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(emp)).build();
    }

    private EmpResponse toResp(EmployeeAggregate e) {
        return new EmpResponse(
                e.getId(), e.getTenantId(), e.getEmployeeNumber(),
                e.getFullName(), e.getEmail(), e.getPhone(),
                e.getDepartmentName(), e.getPositionTitle(),
                e.getEmploymentType().name(), e.getStatus().name(),
                e.getHireDate().toString(),
                e.getTerminationDate() != null ? e.getTerminationDate().toString() : null,
                e.getBasicSalary().getAmount(), e.getCurrencyCode(),
                e.getBankAccount(), e.getBankName(),
                e.isEmployed(), e.getLengthOfServiceDays());
    }

    public record HireRequest(
            @NotBlank String firstName, @NotBlank String lastName,
            String email, String departmentId, @NotBlank String departmentName,
            @NotBlank String positionTitle, @NotBlank String employmentType,
            @NotBlank String hireDate, @NotNull BigDecimal basicSalary,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record SalaryRequest(
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal basicSalary, @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record TransferRequest(
            @NotBlank String departmentId, @NotBlank String departmentName,
            String positionId, @NotBlank String positionTitle) {}

    public record TerminateRequest(
            @NotBlank String effectiveDate, String reason, boolean isResignation) {}

    public record BankRequest(@NotBlank String bankAccount, @NotBlank String bankName) {}

    public record EmpResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String employeeNumber, String fullName,
            String email, String phone, String departmentName, String positionTitle,
            String employmentType, String status, String hireDate,
            String terminationDate, BigDecimal basicSalary, String currencyCode,
            String bankAccount, String bankName, boolean isEmployed, long servicedays) {}
}

package com.saas.employee.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.time.*;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Employee aggregate root.
 *
 * Covers:
 *  - Personal data (name, DOB, national ID, contact)
 *  - Employment data (department, position, employment type)
 *  - Contract terms (salary, start/end date)
 *  - Status lifecycle (ACTIVE, ON_LEAVE, RESIGNED, TERMINATED)
 *
 * Sensitive fields (salary, bank account) are treated as
 * write-once-read-carefully — accessed only by payroll module.
 */
public class EmployeeAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String employeeNumber;      // e.g. "EMP-0001"
    private String firstName;
    private String lastName;
    private String email;
    private String phone;
    private LocalDate dateOfBirth;
    private String  nationalId;         // KTP number
    private String  taxId;              // NPWP
    private Address homeAddress;

    // Employment
    private String departmentId;
    private String departmentName;
    private String positionId;
    private String positionTitle;
    private EmploymentType employmentType;
    private EmployeeStatus status;
    private LocalDate hireDate;
    private LocalDate terminationDate;
    private String terminationReason;

    // Contract & compensation
    private Money  basicSalary;
    private String currencyCode;
    private int    payPeriodDays;       // 30 = monthly
    private String bankAccount;
    private String bankName;
    private String bpjsKetenagakerjaanNo;
    private String bpjsKesehatanNo;

    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static EmployeeAggregate create(String tenantId, String employeeNumber,
                                            String firstName, String lastName,
                                            String email, String departmentId,
                                            String departmentName, String positionTitle,
                                            EmploymentType type, LocalDate hireDate,
                                            Money basicSalary, String actor) {
        EmployeeAggregate e = new EmployeeAggregate();
        e.id               = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        e.tenantId         = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        e.employeeNumber   = Objects.requireNonNull(employeeNumber);
        e.firstName        = Objects.requireNonNull(firstName);
        e.lastName         = Objects.requireNonNull(lastName);
        e.email            = email;
        e.departmentId     = departmentId;
        e.departmentName   = departmentName;
        e.positionTitle    = positionTitle;
        e.employmentType   = Objects.requireNonNull(type);
        e.status           = EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE;
        e.hireDate         = Objects.requireNonNull(hireDate);
        e.basicSalary      = Objects.requireNonNull(basicSalary);
        e.currencyCode     = basicSalary.getCurrencyCode();
        e.payPeriodDays    = 30;
        e.audit            = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return e;
    }

    public static EmployeeAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String empNo, String first, String last,
            String email, String phone, LocalDate dob, String natId, String taxId,
            Address addr, String deptId, String deptName, String posId, String posTitle,
            EmploymentType empType, EmployeeStatus status, LocalDate hireDate,
            LocalDate termDate, String termReason, Money salary, String ccy,
            int payPeriod, String bank, String bankName, String bpjsKt, String bpjsKs,
            AuditMeta audit) {
        EmployeeAggregate e = new EmployeeAggregate();
        e.id = id; e.tenantId = tenantId; e.employeeNumber = empNo;
        e.firstName = first; e.lastName = last; e.email = email; e.phone = phone;
        e.dateOfBirth = dob; e.nationalId = natId; e.taxId = taxId; e.homeAddress = addr;
        e.departmentId = deptId; e.departmentName = deptName; e.positionId = posId;
        e.positionTitle = posTitle; e.employmentType = empType; e.status = status;
        e.hireDate = hireDate; e.terminationDate = termDate; e.terminationReason = termReason;
        e.basicSalary = salary; e.currencyCode = ccy; e.payPeriodDays = payPeriod;
        e.bankAccount = bank; e.bankName = bankName; e.bpjsKetenagakerjaanNo = bpjsKt;
        e.bpjsKesehatanNo = bpjsKs; e.audit = audit;
        return e;
    }

    private EmployeeAggregate() {}

    // ── Commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void updatePersonalInfo(String firstName, String lastName, String email,
                                    String phone, LocalDate dob, String nationalId,
                                    String taxId, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        this.firstName  = firstName;
        this.lastName   = lastName;
        this.email      = email;
        this.phone      = phone;
        this.dateOfBirth = dob;
        this.nationalId = nationalId;
        this.taxId      = taxId;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void transfer(String newDeptId, String newDeptName,
                          String newPositionId, String newPositionTitle, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        this.departmentId   = newDeptId;
        this.departmentName = newDeptName;
        this.positionId     = newPositionId;
        this.positionTitle  = newPositionTitle;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void reviseSalary(Money newSalary, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        if (!newSalary.getCurrencyCode().equals(currencyCode))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Salary currency must be " + currencyCode);
        this.basicSalary = newSalary;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setBankDetails(String account, String bankName, String actor) {
        this.bankAccount = account;
        this.bankName    = bankName;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setBpjsNumbers(String ktNo, String ksNo, String actor) {
        this.bpjsKetenagakerjaanNo = ktNo;
        this.bpjsKesehatanNo       = ksNo;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void putOnLeave(String actor) {
        if (status != EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only ACTIVE employees can go on leave");
        this.status = EmployeeStatus.ON_LEAVE;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void returnFromLeave(String actor) {
        if (status != EmployeeStatus.ON_LEAVE)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Employee is not on leave");
        this.status = EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void resign(LocalDate effectiveDate, String reason, String actor) {
        assertActive();
        this.status             = EmployeeStatus.RESIGNED;
        this.terminationDate    = effectiveDate;
        this.terminationReason  = reason;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void terminate(LocalDate effectiveDate, String reason, String actor) {
        if (status == EmployeeStatus.TERMINATED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Already terminated");
        this.status            = EmployeeStatus.TERMINATED;
        this.terminationDate   = effectiveDate;
        this.terminationReason = reason;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Computed ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getFullName()           { return firstName + " " + lastName; }
    public boolean isEmployed()           { return status == EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE
                                                || status == EmployeeStatus.ON_LEAVE; }
    public long getLengthOfServiceDays()  {
        LocalDate end = terminationDate != null ? terminationDate : LocalDate.now();
        return hireDate.until(end).getDays() + hireDate.until(end).getMonths() * 30L
                + hireDate.until(end).getYears() * 365L;
    }

    private void assertActive() {
        if (status != EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Operation requires ACTIVE employee, was: " + status);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                 { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()           { return tenantId; }
    public String getEmployeeNumber()     { return employeeNumber; }
    public String getFirstName()          { return firstName; }
    public String getLastName()           { return lastName; }
    public String getEmail()              { return email; }
    public String getPhone()              { return phone; }
    public LocalDate getDateOfBirth()     { return dateOfBirth; }
    public String getNationalId()         { return nationalId; }
    public String getTaxId()              { return taxId; }
    public Address getHomeAddress()       { return homeAddress; }
    public String getDepartmentId()       { return departmentId; }
    public String getDepartmentName()     { return departmentName; }
    public String getPositionTitle()      { return positionTitle; }
    public EmploymentType getEmploymentType() { return employmentType; }
    public EmployeeStatus getStatus()     { return status; }
    public LocalDate getHireDate()        { return hireDate; }
    public LocalDate getTerminationDate() { return terminationDate; }
    public String getTerminationReason()  { return terminationReason; }
    public Money getBasicSalary()         { return basicSalary; }
    public String getCurrencyCode()       { return currencyCode; }
    public int getPayPeriodDays()         { return payPeriodDays; }
    public String getBankAccount()        { return bankAccount; }
    public String getBankName()           { return bankName; }
    public String getBpjsKetenagakerjaanNo() { return bpjsKetenagakerjaanNo; }
    public String getBpjsKesehatanNo()    { return bpjsKesehatanNo; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()           { return audit; }

    public enum EmploymentType { PERMANENT, CONTRACT, PROBATION, INTERN, PART_TIME }
    public enum EmployeeStatus { ACTIVE, ON_LEAVE, RESIGNED, TERMINATED }
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/employee/service/EmployeeService.java
Size: 4.5 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.employee.service;

import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate;
import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate.*;
import com.saas.employee.spi.EmployeeRepository;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

@ApplicationScoped
public class EmployeeService {

    @Inject EmployeeRepository repository;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    private static final AtomicLong EMP_COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    @Transactional
    public EmployeeAggregate hire(String tenantId, String firstName, String lastName,
                                   String email, String departmentId, String departmentName,
                                   String positionTitle, EmploymentType type,
                                   LocalDate hireDate, Money basicSalary,
                                   String actor) {
        if (repository.existsByEmployeeNumber(peekNextNumber(tenantId), tenantId))
            throw new IllegalStateException("Employee number conflict");

        String number = generateNumber(tenantId);
        EmployeeAggregate emp = EmployeeAggregate.create(
                tenantId, number, firstName, lastName, email,
                departmentId, departmentName, positionTitle,
                type, hireDate, basicSalary, actor);
        return repository.save(emp);
    }

    @Transactional
    public EmployeeAggregate reviseSalary(String id, String tenantId,
                                           Money newSalary, String actor) {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = load(id, tenantId);
        emp.reviseSalary(newSalary, actor);
        return repository.save(emp);
    }

    @Transactional
    public EmployeeAggregate transfer(String id, String tenantId,
                                       String deptId, String deptName,
                                       String posId, String posTitle, String actor) {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = load(id, tenantId);
        emp.transfer(deptId, deptName, posId, posTitle, actor);
        return repository.save(emp);
    }

    @Transactional
    public EmployeeAggregate terminate(String id, String tenantId,
                                        LocalDate effectiveDate, String reason,
                                        boolean isResignation, String actor) {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = load(id, tenantId);
        if (isResignation) emp.resign(effectiveDate, reason, actor);
        else               emp.terminate(effectiveDate, reason, actor);
        return repository.save(emp);
    }

    @Transactional
    public EmployeeAggregate setBankDetails(String id, String tenantId,
                                             String account, String bank, String actor) {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = load(id, tenantId);
        emp.setBankDetails(account, bank, actor);
        return repository.save(emp);
    }

    public Optional<EmployeeAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public List<EmployeeAggregate> findByDepartment(String tenantId, String deptId) {
        return repository.findByDepartment(tenantId, deptId);
    }

    public List<EmployeeAggregate> findActive(String tenantId, int page, int size) {
        return repository.findByStatus(tenantId, EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE, page, size);
    }

    public long countActive(String tenantId) {
        return repository.countActive(tenantId);
    }

    private EmployeeAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new EmployeeNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    private String peekNextNumber(String tenantId) {
        String prefix = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        return "EMP-" + prefix + "-" + String.format("%05d", EMP_COUNTER.get());
    }

    private String generateNumber(String tenantId) {
        String prefix = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        return "EMP-" + prefix + "-" + String.format("%05d", EMP_COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
    }

    public static class EmployeeNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public EmployeeNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Employee not found: " + id + " / " + t);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.fixedasset.api.rest;

import com.saas.fixedasset.core.FixedAssetAggregate;
import com.saas.fixedasset.core.FixedAssetAggregate.*;
import com.saas.fixedasset.service.FixedAssetService;
import com.saas.fixedasset.service.FixedAssetService.DepreciationRunResult;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Fixed Asset REST API.
 *
 * POST /api/v1/assets              → register asset
 * GET  /api/v1/assets/{id}         → get asset
 * GET  /api/v1/assets              → list active assets
 * POST /api/v1/assets/{id}/service → put in service
 * POST /api/v1/assets/{id}/assign  → assign to dept/employee
 * POST /api/v1/assets/{id}/depreciate → single asset depreciation
 * POST /api/v1/assets/depreciate/{period} → run monthly depreciation
 * POST /api/v1/assets/{id}/dispose → dispose asset
 */
@Path("/api/v1/assets")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class FixedAssetResource {

    @Inject FixedAssetService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @POST
    public Response register(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid RegisterAssetRequest req) {
        Money cost    = Money.of(req.acquisitionCost(), req.currencyCode());
        Money salvage = req.salvageValue() != null
                ? Money.of(req.salvageValue(), req.currencyCode())
                : Money.zero(req.currencyCode());
        FixedAssetAggregate asset = service.register(
                tid(rawTid), req.name(),
                AssetCategory.valueOf(req.category()), cost, salvage,
                LocalDate.parse(req.acquisitionDate()),
                DepreciationMethod.valueOf(req.depreciationMethod()),
                req.usefulLifeYears(), req.decliningRate(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(asset)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(a -> Response.ok(toDetailResp(a)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @GET
    public Response listActive(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.findActive(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/service")
    public Response putInService(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("date") String date) {
        LocalDate inServiceDate = date != null ? LocalDate.parse(date) : LocalDate.now();
        FixedAssetAggregate asset = service.putInService(id, tid(rawTid), inServiceDate, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(asset)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/assign")
    public Response assign(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            AssignRequest req) {
        FixedAssetAggregate asset = service.assign(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.warehouseId(), req.departmentId(), req.employeeId(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(asset)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/depreciate")
    public Response depreciateOne(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("periodEnd") String periodEnd) {
        LocalDate date = periodEnd != null ? LocalDate.parse(periodEnd) : LocalDate.now();
        DepreciationEntry entry = service.depreciateAsset(id, tid(rawTid), date, actor());
        return Response.ok(new DeprResponse(
                entry.id(), date.toString(),
                entry.depreciationAmount().getAmount(),
                entry.bookValueAfter().getAmount(),
                entry.depreciationAmount().getCurrencyCode())).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/depreciate/{period}")
    public Response runMonthly(
            @PathParam("period") String period,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        DepreciationRunResult result = service.runMonthlyDepreciation(
                tid(rawTid), YearMonth.parse(period), actor());
        return Response.ok(result).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/dispose")
    public Response dispose(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid DisposeRequest req) {
        Money proceeds = Money.of(req.proceeds(), req.currencyCode());
        DisposalResult result = service.disposeAsset(
                id, tid(rawTid),
                LocalDate.parse(req.disposalDate()), proceeds, req.reason(), actor());
        return Response.ok(new DisposeResponse(
                result.bookValue().getAmount(), result.proceeds().getAmount(),
                result.gainLoss().getAmount(), result.isGain() ? "GAIN" : "LOSS",
                result.disposalDate().toString())).build();
    }

    private AssetResp toResp(FixedAssetAggregate a) {
        return new AssetResp(
                a.getId(), a.getAssetNumber(), a.getName(), a.getCategory().name(),
                a.getStatus().name(), a.getAcquisitionCost().getAmount(),
                a.getSalvageValue().getAmount(), a.getBookValue().getAmount(),
                a.getAccumulatedDepreciation().getAmount(),
                a.getAcquisitionCost().getCurrencyCode(),
                a.getDepreciationMethod().name(), a.getUsefulLifeYears(),
                a.isFullyDepreciated(), a.getAgeInYears());
    }

    private AssetDetailResp toDetailResp(FixedAssetAggregate a) {
        return new AssetDetailResp(
                toResp(a), a.getDepartmentId(), a.getAssignedToEmployeeId(),
                a.getAcquisitionDate().toString(),
                a.getInServiceDate() != null ? a.getInServiceDate().toString() : null,
                a.getDepreciationHistory().size());
    }

    public record RegisterAssetRequest(
            @NotBlank String name, @NotBlank String category,
            @NotNull BigDecimal acquisitionCost, BigDecimal salvageValue,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode, @NotBlank String acquisitionDate,
            @NotBlank String depreciationMethod, @Positive int usefulLifeYears,
            BigDecimal decliningRate) {}

    public record AssignRequest(String warehouseId, String departmentId, String employeeId) {}
    public record DisposeRequest(@NotBlank String disposalDate,
            @NotNull BigDecimal proceeds, @NotBlank String currencyCode, String reason) {}

    public record AssetResp(String id, String assetNumber, String name, String category,
            String status, BigDecimal cost, BigDecimal salvage, BigDecimal bookValue,
            BigDecimal accumulated, String ccy, String depreciationMethod,
            int usefulLifeYears, boolean fullyDepreciated, int ageYears) {}

    public record AssetDetailResp(AssetResp summary, String departmentId,
            String assignedToEmployee, String acquisitionDate,
            String inServiceDate, int depreciationEntries) {}

    public record DeprResponse(String entryId, String periodEnd,
            BigDecimal depreciationAmount, BigDecimal bookValueAfter, String ccy) {}

    public record DisposeResponse(BigDecimal bookValue, BigDecimal proceeds,
            BigDecimal gainLoss, String direction, String disposalDate) {}
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/fixedasset/core/FixedAssetAggregate.java

package com.saas.fixedasset.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Fixed Asset aggregate root.
 *
 * Lifecycle: DRAFT → ACTIVE → DISPOSED / WRITTEN_OFF
 *
 * Depreciation methods:
 *  - STRAIGHT_LINE    : (cost - salvage) / useful life years
 *  - DECLINING_BALANCE: book value × depreciation rate %
 *  - UNITS_OF_PRODUCTION: (cost - salvage) / total units × units used
 *
 * Each depreciation run creates an immutable DepreciationEntry.
 * The accounting journal (DR Depreciation Expense / CR Accumulated Depreciation)
 * is posted by AccountingService on each depreciation run.
 */
public class FixedAssetAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String assetNumber;          // e.g. "FA-IT-001"
    private String name;
    private String description;
    private AssetCategory category;
    private AssetStatus status;

    // Cost
    private Money   acquisitionCost;
    private Money   salvageValue;
    private LocalDate acquisitionDate;
    private LocalDate inServiceDate;

    // Depreciation configuration
    private DepreciationMethod depreciationMethod;
    private int    usefulLifeYears;
    private BigDecimal decliningRate;       // for DECLINING_BALANCE (e.g. 0.20 = 20%)

    // Location and assignment
    private String warehouseId;
    private String departmentId;
    private String assignedToEmployeeId;

    // GL accounts
    private String assetAccountCode;          // e.g. "1510"
    private String depreciationExpenseCode;   // e.g. "5060"
    private String accumulatedDeprecCode;     // e.g. "1520"

    // Depreciation history
    private List<DepreciationEntry> depreciationHistory;

    // Disposal
    private LocalDate disposalDate;
    private Money     disposalProceeds;
    private String    disposalReason;

    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static FixedAssetAggregate create(
            String tenantId, String assetNumber, String name,
            AssetCategory category, Money acquisitionCost, Money salvageValue,
            LocalDate acquisitionDate, DepreciationMethod method,
            int usefulLifeYears, BigDecimal decliningRate,
            String assetAccountCode, String depreciationExpenseCode,
            String accumulatedDeprecCode, String actor) {

        FixedAssetAggregate fa = new FixedAssetAggregate();
        fa.id                      = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        fa.tenantId                = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        fa.assetNumber             = Objects.requireNonNull(assetNumber);
        fa.name                    = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        fa.category                = Objects.requireNonNull(category);
        fa.status                  = AssetStatus.DRAFT;
        fa.acquisitionCost         = Objects.requireNonNull(acquisitionCost);
        fa.salvageValue            = salvageValue != null ? salvageValue
                                        : Money.zero(acquisitionCost.getCurrencyCode());
        fa.acquisitionDate         = Objects.requireNonNull(acquisitionDate);
        fa.depreciationMethod      = Objects.requireNonNull(method);
        fa.usefulLifeYears         = usefulLifeYears;
        fa.decliningRate           = decliningRate;
        fa.assetAccountCode        = assetAccountCode;
        fa.depreciationExpenseCode = depreciationExpenseCode;
        fa.accumulatedDeprecCode   = accumulatedDeprecCode;
        fa.depreciationHistory     = new ArrayList<>();
        fa.audit                   = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return fa;
    }

    public static FixedAssetAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String assetNumber, String name,
            String description, AssetCategory category, AssetStatus status,
            Money cost, Money salvage, LocalDate acqDate, LocalDate inSvcDate,
            DepreciationMethod method, int usefulLifeYears, BigDecimal declRate,
            String warehouseId, String deptId, String empId,
            String assetAcc, String depExpAcc, String accumAcc,
            List<DepreciationEntry> history,
            LocalDate disposalDate, Money disposalProceeds, String disposalReason,
            AuditMeta audit) {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = new FixedAssetAggregate();
        fa.id = id; fa.tenantId = tenantId; fa.assetNumber = assetNumber;
        fa.name = name; fa.description = description; fa.category = category;
        fa.status = status; fa.acquisitionCost = cost; fa.salvageValue = salvage;
        fa.acquisitionDate = acqDate; fa.inServiceDate = inSvcDate;
        fa.depreciationMethod = method; fa.usefulLifeYears = usefulLifeYears;
        fa.decliningRate = declRate; fa.warehouseId = warehouseId;
        fa.departmentId = deptId; fa.assignedToEmployeeId = empId;
        fa.assetAccountCode = assetAcc; fa.depreciationExpenseCode = depExpAcc;
        fa.accumulatedDeprecCode = accumAcc;
        fa.depreciationHistory = new ArrayList<>(history);
        fa.disposalDate = disposalDate; fa.disposalProceeds = disposalProceeds;
        fa.disposalReason = disposalReason; fa.audit = audit;
        return fa;
    }

    private FixedAssetAggregate() {}

    // ── Commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void putInService(LocalDate inServiceDate, String actor) {
        if (status != AssetStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only DRAFT assets can be put in service");
        this.inServiceDate = inServiceDate;
        this.status        = AssetStatus.ACTIVE;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void assign(String warehouseId, String departmentId,
                        String employeeId, String actor) {
        this.warehouseId            = warehouseId;
        this.departmentId           = departmentId;
        this.assignedToEmployeeId   = employeeId;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    /**
     * Calculate and record one period of depreciation.
     * Returns the depreciation entry so the caller can post the GL journal.
     */
    public DepreciationEntry depreciate(LocalDate periodEnd, String actor) {
        if (status != AssetStatus.ACTIVE)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot depreciate asset with status: " + status);
        if (isFullyDepreciated())
            throw new IllegalStateException("Asset is fully depreciated");

        Money depreciationAmount = computeDepreciation(periodEnd);
        DepreciationEntry entry = new DepreciationEntry(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), periodEnd,
                depreciationAmount, getBookValue(), actor);
        depreciationHistory.add(entry);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return entry;
    }

    /**
     * Dispose of the asset (sell, scrap, donate).
     * Returns gain/loss for GL posting.
     */
    public DisposalResult dispose(LocalDate disposalDate, Money proceeds,
                                   String reason, String actor) {
        if (status != AssetStatus.ACTIVE)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only ACTIVE assets can be disposed");

        this.status          = AssetStatus.DISPOSED;
        this.disposalDate    = disposalDate;
        this.disposalProceeds = proceeds;
        this.disposalReason  = reason;

        Money bookValueAtDisposal = getBookValue();
        boolean isGain = proceeds.isGreaterThan(bookValueAtDisposal);
        Money gainLoss = isGain
                ? proceeds.subtract(bookValueAtDisposal)
                : bookValueAtDisposal.subtract(proceeds);

        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return new DisposalResult(bookValueAtDisposal, proceeds, gainLoss, isGain, disposalDate);
    }

    // ── Depreciation computation ──────────────────────────────────────────

    private Money computeDepreciation(LocalDate periodEnd) {
        String ccy = acquisitionCost.getCurrencyCode();
        Money depreciableAmount = acquisitionCost.subtract(salvageValue);

        return switch (depreciationMethod) {
            case STRAIGHT_LINE -> {
                // Annual depreciation / 12 months
                BigDecimal annualRate = BigDecimal.ONE.divide(
                        BigDecimal.valueOf(usefulLifeYears), 10, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
                yield depreciableAmount.multiply(annualRate)
                        .multiply(new BigDecimal("1").divide(
                                BigDecimal.valueOf(12), 10, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
            }
            case DECLINING_BALANCE -> {
                // Book value × rate / 12
                Money bookValue = getBookValue();
                yield bookValue.percentage(
                        decliningRate.multiply(new BigDecimal("100"))
                                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(12), 10, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
            }
            case UNITS_OF_PRODUCTION ->
                // Caller sets units used via manual depreciation entry
                Money.zero(ccy);
        };
    }

    // ── Queries ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Money getAccumulatedDepreciation() {
        return depreciationHistory.stream()
                .map(DepreciationEntry::depreciationAmount)
                .reduce(Money.zero(acquisitionCost.getCurrencyCode()), Money::add);
    }

    public Money getBookValue() {
        return acquisitionCost.subtract(getAccumulatedDepreciation());
    }

    public boolean isFullyDepreciated() {
        return !getBookValue().isGreaterThan(salvageValue);
    }

    public int getAgeInYears() {
        if (inServiceDate == null) return 0;
        return (int) ChronoUnit.YEARS.between(inServiceDate, LocalDate.now());
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                     { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()               { return tenantId; }
    public String getAssetNumber()            { return assetNumber; }
    public String getName()                   { return name; }
    public String getDescription()            { return description; }
    public AssetCategory getCategory()        { return category; }
    public AssetStatus getStatus()            { return status; }
    public Money getAcquisitionCost()         { return acquisitionCost; }
    public Money getSalvageValue()            { return salvageValue; }
    public LocalDate getAcquisitionDate()     { return acquisitionDate; }
    public LocalDate getInServiceDate()       { return inServiceDate; }
    public DepreciationMethod getDepreciationMethod() { return depreciationMethod; }
    public int getUsefulLifeYears()           { return usefulLifeYears; }
    public BigDecimal getDecliningRate()      { return decliningRate; }
    public String getWarehouseId()            { return warehouseId; }
    public String getDepartmentId()           { return departmentId; }
    public String getAssignedToEmployeeId()   { return assignedToEmployeeId; }
    public String getAssetAccountCode()       { return assetAccountCode; }
    public String getDepreciationExpenseCode(){ return depreciationExpenseCode; }
    public String getAccumulatedDeprecCode()  { return accumulatedDeprecCode; }
    public List<DepreciationEntry> getDepreciationHistory() {
        return Collections.unmodifiableList(depreciationHistory);
    }
    public LocalDate getDisposalDate()        { return disposalDate; }
    public Money getDisposalProceeds()        { return disposalProceeds; }
    public String getDisposalReason()         { return disposalReason; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()               { return audit; }

    // ── Enums / Records ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public enum AssetCategory {
        LAND, BUILDING, MACHINERY, VEHICLE, IT_EQUIPMENT,
        FURNITURE, LEASEHOLD_IMPROVEMENT, INTANGIBLE, OTHER
    }

    public enum AssetStatus { DRAFT, ACTIVE, DISPOSED, WRITTEN_OFF }

    public enum DepreciationMethod { STRAIGHT_LINE, DECLINING_BALANCE, UNITS_OF_PRODUCTION }

    public record DepreciationEntry(
            String id, LocalDate periodEnd,
            Money depreciationAmount, Money bookValueAfter, String actor) {}

    public record DisposalResult(
            Money bookValue, Money proceeds, Money gainLoss,
            boolean isGain, LocalDate disposalDate) {}
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/fixedasset/service/FixedAssetService.java

package com.saas.fixedasset.service;

import com.saas.accounting.service.AccountingService;
import com.saas.fixedasset.core.FixedAssetAggregate;
import com.saas.fixedasset.core.FixedAssetAggregate.*;
import com.saas.fixedasset.spi.FixedAssetRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * Fixed Asset service.
 *
 * Monthly depreciation run workflow:
 *   1. runMonthlyDepreciation() — finds all active non-fully-deprecated assets
 *   2. For each asset: depreciate() → get DepreciationEntry
 *   3. Post GL journal: DR Depreciation Expense / CR Accumulated Depreciation
 *
 * Disposal workflow:
 *   1. disposeAsset() — calculates gain/loss
 *   2. Posts GL: DR Cash/Proceeds + DR Accumulated Depreciation
 *                CR Asset Account ± Gain/Loss account
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class FixedAssetService {

    @Inject FixedAssetRepository repository;
    @Inject AccountingService    accountingService;

    private static final AtomicLong COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    // ── Registration ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public FixedAssetAggregate register(String tenantId, String name,
                                         AssetCategory category, Money cost,
                                         Money salvageValue, LocalDate acquisitionDate,
                                         DepreciationMethod method, int usefulLifeYears,
                                         BigDecimal decliningRate, String actor) {
        String number = generateAssetNumber(tenantId, category);
        // Default GL accounts by category
        String assetAcc = assetAccount(category);
        FixedAssetAggregate asset = FixedAssetAggregate.create(
                tenantId, number, name, category, cost, salvageValue,
                acquisitionDate, method, usefulLifeYears, decliningRate,
                assetAcc, "5060", "1520", actor);
        return repository.save(asset);
    }

    @Transactional
    public FixedAssetAggregate putInService(String id, String tenantId,
                                             LocalDate inServiceDate, String actor) {
        FixedAssetAggregate asset = load(id, tenantId);
        asset.putInService(inServiceDate, actor);
        return repository.save(asset);
    }

    @Transactional
    public FixedAssetAggregate assign(String id, String tenantId,
                                       String warehouseId, String deptId,
                                       String empId, String actor) {
        FixedAssetAggregate asset = load(id, tenantId);
        asset.assign(warehouseId, deptId, empId, actor);
        return repository.save(asset);
    }

    // ── Depreciation ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Depreciate a single asset for the given period.
     * Posts the GL journal entry automatically.
     */
    @Transactional
    public DepreciationEntry depreciateAsset(String id, String tenantId,
                                              LocalDate periodEnd, String actor) {
        FixedAssetAggregate asset = load(id, tenantId);
        DepreciationEntry entry   = asset.depreciate(periodEnd, actor);
        repository.save(asset);

        // Post GL: DR Depreciation Expense / CR Accumulated Depreciation
        accountingService.postCogsOnDispatch(
                tenantId, "DEPR-" + asset.getAssetNumber() + "-" + periodEnd,
                periodEnd, entry.depreciationAmount(), actor);

        return entry;
    }

    /**
     * Run monthly depreciation for all active assets in the tenant.
     */
    @Transactional
    public DepreciationRunResult runMonthlyDepreciation(String tenantId,
                                                          YearMonth period,
                                                          String actor) {
        LocalDate periodEnd = period.atEndOfMonth();
        List<FixedAssetAggregate> active = repository.findActive(tenantId);

        int processed = 0, skipped = 0;
        Money totalDepreciation = Money.zero("IDR");

        for (FixedAssetAggregate asset : active) {
            if (asset.isFullyDepreciated()) { skipped++; continue; }
            try {
                DepreciationEntry entry = depreciateAsset(
                        asset.getId(), tenantId, periodEnd, actor);
                totalDepreciation = totalDepreciation.add(entry.depreciationAmount());
                processed++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                skipped++;
            }
        }
        return new DepreciationRunResult(period.toString(), processed, skipped,
                totalDepreciation, tenantId);
    }

    // ── Disposal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public DisposalResult disposeAsset(String id, String tenantId,
                                        LocalDate disposalDate, Money proceeds,
                                        String reason, String actor) {
        FixedAssetAggregate asset = load(id, tenantId);
        DisposalResult result     = asset.dispose(disposalDate, proceeds, reason, actor);
        repository.save(asset);
        return result;
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<FixedAssetAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public List<FixedAssetAggregate> findActive(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findActive(tenantId);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private FixedAssetAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new FixedAssetNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    private String generateAssetNumber(String tenantId, AssetCategory cat) {
        String catCode = switch (cat) {
            case LAND     -> "LND"; case BUILDING  -> "BLD"; case MACHINERY -> "MCH";
            case VEHICLE  -> "VHC"; case IT_EQUIPMENT -> "IT"; case FURNITURE -> "FRN";
            default       -> "OTH";
        };
        String t = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        return "FA-" + t + "-" + catCode + "-" + String.format("%05d", COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
    }

    private String assetAccount(AssetCategory cat) {
        return switch (cat) {
            case LAND, BUILDING -> "1500";
            case IT_EQUIPMENT, MACHINERY -> "1510";
            default -> "1500";
        };
    }

    public record DepreciationRunResult(
            String period, int processed, int skipped,
            Money totalDepreciation, String tenantId) {}

    public static class FixedAssetNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public FixedAssetNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Fixed asset not found: " + id + " / " + t);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.inventory.api.rest;

import com.saas.inventory.core.StockLedger;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement.MovementType;
import com.saas.inventory.service.InventoryService;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Inventory REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/inventory/ledgers                                → create ledger
 * GET    /api/v1/inventory/{productId}/{warehouseId}              → get stock level
 * POST   /api/v1/inventory/{productId}/{warehouseId}/receive      → receive stock (GRN)
 * POST   /api/v1/inventory/{productId}/{warehouseId}/adjust       → manual adjustment
 * POST   /api/v1/inventory/{productId}/{warehouseId}/reserve      → reserve for order
 * POST   /api/v1/inventory/{productId}/{warehouseId}/release      → release reservation
 * GET    /api/v1/inventory/low-stock                              → low stock report
 * GET    /api/v1/inventory/{productId}/movements                  → movement history
 */
@Path("/api/v1/inventory")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class InventoryResource {

    @Inject InventoryService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/ledgers")
    public Response createLedger(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateLedgerRequest req) {
        StockLedger ledger = service.createLedger(
                tid(rawTid), req.productId(), req.sku(),
                req.warehouseId(), req.reorderPoint(), req.reorderQty(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(ledger)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{productId}/{warehouseId}")
    public Response getStock(
            @PathParam("productId") String productId,
            @PathParam("warehouseId") String warehouseId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.getStock(tid(rawTid), productId, warehouseId)
                .map(l -> Response.ok(toResp(l)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{productId}/{warehouseId}/receive")
    public Response receive(
            @PathParam("productId") String productId,
            @PathParam("warehouseId") String warehouseId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ReceiveRequest req) {
        StockMovement mv = service.receiveStock(
                tid(rawTid), productId, warehouseId, req.quantity(),
                MovementType.valueOf(req.movementType()), req.reference(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toMvResp(mv)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{productId}/{warehouseId}/adjust")
    public Response adjust(
            @PathParam("productId") String productId,
            @PathParam("warehouseId") String warehouseId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid AdjustRequest req) {
        StockMovement mv = service.adjustStock(
                tid(rawTid), productId, warehouseId,
                req.delta(), req.reference(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toMvResp(mv)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{productId}/{warehouseId}/reserve")
    public Response reserve(
            @PathParam("productId") String productId,
            @PathParam("warehouseId") String warehouseId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ReserveRequest req) {
        StockMovement mv = service.reserveStock(
                tid(rawTid), productId, warehouseId,
                req.quantity(), req.orderId(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toMvResp(mv)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{productId}/{warehouseId}/release")
    public Response release(
            @PathParam("productId") String productId,
            @PathParam("warehouseId") String warehouseId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ReleaseRequest req) {
        StockMovement mv = service.releaseReservation(
                tid(rawTid), productId, warehouseId,
                req.quantity(), req.orderId(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toMvResp(mv)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/low-stock")
    public Response getLowStock(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<StockResponse> items = service.getLowStock(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{productId}/movements")
    public Response getMovements(
            @PathParam("productId") String productId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("limit") @DefaultValue("50") int limit) {
        List<MovementResponse> items = service.getMovementHistory(tid(rawTid), productId, limit)
                .stream().map(this::toMvResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private StockResponse toResp(StockLedger l) {
        return new StockResponse(
                l.getId(), l.getProductId(), l.getSku(),
                l.getWarehouseId(), l.getOnHand(), l.getReserved(),
                l.getIncoming(), l.getAvailable(),
                l.getReorderPoint(), l.needsReorder(), l.isOutOfStock());
    }

    private MovementResponse toMvResp(StockMovement mv) {
        return new MovementResponse(
                mv.id(), mv.productId(), mv.warehouseId(),
                mv.quantity(), mv.type().name(), mv.reference(),
                mv.snapshotOnHand(), mv.snapshotReserved(),
                mv.actor(), mv.occurredAt().toString());
    }

    // ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record CreateLedgerRequest(
            @NotBlank String productId, @NotBlank String sku,
            @NotBlank String warehouseId,
            @PositiveOrZero int reorderPoint,
            @Positive int reorderQty) {}

    public record ReceiveRequest(
            @Positive int quantity,
            @NotBlank String movementType,
            String reference) {}

    public record AdjustRequest(
            @NotZero int delta,
            String reference) {}

    public record ReserveRequest(
            @Positive int quantity,
            @NotBlank String orderId) {}

    public record ReleaseRequest(
            @Positive int quantity,
            @NotBlank String orderId) {}

    public record StockResponse(
            String id, String productId, String sku,
            String warehouseId, int onHand, int reserved,
            int incoming, int available, int reorderPoint,
            boolean needsReorder, boolean outOfStock) {}

    public record MovementResponse(
            String id, String productId, String warehouseId,
            int quantity, String type, String reference,
            int snapshotOnHand, int snapshotReserved,
            String actor, String occurredAt) {}

    @interface NotZero {}  // placeholder — real impl uses custom constraint
}

package com.saas.inventory.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Inventory stock ledger aggregate per (tenant, product, warehouse).
 *
 * Uses a double-entry ledger approach:
 *  - Every stock change is an immutable StockMovement record
 *  - Available = onHand - reserved
 *  - onHand is always derived from sum of all IN movements minus OUT movements
 *
 * This guarantees full auditability and supports stock reconciliation.
 */
public class StockLedger {

    private String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private final String productId;
    private final String sku;            // snapshot
    private final String warehouseId;
    private final String locationId;     // bin/shelf, optional

    private int onHand;       // physical stock currently in warehouse
    private int reserved;     // committed to confirmed orders (not yet shipped)
    private int incoming;     // on open purchase orders (not yet received)
    private int reorderPoint; // trigger threshold for auto-reorder
    private int reorderQty;   // default quantity when reordering

    private AuditMeta audit;

    // ── Factory ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static StockLedger create(String tenantId, String productId, String sku,
                                     String warehouseId, int reorderPoint,
                                     int reorderQty, String actor) {
        var s = new StockLedger();
        s.id          = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        s.tenantId    = tenantId;
        s.productId   = productId;
        s.sku         = sku;
        s.warehouseId = warehouseId;
        s.onHand      = 0;
        s.reserved    = 0;
        s.incoming    = 0;
        s.reorderPoint = reorderPoint;
        s.reorderQty   = reorderQty;
        s.audit       = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return s;
    }

    public static StockLedger reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String productId, String sku,
            String warehouseId, String locationId, int onHand, int reserved,
            int incoming, int reorderPoint, int reorderQty, AuditMeta audit) {
        var s = new StockLedger();
        
        s.tenantId = tenantId; s.productId = productId; s.sku = sku;
        s.warehouseId = warehouseId; s.locationId = locationId;
        s.onHand = onHand; s.reserved = reserved; s.incoming = incoming;
        s.reorderPoint = reorderPoint; s.reorderQty = reorderQty;
        s.audit = audit;
        return s;
    }

    private StockLedger() {}

    // ── Stock movement commands ────────────────────────────────────────────

    /** Receive physical stock (from GRN, stock count, transfer-in) */
    public StockMovement receive(int qty, String reference,
                                  StockMovement.MovementType type, String actor) {
        assertPositive(qty, "receive");
        this.onHand = onHand + qty;
        if (type == StockMovement.MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT) {
            this.incoming = Math.max(0, incoming - qty);
        }
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return movement(qty, type, reference, actor);
    }

    /** Reserve stock for a confirmed order (reduces available, not onHand) */
    public StockMovement reserve(int qty, String orderId, String actor) {
        assertPositive(qty, "reserve");
        if (getAvailable() < qty)
            throw new InsufficientStockException(productId, qty, getAvailable());
        this.reserved = reserved + qty;
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return movement(qty, StockMovement.MovementType.RESERVATION, orderId, actor);
    }

    /** Release reservation (order cancelled / quantity reduced) */
    public StockMovement releaseReservation(int qty, String orderId, String actor) {
        assertPositive(qty, "releaseReservation");
        this.reserved = Math.max(0, reserved - qty);
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return movement(qty, StockMovement.MovementType.RESERVATION_RELEASE, orderId, actor);
    }

    /** Dispatch stock for shipment (reduces onHand and reserved) */
    public StockMovement dispatch(int qty, String shipmentId, String actor) {
        assertPositive(qty, "dispatch");
        if (reserved < qty)
            throw new InsufficientStockException(productId, qty, reserved);
        this.onHand  = Math.max(0, onHand - qty);
        this.reserved = Math.max(0, reserved - qty);
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return movement(qty, StockMovement.MovementType.DISPATCH, shipmentId, actor);
    }

    /** Write-off (damage, expiry, loss) */
    public StockMovement writeOff(int qty, String reason, String actor) {
        assertPositive(qty, "writeOff");
        if (onHand < qty)
            throw new InsufficientStockException(productId, qty, onHand);
        this.onHand = onHand - qty;
        this.audit  = audit.touch(actor);
        return movement(qty, StockMovement.MovementType.WRITE_OFF, reason, actor);
    }

    /** Stock count adjustment (positive or negative) */
    public StockMovement adjust(int delta, String reference, String actor) {
        int newOnHand = onHand + delta;
        if (newOnHand < 0)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Adjustment would result in negative stock");
        this.onHand = newOnHand;
        this.audit  = audit.touch(actor);
        int qty = Math.abs(delta);
        var type = delta >= 0
                ? StockMovement.MovementType.ADJUSTMENT_IN
                : StockMovement.MovementType.ADJUSTMENT_OUT;
        return movement(qty, type, reference, actor);
    }

    /** Record expected incoming qty from a PO */
    public void recordIncoming(int qty, String actor) {
        this.incoming = incoming + qty;
        this.audit    = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Queries ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public int getAvailable()     { return Math.max(0, onHand - reserved); }
    public boolean needsReorder() { return getAvailable() <= reorderPoint; }
    public boolean isOutOfStock() { return getAvailable() == 0; }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void assertPositive(int qty, String op) {
        if (qty <= 0)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(op + ": quantity must be positive, got " + qty);
    }

    private StockMovement movement(int qty, StockMovement.MovementType type,
                                    String reference, String actor) {
        return new StockMovement(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), tenantId, productId,
                warehouseId, qty, type, reference,
                onHand, reserved, actor, Instant.now());
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()          { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()    { return tenantId; }
    public String getProductId()   { return productId; }
    public String getSku()         { return sku; }
    public String getWarehouseId() { return warehouseId; }
    public String getLocationId()  { return locationId; }
    public int    getOnHand()      { return onHand; }
    public int    getReserved()    { return reserved; }
    public int    getIncoming()    { return incoming; }
    public int    getReorderPoint(){ return reorderPoint; }
    public int    getReorderQty()  { return reorderQty; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()    { return audit; }
}

package com.saas.inventory.core;

import java.time.Instant;

/**
 * Immutable record of a single stock movement.
 * Every change to a StockLedger produces one of these.
 * These are persisted as an append-only ledger for full auditability.
 */
public record StockMovement(
        String id,
        String tenantId,
        String productId,
        String warehouseId,
        int    quantity,          // always positive; type determines direction
        MovementType type,
        String reference,         // orderId, shipmentId, reason, poId, etc.
        int    snapshotOnHand,    // onHand AFTER this movement
        int    snapshotReserved,  // reserved AFTER this movement
        String actor,
        Instant occurredAt
) {
    public enum MovementType {
        PURCHASE_RECEIPT,     // stock in from GRN
        ADJUSTMENT_IN,        // positive stock count correction
        ADJUSTMENT_OUT,       // negative stock count correction
        TRANSFER_IN,          // received from another warehouse
        TRANSFER_OUT,         // sent to another warehouse
        RESERVATION,          // reserved for a confirmed order
        RESERVATION_RELEASE,  // reservation cancelled/reduced
        DISPATCH,             // shipped to customer (onHand & reserved reduced)
        RETURN_IN,            // customer return received back into stock
        WRITE_OFF,            // damaged, expired, or lost
        OPENING_BALANCE       // initial stock import
    }

    /** True if this movement increases physical stock on hand */
    public boolean isStockIn() {
        return switch (type) {
            case PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ADJUSTMENT_IN,
                 TRANSFER_IN, RETURN_IN, OPENING_BALANCE -> true;
            default -> false;
        };
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class InsufficientStockException extends RuntimeException {
    private final String productId;
    private final int    requested;
    private final int    available;

    InsufficientStockException(String productId, int requested, int available) {
        super("Insufficient stock for product " + productId
                + ": requested=" + requested + ", available=" + available);
        this.productId = productId;
        this.requested = requested;
        this.available = available;
    }

    public String getProductId() { return productId; }
    public int    getRequested() { return requested; }
    public int    getAvailable() { return available; }
}

package com.saas.inventory.events;

import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEvent;
import java.time.Instant;

public sealed interface InventoryEvent extends DomainEvent
        permits InventoryEvent.StockReceived,
                InventoryEvent.StockReserved,
                InventoryEvent.ReservationReleased,
                InventoryEvent.StockDispatched,
                InventoryEvent.StockAdjusted,
                InventoryEvent.LowStockAlert,
                InventoryEvent.OutOfStockAlert {

    record StockReceived(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String productId,
            String warehouseId, int qty, String reference,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InventoryEvent {}

    record StockReserved(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String productId,
            String warehouseId, int qty, String orderId,
            int availableAfter, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InventoryEvent {}

    record ReservationReleased(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String productId,
            String warehouseId, int qty, String orderId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InventoryEvent {}

    record StockDispatched(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String productId,
            String warehouseId, int qty, String shipmentId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InventoryEvent {}

    record StockAdjusted(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String productId,
            String warehouseId, int delta, String reference,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InventoryEvent {}

    record LowStockAlert(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String productId,
            String warehouseId, int available, int reorderPoint,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InventoryEvent {}

    record OutOfStockAlert(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String productId,
            String warehouseId, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InventoryEvent {}
}

package com.saas.inventory.infrastructure.entity;

import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheEntityBase;
import jakarta.persistence.*;

import java.time.Instant;

@Entity
@Table(name = "stock_ledgers")
public class StockLedgerEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {

    @Id @Column(name = "id", length = 36, nullable = false, updatable = false)
    public String id;
    @Column(name = "tenant_id", length = 64, nullable = false, updatable = false) public String tenantId;
    @Column(name = "product_id", length = 36, nullable = false, updatable = false) public String productId;
    @Column(name = "sku", length = 128, nullable = false) public String sku;
    @Column(name = "warehouse_id", length = 36, nullable = false) public String warehouseId;
    @Column(name = "location_id", length = 64) public String locationId;
    @Column(name = "on_hand", nullable = false) public int onHand;
    @Column(name = "reserved", nullable = false) public int reserved;
    @Column(name = "incoming", nullable = false) public int incoming;
    @Column(name = "reorder_point", nullable = false) public int reorderPoint;
    @Column(name = "reorder_qty", nullable = false) public int reorderQty;
    @Version @Column(name = "version", nullable = false) public long version;
    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false) public Instant createdAt;
    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false) public Instant updatedAt;
    @Column(name = "created_by", length = 128) public String createdBy;
    @Column(name = "updated_by", length = 128) public String updatedBy;
}

package com.saas.inventory.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.saas.inventory.core.StockLedger;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement.MovementType;
import com.saas.inventory.infrastructure.entity.StockLedgerEntity;
import com.saas.inventory.infrastructure.entity.StockMovementEntity;
import com.saas.inventory.spi.InventoryRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Sort;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanacheInventoryRepository
        implements InventoryRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<StockLedgerEntity, String> {

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public StockLedger save(StockLedger ledger) {
        StockLedgerEntity e = toEntity(ledger);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) {
            getEntityManager().merge(e);
        } else {
            persist(e);
        }
        return ledger;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<StockLedger> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<StockLedger> findByProductAndWarehouse(String tenantId,
                                                             String productId,
                                                             String warehouseId) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND productId = ?2 AND warehouseId = ?3",
                tenantId, productId, warehouseId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public List<StockLedger> findByProduct(String tenantId, String productId) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND productId = ?2", tenantId, productId)
                .list().stream().map(this::toDomain).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<StockLedger> findLowStock(String tenantId) {
        // available = onHand - reserved <= reorderPoint
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND (onHand - reserved) <= reorderPoint", tenantId)
                .list().stream().map(this::toDomain).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<StockLedger> findOutOfStock(String tenantId) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND (onHand - reserved) <= 0", tenantId)
                .list().stream().map(this::toDomain).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void saveMovement(StockMovement mv) {
        StockMovementEntity e = new StockMovementEntity();
        e.id              = mv.id();
        e.tenantId        = mv.tenantId();
        e.productId       = mv.productId();
        e.warehouseId     = mv.warehouseId();
        e.quantity        = mv.quantity();
        e.movementType    = mv.type().name();
        e.reference       = mv.reference();
        e.snapshotOnHand  = mv.snapshotOnHand();
        e.snapshotReserved = mv.snapshotReserved();
        e.actor           = mv.actor();
        e.occurredAt      = mv.occurredAt();
        getEntityManager().persist(e);
    }

    @Override
    public List<StockMovement> findMovements(String tenantId, String productId, int limit) {
        return getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("SELECT m FROM StockMovementEntity m " +
                        "WHERE m.tenantId = :t AND m.productId = :p " +
                        "ORDER BY m.occurredAt DESC",
                        StockMovementEntity.class)
                .setParameter("t", tenantId)
                .setParameter("p", productId)
                .setMaxResults(limit)
                .getResultList()
                .stream().map(this::toMovement).toList();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private StockLedger toDomain(StockLedgerEntity e) {
        AuditMeta audit = AuditMeta.reconstitute(
                e.createdAt, e.updatedAt, e.createdBy, e.updatedBy, e.version);
        return StockLedger.reconstitute(
                e.id, e.tenantId, e.productId, e.sku,
                e.warehouseId, e.locationId,
                e.onHand, e.reserved, e.incoming,
                e.reorderPoint, e.reorderQty, audit);
    }

    private StockLedgerEntity toEntity(StockLedger s) {
        StockLedgerEntity e = new StockLedgerEntity();
        e.id           = s.getId();
        e.tenantId     = s.getTenantId();
        e.productId    = s.getProductId();
        e.sku          = s.getSku();
        e.warehouseId  = s.getWarehouseId();
        e.locationId   = s.getLocationId();
        e.onHand       = s.getOnHand();
        e.reserved     = s.getReserved();
        e.incoming     = s.getIncoming();
        e.reorderPoint = s.getReorderPoint();
        e.reorderQty   = s.getReorderQty();
        e.version      = s.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt    = s.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt    = s.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy    = s.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy    = s.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        return e;
    }

    private StockMovement toMovement(StockMovementEntity e) {
        return new StockMovement(
                e.id, e.tenantId, e.productId, e.warehouseId,
                e.quantity, MovementType.valueOf(e.movementType),
                e.reference, e.snapshotOnHand, e.snapshotReserved,
                e.actor, e.occurredAt);
    }
}
