package com.saas.inventory.service;

import com.saas.inventory.core.StockLedger;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.events.InventoryEvent;
import com.saas.inventory.spi.InventoryRepository;
import com.saas.order.events.OrderEvent;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.enterprise.event.Observes;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Inventory application service.
 *
 * Listens to order events to drive automatic reservation/release:
 *  - OrderConfirmed  → reserve stock for all lines
 *  - OrderCancelled  → release all reservations
 *  - OrderLineFulfilled → dispatch stock
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class InventoryService {

    @Inject InventoryRepository repository;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    private static final String DEFAULT_WAREHOUSE = "main";

    // ── Setup ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public StockLedger createLedger(String tenantId, String productId, String sku,
                                     String warehouseId, int reorderPoint,
                                     int reorderQty, String actor) {
        Optional<StockLedger> existing =
                repository.findByProductAndWarehouse(tenantId, productId, warehouseId);
        if (existing.isPresent()) return existing.get();

        StockLedger ledger = StockLedger.create(tenantId, productId, sku,
                warehouseId, reorderPoint, reorderQty, actor);
        return repository.save(ledger);
    }

    // ── Receive (GRN, opening balance, return) ────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public StockMovement receiveStock(String tenantId, String productId,
                                       String warehouseId, int qty,
                                       StockMovement.MovementType type,
                                       String reference, String actor) {
        StockLedger ledger = loadOrCreate(tenantId, productId, warehouseId, actor);
        StockMovement movement = ledger.receive(qty, reference, type, actor);
        repository.save(ledger);
        repository.saveMovement(movement);

        publisher.publish(new InventoryEvent.StockReceived(
                ledger.getId(), tenantId, productId,
                warehouseId, qty, reference, actor, Instant.now()));

        checkAlerts(ledger, actor);
        return movement;
    }

    // ── Reserve ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public StockMovement reserveStock(String tenantId, String productId,
                                       String warehouseId, int qty,
                                       String orderId, String actor) {
        StockLedger ledger = load(tenantId, productId, warehouseId);
        StockMovement movement = ledger.reserve(qty, orderId, actor);
        repository.save(ledger);
        repository.saveMovement(movement);

        publisher.publish(new InventoryEvent.StockReserved(
                ledger.getId(), tenantId, productId, warehouseId,
                qty, orderId, ledger.getAvailable(), actor, Instant.now()));

        checkAlerts(ledger, actor);
        return movement;
    }

    // ── Release ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public StockMovement releaseReservation(String tenantId, String productId,
                                             String warehouseId, int qty,
                                             String orderId, String actor) {
        StockLedger ledger = load(tenantId, productId, warehouseId);
        StockMovement movement = ledger.releaseReservation(qty, orderId, actor);
        repository.save(ledger);
        repository.saveMovement(movement);

        publisher.publish(new InventoryEvent.ReservationReleased(
                ledger.getId(), tenantId, productId, warehouseId,
                qty, orderId, actor, Instant.now()));
        return movement;
    }

    // ── Dispatch ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public StockMovement dispatchStock(String tenantId, String productId,
                                        String warehouseId, int qty,
                                        String shipmentId, String actor) {
        StockLedger ledger = load(tenantId, productId, warehouseId);
        StockMovement movement = ledger.dispatch(qty, shipmentId, actor);
        repository.save(ledger);
        repository.saveMovement(movement);

        publisher.publish(new InventoryEvent.StockDispatched(
                ledger.getId(), tenantId, productId, warehouseId,
                qty, shipmentId, actor, Instant.now()));
        return movement;
    }

    // ── Adjust ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public StockMovement adjustStock(String tenantId, String productId,
                                      String warehouseId, int delta,
                                      String reference, String actor) {
        StockLedger ledger = load(tenantId, productId, warehouseId);
        StockMovement movement = ledger.adjust(delta, reference, actor);
        repository.save(ledger);
        repository.saveMovement(movement);

        publisher.publish(new InventoryEvent.StockAdjusted(
                ledger.getId(), tenantId, productId, warehouseId,
                delta, reference, actor, Instant.now()));
        checkAlerts(ledger, actor);
        return movement;
    }

    // ── Event listeners (order integration) ──────────────────────────────

    /**
     * When an order is confirmed, reserve stock for every line.
     * Called via CDI @Observes — decoupled from OrderService.
     */
    @Transactional
    public void onOrderConfirmed(@Observes OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed event) {
        // In production: iterate order lines via OrderRepository lookup
        // For now: pattern shows the listener wiring
    }

    @Transactional
    public void onOrderCancelled(@Observes OrderEvent.OrderCancelled event) {
        // Release all reservations for this order
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<StockLedger> getStock(String tenantId, String productId, String warehouseId) {
        return repository.findByProductAndWarehouse(tenantId, productId, warehouseId);
    }

    public List<StockLedger> getLowStock(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findLowStock(tenantId);
    }

    public List<StockMovement> getMovementHistory(String tenantId, String productId, int limit) {
        return repository.findMovements(tenantId, productId, limit);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private StockLedger load(String tenantId, String productId, String warehouseId) {
        return repository.findByProductAndWarehouse(tenantId, productId, warehouseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new StockLedgerNotFoundException(tenantId, productId, warehouseId));
    }

    private StockLedger loadOrCreate(String tenantId, String productId,
                                      String warehouseId, String actor) {
        return repository.findByProductAndWarehouse(tenantId, productId, warehouseId)
                .orElseGet(() -> {
                    StockLedger l = StockLedger.create(tenantId, productId, "UNKNOWN",
                            warehouseId, 5, 10, actor);
                    return repository.save(l);
                });
    }

    private void checkAlerts(StockLedger ledger, String actor) {
        if (ledger.isOutOfStock()) {
            publisher.publish(new InventoryEvent.OutOfStockAlert(
                    ledger.getId(), ledger.getTenantId(), ledger.getProductId(),
                    ledger.getWarehouseId(), actor, Instant.now()));
        } else if (ledger.needsReorder()) {
            publisher.publish(new InventoryEvent.LowStockAlert(
                    ledger.getId(), ledger.getTenantId(), ledger.getProductId(),
                    ledger.getWarehouseId(), ledger.getAvailable(),
                    ledger.getReorderPoint(), actor, Instant.now()));
        }
    }

    public static class StockLedgerNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public StockLedgerNotFoundException(String t, String p, String w) {
            super("Stock ledger not found: tenant=" + t + " product=" + p + " warehouse=" + w);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.invoice.api.rest;

import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate.*;
import com.saas.invoice.service.InvoiceService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Invoice REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/invoices                     → create draft invoice
 * POST   /api/v1/invoices/{id}/lines          → add line
 * POST   /api/v1/invoices/{id}/tax/ppn        → add PPN 11% automatically
 * POST   /api/v1/invoices/{id}/issue          → DRAFT → ISSUED
 * POST   /api/v1/invoices/{id}/payment        → record payment
 * POST   /api/v1/invoices/{id}/void           → void invoice
 * GET    /api/v1/invoices/{id}                → get invoice
 * GET    /api/v1/invoices/overdue             → list overdue invoices
 */
@Path("/api/v1/invoices")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class InvoiceResource {

    @Inject InvoiceService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "anonymous";
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateInvoiceRequest req) {

        Party party = req.partyType().equalsIgnoreCase("CUSTOMER")
                ? Party.customer(req.partyId(), req.partyName(), req.partyTaxId())
                : Party.supplier(req.partyId(), req.partyName(), req.partyTaxId());

        InvoiceAggregate inv = service.createInvoice(
                tid(rawTid), InvoiceType.valueOf(req.type()),
                party, req.currencyCode(),
                LocalDate.parse(req.issueDate()),
                req.paymentTermsDays(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(inv)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/lines")
    public Response addLine(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid AddLineRequest req) {

        Money unitPrice = Money.of(req.unitPrice(), req.currencyCode());
        InvoiceAggregate inv = service.addLine(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.description(),
                req.productId(), req.qty(), unitPrice, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(inv)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/tax/ppn")
    public Response addPpn(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = service.addPpnTax(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(inv)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/issue")
    public Response issue(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = service.issueInvoice(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(inv)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/payment")
    public Response recordPayment(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid PaymentRequest req) {
        Money amount = Money.of(req.amount(), req.currencyCode());
        InvoiceAggregate inv = service.recordPayment(id, tid(rawTid), amount, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(inv)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/void")
    public Response voidInvoice(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = service.voidInvoice(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(inv)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(inv -> Response.ok(toResp(inv)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/overdue")
    public Response listOverdue(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<InvoiceResponse> items = service.findOverdue(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private InvoiceResponse toResp(InvoiceAggregate inv) {
        return new InvoiceResponse(
                inv.getId(), inv.getTenantId(), inv.getInvoiceNumber(),
                inv.getType().name(), inv.getParty().getName(),
                inv.getStatus().name(), inv.getCurrencyCode(),
                inv.getSubtotal().getAmount(),
                inv.getTotalTax().getAmount(),
                inv.getTotal().getAmount(),
                inv.getAmountPaid().getAmount(),
                inv.getAmountDue().getAmount(),
                inv.getIssueDate() != null ? inv.getIssueDate().toString() : null,
                inv.getDueDate() != null ? inv.getDueDate().toString() : null,
                inv.getOrderId(), inv.getPoId());
    }

    // ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record CreateInvoiceRequest(
            @NotBlank String type, @NotBlank String partyId,
            @NotBlank String partyName, String partyTaxId,
            @NotBlank String partyType, @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            @NotBlank String issueDate, @PositiveOrZero int paymentTermsDays) {}

    public record AddLineRequest(
            @NotBlank String description, String productId,
            @Positive int qty, @NotNull BigDecimal unitPrice,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record PaymentRequest(
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal amount,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record InvoiceResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String invoiceNumber,
            String type, String partyName, String status,
            String currencyCode, BigDecimal subtotal, BigDecimal tax,
            BigDecimal total, BigDecimal amountPaid, BigDecimal amountDue,
            String issueDate, String dueDate,
            String orderId, String poId) {}
}

package com.saas.invoice.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.*;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Invoice aggregate root.
 *
 * Covers both:
 *  - AR invoices (we bill a customer, type=RECEIVABLE)
 *  - AP invoices (supplier bills us, type=PAYABLE)
 *
 * Lifecycle:
 *   DRAFT → ISSUED → PARTIALLY_PAID → PAID → VOID
 *                 ↘ OVERDUE (set by scheduler if due date passes)
 *
 * Tax lines are first-class citizens (not embedded in order lines)
 * to support separate tax reporting.
 */
public class InvoiceAggregate {

    private String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private       String invoiceNumber;
    private       InvoiceType type;          // RECEIVABLE (AR) or PAYABLE (AP)
    private       Party  party;              // customer (AR) or supplier (AP)
    private       String currencyCode;
    private       InvoiceStatus status;
    private       LocalDate issueDate;
    private       LocalDate dueDate;
    private       String  orderId;           // linked sales order (AR only)
    private       String  poId;              // linked purchase order (AP only)
    private final List<InvoiceLine> lines   = new ArrayList<>();
    private final List<TaxLine>     taxLines = new ArrayList<>();
    private       Money  amountPaid;
    private       String notes;
    private       AuditMeta audit;

    public static InvoiceAggregate create(String tenantId, InvoiceType type,
                                           Party party, String currencyCode,
                                           LocalDate issueDate, int paymentTermsDays,
                                           String actor) {
        var inv = new InvoiceAggregate();
        inv.id       = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        inv.type         = type;
        inv.party        = party;
        inv.currencyCode = currencyCode;
        inv.status       = InvoiceStatus.DRAFT;
        inv.issueDate    = issueDate;
        inv.dueDate      = issueDate.plusDays(paymentTermsDays);
        inv.amountPaid   = Money.zero(currencyCode);
        inv.audit        = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return inv;
    }

    private InvoiceAggregate() {}

    // ── Line commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public InvoiceLine addLine(String description, String productId,
                                int qty, Money unitPrice, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        InvoiceLine line = new InvoiceLine(UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                description, productId, qty, unitPrice);
        lines.add(line);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return line;
    }

    public void addTaxLine(String taxName, String taxCode,
                            java.math.BigDecimal rate, Money amount, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        taxLines.add(new TaxLine(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), taxName, taxCode, rate, amount));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // Helper to add common PPN 11%
    public void addPpnTax(String actor) {
        Money taxableAmount = getSubtotal();
        Money tax = taxableAmount.percentage(new java.math.BigDecimal("11"));
        addTaxLine("PPN", "PPN-11", new java.math.BigDecimal("11"), tax, actor);
    }

    // ── Lifecycle commands ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void issue(String invoiceNumber, String actor) {
        if (status != InvoiceStatus.DRAFT) throw new IllegalStateException("Only DRAFT can be issued");
        if (lines.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot issue empty invoice");
        this.invoiceNumber = invoiceNumber;
        this.status        = InvoiceStatus.ISSUED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    /**
     * Record a payment against this invoice.
     * Returns true if now fully paid.
     */
    public boolean recordPayment(Money payment, String actor) {
        if (status == InvoiceStatus.VOID || status == InvoiceStatus.PAID)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot pay " + status + " invoice");

        this.amountPaid = amountPaid.add(payment);
        boolean fullyPaid = amountPaid.isGreaterOrEqual(getTotal());

        if (fullyPaid) {
            this.status = InvoiceStatus.PAID;
        } else {
            this.status = InvoiceStatus.PARTIALLY_PAID;
        }
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return fullyPaid;
    }

    public void markOverdue(String actor) {
        if (status == InvoiceStatus.ISSUED || status == InvoiceStatus.PARTIALLY_PAID) {
            this.status = InvoiceStatus.OVERDUE;
            audit = audit.touch(actor);
        }
    }

    public void voidInvoice(String actor) {
        if (status == InvoiceStatus.PAID) throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot void a paid invoice");
        this.status = InvoiceStatus.VOID;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void linkOrder(String orderId, String actor) {
        this.orderId = orderId;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void linkPurchaseOrder(String poId, String actor) {
        this.poId = poId;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Computed totals ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Money getSubtotal() {
        return lines.stream()
                .map(InvoiceLine::lineTotal)
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }

    public Money getTotalTax() {
        return taxLines.stream()
                .map(TaxLine::amount)
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }

    public Money getTotal() {
        return getSubtotal().add(getTotalTax());
    }

    public Money getAmountDue() {
        Money total = getTotal();
        if (amountPaid.isGreaterOrEqual(total)) return Money.zero(currencyCode);
        return total.subtract(amountPaid);
    }

    private void assertDraft() {
        if (status != InvoiceStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Invoice is not in DRAFT, status=" + status);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                      { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()                { return tenantId; }
    public String getInvoiceNumber()           { return invoiceNumber; }
    public InvoiceType getType()               { return type; }
    public Party getParty()                    { return party; }
    public String getCurrencyCode()            { return currencyCode; }
    public InvoiceStatus getStatus()           { return status; }
    public LocalDate getIssueDate()            { return issueDate; }
    public LocalDate getDueDate()              { return dueDate; }
    public String getOrderId()                 { return orderId; }
    public String getPoId()                    { return poId; }
    public List<InvoiceLine> getLines()        { return Collections.unmodifiableList(lines); }
    public List<TaxLine> getTaxLines()         { return Collections.unmodifiableList(taxLines); }
    public Money getAmountPaid()               { return amountPaid; }
    public String getNotes()                   { return notes; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()                { return audit; }

    // ── Nested types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public enum InvoiceType   { RECEIVABLE, PAYABLE }
    public enum InvoiceStatus { DRAFT, ISSUED, PARTIALLY_PAID, PAID, OVERDUE, VOID }

    public record InvoiceLine(
            String id, String description, String productId,
            int qty, Money unitPrice) {
        public Money lineTotal() { return unitPrice.multiply(qty); }
    }

    public record TaxLine(
            String id, String taxName, String taxCode,
            java.math.BigDecimal rate, Money amount) {}
}

package com.saas.invoice.events;

import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEvent;
import java.time.Instant;

public sealed interface InvoiceEvent extends DomainEvent
        permits InvoiceEvent.InvoiceIssued,
                InvoiceEvent.InvoicePaymentRecorded,
                InvoiceEvent.InvoicePaid,
                InvoiceEvent.InvoiceOverdue,
                InvoiceEvent.InvoiceVoided {

    record InvoiceIssued(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String invoiceNumber,
            String partyId, String type, String total, String currencyCode,
            String dueDate, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InvoiceEvent {}

    record InvoicePaymentRecorded(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String amount,
            String amountDue, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InvoiceEvent {}

    record InvoicePaid(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String invoiceNumber,
            String partyId, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InvoiceEvent {}

    record InvoiceOverdue(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String invoiceNumber,
            String daysOverdue, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InvoiceEvent {}

    record InvoiceVoided(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String invoiceNumber,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements InvoiceEvent {}
}

import java.math.BigDecimal;

public class InvoiceEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {
    @Id @Column(name="id",length=36,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String id;
    @Column(name="tenant_id",length=64,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String tenantId;
    @Column(name="invoice_number",length=64) public String invoiceNumber;
    @Column(name="type",length=12,nullable=false) public String type;
    @Column(name="party_id",length=36,nullable=false) public String partyId;
    @Column(name="party_name",length=512,nullable=false) public String partyName;
    @Column(name="party_type",length=12,nullable=false) public String partyType;
    @Column(name="currency_code",length=3,nullable=false) public String currencyCode;
    @Column(name="status",length=20,nullable=false) public String status;
    @Column(name="issue_date") public LocalDate issueDate;
    @Column(name="due_date") public LocalDate dueDate;
    @Column(name="order_id",length=36) public String orderId;
    @Column(name="po_id",length=36) public String poId;
    @Column(name="amount_paid_amt",precision=19,scale=4,nullable=false) public BigDecimal amountPaidAmt;
    @Column(name="amount_paid_ccy",length=3) public String amountPaidCcy;

    @Column(name="created_at",nullable=false,updatable=false) public Instant createdAt;
    @Column(name="updated_at",nullable=false) public Instant updatedAt;
    @Column(name="created_by",length=128) public String createdBy;
    @Column(name="updated_by",length=128) public String updatedBy;
}

package com.saas.invoice.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate.*;
import com.saas.invoice.infrastructure.entity.InvoiceEntity;
import com.saas.invoice.infrastructure.entity.InvoiceLineEntity;
import com.saas.invoice.spi.InvoiceRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanacheInvoiceRepository
        implements InvoiceRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<InvoiceEntity, String> {

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public InvoiceAggregate save(InvoiceAggregate inv) {
        InvoiceEntity e = toEntity(inv);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) {
            getEntityManager().merge(e);
        } else {
            persist(e);
        }
        saveLines(inv);
        return inv;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<InvoiceAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<InvoiceAggregate> findByNumber(String number, String tenantId) {
        return find("invoiceNumber = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", number, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<InvoiceAggregate> findByOrderId(String orderId, String tenantId) {
        return find("orderId = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", orderId, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public List<InvoiceAggregate> findByStatus(String tenantId, InvoiceType type,
                                                 InvoiceStatus status, int page, int size) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND type = ?2 AND status = ?3",
                tenantId, type.name(), status.name())
                .page(page, size).list()
                .stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<InvoiceAggregate> findOverdue(String tenantId) {
        // "*" is a sentinel meaning all tenants (used by scheduler)
        if ("*".equals(tenantId)) {
            return find("status IN ('ISSUED','PARTIALLY_PAID') " +
                    "AND dueDate < CURRENT_DATE")
                    .list().stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
        }
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND status IN ('ISSUED','PARTIALLY_PAID') " +
                "AND dueDate < CURRENT_DATE", tenantId)
                .list().stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    // ── Lines ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private List<InvoiceLineEntity> loadLines(String invoiceId) {
        return getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("SELECT l FROM InvoiceLineEntity l WHERE l.invoiceId = :id",
                        InvoiceLineEntity.class)
                .setParameter("id", invoiceId)
                .getResultList();
    }

    private void saveLines(InvoiceAggregate inv) {
        getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("DELETE FROM InvoiceLineEntity l WHERE l.invoiceId = :id")
                .setParameter("id", inv.getId())
                .executeUpdate();
        for (InvoiceLine line : inv.getLines()) {
            InvoiceLineEntity le = new InvoiceLineEntity();
            le.id          = line.id();
            le.invoiceId   = inv.getId();
            le.description = line.description();
            le.productId   = line.productId();
            le.qty         = line.qty();
            le.unitPriceAmt = line.unitPrice().getAmount();
            le.unitPriceCcy = line.unitPrice().getCurrencyCode();
            getEntityManager().persist(le);
        }
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private InvoiceAggregate toDomain(InvoiceEntity e, List<InvoiceLineEntity> lines) {
        Party party = e.partyType.equals("CUSTOMER")
                ? Party.customer(e.partyId, e.partyName, null)
                : Party.supplier(e.partyId, e.partyName, null);

        int termsDays = e.issueDate != null && e.dueDate != null
                ? (int) e.issueDate.until(e.dueDate, java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit.DAYS)
                : 0;

        InvoiceAggregate inv = InvoiceAggregate.create(
                e.tenantId, InvoiceType.valueOf(e.type),
                party, e.currencyCode,
                e.issueDate != null ? e.issueDate : java.time.LocalDate.now(),
                termsDays,
                e.createdBy != null ? e.createdBy : "system");

        // Restore lines
        for (InvoiceLineEntity le : lines) {
            inv.addLine(le.description, le.productId, le.qty,
                    Money.of(le.unitPriceAmt, le.unitPriceCcy),
                    e.createdBy != null ? e.createdBy : "system");
        }

        // Restore payment state by re-applying amountPaid
        // (simpler than a full reconstitute factory — safe because lines drive subtotal)
        if (e.amountPaidAmt != null &&
                e.amountPaidAmt.compareTo(java.math.BigDecimal.ZERO) > 0) {
            Money paid = Money.of(e.amountPaidAmt,
                    e.amountPaidCcy != null ? e.amountPaidCcy : e.currencyCode);
            inv.recordPayment(paid, "system");
        }
        return inv;
    }

    private InvoiceEntity toEntity(InvoiceAggregate inv) {
        InvoiceEntity e   = new InvoiceEntity();
        e.id              = inv.getId();
        e.tenantId        = inv.getTenantId();
        e.invoiceNumber   = inv.getInvoiceNumber();
        e.type            = inv.getType().name();
        e.partyId         = inv.getParty().getId();
        e.partyName       = inv.getParty().getName();
        e.partyType       = inv.getParty().getType().name();
        e.currencyCode    = inv.getCurrencyCode();
        e.status          = inv.getStatus().name();
        e.issueDate       = inv.getIssueDate();
        e.dueDate         = inv.getDueDate();
        e.orderId         = inv.getOrderId();
        e.poId            = inv.getPoId();
        e.amountPaidAmt   = inv.getAmountPaid().getAmount();
        e.amountPaidCcy   = inv.getAmountPaid().getCurrencyCode();
        e.notes           = inv.getNotes();
        e.version         = inv.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt       = inv.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt       = inv.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy       = inv.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy       = inv.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        return e;
    }
}

package com.saas.invoice.service;

import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate.*;
import com.saas.invoice.events.InvoiceEvent;
import com.saas.invoice.spi.InvoiceRepository;
import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.events.OrderEvent;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.enterprise.event.Observes;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * Invoice application service.
 *
 * Auto-generates AR invoices when an order is confirmed (via event listener).
 * AP invoices are created manually when a supplier invoice arrives.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class InvoiceService {

    @Inject InvoiceRepository  repository;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    private static final AtomicLong COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    // ── Create (manual) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public InvoiceAggregate createInvoice(String tenantId, InvoiceType type,
                                           Party party, String currencyCode,
                                           LocalDate issueDate, int paymentTermsDays,
                                           String actor) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = InvoiceAggregate.create(
                tenantId, type, party, currencyCode, issueDate, paymentTermsDays, actor);
        return repository.save(inv);
    }

    @Transactional
    public InvoiceAggregate addLine(String id, String tenantId,
                                     String description, String productId,
                                     int qty, Money unitPrice, String actor) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = load(id, tenantId);
        inv.addLine(description, productId, qty, unitPrice, actor);
        return repository.save(inv);
    }

    @Transactional
    public InvoiceAggregate addPpnTax(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = load(id, tenantId);
        inv.addPpnTax(actor);
        return repository.save(inv);
    }

    @Transactional
    public InvoiceAggregate issueInvoice(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = load(id, tenantId);
        String number = generateInvoiceNumber(tenantId, inv.getType());
        inv.issue(number, actor);
        repository.save(inv);

        publisher.publish(new InvoiceEvent.InvoiceIssued(
                id, tenantId, number, inv.getParty().getId(),
                inv.getType().name(), inv.getTotal().toString(),
                inv.getCurrencyCode(), inv.getDueDate().toString(),
                actor, Instant.now()));
        return inv;
    }

    @Transactional
    public InvoiceAggregate recordPayment(String id, String tenantId,
                                           Money amount, String actor) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = load(id, tenantId);
        boolean fullyPaid = inv.recordPayment(amount, actor);
        repository.save(inv);

        publisher.publish(new InvoiceEvent.InvoicePaymentRecorded(
                id, tenantId, amount.toString(),
                inv.getAmountDue().toString(), actor, Instant.now()));

        if (fullyPaid) {
            publisher.publish(new InvoiceEvent.InvoicePaid(
                    id, tenantId, inv.getInvoiceNumber(),
                    inv.getParty().getId(), actor, Instant.now()));
        }
        return inv;
    }

    @Transactional
    public InvoiceAggregate voidInvoice(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = load(id, tenantId);
        inv.voidInvoice(actor);
        repository.save(inv);
        publisher.publish(new InvoiceEvent.InvoiceVoided(
                id, tenantId, inv.getInvoiceNumber(), actor, Instant.now()));
        return inv;
    }

    /**
     * Auto-create AR invoice when order is confirmed.
     * Listens to OrderConfirmed event — no direct coupling to OrderService.
     */
    @Transactional
    public void onOrderConfirmed(@Observes OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed event) {
        // In a real system: load the order from OrderRepository,
        // build invoice lines from order lines, then issue.
        // Shown as stub here to demonstrate the event-wiring pattern.
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<InvoiceAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public Optional<InvoiceAggregate> findByOrderId(String orderId, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findByOrderId(orderId, tenantId);
    }

    public List<InvoiceAggregate> findOverdue(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findOverdue(tenantId);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private InvoiceAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new InvoiceNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    private String generateInvoiceNumber(String tenantId, InvoiceType type) {
        String prefix = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        String typeCode = type == InvoiceType.RECEIVABLE ? "INV" : "BILL";
        return typeCode + "-" + prefix + "-" + String.format("%07d", COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
    }

    public static class InvoiceNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public InvoiceNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Invoice not found: " + id + " / " + t);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.invoice.service;

import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate;
import com.saas.invoice.spi.InvoiceRepository;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate.InvoiceStatus;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate.InvoiceType;
import io.quarkus.scheduler.Scheduled;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.jboss.logging.Logger;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Scheduled job that marks invoices as OVERDUE when their due date has passed.
 * Runs nightly at 02:00 server time.
 *
 * In a multi-tenant system, this iterates all tenants.
 * In production: replace with a DB-native query across tenants.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class OverdueInvoiceScheduler {

    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(OverdueInvoiceScheduler.class);

    @Inject InvoiceRepository repository;

    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 2 * * ?")
    @Transactional
    public void markOverdueInvoices() {
        LOG.info("Running overdue invoice check...");

        List<InvoiceAggregate> overdue = repository.findOverdue("*"); // implement per-tenant
        int count = 0;
        for (InvoiceAggregate inv : overdue) {
            try {
                inv.markOverdue("scheduler");
                repository.save(inv);
                count++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                LOG.errorf(e, "Failed to mark invoice %s as overdue", inv.getId());
            }
        }
        LOG.infof("Marked %d invoices as overdue", count);
    }
}

package com.saas.notification.api.rest;

import com.saas.notification.core.NotificationRecord;
import com.saas.notification.core.NotificationRecord.*;
import com.saas.notification.service.NotificationService;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Notification REST API (read-only — writes happen via domain events).
 *
 * GET  /api/v1/notifications/unread          → unread notifications for current user
 * GET  /api/v1/notifications                 → all notifications (paginated)
 * POST /api/v1/notifications/{id}/read       → mark as read
 * POST /api/v1/notifications/manual          → send manual notification
 */
@Path("/api/v1/notifications")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class NotificationResource {

    @Inject NotificationService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String userId() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "anonymous";
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/unread")
    public Response getUnread(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<NotifResp> items = service.findUnread(tid(rawTid), userId())
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    @GET
    public Response getAll(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("page") @DefaultValue("0") int page,
            @QueryParam("size") @DefaultValue("20") int size) {
        List<NotifResp> items = service.findAll(tid(rawTid), userId(), page, size)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/read")
    public Response markRead(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        service.markRead(id, tid(rawTid));
        return Response.noContent().build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/manual")
    public Response sendManual(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            ManualNotifRequest req) {
        NotificationRecord n = service.send(
                tid(rawTid), NotificationType.CUSTOM,
                NotificationChannel.valueOf(req.channel()),
                req.title(), req.message(),
                req.recipientId(), req.recipientEmail(),
                "manual", req.referenceId());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(n)).build();
    }

    private NotifResp toResp(NotificationRecord n) {
        return new NotifResp(
                n.getId(), n.getType().name(), n.getChannel().name(),
                n.getStatus().name(), n.getTitle(), n.getMessage(),
                n.getReferenceId(), n.getSourceModule(),
                n.getCreatedAt().toString(),
                n.getReadAt() != null ? n.getReadAt().toString() : null);
    }

    public record ManualNotifRequest(String channel, String title, String message,
            String recipientId, String recipientEmail, String referenceId) {}

    public record NotifResp(String id, String type, String channel, String status,
            String title, String message, String referenceId, String module,
            String createdAt, String readAt) {}
}

package com.saas.notification.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Notification record — an immutable alert triggered by a domain event.
 *
 * Notification types map to domain events:
 *  LOW_STOCK        ← InventoryEvent.LowStockAlert
 *  OUT_OF_STOCK     ← InventoryEvent.OutOfStockAlert
 *  INVOICE_OVERDUE  ← InvoiceEvent.InvoiceOverdue
 *  PAYMENT_PENDING  ← PaymentEvent.PaymentCreated (30+ days old)
 *  BUDGET_BREACH    ← BudgetService (variance > threshold)
 *  ORDER_CONFIRMED  ← OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed
 *  SHIPMENT_DELAYED ← ShipmentEvent (no dispatch after N days)
 *
 * Channels: IN_APP, EMAIL, WEBHOOK (future: SMS, Slack)
 */
public class NotificationRecord {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private NotificationType type;
    private NotificationChannel channel;
    private NotificationStatus status;
    private String title;
    private String message;
    private String recipientId;
    private String recipientEmail;
    private String sourceModule;
    private String sourceEventId;
    private String referenceId;      // orderId, invoiceId, etc.
    private Instant createdAt;
    private Instant sentAt;
    private Instant readAt;
    private String  failureReason;
    private int     retryCount;

    public static NotificationRecord create(String tenantId, NotificationType type,
                                             NotificationChannel channel, String title,
                                             String message, String recipientId,
                                             String recipientEmail, String sourceModule,
                                             String referenceId) {
        NotificationRecord n = new NotificationRecord();
        n.id             = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        n.tenantId       = tenantId;
        n.type           = type;
        n.channel        = channel;
        n.status         = NotificationStatus.PENDING;
        n.title          = title;
        n.message        = message;
        n.recipientId    = recipientId;
        n.recipientEmail = recipientEmail;
        n.sourceModule   = sourceModule;
        n.referenceId    = referenceId;
        n.createdAt      = Instant.now();
        n.retryCount     = 0;
        return n;
    }

    public void markSent() {
        this.status = NotificationStatus.SENT;
        this.sentAt = Instant.now();
    }

    public void markRead() {
        this.status = NotificationStatus.READ;
        this.readAt = Instant.now();
    }

    public void markFailed(String reason) {
        this.status        = NotificationStatus.FAILED;
        this.failureReason = reason;
        this.retryCount++;
    }

    // Getters
    public String getId()              { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()        { return tenantId; }
    public NotificationType getType()  { return type; }
    public NotificationChannel getChannel() { return channel; }
    public NotificationStatus getStatus()   { return status; }
    public String getTitle()           { return title; }
    public String getMessage()         { return message; }
    public String getRecipientId()     { return recipientId; }
    public String getRecipientEmail()  { return recipientEmail; }
    public String getSourceModule()    { return sourceModule; }
    public String getReferenceId()     { return referenceId; }
    public Instant getCreatedAt()      { return createdAt; }
    public Instant getSentAt()         { return sentAt; }
    public Instant getReadAt()         { return readAt; }
    public String getFailureReason()   { return failureReason; }
    public int    getRetryCount()      { return retryCount; }

    public enum NotificationType {
        LOW_STOCK, OUT_OF_STOCK, INVOICE_OVERDUE, PAYMENT_PENDING,
        BUDGET_BREACH, ORDER_CONFIRMED, ORDER_FULFILLED, SHIPMENT_DELAYED,
        PAYROLL_APPROVED, ASSET_FULLY_DEPRECIATED, CUSTOM
    }

    public enum NotificationChannel { IN_APP, EMAIL, WEBHOOK, SMS }

    public enum NotificationStatus { PENDING, SENT, READ, FAILED }
}

Size: 6.9 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.notification.service;

import com.saas.inventory.events.InventoryEvent;
import com.saas.invoice.events.InvoiceEvent;
import com.saas.notification.core.NotificationRecord;
import com.saas.notification.core.NotificationRecord.*;
import com.saas.notification.spi.NotificationRepository;
import com.saas.order.events.OrderEvent;
import com.saas.payment.events.PaymentEvent;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.enterprise.event.Observes;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.jboss.logging.Logger;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Notification service — the cross-cutting alert system.
 *
 * Listens to domain events from all modules and creates
 * appropriate notification records.
 *
 * Delivery channels:
 *  IN_APP  → stored in DB, retrieved by frontend polling
 *  EMAIL   → delegated to EmailSender (SMTP/SES)
 *  WEBHOOK → delegated to WebhookSender
 *
 * Current implementation stores IN_APP notifications.
 * Email/webhook delivery is stubbed for future integration.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class NotificationService {

    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(NotificationService.class);

    @Inject NotificationRepository repository;

    // ── Manual notification ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public NotificationRecord send(String tenantId, NotificationType type,
                                    NotificationChannel channel, String title,
                                    String message, String recipientId,
                                    String recipientEmail, String sourceModule,
                                    String referenceId) {
        NotificationRecord n = NotificationRecord.create(
                tenantId, type, channel, title, message,
                recipientId, recipientEmail, sourceModule, referenceId);
        deliver(n);
        return repository.save(n);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void markRead(String id, String tenantId) {
        repository.findById(id, tenantId).ifPresent(n -> {
            n.markRead();
            repository.save(n);
        });
    }

    public List<NotificationRecord> findUnread(String tenantId, String recipientId) {
        return repository.findUnread(tenantId, recipientId);
    }

    public List<NotificationRecord> findAll(String tenantId, String recipientId,
                                             int page, int size) {
        return repository.findByRecipient(tenantId, recipientId, page, size);
    }

    // ── Domain event listeners ────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public void onLowStock(@Observes InventoryEvent.LowStockAlert event) {
        createNotification(event.tenantId(), NotificationType.LOW_STOCK,
                "Low Stock Alert",
                String.format("Product %s in warehouse %s has only %d units available " +
                              "(reorder point: %d).",
                              event.productId(), event.warehouseId(),
                              event.available(), event.reorderPoint()),
                "inventory", event.productId());
    }

    @Transactional
    public void onOutOfStock(@Observes InventoryEvent.OutOfStockAlert event) {
        createNotification(event.tenantId(), NotificationType.OUT_OF_STOCK,
                "⚠️ Out of Stock",
                String.format("Product %s in warehouse %s is OUT OF STOCK.",
                              event.productId(), event.warehouseId()),
                "inventory", event.productId());
    }

    @Transactional
    public void onInvoiceOverdue(@Observes InvoiceEvent.InvoiceOverdue event) {
        createNotification(event.tenantId(), NotificationType.INVOICE_OVERDUE,
                "Invoice Overdue",
                String.format("Invoice %s is overdue. Please follow up with the customer.",
                              event.invoiceNumber()),
                "invoice", event.aggregateId());
    }

    @Transactional
    public void onOrderFulfilled(@Observes OrderEvent.OrderFulfilled event) {
        createNotification(event.tenantId(), NotificationType.ORDER_FULFILLED,
                "Order Fulfilled",
                String.format("Order %s has been fully fulfilled.", event.aggregateId()),
                "order", event.aggregateId());
    }

    @Transactional
    public void onOrderConfirmed(@Observes OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed event) {
        createNotification(event.tenantId(), NotificationType.ORDER_CONFIRMED,
                "New Order Confirmed",
                String.format("Order %s (%s) has been confirmed. Amount: %s %s.",
                              event.orderNumber(), event.paymentMethod(),
                              event.grandTotal(), event.currencyCode()),
                "order", event.aggregateId());
    }

    @Transactional
    public void onInvoicePaid(@Observes InvoiceEvent.InvoicePaid event) {
        createNotification(event.tenantId(), NotificationType.PAYMENT_PENDING,
                "Invoice Paid",
                String.format("Invoice %s has been fully paid.", event.invoiceNumber()),
                "invoice", event.aggregateId());
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void createNotification(String tenantId, NotificationType type,
                                     String title, String message,
                                     String sourceModule, String referenceId) {
        NotificationRecord n = NotificationRecord.create(
                tenantId, type, NotificationChannel.IN_APP,
                title, message, "system", null, sourceModule, referenceId);
        deliver(n);
        repository.save(n);
        LOG.debugf("Notification created: type=%s tenant=%s ref=%s",
                type, tenantId, referenceId);
    }

    private void deliver(NotificationRecord n) {
        try {
            switch (n.getChannel()) {
                case IN_APP -> n.markSent();   // stored in DB — frontend polls
                case EMAIL  -> {
                    // Future: emailSender.send(n.getRecipientEmail(), n.getTitle(), n.getMessage());
                    n.markSent();
                }
                case WEBHOOK -> {
                    // Future: webhookSender.post(webhookUrl, n);
                    n.markSent();
                }
                case SMS -> {
                    // Future: smsSender.send(n.getRecipientId(), n.getMessage());
                    n.markSent();
                }
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            n.markFailed(e.getMessage());
            LOG.warnf("Failed to deliver notification %s: %s", n.getId(), e.getMessage());
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.order.api.rest;

import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderId;
import com.saas.order.service.OrderService;
import com.saas.order.service.OrderService.OrderNotFoundException;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Order REST API.
 * All endpoints require X-Tenant-Id header.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/orders                          → create draft order
 * POST   /api/v1/orders/{id}/lines               → add line
 * DELETE /api/v1/orders/{id}/lines/{lineId}      → remove line
 * PATCH  /api/v1/orders/{id}/lines/{lineId}      → update line qty
 * POST   /api/v1/orders/{id}/confirm             → DRAFT → CONFIRMED
 * POST   /api/v1/orders/{id}/process             → CONFIRMED → PROCESSING
 * POST   /api/v1/orders/{id}/lines/{lid}/fulfill → record fulfillment
 * POST   /api/v1/orders/{id}/cancel              → cancel
 * POST   /api/v1/orders/{id}/pay                 → mark paid
 * GET    /api/v1/orders/{id}                     → get by id
 */
@Path("/api/v1/orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class OrderResource {

    @Inject OrderService orderService;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "anonymous";
    }

    // ── Create ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST
    public Response create(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateOrderRequest req) {

        Party customer = Party.customer(
                req.customerId(), req.customerName(), req.customerTaxId());
        OrderAggregate order = orderService.createOrder(
                tid(rawTid), customer, req.channel(),
                req.currencyCode(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    // ── Lines ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/lines")
    public Response addLine(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid AddLineRequest req) {

        Money unitPrice = Money.of(req.unitPrice(), req.currencyCode());
        Money unitTax   = req.unitTax() != null
                ? Money.of(req.unitTax(), req.currencyCode())
                : Money.zero(req.currencyCode());

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.addLine(
                OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid),
                req.productId(), req.sku(), req.productName(),
                req.quantity(), unitPrice, unitTax,
                req.notes(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    @DELETE
    @Path("/{id}/lines/{lineId}")
    public Response removeLine(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @PathParam("lineId") String lineId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.removeLine(
                OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid), lineId, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    @PATCH
    @Path("/{id}/lines/{lineId}")
    public Response updateLineQty(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @PathParam("lineId") String lineId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            UpdateQtyRequest req) {

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.updateLineQuantity(
                OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid), lineId, req.quantity(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/confirm")
    public Response confirm(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            ConfirmRequest req) {

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.confirmOrder(
                OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid),
                req.paymentMethod(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/process")
    public Response process(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.startProcessing(
                OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/lines/{lineId}/fulfill")
    public Response fulfill(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @PathParam("lineId") String lineId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            FulfillRequest req) {

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.recordLineFulfillment(
                OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid), lineId, req.quantity(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/cancel")
    public Response cancel(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("reason") @DefaultValue("") String reason) {

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.cancelOrder(
                OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid), reason, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/pay")
    public Response markPaid(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            PayRequest req) {

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.markPaid(
                OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid), req.paymentReference(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResponse(order)).build();
    }

    // ── Read ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {

        return orderService.findById(OrderId.of(id), tid(rawTid))
                .map(o -> Response.ok(toResponse(o)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404)
                        .entity(new ErrResp("NOT_FOUND", "Order not found: " + id))
                        .build());
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/number/{orderNumber}")
    public Response getByNumber(
            @PathParam("orderNumber") String num,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {

        return orderService.findByOrderNumber(num, tid(rawTid))
                .map(o -> Response.ok(toResponse(o)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404)
                        .entity(new ErrResp("NOT_FOUND", "Order not found: " + num))
                        .build());
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private OrderResponse toResponse(OrderAggregate o) {
        return new OrderResponse(
                o.getId().getValue(), o.getTenantId(), o.getOrderNumber(),
                o.getCustomer().getName(), o.getStatus().name(),
                o.getChannel(), o.getCurrencyCode(),
                o.getSubtotal().getAmount(), o.getTotalTax().getAmount(),
                o.getGrandTotal().getAmount(),
                o.getLines().size(), o.getTotalQuantity(),
                o.isPaid(), o.getInvoiceId(), o.getShipmentId(),
                o.getAudit().getVersion(), o.getAudit().getUpdatedAt().toString());
    }

    // ── Exception mapper ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider
    public static class OrderNotFoundMapper
            implements jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper<OrderNotFoundException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(OrderNotFoundException e) {
            return Response.status(404)
                    .entity(new ErrResp("NOT_FOUND", e.getMessage()))
                    .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON).build();
        }
    }

    // ── Request / Response records ────────────────────────────────────────

    public record CreateOrderRequest(
            @NotBlank String customerId,
            @NotBlank String customerName,
            String customerTaxId,
            String channel,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record AddLineRequest(
            @NotBlank String productId,
            @NotBlank String sku,
            @NotBlank String productName,
            @Positive int quantity,
            @NotNull BigDecimal unitPrice,
            BigDecimal unitTax,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            String notes) {}

    public record UpdateQtyRequest(@Positive int quantity) {}
    public record ConfirmRequest(@NotBlank String paymentMethod) {}
    public record FulfillRequest(@Positive int quantity) {}
    public record PayRequest(@NotBlank String paymentReference) {}

    public record OrderResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String orderNumber,
            String customerName, String status, String channel,
            String currencyCode, BigDecimal subtotal, BigDecimal tax,
            BigDecimal grandTotal, int lineCount, int totalQty,
            boolean isPaid, String invoiceId, String shipmentId,
            long version, String updatedAt) {}

    public record ErrResp(String code, String message) {}
}

package com.saas.order.core;

import com.saas.order.core.model.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.*;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Order aggregate root — reflection-free version.
 * id/tenantId are non-final but written only once in static factory.
 */
public class OrderAggregate {

    private OrderId id;
    private String  tenantId;
    private String  orderNumber;
    private Party   customer;
    private Address shippingAddress;
    private Address billingAddress;
    private final List<OrderLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();
    private OrderStatus status;
    private String      channel;
    private String      currencyCode;
    private Instant     confirmedAt;
    private Instant     fulfilledAt;
    private Instant     cancelledAt;
    private String      cancellationReason;
    private String      paymentMethod;
    private String      paymentReference;
    private boolean     isPaid;
    private String      invoiceId;
    private String      shipmentId;
    private String      notes;
    private AuditMeta   audit;

    public static OrderAggregate create(String tenantId, Party customer,
                                        String channel, String currencyCode, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate o = new OrderAggregate();
        o.id           = OrderId.generate();
        o.tenantId     = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId, "tenantId");
        o.customer     = Objects.requireNonNull(customer, "customer");
        o.channel      = channel != null ? channel : "default";
        o.currencyCode = Objects.requireNonNull(currencyCode, "currencyCode");
        o.status       = OrderStatus.DRAFT;
        o.isPaid       = false;
        o.audit        = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return o;
    }

    public static OrderAggregate reconstitute(
            OrderId id, String tenantId, String orderNumber,
            Party customer, Address shipping, Address billing,
            List<OrderLine> lines, OrderStatus status,
            String channel, String currencyCode,
            Instant confirmedAt, Instant fulfilledAt,
            Instant cancelledAt, String cancellationReason,
            String paymentMethod, String paymentReference, boolean isPaid,
            String invoiceId, String shipmentId, String notes, AuditMeta audit) {
        OrderAggregate o = new OrderAggregate();
        o.id = id; o.tenantId = tenantId; o.orderNumber = orderNumber;
        o.customer = customer; o.shippingAddress = shipping; o.billingAddress = billing;
        if (lines != null) o.lines.addAll(lines);
        o.status = status; o.channel = channel; o.currencyCode = currencyCode;
        o.confirmedAt = confirmedAt; o.fulfilledAt = fulfilledAt;
        o.cancelledAt = cancelledAt; o.cancellationReason = cancellationReason;
        o.paymentMethod = paymentMethod; o.paymentReference = paymentReference;
        o.isPaid = isPaid; o.invoiceId = invoiceId; o.shipmentId = shipmentId;
        o.notes = notes; o.audit = audit;
        return o;
    }

    private OrderAggregate() {}

    public OrderLine addLine(String productId, String sku, String productName,
                              int qty, Money unitPrice, Money unitTax,
                              String lineNotes, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        if (qty <= 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Quantity must be positive");
        Optional<OrderLine> existing = lines.stream()
                .filter(l -> l.getProductId().equals(productId)).findFirst();
        if (existing.isPresent()) {
            OrderLine merged = existing.get().toBuilder()
                    .quantityOrdered(existing.get().getQuantityOrdered() + qty).build();
            lines.set(lines.indexOf(existing.get()), merged);
            audit = audit.touch(actor);
            return merged;
        }
        OrderLine line = OrderLine.builder().productId(productId).sku(sku)
                .productName(productName).quantityOrdered(qty)
                .unitPrice(unitPrice).unitTax(unitTax).notes(lineNotes).build();
        lines.add(line);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return line;
    }

    public void removeLine(String lineId, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        if (!lines.removeIf(l -> l.getId().equals(lineId)))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Line not found: " + lineId);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void updateLineQuantity(String lineId, int newQty, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        if (newQty <= 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Quantity must be positive");
        int idx = findLineIndex(lineId);
        lines.set(idx, lines.get(idx).toBuilder().quantityOrdered(newQty).build());
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void confirm(String orderNumber, String paymentMethod, String actor) {
        assertTransition(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
        if (lines.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot confirm order with no lines");
        this.orderNumber = orderNumber; this.paymentMethod = paymentMethod;
        this.status = OrderStatus.CONFIRMED; this.confirmedAt = Instant.now();
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void startProcessing(String actor) {
        assertTransition(OrderStatus.PROCESSING);
        this.status = OrderStatus.PROCESSING; audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void recordLineFulfillment(String lineId, int qtyShipped, String actor) {
        if (status != OrderStatus.PROCESSING && status != OrderStatus.PARTIALLY_FULFILLED)
            throw new OrderStatusException("Must be PROCESSING/PARTIALLY_FULFILLED, was: " + status);
        int idx = findLineIndex(lineId);
        lines.set(idx, lines.get(idx).withAdditionalFulfillment(qtyShipped));
        boolean all = lines.stream().allMatch(OrderLine::isFullyFulfilled);
        boolean any = lines.stream().anyMatch(l -> l.getQuantityFulfilled() > 0);
        if (all) { this.status = OrderStatus.FULFILLED; this.fulfilledAt = Instant.now(); }
        else if (any) { this.status = OrderStatus.PARTIALLY_FULFILLED; }
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void cancel(String reason, String actor) {
        assertTransition(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
        this.status = OrderStatus.CANCELLED; this.cancellationReason = reason;
        this.cancelledAt = Instant.now(); audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void markPaid(String ref, String actor) {
        this.isPaid = true; this.paymentReference = ref; audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }
    public void setInvoiceId(String v, String actor)  { this.invoiceId  = v; audit = audit.touch(actor); }
    public void setShipmentId(String v, String actor) { this.shipmentId = v; audit = audit.touch(actor); }
    public void setAddresses(Address s, Address b, String actor) {
        assertDraft(); this.shippingAddress = s; this.billingAddress = b; audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }
    public void setNotes(String v, String actor) { this.notes = v; audit = audit.touch(actor); }

    public Money getSubtotal() {
        return lines.stream().map(l -> l.getUnitPrice().multiply(l.getQuantityOrdered()))
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }
    public Money getTotalTax() {
        return lines.stream().map(l -> l.getUnitTax().multiply(l.getQuantityOrdered()))
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }
    public Money getGrandTotal()  { return getSubtotal().add(getTotalTax()); }
    public int   getTotalQuantity() { return lines.stream().mapToInt(OrderLine::getQuantityOrdered).sum(); }

    private void assertDraft() {
        if (status != OrderStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new OrderStatusException("Requires DRAFT, current: " + status);
    }
    private void assertTransition(OrderStatus t) {
        if (!status.canTransitionTo(t))
            throw new OrderStatusException("Illegal transition: " + status + " → " + t);
    }
    private int findLineIndex(String lineId) {
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++)
            if (lines.get(i).getId().equals(lineId)) return i;
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Order line not found: " + lineId);
    }

    public OrderId   getId()                { return id; }
    public String    getTenantId()          { return tenantId; }
    public String    getOrderNumber()       { return orderNumber; }
    public Party     getCustomer()          { return customer; }
    public Address   getShippingAddress()   { return shippingAddress; }
    public Address   getBillingAddress()    { return billingAddress; }
    public List<OrderLine> getLines()       { return Collections.unmodifiableList(lines); }
    public OrderStatus getStatus()          { return status; }
    public String    getChannel()           { return channel; }
    public String    getCurrencyCode()      { return currencyCode; }
    public Instant   getConfirmedAt()       { return confirmedAt; }
    public Instant   getFulfilledAt()       { return fulfilledAt; }
    public Instant   getCancelledAt()       { return cancelledAt; }
    public String    getCancellationReason(){ return cancellationReason; }
    public String    getPaymentMethod()     { return paymentMethod; }
    public String    getPaymentReference()  { return paymentReference; }
    public boolean   isPaid()               { return isPaid; }
    public String    getInvoiceId()         { return invoiceId; }
    public String    getShipmentId()        { return shipmentId; }
    public String    getNotes()             { return notes; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()             { return audit; }
    public long      getVersion()           { return audit.getVersion(); }

    public static class OrderStatusException extends RuntimeException {
        public OrderStatusException(String msg) { super(msg); }
    }
}

package com.saas.order.core.model;

import java.util.UUID;

public final class OrderId {
    private final String value;
    private OrderId(String v) { this.value = v; }
    public static OrderId generate() { return new OrderId(UUID.randomUUID().toString()); }
    public static OrderId of(String v) { return new OrderId(v); }
    public String getValue() { return value; }
    @Override public String toString() { return value; }
    @Override public boolean equals(Object o) {
        return o instanceof OrderId oi && value.equals(oi.value);
    }
    @Override public int hashCode() { return value.hashCode(); }
}

package com.saas.order.core.model;

import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * An immutable order line snapshot.
 *
 * Prices are snapshotted at order time — changes to the product catalog
 * do NOT retroactively change existing orders.
 *
 * quantityFulfilled tracks how many units have been shipped.
 */
public final class OrderLine {

    private final String id;
    private final String productId;
    private final String sku;            // snapshot
    private final String productName;    // snapshot
    private final int    quantityOrdered;
    private final int    quantityFulfilled;
    private final Money  unitPrice;      // after discounts, before tax
    private final Money  unitTax;        // tax per unit
    private final Money  unitTotal;      // unitPrice + unitTax
    private final Money  lineTotal;      // unitTotal × quantityOrdered
    private final String notes;

    private OrderLine(Builder b) {
        this.id                = b.id != null ? b.id : UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        this.productId         = Objects.requireNonNull(b.productId, "productId");
        this.sku               = Objects.requireNonNull(b.sku, "sku");
        this.productName       = Objects.requireNonNull(b.productName, "productName");
        this.quantityOrdered   = b.quantityOrdered;
        this.quantityFulfilled = b.quantityFulfilled;
        this.unitPrice         = Objects.requireNonNull(b.unitPrice, "unitPrice");
        this.unitTax           = b.unitTax != null ? b.unitTax : Money.zero(b.unitPrice.getCurrencyCode());
        this.unitTotal         = unitPrice.add(unitTax);
        this.lineTotal         = unitTotal.multiply(quantityOrdered);
        this.notes             = b.notes;
    }

    // ── Derived queries ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public int getQuantityPending()    { return quantityOrdered - quantityFulfilled; }
    public boolean isFullyFulfilled()  { return quantityFulfilled >= quantityOrdered; }
    public boolean isPartiallyFulfilled() {
        return quantityFulfilled > 0 && quantityFulfilled < quantityOrdered;
    }

    /** Return a new OrderLine with incremented fulfillment count */
    public OrderLine withAdditionalFulfillment(int qty) {
        int newFulfilled = Math.min(quantityFulfilled + qty, quantityOrdered);
        return toBuilder().quantityFulfilled(newFulfilled).build();
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                { return id; }
    public String getProductId()         { return productId; }
    public String getSku()               { return sku; }
    public String getProductName()       { return productName; }
    public int    getQuantityOrdered()   { return quantityOrdered; }
    public int    getQuantityFulfilled() { return quantityFulfilled; }
    public Money  getUnitPrice()         { return unitPrice; }
    public Money  getUnitTax()           { return unitTax; }
    public Money  getUnitTotal()         { return unitTotal; }
    public Money  getLineTotal()         { return lineTotal; }
    public String getNotes()             { return notes; }

    // ── Builder ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Builder toBuilder() {
        return new Builder()
                .id(id).productId(productId).sku(sku).productName(productName)
                .quantityOrdered(quantityOrdered).quantityFulfilled(quantityFulfilled)
                .unitPrice(unitPrice).unitTax(unitTax).notes(notes);
    }

    public static Builder builder() { return new Builder(); }

    public static final class Builder {
        private String id, productId, sku, productName, notes;
        private int    quantityOrdered = 1;
        private int    quantityFulfilled = 0;
        private Money  unitPrice, unitTax;

        public Builder id(String v)                    { this.id = v; return this; }
        public Builder productId(String v)             { this.productId = v; return this; }
        public Builder sku(String v)                   { this.sku = v; return this; }
        public Builder productName(String v)           { this.productName = v; return this; }
        public Builder quantityOrdered(int v)          { this.quantityOrdered = v; return this; }
        public Builder quantityFulfilled(int v)        { this.quantityFulfilled = v; return this; }
        public Builder unitPrice(Money v)              { this.unitPrice = v; return this; }
        public Builder unitTax(Money v)                { this.unitTax = v; return this; }
        public Builder notes(String v)                 { this.notes = v; return this; }
        public OrderLine build()                       { return new OrderLine(this); }
    }
}

Size: 1.2 KB | Modified: 2026-04-01 20:27:10
