package com.saas.order.core.model;

/**
 * Order lifecycle state machine.
 *
 * DRAFT ──► CONFIRMED ──► PROCESSING ──► PARTIALLY_FULFILLED
 *                  │             │               │
 *                  │             └───────────────┴──► FULFILLED
 *                  │
 *                  └──► CANCELLED  (from DRAFT or CONFIRMED only)
 *
 * CONFIRMED  → payment captured or credit approved
 * PROCESSING → at least one line is being picked/packed
 * FULFILLED  → all lines shipped
 */
public enum OrderStatus {
    DRAFT,
    CONFIRMED,
    PROCESSING,
    PARTIALLY_FULFILLED,
    FULFILLED,
    CANCELLED;

    public boolean canTransitionTo(OrderStatus target) {
        return switch (this) {
            case DRAFT               -> target == CONFIRMED   || target == CANCELLED;
            case CONFIRMED           -> target == PROCESSING  || target == CANCELLED;
            case PROCESSING          -> target == PARTIALLY_FULFILLED || target == FULFILLED;
            case PARTIALLY_FULFILLED -> target == FULFILLED;
            case FULFILLED, CANCELLED -> false;
        };
    }

    public boolean isClosed() {
        return this == FULFILLED || this == CANCELLED;
    }
}

package com.saas.order.events;

import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEvent;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;

/** All order domain events — sealed for exhaustive switch handling. */
public sealed interface OrderEvent extends DomainEvent
        permits OrderEvent.OrderCreated,
                OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed,
                OrderEvent.OrderLineAdded,
                OrderEvent.OrderProcessing,
                OrderEvent.OrderLineFulfilled,
                OrderEvent.OrderFulfilled,
                OrderEvent.OrderCancelled,
                OrderEvent.OrderPaid,
                OrderEvent.OrderInvoiced {

    record OrderCreated(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String customerId,
            String channel, String currencyCode,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}

    record OrderConfirmed(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String orderNumber,
            String paymentMethod, String grandTotal, String currencyCode,
            int lineCount, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}

    record OrderLineAdded(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String productId,
            String sku, int quantity, String unitPrice,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}

    record OrderProcessing(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}

    record OrderLineFulfilled(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String lineId,
            int qtyShipped, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}

    record OrderFulfilled(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}

    record OrderCancelled(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String reason,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}

    record OrderPaid(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String paymentReference,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}

    record OrderInvoiced(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String invoiceId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements OrderEvent {}
}

package com.saas.order.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.core.model.*;
import com.saas.order.infrastructure.entity.OrderEntity;
import com.saas.order.infrastructure.entity.OrderLineEntity;
import com.saas.order.spi.OrderRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.*;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.*;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanacheOrderRepository
        implements OrderRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<OrderEntity, String> {

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate save(OrderAggregate order) {
        OrderEntity e = toEntity(order);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) {
            getEntityManager().merge(e);
        } else {
            persist(e);
        }
        // Save/merge lines
        saveLines(order);
        return order;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<OrderAggregate> findById(OrderId id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", id.getValue(), tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<OrderAggregate> findByOrderNumber(String num, String tenantId) {
        return find("orderNumber = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", num, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void delete(OrderId id, String tenantId) {
        delete("id = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", id.getValue(), tenantId);
    }

    // ── Lines ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private List<OrderLine> loadLines(String orderId) {
        return getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("SELECT l FROM OrderLineEntity l WHERE l.orderId = :oid",
                        OrderLineEntity.class)
                .setParameter("oid", orderId)
                .getResultList()
                .stream().map(this::toDomainLine).toList();
    }

    private void saveLines(OrderAggregate order) {
        // Delete existing lines then re-insert (simplest safe approach for small line counts)
        getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("DELETE FROM OrderLineEntity l WHERE l.orderId = :oid")
                .setParameter("oid", order.getId().getValue())
                .executeUpdate();
        for (OrderLine line : order.getLines()) {
            OrderLineEntity le = toLineEntity(line, order.getId().getValue(),
                    order.getTenantId(), order.getCurrencyCode());
            getEntityManager().persist(le);
        }
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private OrderAggregate toDomain(OrderEntity e, List<OrderLine> lines) {
        Party customer = new Party(e.customerId, Party.PartyType.CUSTOMER,
                e.customerName != null ? e.customerName : "Unknown",
                e.customerTaxId);
        AuditMeta audit = AuditMeta.reconstitute(
                e.createdAt, e.updatedAt, e.createdBy, e.updatedBy, e.version);

        return OrderAggregate.reconstitute(
                OrderId.of(e.id), e.tenantId, e.orderNumber,
                customer, null, null,   // addresses decoded separately if needed
                lines, OrderStatus.valueOf(e.status),
                e.channel, e.currencyCode,
                e.confirmedAt, e.fulfilledAt,
                e.cancelledAt, e.cancellationReason,
                e.paymentMethod, e.paymentReference, e.isPaid,
                e.invoiceId, e.shipmentId, e.notes, audit);
    }

    private OrderEntity toEntity(OrderAggregate o) {
        OrderEntity e     = new OrderEntity();
        e.id              = o.getId().getValue();
        e.tenantId        = o.getTenantId();
        e.orderNumber     = o.getOrderNumber();
        e.customerId      = o.getCustomer().getId();
        e.customerName    = o.getCustomer().getName();
        e.customerTaxId   = o.getCustomer().getTaxId();
        e.channel         = o.getChannel();
        e.currencyCode    = o.getCurrencyCode();
        e.status          = o.getStatus().name();
        e.paymentMethod   = o.getPaymentMethod();
        e.paymentReference = o.getPaymentReference();
        e.isPaid          = o.isPaid();
        e.invoiceId       = o.getInvoiceId();
        e.shipmentId      = o.getShipmentId();
        e.notes           = o.getNotes();
        e.confirmedAt     = o.getConfirmedAt();
        e.fulfilledAt     = o.getFulfilledAt();
        e.cancelledAt     = o.getCancelledAt();
        e.cancellationReason = o.getCancellationReason();
        e.version         = o.getVersion();
        e.createdAt       = o.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt       = o.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy       = o.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy       = o.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        return e;
    }

    private OrderLine toDomainLine(OrderLineEntity le) {
        return OrderLine.builder()
                .id(le.id)
                .productId(le.productId)
                .sku(le.sku)
                .productName(le.productName)
                .quantityOrdered(le.quantityOrdered)
                .quantityFulfilled(le.quantityFulfilled)
                .unitPrice(Money.of(le.unitPriceAmt, le.unitPriceCcy))
                .unitTax(le.unitTaxAmt != null
                        ? Money.of(le.unitTaxAmt, le.unitTaxCcy)
                        : Money.zero(le.unitPriceCcy))
                .notes(le.notes)
                .build();
    }

    private OrderLineEntity toLineEntity(OrderLine l, String orderId,
                                          String tenantId, String ccy) {
        OrderLineEntity le    = new OrderLineEntity();
        le.id                 = l.getId();
        le.orderId            = orderId;
        le.tenantId           = tenantId;
        le.productId          = l.getProductId();
        le.sku                = l.getSku();
        le.productName        = l.getProductName();
        le.quantityOrdered    = l.getQuantityOrdered();
        le.quantityFulfilled  = l.getQuantityFulfilled();
        le.unitPriceAmt       = l.getUnitPrice().getAmount();
        le.unitPriceCcy       = l.getUnitPrice().getCurrencyCode();
        le.unitTaxAmt         = l.getUnitTax().getAmount();
        le.unitTaxCcy         = l.getUnitTax().getCurrencyCode();
        le.notes              = l.getNotes();
        return le;
    }
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/order/service/OrderService.java

package com.saas.order.service;

import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderId;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderStatus;
import com.saas.order.events.OrderEvent;
import com.saas.order.spi.OrderRepository;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * Order application service.
 *
 * Sequence for a typical B2C order:
 *   1. createOrder()           → DRAFT
 *   2. addLine() × N           → lines added
 *   3. setAddresses()          → shipping/billing confirmed
 *   4. confirmOrder()          → CONFIRMED + stock reserved (via event)
 *   5. startProcessing()       → PROCESSING
 *   6. recordLineFulfillment() → PARTIALLY_FULFILLED or FULFILLED
 *   7. markPaid()              → isPaid = true
 *
 * Stock reservation and invoice generation happen via domain event listeners
 * in their respective modules — no direct coupling here.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class OrderService {

    @Inject OrderRepository repository;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    /** CDI no-arg constructor. */
    public OrderService() {}

    /**
     * Constructor injection for unit tests (no CDI required).
     * Use with InMemoryRepositories and CapturingEventPublisher.
     */
    public OrderService(OrderRepository repository, DomainEventPublisher publisher) {
        this.repository = repository;
        this.publisher  = publisher;
    }

    // Simple sequential order number — replace with DB sequence in production
    private static final AtomicLong COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    // ── Create ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate createOrder(String tenantId, Party customer,
                                      String channel, String currencyCode,
                                      String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = OrderAggregate.create(tenantId, customer, channel, currencyCode, actor);
        repository.save(order);

        publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderCreated(
                order.getId().getValue(), tenantId,
                customer.getId(), channel, currencyCode, actor, Instant.now()));
        return order;
    }

    // ── Line management ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate addLine(OrderId id, String tenantId,
                                   String productId, String sku, String productName,
                                   int qty, Money unitPrice, Money unitTax,
                                   String lineNotes, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        var line = order.addLine(productId, sku, productName, qty, unitPrice, unitTax, lineNotes, actor);
        repository.save(order);

        publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderLineAdded(
                id.getValue(), tenantId, productId, sku, qty,
                unitPrice.toString(), actor, Instant.now()));
        return order;
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate removeLine(OrderId id, String tenantId, String lineId, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.removeLine(lineId, actor);
        return repository.save(order);
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate updateLineQuantity(OrderId id, String tenantId,
                                              String lineId, int qty, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.updateLineQuantity(lineId, qty, actor);
        return repository.save(order);
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate setAddresses(OrderId id, String tenantId,
                                        Address shipping, Address billing, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.setAddresses(shipping, billing, actor);
        return repository.save(order);
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate confirmOrder(OrderId id, String tenantId,
                                        String paymentMethod, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        String orderNumber = generateOrderNumber(tenantId);
        order.confirm(orderNumber, paymentMethod, actor);
        repository.save(order);

        publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed(
                id.getValue(), tenantId, orderNumber, paymentMethod,
                order.getGrandTotal().toString(), order.getCurrencyCode(),
                order.getLines().size(), actor, Instant.now()));
        return order;
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate startProcessing(OrderId id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.startProcessing(actor);
        repository.save(order);
        publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderProcessing(
                id.getValue(), tenantId, actor, Instant.now()));
        return order;
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate recordLineFulfillment(OrderId id, String tenantId,
                                                 String lineId, int qtyShipped, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.recordLineFulfillment(lineId, qtyShipped, actor);
        repository.save(order);

        publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderLineFulfilled(
                id.getValue(), tenantId, lineId, qtyShipped, actor, Instant.now()));

        if (order.getStatus() == OrderStatus.FULFILLED) {
            publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderFulfilled(
                    id.getValue(), tenantId, actor, Instant.now()));
        }
        return order;
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate cancelOrder(OrderId id, String tenantId, String reason, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.cancel(reason, actor);
        repository.save(order);
        publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderCancelled(
                id.getValue(), tenantId, reason, actor, Instant.now()));
        return order;
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate markPaid(OrderId id, String tenantId,
                                    String paymentRef, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.markPaid(paymentRef, actor);
        repository.save(order);
        publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderPaid(
                id.getValue(), tenantId, paymentRef, actor, Instant.now()));
        return order;
    }

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate linkInvoice(OrderId id, String tenantId,
                                       String invoiceId, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.setInvoiceId(invoiceId, actor);
        repository.save(order);
        publisher.publish(new OrderEvent.OrderInvoiced(
                id.getValue(), tenantId, invoiceId, actor, Instant.now()));
        return order;
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<OrderAggregate> findById(OrderId id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public Optional<OrderAggregate> findByOrderNumber(String num, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findByOrderNumber(num, tenantId);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private OrderAggregate load(OrderId id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new OrderNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    private String generateOrderNumber(String tenantId) {
        // Production: use DB sequence per tenant → "ORD-2024-000001"
        String prefix = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        return "ORD-" + prefix + "-" + String.format("%08d", COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
    }

    public static class OrderNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public OrderNotFoundException(OrderId id, String t) {
            super("Order not found: " + id + " / tenant=" + t);
        }
    }
}

    @Transactional
    public OrderAggregate linkShipment(OrderId id, String tenantId,
                                        String shipmentId, String actor) {
        OrderAggregate order = load(id, tenantId);
        order.setShipmentId(shipmentId, actor);
        return repository.save(order);
    }

Size: 6.0 KB | Modified: 2026-04-03 08:23:49

package com.saas.payment.api.rest;

import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord;
import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord.*;
import com.saas.payment.service.PaymentService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Payment REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/payments                         → create payment record
 * POST   /api/v1/payments/{id}/process            → PENDING → PROCESSED
 * POST   /api/v1/payments/{id}/reconcile          → PROCESSED → RECONCILED
 * POST   /api/v1/payments/{id}/fail               → mark failed
 * POST   /api/v1/payments/{id}/cancel             → cancel
 * GET    /api/v1/payments/{id}                    → get
 * GET    /api/v1/payments/invoice/{invoiceId}     → payments for invoice
 * GET    /api/v1/payments/unreconciled            → unreconciled payments
 */
@Path("/api/v1/payments")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class PaymentResource {

    @Inject PaymentService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "anonymous";
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreatePaymentRequest req) {
        Party party = req.direction().equalsIgnoreCase("INBOUND")
                ? Party.customer(req.partyId(), req.partyName(), null)
                : Party.supplier(req.partyId(), req.partyName(), null);
        Money amount = Money.of(req.amount(), req.currencyCode());

        PaymentRecord p = service.createPayment(
                tid(rawTid),
                PaymentDirection.valueOf(req.direction().toUpperCase()),
                party, amount,
                PaymentMethod.valueOf(req.method().toUpperCase()),
                req.invoiceId(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(p)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/process")
    public Response process(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ProcessRequest req) {
        PaymentRecord p = service.processPayment(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.referenceNumber(),
                req.gatewayTransactionId(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(p)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/reconcile")
    public Response reconcile(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        PaymentRecord p = service.reconcile(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(p)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/fail")
    public Response fail(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            FailRequest req) {
        PaymentRecord p = service.failPayment(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.reason(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(p)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/cancel")
    public Response cancel(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        PaymentRecord p = service.cancelPayment(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(p)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(p -> Response.ok(toResp(p)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/invoice/{invoiceId}")
    public Response byInvoice(
            @PathParam("invoiceId") String invoiceId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<PaymentResponse> items = service.findByInvoice(tid(rawTid), invoiceId)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/unreconciled")
    public Response unreconciled(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<PaymentResponse> items = service.findUnreconciled(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    private PaymentResponse toResp(PaymentRecord p) {
        return new PaymentResponse(
                p.getId(), p.getTenantId(), p.getPaymentNumber(),
                p.getDirection().name(), p.getParty().getName(),
                p.getAmount().getAmount(), p.getAmount().getCurrencyCode(),
                p.getMethod().name(), p.getStatus().name(),
                p.getInvoiceId(), p.getReferenceNumber(),
                p.getGatewayTransactionId(),
                p.getProcessedAt() != null ? p.getProcessedAt().toString() : null,
                p.getReconciledAt() != null ? p.getReconciledAt().toString() : null);
    }

    public record CreatePaymentRequest(
            @NotBlank String direction,
            @NotBlank String partyId, @NotBlank String partyName,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal amount,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            @NotBlank String method,
            String invoiceId) {}

    public record ProcessRequest(
            String referenceNumber,
            String gatewayTransactionId) {}

    public record FailRequest(String reason) {}

    public record PaymentResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String paymentNumber,
            String direction, String partyName,
            BigDecimal amount, String currencyCode,
            String method, String status,
            String invoiceId, String referenceNumber,
            String gatewayTransactionId,
            String processedAt, String reconciledAt) {}
}

package com.saas.payment.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Payment record aggregate.
 *
 * Represents a single payment transaction — either inbound (AR) or outbound (AP).
 *
 * A payment may:
 *  - Fully clear one invoice
 *  - Partially clear one invoice (split payment)
 *  - Clear multiple invoices (bulk payment run)
 *
 * Reconciliation: once a payment is matched to a bank statement line,
 * it moves to RECONCILED status.
 */
public class PaymentRecord {

    private String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private       String paymentNumber;
    private       PaymentDirection direction;    // INBOUND (receive) or OUTBOUND (send)
    private       Party  party;                  // customer (in) or supplier (out)
    private       Money  amount;
    private       PaymentMethod method;
    private       PaymentStatus status;
    private       String invoiceId;              // primary invoice being paid
    private       String referenceNumber;        // bank ref, cheque number, etc.
    private       String gatewayTransactionId;   // payment gateway ID
    private       String bankAccountId;          // which bank account used
    private       Instant processedAt;
    private       Instant reconciledAt;
    private       String notes;
    private       AuditMeta audit;

    public static PaymentRecord create(String tenantId, PaymentDirection direction,
                                        Party party, Money amount,
                                        PaymentMethod method, String invoiceId,
                                        String actor) {
        var p = new PaymentRecord();
        p.id        = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        p.direction = direction;
        p.party     = party;
        p.amount    = amount;
        p.method    = method;
        p.invoiceId = invoiceId;
        p.status    = PaymentStatus.PENDING;
        p.audit     = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return p;
    }

    private PaymentRecord() {}

    // ── Commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void process(String paymentNumber, String referenceNumber,
                         String gatewayTxId, String actor) {
        if (status != PaymentStatus.PENDING)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Payment is not PENDING: " + status);
        this.paymentNumber       = paymentNumber;
        this.referenceNumber     = referenceNumber;
        this.gatewayTransactionId = gatewayTxId;
        this.status              = PaymentStatus.PROCESSED;
        this.processedAt         = Instant.now();
        this.audit               = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void reconcile(String actor) {
        if (status != PaymentStatus.PROCESSED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Can only reconcile PROCESSED payments");
        this.status        = PaymentStatus.RECONCILED;
        this.reconciledAt  = Instant.now();
        this.audit         = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void fail(String reason, String actor) {
        this.status = PaymentStatus.FAILED;
        this.notes  = reason;
        this.audit  = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void cancel(String actor) {
        if (status == PaymentStatus.RECONCILED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot cancel a reconciled payment");
        this.status = PaymentStatus.CANCELLED;
        this.audit  = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String          getId()                 { return id; }
    public String          getTenantId()           { return tenantId; }
    public String          getPaymentNumber()      { return paymentNumber; }
    public PaymentDirection getDirection()         { return direction; }
    public Party           getParty()              { return party; }
    public Money           getAmount()             { return amount; }
    public PaymentMethod   getMethod()             { return method; }
    public PaymentStatus   getStatus()             { return status; }
    public String          getInvoiceId()          { return invoiceId; }
    public String          getReferenceNumber()    { return referenceNumber; }
    public String          getGatewayTransactionId(){ return gatewayTransactionId; }
    public String          getBankAccountId()      { return bankAccountId; }
    public Instant         getProcessedAt()        { return processedAt; }
    public Instant         getReconciledAt()       { return reconciledAt; }
    public String          getNotes()              { return notes; }
    public AuditMeta       getAudit()              { return audit; }

    // ── Enums ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public enum PaymentDirection { INBOUND, OUTBOUND }
    public enum PaymentStatus    { PENDING, PROCESSED, RECONCILED, FAILED, CANCELLED }
    public enum PaymentMethod    {
        CASH, BANK_TRANSFER, CREDIT_CARD, DEBIT_CARD,
        VIRTUAL_ACCOUNT, QRIS, EWALLET, CHEQUE, COD
    }
}

package com.saas.payment.events;

import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEvent;
import java.time.Instant;

public sealed interface PaymentEvent extends DomainEvent
        permits PaymentEvent.PaymentCreated,
                PaymentEvent.PaymentProcessed,
                PaymentEvent.PaymentReconciled,
                PaymentEvent.PaymentFailed,
                PaymentEvent.PaymentCancelled {

    record PaymentCreated(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String invoiceId,
            String direction, String amount, String method,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements PaymentEvent {}

    record PaymentProcessed(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String paymentNumber,
            String invoiceId, String amount, String referenceNumber,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements PaymentEvent {}

    record PaymentReconciled(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements PaymentEvent {}

    record PaymentFailed(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String reason,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements PaymentEvent {}

    record PaymentCancelled(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements PaymentEvent {}
}

package com.saas.payment.infrastructure.entity;

import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheEntityBase;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;

@Entity @Table(name = "payments")
public class PaymentEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {
    @Id @Column(name="id",length=36,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String id;
    @Column(name="tenant_id",length=64,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String tenantId;
    @Column(name="payment_number",length=64) public String paymentNumber;
    @Column(name="direction",length=12,nullable=false) public String direction;
    @Column(name="party_id",length=36,nullable=false) public String partyId;
    @Column(name="party_name",length=512,nullable=false) public String partyName;
    @Column(name="amount_amt",precision=19,scale=4,nullable=false) public BigDecimal amountAmt;
    @Column(name="amount_ccy",length=3,nullable=false) public String amountCcy;
    @Column(name="method",length=32,nullable=false) public String method;
    @Column(name="status",length=20,nullable=false) public String status;
    @Column(name="invoice_id",length=36) public String invoiceId;
    @Column(name="reference_number",length=128) public String referenceNumber;
    @Column(name="gateway_transaction_id",length=256) public String gatewayTransactionId;
    @Column(name="bank_account_id",length=36) public String bankAccountId;
    @Column(name="processed_at") public Instant processedAt;
    @Column(name="reconciled_at") public Instant reconciledAt;
    @Column(name="notes",columnDefinition="TEXT") public String notes;
    @Version @Column(name="version",nullable=false) public long version;
    @Column(name="created_at",nullable=false,updatable=false) public Instant createdAt;
    @Column(name="updated_at",nullable=false) public Instant updatedAt;
    @Column(name="created_by",length=128) public String createdBy;
    @Column(name="updated_by",length=128) public String updatedBy;
}

Size: 5.1 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.payment.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord;
import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord.*;
import com.saas.payment.infrastructure.entity.PaymentEntity;
import com.saas.payment.spi.PaymentRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanachePaymentRepository
        implements PaymentRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<PaymentEntity, String> {

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public PaymentRecord save(PaymentRecord p) {
        PaymentEntity e = toEntity(p);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) {
            getEntityManager().merge(e);
        } else {
            persist(e);
        }
        return p;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<PaymentRecord> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<PaymentRecord> findByPaymentNumber(String number, String tenantId) {
        return find("paymentNumber = ?1 AND tenantId = ?2", number, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public List<PaymentRecord> findByInvoice(String tenantId, String invoiceId) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND invoiceId = ?2", tenantId, invoiceId)
                .list().stream().map(this::toDomain).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<PaymentRecord> findByStatus(String tenantId, PaymentStatus status,
                                              int page, int size) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND status = ?2", tenantId, status.name())
                .page(page, size).list().stream().map(this::toDomain).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<PaymentRecord> findUnreconciled(String tenantId) {
        return find("tenantId = ?1 AND status = 'PROCESSED'", tenantId)
                .list().stream().map(this::toDomain).toList();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private PaymentRecord toDomain(PaymentEntity e) {
        Party party = e.direction.equals("INBOUND")
                ? Party.customer(e.partyId, e.partyName, null)
                : Party.supplier(e.partyId, e.partyName, null);
        Money amount = Money.of(e.amountAmt, e.amountCcy);

        PaymentRecord p = PaymentRecord.create(
                e.tenantId, PaymentDirection.valueOf(e.direction),
                party, amount, PaymentMethod.valueOf(e.method),
                e.invoiceId, e.createdBy != null ? e.createdBy : "system");

        // Advance to correct status
        if (e.status.equals("PROCESSED") || e.status.equals("RECONCILED")) {
            p.process(e.paymentNumber, e.referenceNumber, e.gatewayTransactionId,
                    e.updatedBy != null ? e.updatedBy : "system");
        }
        if (e.status.equals("RECONCILED")) {
            p.reconcile(e.updatedBy != null ? e.updatedBy : "system");
        }
        if (e.status.equals("FAILED")) {
            p.fail(e.notes, e.updatedBy != null ? e.updatedBy : "system");
        }
        if (e.status.equals("CANCELLED")) {
            p.cancel(e.updatedBy != null ? e.updatedBy : "system");
        }
        return p;
    }

    private PaymentEntity toEntity(PaymentRecord p) {
        PaymentEntity e             = new PaymentEntity();
        e.id                        = p.getId();
        e.tenantId                  = p.getTenantId();
        e.paymentNumber             = p.getPaymentNumber();
        e.direction                 = p.getDirection().name();
        e.partyId                   = p.getParty().getId();
        e.partyName                 = p.getParty().getName();
        e.amountAmt                 = p.getAmount().getAmount();
        e.amountCcy                 = p.getAmount().getCurrencyCode();
        e.method                    = p.getMethod().name();
        e.status                    = p.getStatus().name();
        e.invoiceId                 = p.getInvoiceId();
        e.referenceNumber           = p.getReferenceNumber();
        e.gatewayTransactionId      = p.getGatewayTransactionId();
        e.bankAccountId             = p.getBankAccountId();
        e.processedAt               = p.getProcessedAt();
        e.reconciledAt              = p.getReconciledAt();
        e.notes                     = p.getNotes();
        e.version                   = p.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt                 = p.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt                 = p.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy                 = p.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy                 = p.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        return e;
    }
}

package com.saas.payment.service;

import com.saas.accounting.service.AccountingService;
import com.saas.invoice.service.InvoiceService;
import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord;
import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord.*;
import com.saas.payment.events.PaymentEvent;
import com.saas.payment.spi.PaymentRepository;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * Payment application service.
 *
 * On processPayment():
 *   1. Payment marked as PROCESSED
 *   2. Invoice recordPayment() called (balance updated)
 *   3. Accounting journal entry posted (DR Bank / CR AR or DR AP / CR Bank)
 *   4. PaymentProcessed event published
 *
 * All three steps happen in one @Transactional — atomicity guaranteed.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class PaymentService {

    @Inject PaymentRepository    repository;
    @Inject InvoiceService       invoiceService;
    @Inject AccountingService    accountingService;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    private static final AtomicLong COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    // ── Create ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public PaymentRecord createPayment(String tenantId, PaymentDirection direction,
                                        Party party, Money amount,
                                        PaymentMethod method, String invoiceId,
                                        String actor) {
        PaymentRecord payment = PaymentRecord.create(
                tenantId, direction, party, amount, method, invoiceId, actor);
        repository.save(payment);

        publisher.publish(new PaymentEvent.PaymentCreated(
                payment.getId(), tenantId, invoiceId,
                direction.name(), amount.toString(), method.name(),
                actor, Instant.now()));
        return payment;
    }

    // ── Process ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public PaymentRecord processPayment(String id, String tenantId,
                                         String referenceNumber, String gatewayTxId,
                                         String actor) {
        PaymentRecord payment = load(id, tenantId);
        String paymentNumber  = generatePaymentNumber(tenantId, payment.getDirection());

        // Step 1: mark payment processed
        payment.process(paymentNumber, referenceNumber, gatewayTxId, actor);
        repository.save(payment);

        // Step 2: update invoice balance
        if (payment.getInvoiceId() != null) {
            invoiceService.recordPayment(
                    payment.getInvoiceId(), tenantId,
                    payment.getAmount(), actor);
        }

        // Step 3: post double-entry journal
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now();
        if (payment.getDirection() == PaymentDirection.INBOUND) {
            accountingService.postCustomerPayment(
                    tenantId, payment.getId(), today, payment.getAmount(), actor);
        } else {
            accountingService.postSupplierPayment(
                    tenantId, payment.getId(), today, payment.getAmount(), actor);
        }

        publisher.publish(new PaymentEvent.PaymentProcessed(
                id, tenantId, paymentNumber,
                payment.getInvoiceId(), payment.getAmount().toString(),
                referenceNumber, actor, Instant.now()));
        return payment;
    }

    // ── Reconcile ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public PaymentRecord reconcile(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        PaymentRecord payment = load(id, tenantId);
        payment.reconcile(actor);
        repository.save(payment);

        publisher.publish(new PaymentEvent.PaymentReconciled(
                id, tenantId, actor, Instant.now()));
        return payment;
    }

    // ── Fail / Cancel ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public PaymentRecord failPayment(String id, String tenantId,
                                      String reason, String actor) {
        PaymentRecord payment = load(id, tenantId);
        payment.fail(reason, actor);
        repository.save(payment);
        publisher.publish(new PaymentEvent.PaymentFailed(
                id, tenantId, reason, actor, Instant.now()));
        return payment;
    }

    @Transactional
    public PaymentRecord cancelPayment(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        PaymentRecord payment = load(id, tenantId);
        payment.cancel(actor);
        repository.save(payment);
        publisher.publish(new PaymentEvent.PaymentCancelled(
                id, tenantId, actor, Instant.now()));
        return payment;
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<PaymentRecord> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public List<PaymentRecord> findByInvoice(String tenantId, String invoiceId) {
        return repository.findByInvoice(tenantId, invoiceId);
    }

    public List<PaymentRecord> findUnreconciled(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findUnreconciled(tenantId);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private PaymentRecord load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new PaymentNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    private String generatePaymentNumber(String tenantId, PaymentDirection dir) {
        String prefix = dir == PaymentDirection.INBOUND ? "REC" : "PAY";
        String tenant = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        return prefix + "-" + tenant + "-" + String.format("%08d", COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
    }

    public static class PaymentNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public PaymentNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Payment not found: " + id + " / " + t);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.payroll.api.rest;

import com.saas.payroll.core.PayslipAggregate;
import com.saas.payroll.core.PayslipAggregate.PayItem;
import com.saas.payroll.service.PayrollService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Payroll REST API.
 *
 * POST /api/v1/payroll/generate              → generate payroll for a period
 * GET  /api/v1/payroll/{period}              → list payslips for period
 * GET  /api/v1/payroll/payslip/{id}          → get payslip
 * POST /api/v1/payroll/payslip/{id}/earning  → add manual earning
 * POST /api/v1/payroll/payslip/{id}/deduction→ add manual deduction
 * POST /api/v1/payroll/payslip/{id}/approve  → approve payslip
 * POST /api/v1/payroll/{period}/approve-all  → approve all drafts for period
 * POST /api/v1/payroll/payslip/{id}/pay      → mark paid + post journal
 * POST /api/v1/payroll/{period}/process-all  → process all approved for period
 */
@Path("/api/v1/payroll")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class PayrollResource {

    @Inject PayrollService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/generate")
    public Response generate(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid GenerateRequest req) {
        YearMonth period = YearMonth.parse(req.period());
        Money transport  = req.transportAllowance() != null
                ? Money.of(req.transportAllowance(), req.currencyCode()) : null;
        Money meal       = req.mealAllowance() != null
                ? Money.of(req.mealAllowance(), req.currencyCode()) : null;

        List<PayslipAggregate> generated = service.generatePayroll(
                tid(rawTid), period, transport, meal, actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(
                new GenerateResponse(generated.size(), req.period(),
                        generated.stream().map(this::toResp).toList())).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{period}")
    public Response listByPeriod(
            @PathParam("period") String period,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<PayslipResp> payslips = service.findByPeriod(tid(rawTid), YearMonth.parse(period))
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(payslips).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/payslip/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(ps -> Response.ok(toDetailResp(ps)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/payslip/{id}/earning")
    public Response addEarning(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid PayItemRequest req) {
        PayslipAggregate ps = service.addEarning(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.code(), req.description(),
                Money.of(req.amount(), req.currencyCode()), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(ps)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/payslip/{id}/deduction")
    public Response addDeduction(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid PayItemRequest req) {
        PayslipAggregate ps = service.addDeduction(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.code(), req.description(),
                Money.of(req.amount(), req.currencyCode()), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(ps)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/payslip/{id}/approve")
    public Response approve(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(toResp(service.approve(id, tid(rawTid), actor()))).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{period}/approve-all")
    public Response approveAll(
            @PathParam("period") String period,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<PayslipResp> approved = service.approveAll(
                tid(rawTid), YearMonth.parse(period), actor())
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(approved).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/payslip/{id}/pay")
    public Response pay(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            PayRequest req) {
        return Response.ok(toResp(
                service.processPayment(id, tid(rawTid), req.paymentReference(), actor()))).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{period}/process-all")
    public Response processAll(
            @PathParam("period") String period,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            PayRequest req) {
        List<PayslipResp> paid = service.processAllApproved(
                tid(rawTid), YearMonth.parse(period), req.paymentReference(), actor())
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(paid).build();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private PayslipResp toResp(PayslipAggregate ps) {
        return new PayslipResp(
                ps.getId(), ps.getEmployeeNumber(), ps.getEmployeeName(),
                ps.getPayPeriod().toString(), ps.getStatus().name(),
                ps.getGrossEarnings().getAmount(), ps.getTotalDeductions().getAmount(),
                ps.getNetPay().getAmount(), ps.getCurrencyCode());
    }

    private PayslipDetailResp toDetailResp(PayslipAggregate ps) {
        return new PayslipDetailResp(
                ps.getId(), ps.getEmployeeId(), ps.getEmployeeNumber(),
                ps.getEmployeeName(), ps.getPayPeriod().toString(), ps.getStatus().name(),
                ps.getCurrencyCode(),
                ps.getEarnings().stream()
                        .map(i -> new ItemResp(i.code(), i.description(), i.amount().getAmount()))
                        .toList(),
                ps.getDeductions().stream()
                        .map(i -> new ItemResp(i.code(), i.description(), i.amount().getAmount()))
                        .toList(),
                ps.getGrossEarnings().getAmount(),
                ps.getTotalDeductions().getAmount(),
                ps.getNetPay().getAmount(),
                ps.getPaymentReference());
    }

    // ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record GenerateRequest(
            @NotBlank String period,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            BigDecimal transportAllowance,
            BigDecimal mealAllowance) {}

    public record PayItemRequest(
            @NotBlank String code, @NotBlank String description,
            @NotNull @Positive BigDecimal amount, @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record PayRequest(String paymentReference) {}

    public record GenerateResponse(int count, String period, List<PayslipResp> payslips) {}

    public record PayslipResp(String id, String empNumber, String empName,
            String period, String status,
            BigDecimal gross, BigDecimal deductions, BigDecimal netPay, String ccy) {}

    public record PayslipDetailResp(String id, String employeeId, String empNumber,
            String empName, String period, String status, String ccy,
            List<ItemResp> earnings, List<ItemResp> deductions,
            BigDecimal gross, BigDecimal totalDeductions, BigDecimal netPay,
            String paymentReference) {}

    public record ItemResp(String code, String description, BigDecimal amount) {}
}

package com.saas.payroll.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Payslip aggregate — represents one employee's pay for one period.
 *
 * Lifecycle: DRAFT → APPROVED → PAID
 *
 * Earnings and deductions are line items.
 * Net pay = gross pay - total deductions.
 *
 * Indonesian payroll components:
 *  Earnings:   Basic Salary, Allowances (Transport, Meal, Housing)
 *  Deductions: BPJS Ketenagakerjaan (2%), BPJS Kesehatan (1%),
 *              PPh 21 (progressive), Loan repayment, Absence deduction
 */
public class PayslipAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String employeeId;
    private String employeeNumber;
    private String employeeName;
    private YearMonth payPeriod;
    private PayslipStatus status;
    private String currencyCode;
    private List<PayItem> earnings;
    private List<PayItem> deductions;
    private String paymentReference;
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static PayslipAggregate create(String tenantId, String employeeId,
                                           String employeeNumber, String employeeName,
                                           YearMonth payPeriod, String currencyCode,
                                           String actor) {
        PayslipAggregate p = new PayslipAggregate();
        p.id             = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        p.tenantId       = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        p.employeeId     = Objects.requireNonNull(employeeId);
        p.employeeNumber = Objects.requireNonNull(employeeNumber);
        p.employeeName   = Objects.requireNonNull(employeeName);
        p.payPeriod      = Objects.requireNonNull(payPeriod);
        p.status         = PayslipStatus.DRAFT;
        p.currencyCode   = Objects.requireNonNull(currencyCode);
        p.earnings       = new ArrayList<>();
        p.deductions     = new ArrayList<>();
        p.audit          = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return p;
    }

    private PayslipAggregate() {}

    // ── Pay item commands ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void addEarning(String code, String description, Money amount, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        earnings.add(new PayItem(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), code, description, amount));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void addDeduction(String code, String description, Money amount, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        deductions.add(new PayItem(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), code, description, amount));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Standard Indonesia payroll builder ────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Compute standard Indonesian payroll components automatically.
     * Call this after setting basic salary — it adds all standard items.
     */
    public void computeStandardIndonesiaPayroll(Money basicSalary,
                                                  Money transportAllowance,
                                                  Money mealAllowance,
                                                  int absenceDays,
                                                  String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        earnings.clear();
        deductions.clear();

        // Earnings
        addEarning("BASIC",      "Gaji Pokok",         basicSalary,        actor);
        if (!transportAllowance.isZero())
            addEarning("TRANS",  "Tunjangan Transport", transportAllowance, actor);
        if (!mealAllowance.isZero())
            addEarning("MEAL",   "Tunjangan Makan",     mealAllowance,      actor);

        // Absence deduction (pro-rata)
        if (absenceDays > 0) {
            Money dailyRate = basicSalary.multiply(
                    BigDecimal.ONE.divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(22), 4,
                            java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
            Money absenceDeduction = dailyRate.multiply(absenceDays);
            addDeduction("ABSENT", "Potongan Absen (" + absenceDays + " hari)",
                    absenceDeduction, actor);
        }

        Money grossForBpjs = basicSalary.add(transportAllowance).add(mealAllowance);

        // BPJS Ketenagakerjaan - employee share 2%
        Money bpjsKt = grossForBpjs.percentage(new BigDecimal("2"));
        addDeduction("BPJS-KT", "BPJS Ketenagakerjaan (2%)", bpjsKt, actor);

        // BPJS Kesehatan - employee share 1%
        Money bpjsKs = grossForBpjs.percentage(new BigDecimal("1"));
        addDeduction("BPJS-KS", "BPJS Kesehatan (1%)", bpjsKs, actor);

        // PPh 21 - simplified progressive (real impl uses annual projection)
        Money grossAnnual = getGrossEarnings().multiply(12);
        Money pph21 = computeSimplifiedPph21(grossAnnual, currencyCode);
        Money pph21Monthly = pph21.multiply(
                BigDecimal.ONE.divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(12), 4,
                        java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
        if (!pph21Monthly.isZero())
            addDeduction("PPH21", "PPh Pasal 21", pph21Monthly, actor);
    }

    /** Simplified PPh 21 progressive rate (TK/0 — no dependants). */
    private Money computeSimplifiedPph21(Money annualGross, String ccy) {
        BigDecimal gross = annualGross.getAmount();
        BigDecimal ptkp  = new BigDecimal("54000000"); // PTKP 2023: 54jt
        BigDecimal pkp   = gross.subtract(ptkp).max(BigDecimal.ZERO);

        BigDecimal tax = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        // 5% on first 60jt
        BigDecimal tier1 = pkp.min(new BigDecimal("60000000"));
        tax = tax.add(tier1.multiply(new BigDecimal("0.05")));
        // 15% on 60jt-250jt
        if (pkp.compareTo(new BigDecimal("60000000")) > 0) {
            BigDecimal tier2 = pkp.subtract(new BigDecimal("60000000"))
                    .min(new BigDecimal("190000000"));
            tax = tax.add(tier2.multiply(new BigDecimal("0.15")));
        }
        // 25% on 250jt-500jt
        if (pkp.compareTo(new BigDecimal("250000000")) > 0) {
            BigDecimal tier3 = pkp.subtract(new BigDecimal("250000000"))
                    .min(new BigDecimal("250000000"));
            tax = tax.add(tier3.multiply(new BigDecimal("0.25")));
        }
        // 30% above 500jt
        if (pkp.compareTo(new BigDecimal("500000000")) > 0) {
            BigDecimal tier4 = pkp.subtract(new BigDecimal("500000000"));
            tax = tax.add(tier4.multiply(new BigDecimal("0.30")));
        }
        return Money.of(tax, ccy);
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void approve(String actor) {
        if (status != PayslipStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only DRAFT payslips can be approved");
        if (earnings.isEmpty())
            throw new IllegalStateException("Payslip has no earnings");
        this.status = PayslipStatus.APPROVED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void markPaid(String paymentReference, String actor) {
        if (status != PayslipStatus.APPROVED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only APPROVED payslips can be marked paid");
        this.status           = PayslipStatus.PAID;
        this.paymentReference = paymentReference;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void void_(String actor) {
        if (status == PayslipStatus.PAID)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot void a paid payslip");
        this.status = PayslipStatus.VOID;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Computed totals ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Money getGrossEarnings() {
        return earnings.stream().map(PayItem::amount)
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }

    public Money getTotalDeductions() {
        return deductions.stream().map(PayItem::amount)
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }

    public Money getNetPay() {
        Money gross = getGrossEarnings();
        Money deductions = getTotalDeductions();
        return deductions.isGreaterThan(gross)
                ? Money.zero(currencyCode)
                : gross.subtract(deductions);
    }

    private void assertDraft() {
        if (status != PayslipStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Payslip is not in DRAFT");
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()             { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()       { return tenantId; }
    public String getEmployeeId()     { return employeeId; }
    public String getEmployeeNumber() { return employeeNumber; }
    public String getEmployeeName()   { return employeeName; }
    public YearMonth getPayPeriod()   { return payPeriod; }
    public PayslipStatus getStatus()  { return status; }
    public String getCurrencyCode()   { return currencyCode; }
    public List<PayItem> getEarnings()   { return Collections.unmodifiableList(earnings); }
    public List<PayItem> getDeductions() { return Collections.unmodifiableList(deductions); }
    public String getPaymentReference()  { return paymentReference; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()          { return audit; }

    public enum PayslipStatus { DRAFT, APPROVED, PAID, VOID }

    public record PayItem(String id, String code, String description, Money amount) {}
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/payroll/service/PayrollService.java
Size: 8.4 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.payroll.service;

import com.saas.accounting.service.AccountingService;
import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate;
import com.saas.employee.spi.EmployeeRepository;
import com.saas.payroll.core.PayslipAggregate;
import com.saas.payroll.core.PayslipAggregate.PayslipStatus;
import com.saas.payroll.spi.PayslipRepository;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Payroll application service.
 *
 * Core workflow:
 *   1. generatePayroll()  — creates DRAFT payslips for all active employees
 *   2. reviewPayslip()    — HR reviews individual payslips (can adjust manually)
 *   3. approveAll()       — CFO approves entire payroll run
 *   4. processPayment()   — marks payslips PAID + posts accounting journal entries
 *
 * Accounting integration:
 *   On processPayment() for each payslip:
 *     DR Salary Expense     (5010)
 *     DR BPJS Expense       (5010 sub)
 *     CR BPJS Payable       (2200)
 *     CR Tax Payable PPh21  (2110)
 *     CR Bank               (1020)  ← net pay
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class PayrollService {

    @Inject PayslipRepository   payslipRepo;
    @Inject EmployeeRepository  employeeRepo;
    @Inject AccountingService   accountingService;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    // ── Generate payroll run ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Generate DRAFT payslips for every active employee in the tenant for a period.
     * Skips employees who already have a payslip for this period.
     * Returns the list of generated payslips.
     */
    @Transactional
    public List<PayslipAggregate> generatePayroll(String tenantId, YearMonth period,
                                                    Money defaultTransportAllowance,
                                                    Money defaultMealAllowance,
                                                    String actor) {
        List<EmployeeAggregate> activeEmployees =
                employeeRepo.findByStatus(tenantId,
                        EmployeeAggregate.EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE, 0, Integer.MAX_VALUE);

        List<PayslipAggregate> generated = new ArrayList<>();
        for (EmployeeAggregate emp : activeEmployees) {
            // Idempotent — skip if already generated
            if (payslipRepo.findByEmployeeAndPeriod(
                    tenantId, emp.getId(), period).isPresent()) continue;

            PayslipAggregate payslip = PayslipAggregate.create(
                    tenantId, emp.getId(),
                    emp.getEmployeeNumber(), emp.getFullName(),
                    period, emp.getCurrencyCode(), actor);

            // Calculate absences (0 for now — real impl reads attendance)
            int absenceDays = 0;

            payslip.computeStandardIndonesiaPayroll(
                    emp.getBasicSalary(),
                    defaultTransportAllowance != null
                            ? defaultTransportAllowance
                            : Money.zero(emp.getCurrencyCode()),
                    defaultMealAllowance != null
                            ? defaultMealAllowance
                            : Money.zero(emp.getCurrencyCode()),
                    absenceDays, actor);

            generated.add(payslipRepo.save(payslip));
        }
        return generated;
    }

    // ── Manual adjustment ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public PayslipAggregate addEarning(String id, String tenantId,
                                        String code, String description,
                                        Money amount, String actor) {
        PayslipAggregate ps = load(id, tenantId);
        ps.addEarning(code, description, amount, actor);
        return payslipRepo.save(ps);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PayslipAggregate addDeduction(String id, String tenantId,
                                          String code, String description,
                                          Money amount, String actor) {
        PayslipAggregate ps = load(id, tenantId);
        ps.addDeduction(code, description, amount, actor);
        return payslipRepo.save(ps);
    }

    // ── Approval ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public PayslipAggregate approve(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        PayslipAggregate ps = load(id, tenantId);
        ps.approve(actor);
        return payslipRepo.save(ps);
    }

    @Transactional
    public List<PayslipAggregate> approveAll(String tenantId, YearMonth period, String actor) {
        List<PayslipAggregate> drafts = payslipRepo.findByPeriod(tenantId, period)
                .stream()
                .filter(ps -> ps.getStatus() == PayslipStatus.DRAFT)
                .toList();

        List<PayslipAggregate> approved = new ArrayList<>();
        for (PayslipAggregate ps : drafts) {
            ps.approve(actor);
            approved.add(payslipRepo.save(ps));
        }
        return approved;
    }

    // ── Payment processing ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Mark a payslip as paid and post the double-entry accounting journal.
     */
    @Transactional
    public PayslipAggregate processPayment(String id, String tenantId,
                                            String paymentRef, String actor) {
        PayslipAggregate ps = load(id, tenantId);
        ps.markPaid(paymentRef, actor);
        payslipRepo.save(ps);

        // Post payroll journal: DR Salary Expense / CR Bank (net pay)
        LocalDate payDate = LocalDate.now();
        accountingService.postCustomerPayment(
                tenantId, "PAYROLL-" + ps.getId(),
                payDate, ps.getNetPay(), actor);

        return ps;
    }

    @Transactional
    public List<PayslipAggregate> processAllApproved(String tenantId,
                                                       YearMonth period,
                                                       String batchRef, String actor) {
        List<PayslipAggregate> approved = payslipRepo.findByPeriod(tenantId, period)
                .stream()
                .filter(ps -> ps.getStatus() == PayslipStatus.APPROVED)
                .toList();

        List<PayslipAggregate> paid = new ArrayList<>();
        for (PayslipAggregate ps : approved) {
            paid.add(processPayment(ps.getId(), tenantId,
                    batchRef + "-" + ps.getEmployeeNumber(), actor));
        }
        return paid;
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<PayslipAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return payslipRepo.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public Optional<PayslipAggregate> findByEmployeeAndPeriod(String tenantId,
                                                                String employeeId,
                                                                YearMonth period) {
        return payslipRepo.findByEmployeeAndPeriod(tenantId, employeeId, period);
    }

    public List<PayslipAggregate> findByPeriod(String tenantId, YearMonth period) {
        return payslipRepo.findByPeriod(tenantId, period);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private PayslipAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return payslipRepo.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new PayslipNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    public static class PayslipNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public PayslipNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Payslip not found: " + id + " / " + t);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.pricing.api.rest;

import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceList;
import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceList.*;
import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceRule;
import com.saas.pricing.service.PricingService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Pricing REST API.
 *
 * POST /api/v1/pricing/lists                         → create price list
 * GET  /api/v1/pricing/lists                         → list all
 * POST /api/v1/pricing/lists/{id}/rules/fixed        → add fixed price rule
 * POST /api/v1/pricing/lists/{id}/rules/percent      → add % discount rule
 * POST /api/v1/pricing/lists/{id}/rules/absolute     → add flat discount rule
 * POST /api/v1/pricing/lists/{id}/target             → set segment/channel target
 * POST /api/v1/pricing/resolve                       → resolve price for product
 */
@Path("/api/v1/pricing")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class PricingResource {

    @Inject PricingService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal(); return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @POST @Path("/lists")
    public Response create(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
                            @Valid CreateListRequest req) {
        LocalDate from = req.validFrom() != null ? LocalDate.parse(req.validFrom()) : null;
        LocalDate to   = req.validTo()   != null ? LocalDate.parse(req.validTo())   : null;
        PriceList pl = service.createPriceList(
                tid(rawTid), req.code(), req.name(), req.currencyCode(),
                PriceListType.valueOf(req.type()), from, to, actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(pl)).build();
    }

    @GET @Path("/lists")
    public Response listAll(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.findAll(tid(rawTid)).stream().map(this::toResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/lists/{id}/rules/fixed")
    public Response addFixed(@PathParam("id") String id,
                              @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
                              @Valid FixedRuleRequest req) {
        Money price = Money.of(req.price(), req.currencyCode());
        PriceList pl = service.addFixedPriceRule(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.productId(), req.sku(), price, req.priority(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(pl)).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/lists/{id}/rules/percent")
    public Response addPercent(@PathParam("id") String id,
                                @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
                                @Valid PercentRuleRequest req) {
        PriceList pl = service.addPercentDiscountRule(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.productId(), req.categoryId(),
                req.discountPercent(), req.minQuantity(), req.priority(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(pl)).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/lists/{id}/rules/absolute")
    public Response addAbsolute(@PathParam("id") String id,
                                 @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
                                 @Valid AbsoluteRuleRequest req) {
        Money discount = Money.of(req.discountAmount(), req.currencyCode());
        PriceList pl = service.addAbsoluteDiscountRule(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.productId(), discount, req.minQuantity(), req.priority(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(pl)).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/lists/{id}/target")
    public Response setTarget(@PathParam("id") String id,
                               @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
                               TargetRequest req) {
        PriceList pl = service.setTarget(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.segment(), req.channel(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(pl)).build();
    }

    @POST @Path("/resolve")
    public Response resolve(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
                             @Valid ResolveRequest req) {
        Money base = Money.of(req.basePrice(), req.currencyCode());
        LocalDate date = req.date() != null ? LocalDate.parse(req.date()) : LocalDate.now();

        var result = service.resolvePrice(tid(rawTid), req.productId(), req.categoryId(),
                base, req.quantity(), req.segment(), req.channel(), date);

        if (result.isEmpty()) {
            return Response.ok(new ResolveResponse("BASE_PRICE", null, base.getAmount(),
                    Money.zero(req.currencyCode()).getAmount(), req.currencyCode())).build();
        }

        var r = result.get();
        return Response.ok(new ResolveResponse(
                r.priceListCode(), r.ruleApplied().type().name(),
                r.finalPrice().getAmount(), r.discountAmount().getAmount(),
                req.currencyCode())).build();
    }

    private ListResp toResp(PriceList pl) {
        return new ListResp(pl.getId(), pl.getCode(), pl.getName(),
                pl.getType().name(), pl.getCurrencyCode(), pl.isActive(),
                pl.getTargetSegment(), pl.getTargetChannel(),
                pl.getValidFrom() != null ? pl.getValidFrom().toString() : null,
                pl.getValidTo()   != null ? pl.getValidTo().toString()   : null,
                pl.getRules().size());
    }

    public record CreateListRequest(@NotBlank String code, @NotBlank String name,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode, @NotBlank String type,
            String validFrom, String validTo) {}
    public record FixedRuleRequest(@NotBlank String productId, String sku,
            @NotNull BigDecimal price, @NotBlank String currencyCode, int priority) {}
    public record PercentRuleRequest(String productId, String categoryId,
            @NotNull @DecimalMin("0") @DecimalMax("100") BigDecimal discountPercent,
            int minQuantity, int priority) {}
    public record AbsoluteRuleRequest(@NotBlank String productId,
            @NotNull BigDecimal discountAmount, @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            int minQuantity, int priority) {}
    public record TargetRequest(String segment, String channel) {}
    public record ResolveRequest(@NotBlank String productId, String categoryId,
            @NotNull BigDecimal basePrice, @NotBlank String currencyCode,
            int quantity, String segment, String channel, String date) {}

    public record ListResp(String id, String code, String name, String type,
            String currencyCode, boolean isActive, String segment, String channel,
            String validFrom, String validTo, int ruleCount) {}
    public record ResolveResponse(String priceListCode, String ruleType,
            BigDecimal finalPrice, BigDecimal discountAmount, String currencyCode) {}
}
