package com.saas.pricing.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Price List aggregate.
 *
 * A price list defines product-specific prices and discount rules
 * for a specific customer segment, channel, or date range.
 *
 * Priority-based: a product's final price = first matching rule
 * evaluated in priority order.
 *
 * Examples:
 *  - "Retail 2024"     → base prices for all retail customers
 *  - "VIP Q4"          → 15% off all products for VIP customers in Q4
 *  - "Bulk Electronics"→ tiered pricing for quantity ≥ 10 on electronics category
 */
public class PriceList {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String name;
    private String code;
    private String currencyCode;
    private PriceListType type;
    private String targetSegment;       // "retail", "vip", "wholesale", null=all
    private String targetChannel;       // "web", "pos", null=all
    private LocalDate validFrom;
    private LocalDate validTo;
    private boolean isActive;
    private List<PriceRule> rules;
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static PriceList create(String tenantId, String code, String name,
                                    String currencyCode, PriceListType type,
                                    LocalDate validFrom, LocalDate validTo,
                                    String actor) {
        PriceList pl = new PriceList();
        pl.id            = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        pl.tenantId      = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        pl.code          = Objects.requireNonNull(code);
        pl.name          = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        pl.currencyCode  = Objects.requireNonNull(currencyCode);
        pl.type          = Objects.requireNonNull(type);
        pl.validFrom     = validFrom;
        pl.validTo       = validTo;
        pl.isActive      = true;
        pl.rules         = new ArrayList<>();
        pl.audit         = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return pl;
    }

    private PriceList() {}

    // ── Rule management ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public PriceRule addFixedPriceRule(String productId, String sku,
                                        Money fixedPrice, int priority, String actor) {
        PriceRule rule = PriceRule.fixed(UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                productId, sku, fixedPrice, priority);
        rules.add(rule);
        rules.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(PriceRule::priority));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return rule;
    }

    public PriceRule addPercentDiscountRule(String productId, String categoryId,
                                             BigDecimal discountPercent,
                                             int minQty, int priority, String actor) {
        PriceRule rule = PriceRule.percentDiscount(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), productId, categoryId,
                discountPercent, minQty, priority);
        rules.add(rule);
        rules.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(PriceRule::priority));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return rule;
    }

    public PriceRule addAbsoluteDiscountRule(String productId, Money discount,
                                              int minQty, int priority, String actor) {
        PriceRule rule = PriceRule.absoluteDiscount(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), productId, discount, minQty, priority);
        rules.add(rule);
        rules.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(PriceRule::priority));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return rule;
    }

    // ── Price resolution ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Find the best applicable rule for a product+quantity on a given date.
     * Returns empty if this price list doesn't cover the product.
     */
    public Optional<PriceResolutionResult> resolve(String productId, String categoryId,
                                                    Money basePrice, int qty, LocalDate date) {
        if (!isEffective(date)) return Optional.empty();

        return rules.stream()
                .filter(r -> r.appliesTo(productId, categoryId, qty))
                .findFirst()
                .map(r -> {
                    Money finalPrice = r.apply(basePrice);
                    Money discount   = basePrice.subtract(finalPrice.isGreaterThan(basePrice)
                            ? basePrice : finalPrice);
                    return new PriceResolutionResult(
                            code, r, basePrice, finalPrice,
                            basePrice.isGreaterThan(finalPrice) ? discount : Money.zero(currencyCode),
                            qty, date);
                });
    }

    public boolean isEffective(LocalDate date) {
        if (!isActive) return false;
        if (validFrom != null && date.isBefore(validFrom)) return false;
        if (validTo   != null && date.isAfter(validTo))    return false;
        return true;
    }

    public String    getId()            { return id; }
    public String    getTenantId()      { return tenantId; }
    public String    getCode()          { return code; }
    public String    getName()          { return name; }
    public String    getCurrencyCode()  { return currencyCode; }
    public PriceListType getType()      { return type; }
    public String    getTargetSegment() { return targetSegment; }
    public String    getTargetChannel() { return targetChannel; }
    public LocalDate getValidFrom()     { return validFrom; }
    public LocalDate getValidTo()       { return validTo; }
    public boolean   isActive()         { return isActive; }
    public List<PriceRule> getRules()   { return Collections.unmodifiableList(rules); }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()         { return audit; }

    public void setTargetSegment(String s, String actor) { this.targetSegment = s; audit = audit.touch(actor); }
    public void setTargetChannel(String c, String actor) { this.targetChannel = c; audit = audit.touch(actor); }
    public void deactivate(String actor) { this.isActive = false; audit = audit.touch(actor); }

    public enum PriceListType { STANDARD, PROMOTIONAL, CLEARANCE, WHOLESALE, VIP }

    public record PriceResolutionResult(
            String priceListCode, PriceRule ruleApplied,
            Money basePrice, Money finalPrice,
            Money discountAmount, int qty, LocalDate resolvedOn) {}
}

package com.saas.pricing.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

/**
 * An individual pricing rule within a PriceList.
 *
 * Rules are evaluated in priority order (lower number = higher priority).
 * The first matching rule wins.
 *
 * Rule types:
 *  FIXED_PRICE      : Override the product price entirely
 *  PERCENT_DISCOUNT : Apply % off the base price
 *  ABSOLUTE_DISCOUNT: Subtract a fixed amount from the base price
 */
public record PriceRule(
        String id,
        RuleType type,
        String productId,        // null = applies to all products
        String categoryId,       // null = applies to all categories
        Money  fixedPrice,       // for FIXED_PRICE type
        BigDecimal discountPct,  // for PERCENT_DISCOUNT type
        Money  discountAmt,      // for ABSOLUTE_DISCOUNT type
        int    minQuantity,      // minimum qty for rule to apply (0 = no minimum)
        int    priority          // lower = evaluated first
) {

    // ── Factory methods ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static PriceRule fixed(String id, String productId, String sku,
                                   Money price, int priority) {
        return new PriceRule(id, RuleType.FIXED_PRICE, productId, null,
                price, null, null, 0, priority);
    }

    public static PriceRule percentDiscount(String id, String productId,
                                             String categoryId, BigDecimal pct,
                                             int minQty, int priority) {
        return new PriceRule(id, RuleType.PERCENT_DISCOUNT, productId, categoryId,
                null, pct, null, minQty, priority);
    }

    public static PriceRule absoluteDiscount(String id, String productId,
                                              Money discount, int minQty, int priority) {
        return new PriceRule(id, RuleType.ABSOLUTE_DISCOUNT, productId, null,
                null, null, discount, minQty, priority);
    }

    // ── Applicability ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public boolean appliesTo(String prodId, String catId, int qty) {
        if (qty < minQuantity) return false;
        boolean productMatch  = productId == null  || productId.equals(prodId);
        boolean categoryMatch = categoryId == null || categoryId.equals(catId);
        return productMatch && categoryMatch;
    }

    // ── Price computation ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Money apply(Money basePrice) {
        return switch (type) {
            case FIXED_PRICE      -> fixedPrice;
            case PERCENT_DISCOUNT -> {
                Money discount = basePrice.percentage(discountPct);
                yield basePrice.subtract(discount);
            }
            case ABSOLUTE_DISCOUNT -> {
                if (discountAmt.isGreaterThan(basePrice)) yield Money.zero(basePrice.getCurrencyCode());
                yield basePrice.subtract(discountAmt);
            }
        };
    }

    public enum RuleType { FIXED_PRICE, PERCENT_DISCOUNT, ABSOLUTE_DISCOUNT }
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/pricing/service/PricingService.java

package com.saas.pricing.service;

import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceList;
import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceList.PriceListType;
import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceList.PriceResolutionResult;
import com.saas.pricing.spi.PriceListRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Pricing application service.
 *
 * Price resolution algorithm:
 *  1. Load all active price lists for the tenant valid on today
 *  2. Filter by segment + channel match
 *  3. Sort by type priority: PROMOTIONAL > VIP > WHOLESALE > STANDARD > CLEARANCE
 *  4. Return the first rule match across price lists
 *  5. Fall back to base price if no rule matches
 *
 * Integrates with ProductService/OrderService — replaces hardcoded pricing.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class PricingService {

    @Inject PriceListRepository repository;

    // Type priority (lower = evaluated first)
    private static final Map<PriceListType, Integer> TYPE_PRIORITY = Map.of(
            PriceListType.PROMOTIONAL, 1,
            PriceListType.VIP,         2,
            PriceListType.WHOLESALE,   3,
            PriceListType.STANDARD,    4,
            PriceListType.CLEARANCE,   5
    );

    // ── CRUD ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public PriceList createPriceList(String tenantId, String code, String name,
                                      String currencyCode, PriceListType type,
                                      LocalDate validFrom, LocalDate validTo,
                                      String actor) {
        if (repository.findByCode(code, tenantId).isPresent())
            throw new DuplicatePriceListException(code);
        return repository.save(PriceList.create(
                tenantId, code, name, currencyCode, type, validFrom, validTo, actor));
    }

    @Transactional
    public PriceList addFixedPriceRule(String listId, String tenantId,
                                        String productId, String sku,
                                        Money price, int priority, String actor) {
        PriceList pl = load(listId, tenantId);
        pl.addFixedPriceRule(productId, sku, price, priority, actor);
        return repository.save(pl);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PriceList addPercentDiscountRule(String listId, String tenantId,
                                             String productId, String categoryId,
                                             BigDecimal discountPct, int minQty,
                                             int priority, String actor) {
        PriceList pl = load(listId, tenantId);
        pl.addPercentDiscountRule(productId, categoryId, discountPct, minQty, priority, actor);
        return repository.save(pl);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PriceList addAbsoluteDiscountRule(String listId, String tenantId,
                                              String productId, Money discount,
                                              int minQty, int priority, String actor) {
        PriceList pl = load(listId, tenantId);
        pl.addAbsoluteDiscountRule(productId, discount, minQty, priority, actor);
        return repository.save(pl);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PriceList setTarget(String listId, String tenantId,
                                String segment, String channel, String actor) {
        PriceList pl = load(listId, tenantId);
        if (segment != null) pl.setTargetSegment(segment, actor);
        if (channel != null) pl.setTargetChannel(channel, actor);
        return repository.save(pl);
    }

    // ── Price resolution ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Resolve the best price for a product given context.
     * Returns Optional.empty() if no price list rule applies (use base price).
     */
    public Optional<PriceResolutionResult> resolvePrice(String tenantId,
                                                          String productId,
                                                          String categoryId,
                                                          Money basePrice,
                                                          int qty,
                                                          String segment,
                                                          String channel,
                                                          LocalDate date) {
        List<PriceList> candidates = repository.findActive(tenantId, date)
                .stream()
                .filter(pl -> matchesContext(pl, segment, channel))
                .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(
                        pl -> TYPE_PRIORITY.getOrDefault(pl.getType(), 99)))
                .toList();

        for (PriceList pl : candidates) {
            Optional<PriceResolutionResult> result =
                    pl.resolve(productId, categoryId, basePrice, qty, date);
            if (result.isPresent()) return result;
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    private boolean matchesContext(PriceList pl, String segment, String channel) {
        boolean segmentOk = pl.getTargetSegment() == null
                || pl.getTargetSegment().equals(segment);
        boolean channelOk = pl.getTargetChannel() == null
                || pl.getTargetChannel().equals(channel);
        return segmentOk && channelOk;
    }

    public List<PriceList> findAll(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findAll(tenantId);
    }

    public Optional<PriceList> findByCode(String code, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findByCode(code, tenantId);
    }

    private PriceList load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new PriceListNotFoundException(id));
    }

    public static class PriceListNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public PriceListNotFoundException(String id) {
            super("Price list not found: " + id);
        }
    }

    public static class DuplicatePriceListException extends RuntimeException {
        public DuplicatePriceListException(String code) {
            super("Price list code already exists: " + code);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.promotion.api.rest;

import com.saas.promotion.core.PromotionAggregate;
import com.saas.promotion.core.PromotionAggregate.*;
import com.saas.promotion.service.PromotionService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Promotion REST API.
 *
 * POST /api/v1/promotions                   → create promotion
 * POST /api/v1/promotions/{id}/scope        → set product/segment scope
 * POST /api/v1/promotions/{id}/activate     → activate
 * POST /api/v1/promotions/{id}/pause        → pause
 * GET  /api/v1/promotions/active            → list active promotions
 * POST /api/v1/promotions/apply             → apply code to an order
 * GET  /api/v1/promotions/code/{code}       → get by coupon code
 */
@Path("/api/v1/promotions")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class PromotionResource {

    @Inject PromotionService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateRequest req) {

        Money discountAmt = req.discountAmount() != null && req.currencyCode() != null
                ? Money.of(req.discountAmount(), req.currencyCode()) : null;
        LocalDate from = req.validFrom() != null ? LocalDate.parse(req.validFrom()) : null;
        LocalDate to   = req.validTo()   != null ? LocalDate.parse(req.validTo())   : null;

        PromotionAggregate p = service.createPromotion(
                tid(rawTid), req.code(), req.name(),
                PromotionType.valueOf(req.type()),
                DiscountType.valueOf(req.discountType()),
                req.discountPercent(), discountAmt,
                from, to, req.maxTotalRedemptions(), req.maxPerCustomer(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(p)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/scope")
    public Response setScope(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            ScopeRequest req) {
        Money minOrder = req.minOrderAmount() != null
                ? Money.of(req.minOrderAmount(), req.currencyCode()) : null;
        Money cap = req.maxDiscountCap() != null
                ? Money.of(req.maxDiscountCap(), req.currencyCode()) : null;
        PromotionAggregate p = service.setScope(
                id, tid(rawTid),
                req.productIds(), req.categoryIds(), req.segments(),
                minOrder, cap, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(p)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/activate")
    public Response activate(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(toResp(service.activate(id, tid(rawTid), actor()))).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/pause")
    public Response pause(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(toResp(service.pause(id, tid(rawTid), actor()))).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/active")
    public Response listActive(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return Response.ok(service.findActive(tid(rawTid)).stream().map(this::toResp).toList()).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/code/{code}")
    public Response getByCode(
            @PathParam("code") String code,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findByCode(code, tid(rawTid))
                .map(p -> Response.ok(toResp(p)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/apply")
    public Response apply(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ApplyRequest req) {
        Money orderTotal = Money.of(req.orderTotal(), req.currencyCode());
        var result = service.applyCode(
                tid(rawTid), req.code(), req.orderId(),
                req.customerId(), req.customerSegment(),
                orderTotal, actor());
        return Response.ok(new ApplyResponse(
                result.code(), result.name(),
                result.discountApplied().getAmount(),
                result.discountApplied().getCurrencyCode(),
                result.redemptionId(), result.redeemedAt())).build();
    }

    private PromotionResponse toResp(PromotionAggregate p) {
        return new PromotionResponse(
                p.getId(), p.getCode(), p.getName(),
                p.getType().name(), p.getDiscountType().name(),
                p.getDiscountPercent(),
                p.getDiscountAmount() != null ? p.getDiscountAmount().getAmount() : null,
                p.getStatus().name(),
                p.getValidFrom() != null ? p.getValidFrom().toString() : null,
                p.getValidTo()   != null ? p.getValidTo().toString()   : null,
                p.getMaxTotalRedemptions(), p.getTotalRedemptions(),
                p.getMaxPerCustomer());
    }

    // ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record CreateRequest(
            @NotBlank String code, @NotBlank String name,
            @NotBlank String type, @NotBlank String discountType,
            BigDecimal discountPercent, BigDecimal discountAmount,
            String currencyCode, String validFrom, String validTo,
            int maxTotalRedemptions, int maxPerCustomer) {}

    public record ScopeRequest(
            List<String> productIds, List<String> categoryIds,
            List<String> segments, BigDecimal minOrderAmount,
            BigDecimal maxDiscountCap, String currencyCode) {}

    public record ApplyRequest(
            @NotBlank String code, @NotBlank String orderId,
            @NotBlank String customerId, String customerSegment,
            @NotNull BigDecimal orderTotal, @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record PromotionResponse(
            String id, String code, String name, String type, String discountType,
            BigDecimal discountPercent, BigDecimal discountAmount, String status,
            String validFrom, String validTo,
            int maxRedemptions, int totalRedemptions, int maxPerCustomer) {}

    public record ApplyResponse(
            String code, String name, BigDecimal discountApplied,
            String currencyCode, String redemptionId, String redeemedAt) {}
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/promotion/core/PromotionAggregate.java

package com.saas.promotion.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Promotion/Coupon aggregate.
 *
 * Covers:
 *  - Fixed-amount and percentage discount coupons
 *  - Campaign-based promotions (valid during a date range)
 *  - Usage limits (per-code global limit, per-customer limit)
 *  - Minimum order value requirement
 *  - Applicable product/category scope
 *  - Redemption tracking
 *
 * Integration: OrderService calls PromotionService.validateAndRedeem()
 * before pricing to apply coupon discounts.
 */
public class PromotionAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;
    private String code;                  // e.g. "LEBARAN2024", "WELCOME10"
    private String name;
    private String description;
    private PromotionType type;
    private DiscountType discountType;    // PERCENTAGE or FIXED_AMOUNT

    // Discount value
    private BigDecimal discountPercent;   // for PERCENTAGE type (e.g. 15.00 = 15%)
    private Money      discountAmount;    // for FIXED_AMOUNT type

    // Conditions
    private Money   minimumOrderValue;    // null = no minimum
    private LocalDate validFrom;
    private LocalDate validTo;
    private int     maxTotalRedemptions; // 0 = unlimited
    private int     maxPerCustomer;      // 0 = unlimited
    private Money   maxDiscountCap;      // maximum discount amount (for % types)

    // Scope
    private List<String> applicableProductIds;   // empty = all products
    private List<String> applicableCategoryIds;  // empty = all categories
    private List<String> applicableSegments;     // empty = all segments

    // State
    private PromotionStatus status;
    private int totalRedemptions;
    private List<RedemptionRecord> redemptions;

    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static PromotionAggregate create(String tenantId, String code, String name,
                                             PromotionType type, DiscountType discountType,
                                             BigDecimal discountPercent, Money discountAmount,
                                             LocalDate validFrom, LocalDate validTo,
                                             int maxTotalRedemptions, int maxPerCustomer,
                                             String actor) {
        PromotionAggregate p = new PromotionAggregate();
        p.id                  = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        p.tenantId            = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        p.code                = Objects.requireNonNull(code).toUpperCase();
        p.name                = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        p.type                = Objects.requireNonNull(type);
        p.discountType        = Objects.requireNonNull(discountType);
        p.discountPercent     = discountPercent;
        p.discountAmount      = discountAmount;
        p.validFrom           = validFrom;
        p.validTo             = validTo;
        p.maxTotalRedemptions = maxTotalRedemptions;
        p.maxPerCustomer      = maxPerCustomer;
        p.status              = PromotionStatus.DRAFT;
        p.totalRedemptions    = 0;
        p.applicableProductIds  = new ArrayList<>();
        p.applicableCategoryIds = new ArrayList<>();
        p.applicableSegments    = new ArrayList<>();
        p.redemptions           = new ArrayList<>();
        p.audit               = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return p;
    }

    public static PromotionAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String code, String name, String description,
            PromotionType type, DiscountType discountType, BigDecimal pct, Money amt,
            Money minOrder, LocalDate from, LocalDate to,
            int maxTotal, int maxPerCust, Money cap,
            List<String> productIds, List<String> catIds, List<String> segments,
            PromotionStatus status, int totalRedemptions,
            List<RedemptionRecord> redemptions, AuditMeta audit) {
        PromotionAggregate p = new PromotionAggregate();
        p.id = id; p.tenantId = tenantId; p.code = code; p.name = name;
        p.description = description; p.type = type; p.discountType = discountType;
        p.discountPercent = pct; p.discountAmount = amt; p.minimumOrderValue = minOrder;
        p.validFrom = from; p.validTo = to; p.maxTotalRedemptions = maxTotal;
        p.maxPerCustomer = maxPerCust; p.maxDiscountCap = cap;
        p.applicableProductIds = new ArrayList<>(productIds);
        p.applicableCategoryIds = new ArrayList<>(catIds);
        p.applicableSegments = new ArrayList<>(segments);
        p.status = status; p.totalRedemptions = totalRedemptions;
        p.redemptions = new ArrayList<>(redemptions); p.audit = audit;
        return p;
    }

    private PromotionAggregate() {}

    // ── Commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void activate(String actor) {
        if (status != PromotionStatus.DRAFT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only DRAFT promotions can be activated");
        this.status = PromotionStatus.ACTIVE;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void pause(String actor) {
        if (status != PromotionStatus.ACTIVE)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Only ACTIVE promotions can be paused");
        this.status = PromotionStatus.PAUSED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void expire(String actor) {
        this.status = PromotionStatus.EXPIRED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setScope(List<String> productIds, List<String> categoryIds,
                          List<String> segments, Money minOrder, Money cap, String actor) {
        assertEditable();
        this.applicableProductIds  = new ArrayList<>(productIds != null ? productIds : List.of());
        this.applicableCategoryIds = new ArrayList<>(categoryIds != null ? categoryIds : List.of());
        this.applicableSegments    = new ArrayList<>(segments != null ? segments : List.of());
        this.minimumOrderValue     = minOrder;
        this.maxDiscountCap        = cap;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Redemption ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Validate that the coupon can be applied to an order.
     * Returns the discount amount to apply.
     */
    public Money validateAndCompute(String customerId, String customerSegment,
                                     Money orderTotal, LocalDate today) {
        if (status != PromotionStatus.ACTIVE)
            throw new PromotionNotApplicableException("Promotion is not active: " + status);

        if (validFrom != null && today.isBefore(validFrom))
            throw new PromotionNotApplicableException("Promotion not yet valid (starts " + validFrom + ")");

        if (validTo != null && today.isAfter(validTo))
            throw new PromotionNotApplicableException("Promotion has expired (ended " + validTo + ")");

        if (maxTotalRedemptions > 0 && totalRedemptions >= maxTotalRedemptions)
            throw new PromotionNotApplicableException("Promotion has reached maximum redemptions");

        if (maxPerCustomer > 0) {
            long customerCount = redemptions.stream()
                    .filter(r -> r.customerId().equals(customerId)).count();
            if (customerCount >= maxPerCustomer)
                throw new PromotionNotApplicableException("Customer has reached maximum uses");
        }

        if (minimumOrderValue != null && !orderTotal.isGreaterOrEqual(minimumOrderValue))
            throw new PromotionNotApplicableException(
                    "Order total " + orderTotal + " below minimum " + minimumOrderValue);

        if (!applicableSegments.isEmpty() && !applicableSegments.contains(customerSegment))
            throw new PromotionNotApplicableException("Promotion not applicable to segment: " + customerSegment);

        // Compute discount
        String ccy = orderTotal.getCurrencyCode();
        Money discount = switch (discountType) {
            case PERCENTAGE -> {
                Money raw = orderTotal.percentage(discountPercent);
                yield (maxDiscountCap != null && raw.isGreaterThan(maxDiscountCap))
                        ? maxDiscountCap : raw;
            }
            case FIXED_AMOUNT -> {
                yield discountAmount.isGreaterThan(orderTotal) ? orderTotal : discountAmount;
            }
        };

        return discount;
    }

    public RedemptionRecord redeem(String orderId, String customerId, Money discount, String actor) {
        RedemptionRecord record = new RedemptionRecord(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), orderId, customerId,
                discount, Instant.now(), actor);
        redemptions.add(record);
        totalRedemptions++;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);

        // Auto-expire if max reached
        if (maxTotalRedemptions > 0 && totalRedemptions >= maxTotalRedemptions) {
            this.status = PromotionStatus.EXPIRED;
        }
        return record;
    }

    private void assertEditable() {
        if (status == PromotionStatus.EXPIRED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot modify expired promotion");
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String    getId()                   { return id; }
    public String    getTenantId()             { return tenantId; }
    public String    getCode()                 { return code; }
    public String    getName()                 { return name; }
    public String    getDescription()          { return description; }
    public PromotionType getType()             { return type; }
    public DiscountType getDiscountType()      { return discountType; }
    public BigDecimal getDiscountPercent()     { return discountPercent; }
    public Money     getDiscountAmount()       { return discountAmount; }
    public Money     getMinimumOrderValue()    { return minimumOrderValue; }
    public LocalDate getValidFrom()            { return validFrom; }
    public LocalDate getValidTo()              { return validTo; }
    public int       getMaxTotalRedemptions()  { return maxTotalRedemptions; }
    public int       getMaxPerCustomer()       { return maxPerCustomer; }
    public Money     getMaxDiscountCap()       { return maxDiscountCap; }
    public List<String> getApplicableProductIds()  { return Collections.unmodifiableList(applicableProductIds); }
    public List<String> getApplicableCategoryIds() { return Collections.unmodifiableList(applicableCategoryIds); }
    public List<String> getApplicableSegments()    { return Collections.unmodifiableList(applicableSegments); }
    public PromotionStatus getStatus()         { return status; }
    public int       getTotalRedemptions()     { return totalRedemptions; }
    public List<RedemptionRecord> getRedemptions() { return Collections.unmodifiableList(redemptions); }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()                { return audit; }

    public enum PromotionType  { COUPON, AUTOMATIC, LOYALTY_REWARD }
    public enum DiscountType   { PERCENTAGE, FIXED_AMOUNT }
    public enum PromotionStatus { DRAFT, ACTIVE, PAUSED, EXPIRED }

    public record RedemptionRecord(
            String id, String orderId, String customerId,
            Money discountApplied, Instant redeemedAt, String actor) {}

    public static class PromotionNotApplicableException extends RuntimeException {
        public PromotionNotApplicableException(String msg) { super(msg); }
    }
}

package com.saas.promotion.service;

import com.saas.promotion.core.PromotionAggregate;
import com.saas.promotion.core.PromotionAggregate.*;
import com.saas.promotion.spi.PromotionRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Promotion application service.
 *
 * Core workflow (called from OrderService before totalling):
 *   1. findByCode(tenantId, code)
 *   2. result.validateAndCompute(customerId, segment, orderTotal, today)
 *   3. result.redeem(orderId, customerId, discount, actor)
 *   4. save updated promotion
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class PromotionService {

    @Inject PromotionRepository repository;

    // ── CRUD ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public PromotionAggregate createPromotion(String tenantId, String code, String name,
                                               PromotionType type, DiscountType discountType,
                                               BigDecimal discountPercent, Money discountAmount,
                                               LocalDate validFrom, LocalDate validTo,
                                               int maxTotal, int maxPerCustomer, String actor) {
        if (repository.findByCode(code.toUpperCase(), tenantId).isPresent())
            throw new DuplicatePromotionCodeException(code);

        return repository.save(PromotionAggregate.create(
                tenantId, code, name, type, discountType,
                discountPercent, discountAmount,
                validFrom, validTo, maxTotal, maxPerCustomer, actor));
    }

    @Transactional
    public PromotionAggregate setScope(String id, String tenantId,
                                        List<String> productIds, List<String> categoryIds,
                                        List<String> segments, Money minOrder,
                                        Money cap, String actor) {
        PromotionAggregate p = load(id, tenantId);
        p.setScope(productIds, categoryIds, segments, minOrder, cap, actor);
        return repository.save(p);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PromotionAggregate activate(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        PromotionAggregate p = load(id, tenantId);
        p.activate(actor);
        return repository.save(p);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PromotionAggregate pause(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        PromotionAggregate p = load(id, tenantId);
        p.pause(actor);
        return repository.save(p);
    }

    // ── Redemption ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Validate a coupon code against an order and record a redemption.
     * Called atomically from within the order confirmation transaction.
     */
    @Transactional
    public ApplyResult applyCode(String tenantId, String code, String orderId,
                                  String customerId, String customerSegment,
                                  Money orderTotal, String actor) {
        PromotionAggregate promo = repository.findByCode(code.toUpperCase(), tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new PromotionNotFoundException(code));

        Money discount = promo.validateAndCompute(
                customerId, customerSegment, orderTotal, LocalDate.now());
        RedemptionRecord record = promo.redeem(orderId, customerId, discount, actor);
        repository.save(promo);

        return new ApplyResult(promo.getId(), promo.getCode(), promo.getName(),
                discount, record.id(), record.redeemedAt().toString());
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<PromotionAggregate> findByCode(String code, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findByCode(code.toUpperCase(), tenantId);
    }

    public List<PromotionAggregate> findActive(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findActive(tenantId, LocalDate.now());
    }

    public List<PromotionAggregate> findAll(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findByStatus(tenantId, null);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private PromotionAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new PromotionNotFoundException(id));
    }

    public record ApplyResult(
            String promotionId, String code, String name,
            Money discountApplied, String redemptionId, String redeemedAt) {}

    public static class PromotionNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public PromotionNotFoundException(String ref) {
            super("Promotion not found: " + ref);
        }
    }

    public static class DuplicatePromotionCodeException extends RuntimeException {
        public DuplicatePromotionCodeException(String code) {
            super("Promotion code already exists: " + code);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.promotion.spi;

    Optional<PromotionAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId);
    Optional<PromotionAggregate> findByCode(String code, String tenantId);

package com.saas.purchase.api.rest;

import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderAggregate;
import com.saas.purchase.service.PurchaseOrderService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Purchase Order REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/purchase-orders                              → create PO
 * POST   /api/v1/purchase-orders/{id}/lines                  → add line
 * DELETE /api/v1/purchase-orders/{id}/lines/{lineId}         → remove line
 * POST   /api/v1/purchase-orders/{id}/send                   → DRAFT → SENT
 * POST   /api/v1/purchase-orders/{id}/lines/{lid}/receive    → GRN (receive goods)
 * POST   /api/v1/purchase-orders/{id}/close                  → close
 * POST   /api/v1/purchase-orders/{id}/cancel                 → cancel
 * GET    /api/v1/purchase-orders/{id}                        → get
 * GET    /api/v1/purchase-orders/open                        → list open POs
 */
@Path("/api/v1/purchase-orders")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class PurchaseOrderResource {

    @Inject PurchaseOrderService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "anonymous";
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreatePoRequest req) {
        Party supplier = Party.supplier(req.supplierId(), req.supplierName(), null);
        LocalDate delivery = req.expectedDelivery() != null
                ? LocalDate.parse(req.expectedDelivery()) : null;
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = service.createPO(
                tid(rawTid), supplier, req.currencyCode(),
                delivery, req.warehouseId(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(po)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/lines")
    public Response addLine(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid AddLineRequest req) {
        Money cost = Money.of(req.unitCost(), req.currencyCode());
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = service.addLine(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.productId(), req.sku(),
                req.productName(), req.qty(), cost, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(po)).build();
    }

    @DELETE
    @Path("/{id}/lines/{lineId}")
    public Response removeLine(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @PathParam("lineId") String lineId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = service.removeLine(id, tid(rawTid), lineId, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(po)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/send")
    public Response send(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = service.sendPO(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(po)).build();
    }

    /**
     * Goods Receipt Note (GRN) — records physical arrival of goods.
     * Atomically updates PO line + inventory stock.
     */
    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/lines/{lineId}/receive")
    public Response receiveGoods(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @PathParam("lineId") String lineId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ReceiveRequest req) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = service.receiveGoods(
                id, tid(rawTid), lineId, req.qty(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(po)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/close")
    public Response close(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = service.closePO(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(po)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/cancel")
    public Response cancel(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = service.cancelPO(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(po)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(po -> Response.ok(toResp(po)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/open")
    public Response listOpen(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<PoResponse> items = service.findOpenOrders(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    private PoResponse toResp(PurchaseOrderAggregate po) {
        return new PoResponse(
                po.getId(), po.getTenantId(), po.getPoNumber(),
                po.getSupplier().getName(), po.getStatus().name(),
                po.getCurrencyCode(), po.getSubtotal().getAmount(),
                po.getLines().size(),
                po.getLines().stream()
                        .mapToInt(l -> l.orderedQty() - l.receivedQty()).sum(),
                po.getExpectedDeliveryDate() != null
                        ? po.getExpectedDeliveryDate().toString() : null,
                po.getAudit().getUpdatedAt().toString());
    }

    public record CreatePoRequest(
            @NotBlank String supplierId, @NotBlank String supplierName,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode, String expectedDelivery,
            String warehouseId) {}

    public record AddLineRequest(
            @NotBlank String productId, @NotBlank String sku,
            @NotBlank String productName, @Positive int qty,
            @NotNull BigDecimal unitCost, @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record ReceiveRequest(@Positive int qty) {}

    public record PoResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String poNumber,
            String supplierName, String status, String currencyCode,
            BigDecimal subtotal, int lineCount, int pendingQty,
            String expectedDelivery, String updatedAt) {}
}

package com.saas.purchase.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.*;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Purchase Order aggregate root.
 *
 * Lifecycle:
 *   DRAFT → SENT → PARTIALLY_RECEIVED → RECEIVED → CLOSED
 *                ↘ CANCELLED
 *
 * A PO is the formal request to a supplier.
 * Goods Receipt Notes (GRN) are created when goods arrive
 * and are linked back to PO lines via receivedQty.
 */
public class PurchaseOrderAggregate {

    private String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private       String poNumber;
    private       Party  supplier;
    private       String currencyCode;
    private       POStatus status;
    private       LocalDate expectedDeliveryDate;
    private       Address  deliveryAddress;
    private       String   warehouseId;
    private final List<PurchaseOrderLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();
    private       String notes;
    private       String invoiceRef;   // supplier invoice number once billed
    private       AuditMeta audit;

    public static PurchaseOrderAggregate create(String tenantId, Party supplier,
                                                 String currencyCode,
                                                 LocalDate expectedDelivery,
                                                 String warehouseId,
                                                 String actor) {
        var po = new PurchaseOrderAggregate();
        po.id                    = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        po.tenantId              = tenantId;
        po.supplier              = supplier;
        po.currencyCode          = currencyCode;
        po.status                = POStatus.DRAFT;
        po.expectedDeliveryDate  = expectedDelivery;
        po.warehouseId           = warehouseId;
        po.audit                 = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return po;
    }

    private PurchaseOrderAggregate() {}

    // ── Line commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public PurchaseOrderLine addLine(String productId, String sku, String productName,
                                      int qty, Money unitCost, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        PurchaseOrderLine line = new PurchaseOrderLine(
                UUID.randomUUID().toString(), productId, sku, productName,
                qty, 0, unitCost);
        lines.add(line);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return line;
    }

    public void removeLine(String lineId, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        boolean removed = lines.removeIf(l -> l.id().equals(lineId));
        if (!removed) throw new IllegalArgumentException("PO line not found: " + lineId);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Lifecycle commands ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void send(String poNumber, String actor) {
        if (status != POStatus.DRAFT) throw new IllegalStateException("Only DRAFT can be sent");
        if (lines.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot send empty PO");
        this.poNumber = poNumber;
        this.status   = POStatus.SENT;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    /**
     * Record goods receipt for a specific line (partial or full).
     * Returns true if the entire PO is now fully received.
     */
    public boolean receiveLineGoods(String lineId, int qtyReceived, String actor) {
        if (status != POStatus.SENT && status != POStatus.PARTIALLY_RECEIVED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("PO must be SENT or PARTIALLY_RECEIVED to receive goods");

        int idx = findLineIndex(lineId);
        PurchaseOrderLine line    = lines.get(idx);
        int newReceived           = Math.min(line.receivedQty() + qtyReceived, line.orderedQty());
        lines.set(idx, line.withReceivedQty(newReceived));

        boolean allReceived = lines.stream().allMatch(PurchaseOrderLine::isFullyReceived);
        boolean anyReceived = lines.stream().anyMatch(l -> l.receivedQty() > 0);

        if (allReceived) {
            this.status = POStatus.RECEIVED;
        } else if (anyReceived) {
            this.status = POStatus.PARTIALLY_RECEIVED;
        }
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return allReceived;
    }

    public void close(String actor) {
        if (status != POStatus.RECEIVED && status != POStatus.PARTIALLY_RECEIVED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Can only close RECEIVED or PARTIALLY_RECEIVED PO");
        this.status = POStatus.CLOSED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void cancel(String actor) {
        if (status == POStatus.RECEIVED || status == POStatus.CLOSED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot cancel a received or closed PO");
        this.status = POStatus.CANCELLED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void linkInvoice(String invoiceRef, String actor) {
        this.invoiceRef = invoiceRef;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Computed ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Money getSubtotal() {
        return lines.stream()
                .map(l -> l.unitCost().multiply(l.orderedQty()))
                .reduce(Money.zero(currencyCode), Money::add);
    }

    private void assertDraft() {
        if (status != POStatus.DRAFT) throw new IllegalStateException("PO is not in DRAFT");
    }

    private int findLineIndex(String lineId) {
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++)
            if (lines.get(i).id().equals(lineId)) return i;
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("PO line not found: " + lineId);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                      { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()                { return tenantId; }
    public String getPoNumber()                { return poNumber; }
    public Party  getSupplier()                { return supplier; }
    public String getCurrencyCode()            { return currencyCode; }
    public POStatus getStatus()                { return status; }
    public LocalDate getExpectedDeliveryDate() { return expectedDeliveryDate; }
    public Address getDeliveryAddress()        { return deliveryAddress; }
    public String getWarehouseId()             { return warehouseId; }
    public List<PurchaseOrderLine> getLines()  { return Collections.unmodifiableList(lines); }
    public String getNotes()                   { return notes; }
    public String getInvoiceRef()              { return invoiceRef; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()                { return audit; }

    public enum POStatus {
        DRAFT, SENT, PARTIALLY_RECEIVED, RECEIVED, CLOSED, CANCELLED
    }
}

package com.saas.purchase.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

/** Immutable purchase order line. */
public record PurchaseOrderLine(
        String id,
        String productId,
        String sku,
        String productName,
        int    orderedQty,
        int    receivedQty,
        Money  unitCost
) {
    public Money lineTotal()       { return unitCost.multiply(orderedQty); }
    public int   pendingQty()      { return orderedQty - receivedQty; }
    public boolean isFullyReceived(){ return receivedQty >= orderedQty; }

    public PurchaseOrderLine withReceivedQty(int qty) {
        return new PurchaseOrderLine(id, productId, sku, productName, orderedQty, qty, unitCost);
    }
}

import java.time.LocalDate;

public class PurchaseOrderEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {

    @Column(name="tenant_id",length=64,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String tenantId;
    @Column(name="po_number",length=64) public String poNumber;

    @Column(name="currency_code",length=3,nullable=false) public String currencyCode;

    @Column(name="expected_delivery_date") public LocalDate expectedDeliveryDate;
    @Column(name="warehouse_id",length=36) public String warehouseId;
    @Column(name="notes",columnDefinition="TEXT") public String notes;
    @Column(name="invoice_ref",length=64) public String invoiceRef;
    @Version @Column(name="version",nullable=false) public long version;
    @Column(name="created_at",nullable=false,updatable=false) public Instant createdAt;
    @Column(name="updated_at",nullable=false) public Instant updatedAt;
    @Column(name="created_by",length=128) public String createdBy;
    @Column(name="updated_by",length=128) public String updatedBy;
}

package com.saas.purchase.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderAggregate;
import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderAggregate.POStatus;
import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderLine;
import com.saas.purchase.infrastructure.entity.PurchaseOrderEntity;
import com.saas.purchase.infrastructure.entity.PurchaseOrderLineEntity;
import com.saas.purchase.spi.PurchaseOrderRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Sort;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanachePurchaseOrderRepository
        implements PurchaseOrderRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<PurchaseOrderEntity, String> {

    @Override @Transactional
    public PurchaseOrderAggregate save(PurchaseOrderAggregate po) {
        PurchaseOrderEntity e = toEntity(po);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) getEntityManager().merge(e);
        else persist(e);
        saveLines(po);
        return po;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id=?1 AND tenantId=?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findByPoNumber(String num, String tenantId) {
        return find("poNumber=?1 AND tenantId=?2", num, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public List<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findBySupplier(String tenantId,
                                                         String supplierId,
                                                         int page, int size) {
        return find("tenantId=?1 AND supplierId=?2",
                Sort.descending("createdAt"), tenantId, supplierId)
                .page(page, size).list()
                .stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findOpenOrders(String tenantId) {
        return find("tenantId=?1 AND status IN ('SENT','PARTIALLY_RECEIVED')", tenantId)
                .list().stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    // ── Lines ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private List<PurchaseOrderLineEntity> loadLines(String poId) {
        return getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("SELECT l FROM PurchaseOrderLineEntity l WHERE l.poId=:id",
                        PurchaseOrderLineEntity.class)
                .setParameter("id", poId)
                .getResultList();
    }

    @Transactional
    void saveLines(PurchaseOrderAggregate po) {
        getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("DELETE FROM PurchaseOrderLineEntity l WHERE l.poId=:id")
                .setParameter("id", po.getId()).executeUpdate();
        for (PurchaseOrderLine line : po.getLines()) {
            PurchaseOrderLineEntity le = new PurchaseOrderLineEntity();
            le.id          = line.id();
            le.poId        = po.getId();
            le.tenantId    = po.getTenantId();
            le.productId   = line.productId();
            le.sku         = line.sku();
            le.productName = line.productName();
            le.orderedQty  = line.orderedQty();
            le.receivedQty = line.receivedQty();
            le.unitCostAmt = line.unitCost().getAmount();
            le.unitCostCcy = line.unitCost().getCurrencyCode();
            getEntityManager().persist(le);
        }
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private PurchaseOrderAggregate toDomain(PurchaseOrderEntity e,
                                              List<PurchaseOrderLineEntity> lines) {
        Party supplier = Party.supplier(e.supplierId, e.supplierName, null);
        AuditMeta audit = AuditMeta.reconstitute(
                e.createdAt, e.updatedAt, e.createdBy, e.updatedBy, e.version);

        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = PurchaseOrderAggregate.create(
                e.tenantId, supplier, e.currencyCode,
                e.expectedDeliveryDate, e.warehouseId,
                e.createdBy != null ? e.createdBy : "system");

        for (PurchaseOrderLineEntity le : lines) {
            po.addLine(le.productId, le.sku, le.productName,
                    le.orderedQty, Money.of(le.unitCostAmt, le.unitCostCcy),
                    "system");
        }

        // Advance to correct status
        if (!POStatus.DRAFT.name().equals(e.status)) {
            po.send(e.poNumber != null ? e.poNumber : "PO-UNKNOWN", "system");
        }
        return po;
    }

    private PurchaseOrderEntity toEntity(PurchaseOrderAggregate po) {
        PurchaseOrderEntity e   = new PurchaseOrderEntity();
        e.id                    = po.getId();
        e.tenantId              = po.getTenantId();
        e.poNumber              = po.getPoNumber();
        e.supplierId            = po.getSupplier().getId();
        e.supplierName          = po.getSupplier().getName();
        e.currencyCode          = po.getCurrencyCode();
        e.status                = po.getStatus().name();
        e.expectedDeliveryDate  = po.getExpectedDeliveryDate();
        e.warehouseId           = po.getWarehouseId();
        e.notes                 = po.getNotes();
        e.invoiceRef            = po.getInvoiceRef();
        e.version               = po.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt             = po.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt             = po.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy             = po.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy             = po.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        return e;
    }
}

package com.saas.purchase.service;

import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.service.InventoryService;
import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderAggregate;
import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderLine;
import com.saas.purchase.spi.PurchaseOrderRepository;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * Purchase Order application service.
 *
 * GRN (Goods Receipt Note) flow:
 *   1. createPO()         → DRAFT
 *   2. addLine() × N      → lines added
 *   3. sendPO()           → SENT (triggers incoming stock bump in inventory)
 *   4. receiveGoods()     → PARTIALLY_RECEIVED or RECEIVED
 *                            + stock receipt written to InventoryService
 *   5. close()            → CLOSED
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class PurchaseOrderService {

    @Inject PurchaseOrderRepository repository;
    @Inject InventoryService        inventoryService;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher    publisher;

    private static final AtomicLong COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    @Transactional
    public PurchaseOrderAggregate createPO(String tenantId, Party supplier,
                                            String currencyCode,
                                            LocalDate expectedDelivery,
                                            String warehouseId,
                                            String actor) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = PurchaseOrderAggregate.create(
                tenantId, supplier, currencyCode, expectedDelivery, warehouseId, actor);
        return repository.save(po);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PurchaseOrderAggregate addLine(String id, String tenantId,
                                           String productId, String sku,
                                           String productName, int qty,
                                           Money unitCost, String actor) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = load(id, tenantId);
        po.addLine(productId, sku, productName, qty, unitCost, actor);
        return repository.save(po);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PurchaseOrderAggregate removeLine(String id, String tenantId,
                                              String lineId, String actor) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = load(id, tenantId);
        po.removeLine(lineId, actor);
        return repository.save(po);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PurchaseOrderAggregate sendPO(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = load(id, tenantId);
        String poNumber = generatePoNumber(tenantId);
        po.send(poNumber, actor);
        repository.save(po);

        // Signal inventory: stock is incoming
        for (PurchaseOrderLine line : po.getLines()) {
            inventoryService.getStock(tenantId, line.productId(), po.getWarehouseId())
                    .ifPresent(ledger -> {
                        // bump incoming quantity
                        ledger.recordIncoming(line.orderedQty(), actor);
                    });
        }
        return po;
    }

    /**
     * Record goods received for a specific line (GRN).
     * Automatically triggers stock receipt in InventoryService.
     */
    @Transactional
    public PurchaseOrderAggregate receiveGoods(String id, String tenantId,
                                                String lineId, int qtyReceived,
                                                String actor) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = load(id, tenantId);
        PurchaseOrderLine line = po.getLines().stream()
                .filter(l -> l.id().equals(lineId))
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("PO line not found: " + lineId));

        po.receiveLineGoods(lineId, qtyReceived, actor);
        repository.save(po);

        // Write stock receipt to inventory ledger
        inventoryService.receiveStock(
                tenantId, line.productId(), po.getWarehouseId(),
                qtyReceived, StockMovement.MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT,
                po.getPoNumber(), actor);

        return po;
    }

    @Transactional
    public PurchaseOrderAggregate closePO(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = load(id, tenantId);
        po.close(actor);
        return repository.save(po);
    }

    @Transactional
    public PurchaseOrderAggregate cancelPO(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = load(id, tenantId);
        po.cancel(actor);
        return repository.save(po);
    }

    public Optional<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public List<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findOpenOrders(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findOpenOrders(tenantId);
    }

    private PurchaseOrderAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new PurchaseOrderNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    public static class PurchaseOrderNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public PurchaseOrderNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Purchase order not found: id=" + id + ", tenant=" + t);
        }
    }

    private String generatePoNumber(String tenantId) {
        String prefix = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        return "PO-" + prefix + "-" + String.format("%07d", COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
    }
}

package com.saas.purchase.spi;

import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderAggregate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

public interface PurchaseOrderRepository {
    PurchaseOrderAggregate save(PurchaseOrderAggregate po);
    Optional<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId);
    Optional<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findByPoNumber(String poNumber, String tenantId);
    List<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findBySupplier(String tenantId, String supplierId, int page, int size);
    List<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findOpenOrders(String tenantId);
}

package com.saas.reporting.api.rest;

import com.saas.reporting.service.ReportingService;
import com.saas.reporting.service.ReportingService.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * Financial Reporting REST API.
 *
 * GET /api/v1/reports/profit-loss          → P&L statement
 * GET /api/v1/reports/balance-sheet        → Balance sheet
 * GET /api/v1/reports/cash-flow            → Cash flow statement
 *
 * Query params: from, to (ISO date), asOf (ISO date for balance sheet)
 */
@Path("/api/v1/reports")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class ReportingResource {

    @Inject ReportingService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/profit-loss")
    public Response getProfitLoss(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("from") @DefaultValue("2024-01-01") String from,
            @QueryParam("to")   String to) {

        String toDate = to != null ? to : LocalDate.now().toString();
        ProfitLossReport report = service.getProfitLoss(
                tid(rawTid), LocalDate.parse(from), LocalDate.parse(toDate));

        return Response.ok(new PLResponse(
                report.tenantId(), from, toDate, report.currencyCode(),
                toAmtMap(report.revenue()), report.totalRevenue().getAmount(),
                toAmtMap(report.cogs()), report.totalCogs().getAmount(),
                report.grossProfit().getAmount(),
                toAmtMap(report.expenses()), report.totalExpenses().getAmount(),
                report.operatingIncome().getAmount())).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/balance-sheet")
    public Response getBalanceSheet(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("asOf") String asOf) {

        LocalDate date = asOf != null ? LocalDate.parse(asOf) : LocalDate.now();
        BalanceSheetReport report = service.getBalanceSheet(tid(rawTid), date);

        return Response.ok(new BSResponse(
                report.tenantId(), date.toString(), report.currencyCode(),
                toAmtMap(report.assets()), report.totalAssets().getAmount(),
                toAmtMap(report.liabilities()), report.totalLiabilities().getAmount(),
                toAmtMap(report.equity()), report.totalEquity().getAmount())).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/cash-flow")
    public Response getCashFlow(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("from") @DefaultValue("2024-01-01") String from,
            @QueryParam("to")   String to) {

        String toDate = to != null ? to : LocalDate.now().toString();
        CashFlowReport report = service.getCashFlow(
                tid(rawTid), LocalDate.parse(from), LocalDate.parse(toDate));

        return Response.ok(new CFResponse(
                report.tenantId(), from, toDate, report.currencyCode(),
                report.netIncome().getAmount(),
                report.depreciation().getAmount(),
                report.operatingCashFlow().getAmount(),
                report.investingActivities().getAmount(),
                report.financingActivities().getAmount(),
                report.netCashChange().getAmount(),
                report.openingCash().getAmount(),
                report.closingCash().getAmount())).build();
    }

    private Map<String, BigDecimal> toAmtMap(Map<String, Money> m) {
        return m.entrySet().stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(Map.Entry::getKey,
                        e -> e.getValue().getAmount()));
    }

    public record PLResponse(String tenantId, String from, String to, String ccy,
            Map<String,BigDecimal> revenue, BigDecimal totalRevenue,
            Map<String,BigDecimal> cogs, BigDecimal totalCogs, BigDecimal grossProfit,
            Map<String,BigDecimal> expenses, BigDecimal totalExpenses, BigDecimal operatingIncome) {}

    public record BSResponse(String tenantId, String asOf, String ccy,
            Map<String,BigDecimal> assets, BigDecimal totalAssets,
            Map<String,BigDecimal> liabilities, BigDecimal totalLiabilities,
            Map<String,BigDecimal> equity, BigDecimal totalEquity) {}

    public record CFResponse(String tenantId, String from, String to, String ccy,
            BigDecimal netIncome, BigDecimal depreciation, BigDecimal operatingCashFlow,
            BigDecimal investing, BigDecimal financing,
            BigDecimal netCashChange, BigDecimal openingCash, BigDecimal closingCash) {}
}
