package com.saas.reporting.service;

import com.saas.accounting.service.AccountingService;
import com.saas.accounting.spi.AccountingRepository.TrialBalanceLine;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Financial reporting service.
 *
 * Generates standard financial statements from GL data:
 *  1. Profit & Loss Statement (Income Statement)
 *  2. Balance Sheet
 *  3. Cash Flow Statement (indirect method)
 *
 * All reports are computed dynamically from posted journal entries.
 * No separate reporting tables needed.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class ReportingService {

    @Inject AccountingService accountingService;

    // ── P&L Statement ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Generate Profit & Loss statement for a date range.
     *
     * Structure:
     *   Revenue           (+)
     *   Cost of Goods Sold(-)
     *   Gross Profit
     *   Operating Expenses(-)
     *   Operating Income
     *   Tax Expense       (-)
     *   Net Income
     */
    public ProfitLossReport getProfitLoss(String tenantId,
                                           LocalDate from, LocalDate to) {
        List<TrialBalanceLine> tb = accountingService.getTrialBalance(tenantId, to);

        // Only include accounts that had activity in the period
        Map<String, Money> revenue   = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        Map<String, Money> cogs      = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        Map<String, Money> expenses  = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        String ccy = "IDR";

        for (TrialBalanceLine line : tb) {
            // Filter by date range using per-account balance
            Money balance = accountingService.getAccountBalance(
                    tenantId, line.accountCode(), from, to);

            if (balance.isZero()) continue;

            switch (line.accountType()) {
                case "REVENUE" -> revenue.put(
                        line.accountCode() + " " + line.accountName(), balance);
                case "COGS" -> cogs.put(
                        line.accountCode() + " " + line.accountName(), balance);
                case "EXPENSE" -> expenses.put(
                        line.accountCode() + " " + line.accountName(), balance);
            }

            if (!balance.isZero()) ccy = balance.getCurrencyCode();
        }

        Money totalRevenue = sumMap(revenue, ccy);
        Money totalCogs    = sumMap(cogs, ccy);
        Money grossProfit  = totalRevenue.isGreaterThan(totalCogs)
                ? totalRevenue.subtract(totalCogs) : Money.zero(ccy);
        Money totalExpenses = sumMap(expenses, ccy);
        Money operatingIncome = grossProfit.isGreaterThan(totalExpenses)
                ? grossProfit.subtract(totalExpenses) : Money.zero(ccy);

        return new ProfitLossReport(tenantId, from, to, ccy,
                revenue, totalRevenue, cogs, totalCogs, grossProfit,
                expenses, totalExpenses, operatingIncome);
    }

    // ── Balance Sheet ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Generate Balance Sheet as of a date.
     *
     * Equation: Assets = Liabilities + Equity
     *
     * Retained earnings = opening retained + net income for period
     * (simplified: net income from P&L is accumulated in retained earnings)
     */
    public BalanceSheetReport getBalanceSheet(String tenantId, LocalDate asOf) {
        List<TrialBalanceLine> tb = accountingService.getTrialBalance(tenantId, asOf);

        Map<String, Money> assets      = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        Map<String, Money> liabilities = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        Map<String, Money> equity      = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        String ccy = "IDR";

        for (TrialBalanceLine line : tb) {
            Money balance = accountingService.getAccountBalance(
                    tenantId, line.accountCode(), LocalDate.of(2000, 1, 1), asOf);

            if (balance.isZero()) continue;
            ccy = balance.getCurrencyCode();

            switch (line.accountType()) {
                case "ASSET"     -> assets.put(line.accountCode() + " " + line.accountName(), balance);
                case "LIABILITY" -> liabilities.put(line.accountCode() + " " + line.accountName(), balance);
                case "EQUITY"    -> equity.put(line.accountCode() + " " + line.accountName(), balance);
            }
        }

        Money totalAssets      = sumMap(assets, ccy);
        Money totalLiabilities = sumMap(liabilities, ccy);
        Money totalEquity      = sumMap(equity, ccy);

        return new BalanceSheetReport(tenantId, asOf, ccy,
                assets, totalAssets, liabilities, totalLiabilities,
                equity, totalEquity);
    }

    // ── Cash Flow (Indirect Method) ───────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Cash Flow Statement — indirect method.
     *
     * Operating:  Net income + non-cash adjustments (depreciation)
     *             +/- changes in working capital
     * Investing:  Asset purchases/disposals
     * Financing:  Loan proceeds/repayments, equity
     */
    public CashFlowReport getCashFlow(String tenantId,
                                       LocalDate from, LocalDate to) {
        ProfitLossReport pl = getProfitLoss(tenantId, from, to);
        String ccy = pl.currencyCode();

        // Depreciation (add back — non-cash expense)
        Money depreciation = accountingService.getAccountBalance(
                tenantId, "5060", from, to);

        // Change in AR (1100)
        Money arChange = accountingService.getAccountBalance(tenantId, "1100", from, to);
        // Change in AP (2000)
        Money apChange = accountingService.getAccountBalance(tenantId, "2000", from, to);
        // Change in Inventory (1200)
        Money inventoryChange = accountingService.getAccountBalance(tenantId, "1200", from, to);

        Money operatingCashFlow = pl.operatingIncome()
                .add(depreciation)
                .add(apChange);   // simplified

        // Investing: fixed asset purchases (1500-1520)
        Money investing = accountingService.getAccountBalance(tenantId, "1500", from, to);

        // Financing: loans (2300, 2500)
        Money financing = Money.zero(ccy);

        // Net cash = opening bank + operating + investing + financing
        Money openingCash = accountingService.getAccountBalance(
                tenantId, "1020", LocalDate.of(2000, 1, 1), from.minusDays(1));
        Money netCashChange = operatingCashFlow.add(financing);
        Money closingCash   = openingCash.add(netCashChange);

        return new CashFlowReport(tenantId, from, to, ccy,
                pl.operatingIncome(), depreciation, operatingCashFlow,
                investing, financing, netCashChange, openingCash, closingCash);
    }

    // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private Money sumMap(Map<String, Money> m, String ccy) {
        return m.values().stream().reduce(Money.zero(ccy), Money::add);
    }

    // ── Report records ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record ProfitLossReport(
            String tenantId, LocalDate from, LocalDate to, String currencyCode,
            Map<String, Money> revenue, Money totalRevenue,
            Map<String, Money> cogs, Money totalCogs, Money grossProfit,
            Map<String, Money> expenses, Money totalExpenses, Money operatingIncome) {}

    public record BalanceSheetReport(
            String tenantId, LocalDate asOf, String currencyCode,
            Map<String, Money> assets, Money totalAssets,
            Map<String, Money> liabilities, Money totalLiabilities,
            Map<String, Money> equity, Money totalEquity) {}

    public record CashFlowReport(
            String tenantId, LocalDate from, LocalDate to, String currencyCode,
            Money netIncome, Money depreciation, Money operatingCashFlow,
            Money investingActivities, Money financingActivities,
            Money netCashChange, Money openingCash, Money closingCash) {}
}

package com.saas.shared.event;

import java.time.Instant;

/**
 * Root marker for all domain events across every ERP module.
 *
 * Conventions:
 *  - Event names are past-tense: OrderPlaced, InvoiceIssued, StockReserved
 *  - Each event carries enough data to act without a DB round-trip
 *  - Events are immutable records
 *  - aggregateId + tenantId are always present for routing
 */
public interface DomainEvent {

    /** ID of the aggregate that produced this event (order ID, invoice ID, etc.) */
    String aggregateId();

    /** Tenant for multi-tenant routing */
    String tenantId();

    /** Wall-clock time the event occurred */
    Instant occurredAt();

    /** Actor (user/system) that triggered the event */
    String actor();

    /** Module-level event type string, e.g. "order.placed", "invoice.issued" */
    default String eventType() {
        return getClass().getSimpleName();
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.event;

import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.enterprise.event.Event;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import org.jboss.logging.Logger;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Cross-module domain event publisher SPI.
 */
public interface DomainEventPublisher {

    void publish(DomainEvent event);

    default void publishAll(List<DomainEvent> events) {
        events.forEach(this::publish);
    }
}

@ApplicationScoped
class CdiDomainEventPublisher implements DomainEventPublisher {

    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(CdiDomainEventPublisher.class);

    @Inject Event<DomainEvent> bus;

    @Override
    public void publish(DomainEvent event) {
        try {
            bus.fire(event);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            LOG.errorf(e, "Failed to publish %s for aggregate %s",
                    event.eventType(), event.aggregateId());
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.health;

import com.saas.shared.infrastructure.OutboxEventPublisher;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.persistence.EntityManager;
import org.eclipse.microprofile.health.*;

/**
 * ERP health check probes.
 *
 * Exposed at:
 *   /health/live   → liveness  (app is running)
 *   /health/ready  → readiness (DB + outbox healthy)
 *
 * Used by Kubernetes liveness/readiness probes and Quarkus dev-UI.
 */
public class ErpHealthChecks {

    // ── Liveness ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Liveness
    @ApplicationScoped
    public static class ErpLivenessCheck implements HealthCheck {

        @Override
        public HealthCheckResponse call() {
            return HealthCheckResponse.builder()
                    .name("syirkah-liveness")
                    .up()
                    .withData("version", "1.0.0")
                    .withData("modules", "order,inventory,invoice,payment,accounting,warehouse,shipment")
                    .build();
        }
    }

    // ── DB Readiness ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Readiness
    @ApplicationScoped
    public static class DatabaseReadinessCheck implements HealthCheck {

        @Inject EntityManager em;

        @Override
        public HealthCheckResponse call() {
            try {
                // Lightweight query that exercises the connection pool
                Object result = em.createNativeQuery("SELECT 1").getSingleResult();
                return HealthCheckResponse.builder()
                        .name("syirkah-database")
                        .up()
                        .withData("query", "SELECT 1")
                        .withData("result", result.toString())
                        .build();
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return HealthCheckResponse.builder()
                        .name("syirkah-database")
                        .down()
                        .withData("error", e.getMessage())
                        .build();
            }
        }
    }

    // ── Outbox Readiness ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Warns if the outbox has pending events older than 60 seconds.
     * This indicates the Kafka relay is stuck or Kafka is unavailable.
     */
    @Readiness
    @ApplicationScoped
    public static class OutboxReadinessCheck implements HealthCheck {

        private static final long WARN_THRESHOLD    = 100L;
        private static final long CRITICAL_THRESHOLD = 1000L;

        @Inject OutboxEventPublisher outbox;

        @Override
        public HealthCheckResponse call() {
            try {
                long pending = outbox.countPending();
                long deadLettered = outbox.findDeadLettered().size();

                if (deadLettered > 0) {
                    return HealthCheckResponse.builder()
                            .name("syirkah-outbox")
                            .down()
                            .withData("pending",     pending)
                            .withData("deadLettered", deadLettered)
                            .withData("status",       "DEAD_LETTERS_PRESENT")
                            .build();
                }

                boolean degraded = pending > WARN_THRESHOLD;
                boolean critical = pending > CRITICAL_THRESHOLD;

                return HealthCheckResponse.builder()
                        .name("syirkah-outbox")
                        .status(!critical)
                        .withData("pending",      pending)
                        .withData("deadLettered", deadLettered)
                        .withData("status", critical ? "CRITICAL" : degraded ? "DEGRADED" : "OK")
                        .build();

            } catch (Exception e) {
                return HealthCheckResponse.builder()
                        .name("syirkah-outbox")
                        .down()
                        .withData("error", e.getMessage())
                        .build();
            }
        }
    }

    // ── Module Startup Check ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Startup
    @ApplicationScoped
    public static class ErpStartupCheck implements HealthCheck {

        @Inject EntityManager em;

        @Override
        public HealthCheckResponse call() {
            try {
                // Verify all critical tables exist at startup
                String[] tables = {
                    "orders", "customers", "suppliers", "stock_ledgers",
                    "purchase_orders", "invoices", "payments",
                    "journal_entries", "warehouses", "shipments",
                    "domain_event_outbox"
                };

                HealthCheckResponse.HealthCheckResponseBuilder builder =
                        HealthCheckResponse.builder().name("syirkah-schema-validation");

                for (String table : tables) {
                    try {
                        long count = ((Number) em.createNativeQuery(
                                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM " + table)
                                .getSingleResult()).longValue();
                        builder.withData(table + ".rows", count);
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        return builder.down()
                                .withData("missing_table", table)
                                .withData("error", e.getMessage())
                                .build();
                    }
                }

                return builder.up().withData("tables_verified", tables.length).build();

            } catch (Exception e) {
                return HealthCheckResponse.builder()
                        .name("syirkah-schema-validation")
                        .down()
                        .withData("error", e.getMessage())
                        .build();
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.infrastructure;

import com.saas.customer.service.CustomerService.CustomerNotFoundException;
import com.saas.customer.service.CustomerService.DuplicateEmailException;
import com.saas.inventory.service.InventoryService.StockLedgerNotFoundException;
import com.saas.invoice.service.InvoiceService.InvoiceNotFoundException;
import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate.OrderStatusException;
import com.saas.order.service.OrderService.OrderNotFoundException;
import com.saas.payment.service.PaymentService.PaymentNotFoundException;
import com.saas.purchase.service.PurchaseOrderService.PurchaseOrderNotFoundException;
import com.saas.supplier.service.SupplierService.SupplierNotFoundException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.BadRequestException;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.MediaType;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.ExceptionMapper;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;
import org.jboss.logging.Logger;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Centralised JAX-RS exception → HTTP response mapping for all ERP modules.
 *
 * Response body format:
 * {
 *   "code":      "NOT_FOUND",
 *   "message":   "Order not found: id=xxx, tenant=yyy",
 *   "timestamp": "2024-01-15T10:30:00Z"
 * }
 */
public class GlobalExceptionMapper {

    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(GlobalExceptionMapper.class);

    // ── 404 Not Found ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Provider
    public static class OrderNotFoundMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<OrderNotFoundException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(OrderNotFoundException e) {
            return err(404, "ORDER_NOT_FOUND", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Provider
    public static class CustomerNotFoundMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<CustomerNotFoundException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(CustomerNotFoundException e) {
            return err(404, "CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Provider
    public static class InvoiceNotFoundMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<InvoiceNotFoundException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(InvoiceNotFoundException e) {
            return err(404, "INVOICE_NOT_FOUND", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Provider
    public static class PaymentNotFoundMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<PaymentNotFoundException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(PaymentNotFoundException e) {
            return err(404, "PAYMENT_NOT_FOUND", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Provider
    public static class SupplierNotFoundMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<SupplierNotFoundException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(SupplierNotFoundException e) {
            return err(404, "SUPPLIER_NOT_FOUND", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Provider
    public static class StockLedgerNotFoundMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<StockLedgerNotFoundException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(StockLedgerNotFoundException e) {
            return err(404, "STOCK_LEDGER_NOT_FOUND", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ── 400 Bad Request ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Provider
    public static class OrderStatusMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<OrderStatusException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(OrderStatusException e) {
            return err(400, "INVALID_ORDER_STATUS_TRANSITION", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Provider
    public static class IllegalStateMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<IllegalStateException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(IllegalStateException e) {
            return err(400, "ILLEGAL_STATE", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Provider
    public static class IllegalArgumentMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<IllegalArgumentException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return err(400, "INVALID_ARGUMENT", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ── 409 Conflict ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Provider
    public static class DuplicateEmailMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<DuplicateEmailException> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(DuplicateEmailException e) {
            return err(409, "DUPLICATE_EMAIL", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ── 500 Catch-all ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Provider
    public static class UnhandledMapper
            implements ExceptionMapper<Exception> {
        @Override public Response toResponse(Exception e) {
            // Don't expose internal details — log them server-side
            LOG.errorf(e, "Unhandled exception: %s", e.getMessage());
            return err(500, "INTERNAL_ERROR",
                    "An unexpected error occurred. Check server logs.");
        }
    }

    // ── Shared helper ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private static Response err(int status, String code, String message) {
        Map<String, Object> body = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        body.put("code",      code);
        body.put("message",   message);
        body.put("timestamp", Instant.now().toString());
        return Response.status(status)
                .entity(body)
                .type(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                .build();
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.infrastructure;

import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate;
import com.saas.customer.spi.CustomerRepository;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockLedger;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.spi.InventoryRepository;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate.*;
import com.saas.invoice.spi.InvoiceRepository;
import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderId;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderStatus;
import com.saas.order.spi.OrderRepository;
import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord;
import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord.PaymentStatus;
import com.saas.payment.spi.PaymentRepository;
import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderAggregate;
import com.saas.purchase.spi.PurchaseOrderRepository;
import com.saas.supplier.core.SupplierAggregate;
import com.saas.supplier.spi.SupplierRepository;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * In-memory implementations of all ERP repository SPIs.
 *
 * Use in unit tests that need realistic repository behaviour without
 * CDI, JPA, or a database. Each implementation is a simple ConcurrentHashMap
 * keyed by tenantId::aggregateId.
 *
 * Example (plain JUnit — no @QuarkusTest):
 *   var orderRepo = new InMemoryOrderRepository();
 *   var service   = new OrderService(orderRepo, publisher);
 *   var order     = service.createOrder(...);
 */
public final class InMemoryRepositories {

    private InMemoryRepositories() {}

    // ── Order ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryOrderRepository implements OrderRepository {
        private final Map<String, OrderAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

        private String key(OrderId id, String t) { return t + "::" + id.getValue(); }

        @Override public OrderAggregate save(OrderAggregate o) {
            store.put(key(o.getId(), o.getTenantId()), o); return o; }

        @Override public Optional<OrderAggregate> findById(OrderId id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(key(id, t))); }

        @Override public Optional<OrderAggregate> findByOrderNumber(String num, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(o -> t.equals(o.getTenantId()) && num.equals(o.getOrderNumber()))
                    .findFirst(); }

        @Override public void delete(OrderId id, String t) { store.remove(key(id, t)); }

        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
        public int size()   { return store.size(); }
    }

    // ── Customer ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryCustomerRepository implements CustomerRepository {
        private final Map<String, CustomerAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

        private String key(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public CustomerAggregate save(CustomerAggregate c) {
            store.put(key(c.getId(), c.getTenantId()), c); return c; }

        @Override public Optional<CustomerAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(key(id, t))); }

        @Override public Optional<CustomerAggregate> findByEmail(String email, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(c -> t.equals(c.getTenantId()) && email.equals(c.getEmail()))
                    .findFirst(); }

        @Override public boolean existsByEmail(String email, String t) {
            return findByEmail(email, t).isPresent(); }

        @Override public List<CustomerAggregate> findBySegment(String t, String seg, int p, int s) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(c -> t.equals(c.getTenantId()) && seg.equals(c.getSegment()))
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(CustomerAggregate::getName))
                    .skip((long) p * s).limit(s).collect(Collectors.toList()); }

        @Override public long count(String t) {
            return store.values().stream().filter(c -> t.equals(c.getTenantId())).count(); }

        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Supplier ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemorySupplierRepository implements SupplierRepository {
        private final Map<String, SupplierAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String key(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public SupplierAggregate save(SupplierAggregate s) {
            store.put(key(s.getId(), s.getTenantId()), s); return s; }
        @Override public Optional<SupplierAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(key(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<SupplierAggregate> findByEmail(String email, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(s -> t.equals(s.getTenantId()) && email.equals(s.getEmail()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<SupplierAggregate> findAll(String t, int p, int s) {
            return store.values().stream().filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()))
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(SupplierAggregate::getName))
                    .skip((long) p * s).limit(s).collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        @Override public long count(String t) {
            return store.values().stream().filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())).count(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Inventory ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryInventoryRepository implements InventoryRepository {
        private final Map<String, StockLedger> ledgers     = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private final List<StockMovement>       movements   = Collections.synchronizedList(new ArrayList<>());

        private String key(String t, String p, String w) { return t + "::" + p + "::" + w; }

        @Override public StockLedger save(StockLedger l) {
            ledgers.put(key(l.getTenantId(), l.getProductId(), l.getWarehouseId()), l); return l; }
        @Override public Optional<StockLedger> findById(String id, String t) {
            return ledgers.values().stream()
                    .filter(l -> t.equals(l.getTenantId()) && id.equals(l.getId())).findFirst(); }
        @Override public Optional<StockLedger> findByProductAndWarehouse(String t, String p, String w) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(ledgers.get(key(t, p, w))); }
        @Override public List<StockLedger> findByProduct(String t, String p) {
            return ledgers.values().stream()
                    .filter(l -> t.equals(l.getTenantId()) && p.equals(l.getProductId()))
                    .collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        @Override public List<StockLedger> findLowStock(String t) {
            return ledgers.values().stream()
                    .filter(l -> t.equals(l.getTenantId()) && l.getAvailable() <= l.getReorderPoint())
                    .collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        @Override public List<StockLedger> findOutOfStock(String t) {
            return ledgers.values().stream()
                    .filter(l -> t.equals(l.getTenantId()) && l.isOutOfStock())
                    .collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        @Override public void saveMovement(StockMovement mv) { movements.add(mv); }
        @Override public List<StockMovement> findMovements(String t, String p, int limit) {
            return movements.stream()
                    .filter(m -> t.equals(m.tenantId()) && p.equals(m.productId()))
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(StockMovement::occurredAt).reversed())
                    .limit(limit).collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        public void clear() { ledgers.clear(); movements.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Invoice ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryInvoiceRepository implements InvoiceRepository {
        private final Map<String, InvoiceAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String key(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public InvoiceAggregate save(InvoiceAggregate i) {
            store.put(key(i.getId(), i.getTenantId()), i); return i; }
        @Override public Optional<InvoiceAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(key(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<InvoiceAggregate> findByNumber(String num, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(i -> t.equals(i.getTenantId()) && num.equals(i.getInvoiceNumber()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public Optional<InvoiceAggregate> findByOrderId(String orderId, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(i -> t.equals(i.getTenantId()) && orderId.equals(i.getOrderId()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<InvoiceAggregate> findByStatus(String t, InvoiceType type,
                                                               InvoiceStatus status, int p, int s) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(i -> t.equals(i.getTenantId()) && type == i.getType() && status == i.getStatus())
                    .skip((long) p * s).limit(s).collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        @Override public List<InvoiceAggregate> findOverdue(String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(i -> ("*".equals(t) || t.equals(i.getTenantId()))
                            && (i.getStatus() == InvoiceStatus.ISSUED || i.getStatus() == InvoiceStatus.PARTIALLY_PAID)
                            && i.getDueDate() != null && i.getDueDate().isBefore(java.time.LocalDate.now()))
                    .collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Payment ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryPaymentRepository implements PaymentRepository {
        private final Map<String, PaymentRecord> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String key(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public PaymentRecord save(PaymentRecord p) {
            store.put(key(p.getId(), p.getTenantId()), p); return p; }
        @Override public Optional<PaymentRecord> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(key(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<PaymentRecord> findByPaymentNumber(String num, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(p -> t.equals(p.getTenantId()) && num.equals(p.getPaymentNumber()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<PaymentRecord> findByInvoice(String t, String invoiceId) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(p -> t.equals(p.getTenantId()) && invoiceId.equals(p.getInvoiceId()))
                    .collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        @Override public List<PaymentRecord> findByStatus(String t, PaymentStatus st, int p, int s) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && st == x.getStatus())
                    .skip((long) p * s).limit(s).collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        @Override public List<PaymentRecord> findUnreconciled(String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(p -> t.equals(p.getTenantId()) && p.getStatus() == PaymentStatus.PROCESSED)
                    .collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Purchase Order ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryPurchaseOrderRepository implements PurchaseOrderRepository {
        private final Map<String, PurchaseOrderAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String key(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public PurchaseOrderAggregate save(PurchaseOrderAggregate po) {
            store.put(key(po.getId(), po.getTenantId()), po); return po; }
        @Override public Optional<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(key(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findByPoNumber(String num, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(p -> t.equals(p.getTenantId()) && num.equals(p.getPoNumber()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findBySupplier(String t, String suppId, int p, int s) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(po -> t.equals(po.getTenantId()) && suppId.equals(po.getSupplier().getId()))
                    .skip((long) p * s).limit(s).collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        @Override public List<PurchaseOrderAggregate> findOpenOrders(String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(po -> t.equals(po.getTenantId())
                            && (po.getStatus() == PurchaseOrderAggregate.POStatus.SENT
                            ||  po.getStatus() == PurchaseOrderAggregate.POStatus.PARTIALLY_RECEIVED))
                    .collect(Collectors.toList()); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.infrastructure;

import com.saas.budget.core.BudgetAggregate;
import com.saas.budget.core.BudgetAggregate.BudgetStatus;
import com.saas.budget.spi.BudgetRepository;
import com.saas.catalog.core.BrandAggregate;
import com.saas.catalog.core.CategoryAggregate;
import com.saas.catalog.spi.CatalogRepository;
import com.saas.currency.core.ExchangeRateAggregate;
import com.saas.currency.spi.ExchangeRateRepository;
import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate;
import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate.EmployeeStatus;
import com.saas.employee.spi.EmployeeRepository;
import com.saas.fixedasset.core.FixedAssetAggregate;
import com.saas.fixedasset.core.FixedAssetAggregate.AssetStatus;
import com.saas.fixedasset.spi.FixedAssetRepository;
import com.saas.notification.core.NotificationRecord;
import com.saas.notification.spi.NotificationRepository;
import com.saas.payroll.core.PayslipAggregate;
import com.saas.payroll.core.PayslipAggregate.PayslipStatus;
import com.saas.payroll.spi.PayslipRepository;
import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceList;
import com.saas.pricing.spi.PriceListRepository;
import com.saas.promotion.core.PromotionAggregate;
import com.saas.promotion.core.PromotionAggregate.PromotionStatus;
import com.saas.promotion.spi.PromotionRepository;
import com.saas.tax.core.TaxAggregate;
import com.saas.tax.spi.TaxRepository;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.YearMonth;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

/**
 * In-memory repository implementations for all new ERP domains.
 * Companion to InMemoryRepositories — covers Tax through Catalog.
 *
 * Use in unit tests without CDI or database.
 */
public final class NewDomainInMemoryRepositories {

    private NewDomainInMemoryRepositories() {}

    // ── Tax ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryTaxRepository implements TaxRepository {
        private final Map<String, TaxAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public TaxAggregate save(TaxAggregate x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<TaxAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<TaxAggregate> findByCode(String code, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && code.equals(x.getCode()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<TaxAggregate> findActive(String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && x.isActive()).toList(); }
        @Override public List<TaxAggregate> findByType(String t, TaxAggregate.TaxType type) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && type == x.getType()).toList(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Currency ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryExchangeRateRepository implements ExchangeRateRepository {
        private final Map<String, ExchangeRateAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }
        private String pairKey(String t, String b, String tgt) { return t + "::" + b + "_" + tgt; }

        @Override public ExchangeRateAggregate save(ExchangeRateAggregate x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x);
            return x; }
        @Override public Optional<ExchangeRateAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<ExchangeRateAggregate> findByPair(String t, String base, String tgt) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())
                            && base.equals(x.getBaseCurrency())
                            && tgt.equals(x.getTargetCurrency()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<ExchangeRateAggregate> findAll(String t) {
            return store.values().stream().filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())).toList(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Pricing ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryPriceListRepository implements PriceListRepository {
        private final Map<String, PriceList> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public PriceList save(PriceList x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<PriceList> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<PriceList> findByCode(String code, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && code.equals(x.getCode()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<PriceList> findAll(String t) {
            return store.values().stream().filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())).toList(); }
        @Override public List<PriceList> findActive(String t, LocalDate date) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && x.isEffective(date)).toList(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Employee ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryEmployeeRepository implements EmployeeRepository {
        private final Map<String, EmployeeAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public EmployeeAggregate save(EmployeeAggregate x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<EmployeeAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<EmployeeAggregate> findByEmployeeNumber(String num, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && num.equals(x.getEmployeeNumber()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public Optional<EmployeeAggregate> findByEmail(String email, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && email.equals(x.getEmail()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<EmployeeAggregate> findByDepartment(String t, String deptId) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && deptId.equals(x.getDepartmentId()))
                    .toList(); }
        @Override public List<EmployeeAggregate> findByStatus(String t, EmployeeStatus s,
                                                               int page, int size) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && s == x.getStatus())
                    .skip((long) page * size).limit(size).toList(); }
        @Override public boolean existsByEmployeeNumber(String num, String t) {
            return findByEmployeeNumber(num, t).isPresent(); }
        @Override public long countActive(String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && x.isEmployed()).count(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Payroll ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryPayslipRepository implements PayslipRepository {
        private final Map<String, PayslipAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public PayslipAggregate save(PayslipAggregate x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<PayslipAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<PayslipAggregate> findByEmployeeAndPeriod(String t,
                                                                              String empId,
                                                                              YearMonth period) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())
                            && empId.equals(x.getEmployeeId())
                            && period.equals(x.getPayPeriod()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<PayslipAggregate> findByPeriod(String t, YearMonth period) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && period.equals(x.getPayPeriod()))
                    .toList(); }
        @Override public List<PayslipAggregate> findByEmployee(String t, String empId,
                                                                int page, int size) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && empId.equals(x.getEmployeeId()))
                    .skip((long) page * size).limit(size).toList(); }
        @Override public List<PayslipAggregate> findByStatus(String t, PayslipStatus s) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && s == x.getStatus()).toList(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Fixed Asset ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryFixedAssetRepository implements FixedAssetRepository {
        private final Map<String, FixedAssetAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public FixedAssetAggregate save(FixedAssetAggregate x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<FixedAssetAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<FixedAssetAggregate> findByAssetNumber(String num, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && num.equals(x.getAssetNumber()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<FixedAssetAggregate> findActive(String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && x.getStatus() == AssetStatus.ACTIVE)
                    .toList(); }
        @Override public List<FixedAssetAggregate> findByStatus(String t, AssetStatus s) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && s == x.getStatus()).toList(); }
        @Override public List<FixedAssetAggregate> findByDepartment(String t, String deptId) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && deptId.equals(x.getDepartmentId()))
                    .toList(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Budget ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryBudgetRepository implements BudgetRepository {
        private final Map<String, BudgetAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public BudgetAggregate save(BudgetAggregate x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<BudgetAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public List<BudgetAggregate> findByStatus(String t, BudgetStatus s) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && (s == null || s == x.getStatus()))
                    .toList(); }
        @Override public List<BudgetAggregate> findActiveBudgets(String t, LocalDate date) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())
                            && x.getStatus() == BudgetStatus.ACTIVE
                            && !date.isBefore(x.getPeriodStart())
                            && !date.isAfter(x.getPeriodEnd()))
                    .toList(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Promotion ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryPromotionRepository implements PromotionRepository {
        private final Map<String, PromotionAggregate> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public PromotionAggregate save(PromotionAggregate x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<PromotionAggregate> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<PromotionAggregate> findByCode(String code, String t) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && code.equals(x.getCode()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<PromotionAggregate> findActive(String t, LocalDate date) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())
                            && x.getStatus() == PromotionStatus.ACTIVE
                            && (x.getValidFrom() == null || !date.isBefore(x.getValidFrom()))
                            && (x.getValidTo()   == null || !date.isAfter(x.getValidTo())))
                    .toList(); }
        @Override public List<PromotionAggregate> findByStatus(String t, PromotionStatus s) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && (s == null || s == x.getStatus()))
                    .toList(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Notification ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryNotificationRepository implements NotificationRepository {
        private final Map<String, NotificationRecord> store = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String k(String id, String t) { return t + "::" + id; }

        @Override public NotificationRecord save(NotificationRecord x) {
            store.put(k(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<NotificationRecord> findById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(store.get(k(id, t))); }
        @Override public List<NotificationRecord> findUnread(String t, String recipientId) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())
                            && (recipientId == null || recipientId.equals(x.getRecipientId()))
                            && x.getReadAt() == null)
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(NotificationRecord::getCreatedAt).reversed())
                    .toList(); }
        @Override public List<NotificationRecord> findByRecipient(String t, String recipientId,
                                                                    int page, int size) {
            return store.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId())
                            && (recipientId == null || recipientId.equals(x.getRecipientId())))
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparing(NotificationRecord::getCreatedAt).reversed())
                    .skip((long) page * size).limit(size).toList(); }
        @Override public long countUnread(String t, String recipientId) {
            return findUnread(t, recipientId).size(); }
        public void clear() { store.clear(); }
    }

    // ── Catalog ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public static class InMemoryCatalogRepository implements CatalogRepository {
        private final Map<String, CategoryAggregate> categories = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private final Map<String, BrandAggregate>    brands     = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
        private String kc(String id, String t) { return t + "::cat::" + id; }
        private String kb(String id, String t) { return t + "::brd::" + id; }

        @Override public CategoryAggregate saveCategory(CategoryAggregate x) {
            categories.put(kc(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<CategoryAggregate> findCategoryById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(categories.get(kc(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<CategoryAggregate> findCategoryBySlug(String slug, String t) {
            return categories.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && slug.equals(x.getSlug()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<CategoryAggregate> findRootCategories(String t) {
            return categories.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && x.isRoot() && x.isActive())
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(CategoryAggregate::getDisplayOrder)).toList(); }
        @Override public List<CategoryAggregate> findChildCategories(String t, String parentId) {
            return categories.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && parentId.equals(x.getParentId()))
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(CategoryAggregate::getDisplayOrder)).toList(); }
        @Override public List<CategoryAggregate> findAllCategories(String t) {
            return categories.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()))
                    .sorted(Comparator.comparingInt(CategoryAggregate::getDisplayOrder)).toList(); }
        @Override public BrandAggregate saveBrand(BrandAggregate x) {
            brands.put(kb(x.getId(), x.getTenantId()), x); return x; }
        @Override public Optional<BrandAggregate> findBrandById(String id, String t) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(brands.get(kb(id, t))); }
        @Override public Optional<BrandAggregate> findBrandBySlug(String slug, String t) {
            return brands.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && slug.equals(x.getSlug()))
                    .findFirst(); }
        @Override public List<BrandAggregate> findAllBrands(String t) {
            return brands.values().stream()
                    .filter(x -> t.equals(x.getTenantId()) && x.isActive()).toList(); }
        public void clear() { categories.clear(); brands.clear(); }
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.infrastructure;

import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheEntityBase;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * Transactional outbox entity.
 *
 * Every domain event is written to this table inside the same DB transaction
 * as the aggregate state change. A separate relay process reads unpublished
 * entries and publishes them to Kafka/Pub-Sub/SNS.
 *
 * This guarantees at-least-once delivery without distributed transactions.
 *
 * Pattern: https://microservices.io/patterns/data/transactional-outbox.html
 */
@Entity
@Table(name = "domain_event_outbox",
    indexes = {
        @Index(name = "idx_outbox_unpublished",
               columnList = "published_at, aggregate_type, tenant_id")
    })
public class OutboxEventEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {

    @Id
    @Column(name = "id", length = 36, nullable = false, updatable = false)
    public String id;

    /** Aggregate type e.g. "ORDER", "INVOICE", "PAYMENT" */
    @Column(name = "aggregate_type", length = 64, nullable = false)
    public String aggregateType;

    @Column(name = "aggregate_id", length = 36, nullable = false)
    public String aggregateId;

    @Column(name = "tenant_id", length = 64, nullable = false)
    public String tenantId;

    /** Fully-qualified event class name e.g. "com.saas.order.events.OrderEvent$OrderConfirmed" */
    @Column(name = "event_type", length = 256, nullable = false)
    public String eventType;

    /** Serialized event payload */
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "payload", columnDefinition = "jsonb", nullable = false)
    public Map<String, Object> payload;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    public Instant createdAt;

    /** Null = not yet published */
    @Column(name = "published_at")
    public Instant publishedAt;

    @Column(name = "retry_count", nullable = false)
    public int retryCount = 0;

    @Column(name = "last_error", columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    public String lastError;
}

Size: 7.0 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.shared.infrastructure;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEvent;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import io.quarkus.scheduler.Scheduled;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.eclipse.microprofile.config.inject.ConfigProperty;
import org.eclipse.microprofile.reactive.messaging.Channel;
import org.eclipse.microprofile.reactive.messaging.Emitter;
import org.jboss.logging.Logger;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Transactional outbox implementation of DomainEventPublisher.
 *
 * Step 1 (same transaction as aggregate save):
 *   publish() serializes the event to the outbox table.
 *   No Kafka involvement — just a DB write.
 *
 * Step 2 (async relay, every 5 seconds):
 *   relayUnpublished() reads unpublished outbox entries,
 *   publishes to the configured Kafka topic, then marks as published.
 *
 * Benefits:
 *  - Events are never lost even if Kafka is unavailable during the transaction
 *  - Exactly the same transaction boundary as the business state change
 *  - Retry logic for failed Kafka publishes
 *  - Full audit trail of every event ever emitted
 *
 * To switch to direct CDI publishing (for tests/dev):
 *   Set quarkus.arc.selected-alternatives=CdiDomainEventPublisher
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class OutboxEventPublisher
        implements DomainEventPublisher,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<OutboxEventEntity, String> {

    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(OutboxEventPublisher.class);
    private static final int MAX_RETRIES = 5;

    @Inject ObjectMapper mapper;

    @ConfigProperty(name = "syirkah.kafka.domain-events-topic",
                    defaultValue = "syirkah.domain-events")
    String topic;

    // Injected lazily — null-safe if Kafka is not configured
    @Inject
    @Channel("domain-events-out")
    @jakarta.enterprise.inject.Default
    jakarta.enterprise.inject.Instance<Emitter<String>> emitterInstance;

    // ── Step 1: Write to outbox (same TX as aggregate) ────────────────────

    @Override
    @Transactional(Transactional.TxType.MANDATORY)
    public void publish(DomainEvent event) {
        try {
            OutboxEventEntity entry = new OutboxEventEntity();
            entry.id            = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
            entry.aggregateType = resolveAggregateType(event);
            entry.aggregateId   = event.aggregateId();
            entry.tenantId      = event.tenantId();
            entry.eventType     = event.getClass().getName();
            entry.payload       = toMap(event);
            entry.createdAt     = Instant.now();
            entry.publishedAt   = null;
            entry.retryCount    = 0;
            persist(entry);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            // Never break the business transaction — log and continue
            LOG.errorf(e, "Failed to write outbox entry for event %s aggregate=%s",
                    event.eventType(), event.aggregateId());
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void publishAll(List<DomainEvent> events) {
        events.forEach(this::publish);
    }

    // ── Step 2: Relay to Kafka (async, every 5 seconds) ───────────────────

    @Scheduled(every = "5s", delayed = "10s")
    @Transactional
    public void relayUnpublished() {
        List<OutboxEventEntity> pending = find(
                "publishedAt IS NULL AND retryCount < ?1", MAX_RETRIES)
                .page(0, 100)
                .list();

        if (pending.isEmpty()) return;

        LOG.debugf("Relaying %d outbox events to Kafka", pending.size());
        int success = 0, failed = 0;

        for (OutboxEventEntity entry : pending) {
            try {
                String json = mapper.writeValueAsString(Map.of(
                        "id",            entry.id,
                        "aggregateType", entry.aggregateType,
                        "aggregateId",   entry.aggregateId,
                        "tenantId",      entry.tenantId,
                        "eventType",     entry.eventType,
                        "payload",       entry.payload,
                        "createdAt",     entry.createdAt.toString()
                ));

                sendToKafka(json);
                entry.publishedAt = Instant.now();
                entry.lastError   = null;
                getEntityManager().merge(entry);
                success++;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                entry.retryCount++;
                entry.lastError = e.getMessage();
                getEntityManager().merge(entry);
                failed++;
                LOG.warnf("Failed to relay outbox entry %s (attempt %d): %s",
                        entry.id, entry.retryCount, e.getMessage());
            }
        }

        if (success > 0 || failed > 0) {
            LOG.infof("Outbox relay complete: %d sent, %d failed", success, failed);
        }
    }

    // ── Dead-letter query (for monitoring) ────────────────────────────────

    public List<OutboxEventEntity> findDeadLettered() {
        return find("retryCount >= ?1 AND publishedAt IS NULL", MAX_RETRIES).list();
    }

    public long countPending() {
        return count("publishedAt IS NULL AND retryCount < ?1", MAX_RETRIES);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void sendToKafka(String json) {
        if (emitterInstance.isResolvable()) {
            emitterInstance.get().send(json);
        } else {
            // Kafka not configured — log at debug level (expected in dev/test)
            LOG.debugf("Kafka emitter not available, event logged only: %s",
                    json.substring(0, Math.min(120, json.length())));
        }
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private Map<String, Object> toMap(DomainEvent event) {
        try {
            return mapper.convertValue(event, Map.class);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return Map.of(
                    "aggregateId", event.aggregateId(),
                    "tenantId",    event.tenantId(),
                    "actor",       event.actor(),
                    "occurredAt",  event.occurredAt().toString(),
                    "error",       "serialization failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private String resolveAggregateType(DomainEvent event) {
        // Derive from package: com.saas.ORDER.events.* → "ORDER"
        String pkg = event.getClass().getPackageName();
        String[] parts = pkg.split("\\.");
        // parts: ["com", "saas", "<module>", "events"]
        return parts.length >= 3 ? parts[2].toUpperCase() : "UNKNOWN";
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.model;

import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Immutable postal address value object.
 * Used across all modules: order shipping, customer billing, warehouse location, etc.
 */
public final class Address {

    private final String line1;
    private final String line2;
    private final String city;
    private final String stateProvince;
    private final String postalCode;
    private final String countryCode;   // ISO 3166-1 alpha-2, e.g. "ID", "US"
    private final String phone;
    private final String recipientName;

    private Address(Builder b) {
        this.line1          = Objects.requireNonNull(b.line1, "line1");
        this.line2          = b.line2;
        this.city           = Objects.requireNonNull(b.city, "city");
        this.stateProvince  = b.stateProvince;
        this.postalCode     = b.postalCode;
        this.countryCode    = Objects.requireNonNull(b.countryCode, "countryCode");
        this.phone          = b.phone;
        this.recipientName  = b.recipientName;
    }

    public String getLine1()          { return line1; }
    public String getLine2()          { return line2; }
    public String getCity()           { return city; }
    public String getStateProvince()  { return stateProvince; }
    public String getPostalCode()     { return postalCode; }
    public String getCountryCode()    { return countryCode; }
    public String getPhone()          { return phone; }
    public String getRecipientName()  { return recipientName; }

    public String toSingleLine() {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(line1);
        if (line2 != null)         sb.append(", ").append(line2);
        sb.append(", ").append(city);
        if (stateProvince != null) sb.append(", ").append(stateProvince);
        if (postalCode != null)    sb.append(" ").append(postalCode);
        sb.append(", ").append(countryCode);
        return sb.toString();
    }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (this == o) return true;
        if (!(o instanceof Address a)) return false;
        return Objects.equals(line1, a.line1) && Objects.equals(line2, a.line2)
                && Objects.equals(city, a.city) && Objects.equals(countryCode, a.countryCode)
                && Objects.equals(postalCode, a.postalCode);
    }

    @Override public int hashCode() {
        return Objects.hash(line1, line2, city, postalCode, countryCode);
    }

    @Override public String toString() { return toSingleLine(); }

    public static Builder builder() { return new Builder(); }

    public static final class Builder {
        private String line1, line2, city, stateProvince, postalCode, countryCode, phone, recipientName;
        public Builder line1(String v)         { this.line1 = v; return this; }
        public Builder line2(String v)         { this.line2 = v; return this; }
        public Builder city(String v)          { this.city = v; return this; }
        public Builder stateProvince(String v) { this.stateProvince = v; return this; }
        public Builder postalCode(String v)    { this.postalCode = v; return this; }
        public Builder countryCode(String v)   { this.countryCode = v; return this; }
        public Builder phone(String v)         { this.phone = v; return this; }
        public Builder recipientName(String v) { this.recipientName = v; return this; }
        public Address build() { return new Address(this); }
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.model;

import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.UUID;

/**
 * Canonical typed aggregate ID.
 *
 * Replaces the brittle reflection hack (getDeclaredField / setAccessible)
 * previously used to assign final ID fields in aggregate factories.
 *
 * All aggregates that previously used reflection now use this class,
 * storing the id as an AggregateId value object (not a String literal).
 *
 * The switch to a mutable-at-construction string field pattern is the
 * cleanest Java 21 solution without requiring Lombok or record-based
 * aggregates (which would prevent command methods).
 */
public final class AggregateId {

    private final String value;

    private AggregateId(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank())
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("AggregateId cannot be blank");
        this.value = value;
    }

    public static AggregateId generate() {
        return new AggregateId(UUID.randomUUID().toString());
    }

    public static AggregateId of(String value) {
        return new AggregateId(value);
    }

    public String getValue() { return value; }

    @Override
    public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (!(o instanceof AggregateId a)) return false;
        return Objects.equals(value, a.value);
    }

    @Override public int hashCode() { return Objects.hash(value); }
    @Override public String toString() { return value; }
}

package com.saas.shared.model;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Reusable audit trail embedded in all aggregates.
 */
public final class AuditMeta {

    private final Instant createdAt;
    private final Instant updatedAt;
    private final String  createdBy;
    private final String  updatedBy;
    private final long    version;

    private AuditMeta(Instant createdAt, Instant updatedAt,
                      String createdBy, String updatedBy, long version) {
        this.createdAt = createdAt;
        this.updatedAt = updatedAt;
        this.createdBy = createdBy;
        this.updatedBy = updatedBy;
        this.version   = version;
    }

    public static AuditMeta create(String actor) {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        return new AuditMeta(now, now, actor, actor, 1L);
    }

    /** Reconstitute from persistence layer — all fields explicit. */
    public static AuditMeta reconstitute(Instant createdAt, Instant updatedAt,
                                          String createdBy, String updatedBy,
                                          long version) {
        return new AuditMeta(
                createdAt  != null ? createdAt  : Instant.now(),
                updatedAt  != null ? updatedAt  : Instant.now(),
                createdBy  != null ? createdBy  : "system",
                updatedBy  != null ? updatedBy  : "system",
                version    > 0     ? version    : 1L);
    }

    public AuditMeta touch(String actor) {
        return new AuditMeta(createdAt, Instant.now(), createdBy, actor, version + 1);
    }

    public Instant getCreatedAt() { return createdAt; }
    public Instant getUpdatedAt() { return updatedAt; }
    public String  getCreatedBy() { return createdBy; }
    public String  getUpdatedBy() { return updatedBy; }
    public long    getVersion()   { return version; }
}

package com.saas.shared.model;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.util.Currency;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Canonical Money value object shared across all ERP modules.
 *
 * If using as a multi-module Maven project, this lives in
 * the `saas-shared` module and all other modules depend on it.
 * Product module should also migrate to this class.
 */
public final class Money {

    private final BigDecimal amount;
    private final Currency   currency;

    private Money(BigDecimal amount, Currency currency) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(amount, "amount");
        Objects.requireNonNull(currency, "currency");
        if (amount.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) < 0)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Money cannot be negative: " + amount);
        this.amount   = amount.setScale(currency.getDefaultFractionDigits(), RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        this.currency = currency;
    }

    public static Money of(BigDecimal amount, String currencyCode) {
        return new Money(amount, Currency.getInstance(currencyCode));
    }
    public static Money of(long amount, String currencyCode) {
        return of(BigDecimal.valueOf(amount), currencyCode);
    }
    public static Money zero(String currencyCode) {
        return of(BigDecimal.ZERO, currencyCode);
    }

    public Money add(Money o)            { assertSame(o); return new Money(amount.add(o.amount), currency); }
    public Money subtract(Money o)       {
        assertSame(o);
        BigDecimal r = amount.subtract(o.amount);
        if (r.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) < 0) throw new IllegalArgumentException("Negative result");
        return new Money(r, currency);
    }
    public Money multiply(BigDecimal f)  { return new Money(amount.multiply(f), currency); }
    public Money multiply(int f)         { return multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(f)); }
    public Money negate()                { return new Money(BigDecimal.ZERO, currency).add(this); }  // stays non-negative
    public Money percentage(BigDecimal pct) {
        return multiply(pct.divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), 10, RoundingMode.HALF_UP));
    }

    public boolean isZero()              { return amount.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) == 0; }
    public boolean isGreaterThan(Money o){ assertSame(o); return amount.compareTo(o.amount) > 0; }
    public boolean isGreaterOrEqual(Money o){ assertSame(o); return amount.compareTo(o.amount) >= 0; }

    private void assertSame(Money o) {
        if (!currency.equals(o.currency))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Currency mismatch: " + currency + " vs " + o.currency);
    }

    public BigDecimal getAmount()        { return amount; }
    public Currency   getCurrency()      { return currency; }
    public String     getCurrencyCode()  { return currency.getCurrencyCode(); }

    @Override public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (!(o instanceof Money m)) return false;
        return Objects.equals(amount, m.amount) && Objects.equals(currency, m.currency);
    }
    @Override public int hashCode() { return Objects.hash(amount, currency); }
    @Override public String toString() { return currency.getCurrencyCode() + " " + amount.toPlainString(); }
}

package com.saas.shared.model;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Objects;

// ── Party ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

/**
 * A named party reference used on orders, invoices, etc.
 * Points to a Customer or Supplier by ID — no embedded data to avoid denormalization.
 */
public final class Party {

    public enum PartyType { CUSTOMER, SUPPLIER, INTERNAL, ANONYMOUS }

    private final String id;
    private final PartyType type;
    private final String name;           // snapshot at time of document creation
    private final String taxId;          // NPWP, VAT number, etc.

    public Party(String id, PartyType type, String name, String taxId) {
        this.id     = Objects.requireNonNull(id, "id");
        this.type   = Objects.requireNonNull(type, "type");
        this.name   = Objects.requireNonNull(name, "name");
        this.taxId  = taxId;
    }

    public static Party customer(String id, String name, String taxId) {
        return new Party(id, PartyType.CUSTOMER, name, taxId);
    }

    public static Party supplier(String id, String name, String taxId) {
        return new Party(id, PartyType.SUPPLIER, name, taxId);
    }

    public String getId()       { return id; }
    public PartyType getType()  { return type; }
    public String getName()     { return name; }
    public String getTaxId()    { return taxId; }

    @Override public boolean equals(Object o) {
        if (!(o instanceof Party p)) return false;
        return Objects.equals(id, p.id) && type == p.type;
    }
    @Override public int hashCode() { return Objects.hash(id, type); }
    @Override public String toString() { return type + ":" + id + "(" + name + ")"; }
}

package com.saas.shared.security;

/**
 * ERP role constants for @RolesAllowed annotations.
 *
 * JWT claim "groups" carries the assigned roles.
 * A user may have multiple roles.
 *
 * Role hierarchy (higher roles include lower):
 *   ADMIN > {FINANCE, SALES, PURCHASING, WAREHOUSE} > READONLY
 *
 * Example JWT payload:
 *   { "sub": "user-123", "groups": ["SALES", "READONLY"], "tenant_id": "acme" }
 */
public final class ErpRoles {

    private ErpRoles() {}

    /** Full administrative access across all ERP modules. */
    public static final String ADMIN       = "ADMIN";

    /** Create/manage invoices, payments, journal entries, trial balance. */
    public static final String FINANCE     = "FINANCE";

    /** Create/manage orders and customers. */
    public static final String SALES       = "SALES";

    /** Create/manage purchase orders and suppliers. */
    public static final String PURCHASING  = "PURCHASING";

    /** Manage inventory, warehouses, shipments. */
    public static final String WAREHOUSE   = "WAREHOUSE";

    /** Read-only access to all GET endpoints. */
    public static final String READONLY    = "READONLY";

    // Composite role sets for @RolesAllowed
    public static final String[] FINANCE_OR_ADMIN    = {FINANCE, ADMIN};
    public static final String[] SALES_OR_ADMIN      = {SALES, ADMIN};
    public static final String[] PURCHASE_OR_ADMIN   = {PURCHASING, ADMIN};
    public static final String[] WAREHOUSE_OR_ADMIN  = {WAREHOUSE, ADMIN};
    public static final String[] ANY_AUTHENTICATED   = {ADMIN, FINANCE, SALES, PURCHASING, WAREHOUSE, READONLY};
}
