package com.saas.shared.security;

import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.annotation.security.RolesAllowed;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.ws.rs.Priorities;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ResourceInfo;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Context;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;
import org.eclipse.microprofile.config.inject.ConfigProperty;
import org.eclipse.microprofile.jwt.JsonWebToken;
import org.jboss.logging.Logger;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Set;

/**
 * JWT authentication and role-based access control filter.
 *
 * Roles used across the ERP:
 *  - ADMIN       : full access to all modules
 *  - FINANCE     : invoices, payments, accounting
 *  - SALES       : orders, customers
 *  - PURCHASING  : purchase orders, suppliers
 *  - WAREHOUSE   : inventory, stock movements, shipments
 *  - READONLY    : GET endpoints only
 *
 * Configuration:
 *   syirkah.security.enabled=true   (set false in dev/test)
 *   mp.jwt.verify.publickey.location=META-INF/resources/publicKey.pem
 *   mp.jwt.verify.issuer=https://auth.saas.example.com
 */
@Provider
@Priority(Priorities.AUTHENTICATION)
public class JwtSecurityFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(JwtSecurityFilter.class);

    // Paths that do not require authentication
    private static final Set<String> PUBLIC_PATHS = Set.of(
            "/health", "/health/live", "/health/ready",
            "/openapi", "/swagger-ui", "/metrics"
    );

    @Inject
    JsonWebToken jwt;

    @Context
    ResourceInfo resourceInfo;

    @ConfigProperty(name = "syirkah.security.enabled", defaultValue = "true")
    boolean securityEnabled;

    @Override
    public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) throws IOException {
        if (!securityEnabled) return;

        String path = ctx.getUriInfo().getPath();
        if (PUBLIC_PATHS.stream().anyMatch(path::startsWith)) return;

        // 1. Verify token is present and valid
        if (jwt == null || jwt.getRawToken() == null) {
            abort(ctx, 401, "UNAUTHORIZED", "Valid JWT required");
            return;
        }

        // 2. Check expiry
        if (jwt.getExpirationTime() < System.currentTimeMillis() / 1000) {
            abort(ctx, 401, "TOKEN_EXPIRED", "JWT token has expired");
            return;
        }

        // 3. Check @RolesAllowed on method or class
        if (resourceInfo != null) {
            RolesAllowed methodRoles = getAnnotation(
                    resourceInfo.getResourceMethod(),
                    resourceInfo.getResourceClass());

            if (methodRoles != null) {
                Set<String> allowed = Set.of(methodRoles.value());
                Set<String> userRoles = jwt.getGroups();

                boolean hasRole = allowed.contains("*")                    // any role
                        || userRoles.stream().anyMatch(allowed::contains); // role match

                if (!hasRole) {
                    LOG.warnf("Access denied: user=%s roles=%s required=%s path=%s",
                            jwt.getSubject(), userRoles, allowed, path);
                    abort(ctx, 403, "FORBIDDEN",
                            "Insufficient privileges. Required: " + allowed);
                    return;
                }
            }
        }

        LOG.debugf("Auth OK: user=%s path=%s", jwt.getSubject(), path);
    }

    private RolesAllowed getAnnotation(Method method, Class<?> clazz) {
        if (method != null && method.isAnnotationPresent(RolesAllowed.class))
            return method.getAnnotation(RolesAllowed.class);
        if (clazz != null && clazz.isAnnotationPresent(RolesAllowed.class))
            return clazz.getAnnotation(RolesAllowed.class);
        return null;
    }

    private void abort(ContainerRequestContext ctx, int status, String code, String msg) {
        ctx.abortWith(Response.status(status)
                .entity("{\"code\":\"" + code + "\",\"message\":\"" + msg + "\"}")
                .build());
    }
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/shared/security/TenantContext.java
Size: 5.0 KB | Modified: 2026-04-04 15:58:48

package com.saas.shared.security;

import jakarta.annotation.Priority;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerRequestFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseContext;
import jakarta.ws.rs.container.ContainerResponseFilter;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.Response;
import jakarta.ws.rs.ext.Provider;
import org.jboss.logging.Logger;

import java.io.IOException;

/**
 * Multi-tenant context carrier using thread-local storage.
 *
 * Every inbound request sets the tenant for the duration of that thread.
 * The tenant is resolved from (in priority order):
 *   1. X-Tenant-Id header (explicit override — for internal service calls)
 *   2. JWT claim "tenant_id" (from authenticated user token)
 *   3. Subdomain extraction from Host header (e.g. acme.saas.example.com → "acme")
 *
 * Usage in service code:
 *   String tenantId = TenantContext.current();   // never null after filter
 *
 * Thread safety: InheritableThreadLocal propagates to child threads
 * (used by Quarkus virtual thread executor).
 */
public final class TenantContext {

    private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(TenantContext.class);
    private static final String UNKNOWN_TENANT = "default";

    private static final InheritableThreadLocal<String> TENANT =
            new InheritableThreadLocal<>();

    private TenantContext() {}

    /** Returns the current tenant ID. Never null — falls back to "default". */
    public static String current() {
        String t = TENANT.get();
        return t != null ? t : UNKNOWN_TENANT;
    }

    /** Set from JAX-RS filter — not for application code. */
    static void set(String tenantId) {
        TENANT.set(tenantId != null && !tenantId.isBlank() ? tenantId : UNKNOWN_TENANT);
    }

    /** Clear at end of request — prevents thread pool leakage. */
    static void clear() {
        TENANT.remove();
    }

    // ── JAX-RS Request filter ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Provider
    @Priority(1)   // runs before all other filters
    public static class TenantRequestFilter implements ContainerRequestFilter {

        @Override
        public void filter(ContainerRequestContext ctx) throws IOException {
            String tenantId = resolveFromRequest(ctx);

            if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()) {
                // Reject requests with no tenant identity on secured endpoints
                // Allow health/openapi endpoints through
                String path = ctx.getUriInfo().getPath();
                if (!path.startsWith("/health") && !path.startsWith("/openapi")
                        && !path.startsWith("/swagger")) {
                    LOG.warnf("Request rejected — no tenant identity: %s %s",
                            ctx.getMethod(), path);
                    ctx.abortWith(Response.status(400)
                            .entity("{\"code\":\"MISSING_TENANT\",\"message\":\"X-Tenant-Id header or JWT tenant claim required\"}")
                            .build());
                    return;
                }
            }

            TenantContext.set(tenantId);
            LOG.debugf("Tenant context set: %s for %s %s",
                    tenantId, ctx.getMethod(), ctx.getUriInfo().getPath());
        }

        private String resolveFromRequest(ContainerRequestContext ctx) {
            // Priority 1: explicit header
            String header = ctx.getHeaderString("X-Tenant-Id");
            if (header != null && !header.isBlank()) return header.trim();

            // Priority 2: JWT claim (set by Quarkus SmallRye JWT)
            Object principal = ctx.getSecurityContext().getUserPrincipal();
            if (principal instanceof org.eclipse.microprofile.jwt.JsonWebToken jwt) {
                String claim = jwt.getClaim("tenant_id");
                if (claim != null && !claim.isBlank()) return claim.trim();
            }

            // Priority 3: subdomain from Host header
            String host = ctx.getHeaderString("Host");
            if (host != null && host.contains(".")) {
                String subdomain = host.split("\\.")[0];
                // Only use if it looks like a tenant slug (not "www", "api", etc.)
                if (!subdomain.equals("www") && !subdomain.equals("api")
                        && !subdomain.equals("localhost")) {
                    return subdomain;
                }
            }

            return null;
        }
    }

    // ── JAX-RS Response filter ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Provider
    @Priority(Integer.MAX_VALUE)   // runs last — after response is built
    public static class TenantCleanupFilter implements ContainerResponseFilter {

        @Override
        public void filter(ContainerRequestContext req,
                            ContainerResponseContext resp) throws IOException {
            TenantContext.clear();
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.telemetry;

import io.opentelemetry.api.GlobalOpenTelemetry;
import io.opentelemetry.api.trace.Span;
import io.opentelemetry.api.trace.StatusCode;
import io.opentelemetry.api.trace.Tracer;
import io.opentelemetry.context.Scope;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.interceptor.AroundInvoke;
import jakarta.interceptor.Interceptor;
import jakarta.interceptor.InterceptorBinding;
import jakarta.interceptor.InvocationContext;
import org.eclipse.microprofile.config.inject.ConfigProperty;
import org.jboss.logging.Logger;

import java.lang.annotation.*;

/**
 * OpenTelemetry tracing interceptor for ERP service methods.
 *
 * Usage — annotate any service method:
 *   @Traced("order.confirm")
 *   public OrderAggregate confirmOrder(...) { ... }
 *
 * Each call creates a child span under the current trace context.
 * Span attributes include: tenant.id, module, operation.
 * Exceptions are recorded as span errors.
 *
 * Configuration:
 *   quarkus.otel.exporter.otlp.traces.endpoint=http://otel-collector:4317
 *   quarkus.otel.sdk.disabled=false
 */
public class TracingSupport {

    // ── Annotation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @InterceptorBinding
    @Target({ElementType.METHOD, ElementType.TYPE})
    @Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
    public @interface Traced {
        /** Span name. Defaults to ClassName.methodName if blank. */
        String value() default "";
    }

    // ── Interceptor ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Traced
    @Interceptor
    @ApplicationScoped
    public static class TracingInterceptor {

        private static final Logger LOG = Logger.getLogger(TracingInterceptor.class);

        @ConfigProperty(name = "quarkus.application.name", defaultValue = "syirkah-modules")
        String appName;

        @AroundInvoke
        public Object trace(InvocationContext ctx) throws Exception {
            Traced annotation = resolveAnnotation(ctx);
            String spanName   = annotation != null && !annotation.value().isBlank()
                    ? annotation.value()
                    : ctx.getTarget().getClass().getSimpleName() + "." + ctx.getMethod().getName();

            Tracer tracer = GlobalOpenTelemetry.getTracer(appName);
            Span span = tracer.spanBuilder(spanName)
                    .setAttribute("syirkah.module",    resolveModule(ctx))
                    .setAttribute("syirkah.operation", ctx.getMethod().getName())
                    .startSpan();

            try (Scope ignored = span.makeCurrent()) {
                Object result = ctx.proceed();
                span.setStatus(StatusCode.OK);
                return result;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                span.setStatus(StatusCode.ERROR, e.getMessage());
                span.recordException(e);
                throw e;
            } finally {
                span.end();
            }
        }

        private Traced resolveAnnotation(InvocationContext ctx) {
            Traced m = ctx.getMethod().getAnnotation(Traced.class);
            if (m != null) return m;
            return ctx.getTarget().getClass().getAnnotation(Traced.class);
        }

        private String resolveModule(InvocationContext ctx) {
            // com.saas.order.service.OrderService → "order"
            String pkg = ctx.getTarget().getClass().getPackageName();
            String[] parts = pkg.split("\\.");
            return parts.length >= 3 ? parts[2] : "unknown";
        }
    }

    // ── Span utility helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    /** Add tenant context to the current active span (call from service layer). */
    public static void addTenantAttribute(String tenantId) {
        Span current = Span.current();
        if (current != null) {
            current.setAttribute("tenant.id", tenantId);
        }
    }

    /** Record an aggregate ID on the current span. */
    public static void addAggregateId(String aggregateType, String id) {
        Span current = Span.current();
        if (current != null) {
            current.setAttribute(aggregateType + ".id", id);
        }
    }

    /** Mark current span as a business error (e.g. validation failure). */
    public static void markBusinessError(String reason) {
        Span current = Span.current();
        if (current != null) {
            current.setAttribute("syirkah.error.reason", reason);
            current.setStatus(StatusCode.ERROR, reason);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.shipment.api.rest;

import com.saas.shipment.core.ShipmentAggregate;
import com.saas.shipment.service.ShipmentService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Shipment REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/shipments                      → create shipment
 * POST   /api/v1/shipments/{id}/lines           → add line
 * POST   /api/v1/shipments/{id}/pack            → PENDING → PACKED
 * POST   /api/v1/shipments/{id}/dispatch        → PACKED → DISPATCHED
 * POST   /api/v1/shipments/{id}/deliver         → mark DELIVERED
 * POST   /api/v1/shipments/{id}/cancel          → cancel
 * GET    /api/v1/shipments/{id}                 → get
 * GET    /api/v1/shipments/order/{orderId}      → all shipments for order
 */
@Path("/api/v1/shipments")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class ShipmentResource {

    @Inject ShipmentService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateShipmentRequest req) {
        Address dest = Address.builder()
                .line1(req.addressLine1()).line2(req.addressLine2())
                .city(req.city()).postalCode(req.postalCode())
                .countryCode(req.countryCode()).build();
        ShipmentAggregate s = service.createShipment(
                tid(rawTid), req.orderId(), req.warehouseId(),
                dest, req.carrierCode(), req.serviceLevel(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/lines")
    public Response addLine(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid AddLineRequest req) {
        ShipmentAggregate s = service.addLine(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.orderLineId(),
                req.productId(), req.sku(), req.qty(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/pack")
    public Response pack(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        ShipmentAggregate s = service.packShipment(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/dispatch")
    public Response dispatch(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid DispatchRequest req) {
        Instant eta = req.estimatedDelivery() != null
                ? Instant.parse(req.estimatedDelivery()) : null;
        ShipmentAggregate s = service.dispatchShipment(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.trackingNumber(), eta, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/deliver")
    public Response deliver(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        ShipmentAggregate s = service.markDelivered(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/cancel")
    public Response cancel(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        ShipmentAggregate s = service.cancelShipment(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(s -> Response.ok(toResp(s)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/order/{orderId}")
    public Response getByOrder(
            @PathParam("orderId") String orderId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<ShipmentResponse> items = service.findByOrder(tid(rawTid), orderId)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private ShipmentResponse toResp(ShipmentAggregate s) {
        return new ShipmentResponse(
                s.getId(), s.getTenantId(), s.getShipmentNumber(),
                s.getOrderId(), s.getWarehouseId(), s.getStatus().name(),
                s.getCarrierCode(), s.getTrackingNumber(), s.getServiceLevel(),
                s.getLines().size(),
                s.getDispatchedAt() != null ? s.getDispatchedAt().toString() : null,
                s.getDeliveredAt() != null ? s.getDeliveredAt().toString() : null,
                s.getEstimatedDelivery() != null ? s.getEstimatedDelivery().toString() : null);
    }

    // ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record CreateShipmentRequest(
            @NotBlank String orderId, @NotBlank String warehouseId,
            @NotBlank String addressLine1, String addressLine2,
            @NotBlank String city, String postalCode, @NotBlank String countryCode,
            @NotBlank String carrierCode, @NotBlank String serviceLevel) {}

    public record AddLineRequest(
            @NotBlank String orderLineId, @NotBlank String productId,
            @NotBlank String sku, @Positive int qty) {}

    public record DispatchRequest(
            @NotBlank String trackingNumber,
            String estimatedDelivery) {}

    public record ShipmentResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String shipmentNumber,
            String orderId, String warehouseId, String status,
            String carrier, String trackingNumber, String serviceLevel,
            int lineCount, String dispatchedAt, String deliveredAt,
            String estimatedDelivery) {}
}

package com.saas.shipment.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Shipment aggregate root.
 *
 * A shipment represents physical delivery of goods to a customer.
 * One order may produce multiple shipments (split fulfillment).
 * One shipment may cover one or more order lines (partial or full).
 *
 * Lifecycle:
 *   PENDING → PACKED → DISPATCHED → IN_TRANSIT → DELIVERED
 *           ↘ CANCELLED
 *
 * On DISPATCHED:
 *   - Inventory dispatch movement written (via event/direct call)
 *   - Order line fulfillment recorded (via event/direct call)
 */
public class ShipmentAggregate {

    private String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private       String shipmentNumber;
    private       String orderId;
    private       String warehouseId;
    private       Address destinationAddress;
    private       ShipmentStatus status;
    private       String carrierCode;       // "JNE", "TIKI", "SICEPAT", "DHL"
    private       String trackingNumber;
    private       String serviceLevel;      // "REGULAR", "EXPRESS", "SAME_DAY"
    private final List<ShipmentLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();
    private       Instant packedAt;
    private       Instant dispatchedAt;
    private       Instant deliveredAt;
    private       Instant estimatedDelivery;
    private       String notes;
    private       AuditMeta audit;

    public static ShipmentAggregate create(String tenantId, String orderId,
                                            String warehouseId, Address destination,
                                            String carrierCode, String serviceLevel,
                                            String actor) {
        var s = new ShipmentAggregate();
        s.id = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        s.tenantId           = tenantId;
        s.orderId            = orderId;
        s.warehouseId        = warehouseId;
        s.destinationAddress = destination;
        s.status             = ShipmentStatus.PENDING;
        s.carrierCode        = carrierCode;
        s.serviceLevel       = serviceLevel;
        s.audit              = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return s;
    }

    private ShipmentAggregate() {}

    // ── Line commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public ShipmentLine addLine(String orderLineId, String productId,
                                 String sku, int qty, String actor) {
        assertStatus(ShipmentStatus.PENDING, "add lines");
        ShipmentLine line = new ShipmentLine(UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                orderLineId, productId, sku, qty);
        lines.add(line);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return line;
    }

    // ── Lifecycle commands ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void pack(String shipmentNumber, String actor) {
        assertStatus(ShipmentStatus.PENDING, "pack");
        if (lines.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot pack empty shipment");
        this.shipmentNumber = shipmentNumber;
        this.status         = ShipmentStatus.PACKED;
        this.packedAt       = Instant.now();
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void dispatch(String trackingNumber, Instant estimatedDelivery, String actor) {
        assertStatus(ShipmentStatus.PACKED, "dispatch");
        this.trackingNumber   = trackingNumber;
        this.estimatedDelivery = estimatedDelivery;
        this.status           = ShipmentStatus.DISPATCHED;
        this.dispatchedAt     = Instant.now();
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void markInTransit(String actor) {
        if (status != ShipmentStatus.DISPATCHED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Shipment must be DISPATCHED to mark IN_TRANSIT");
        this.status = ShipmentStatus.IN_TRANSIT;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void markDelivered(String actor) {
        if (status != ShipmentStatus.IN_TRANSIT && status != ShipmentStatus.DISPATCHED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot mark delivered from status: " + status);
        this.status      = ShipmentStatus.DELIVERED;
        this.deliveredAt = Instant.now();
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void cancel(String actor) {
        if (status == ShipmentStatus.DELIVERED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot cancel a delivered shipment");
        this.status = ShipmentStatus.CANCELLED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    private void assertStatus(ShipmentStatus required, String operation) {
        if (status != required)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot " + operation + " — status is " + status
                    + ", required: " + required);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                      { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()                { return tenantId; }
    public String getShipmentNumber()          { return shipmentNumber; }
    public String getOrderId()                 { return orderId; }
    public String getWarehouseId()             { return warehouseId; }
    public Address getDestinationAddress()     { return destinationAddress; }
    public ShipmentStatus getStatus()          { return status; }
    public String getCarrierCode()             { return carrierCode; }
    public String getTrackingNumber()          { return trackingNumber; }
    public String getServiceLevel()            { return serviceLevel; }
    public List<ShipmentLine> getLines()       { return Collections.unmodifiableList(lines); }
    public Instant getPackedAt()               { return packedAt; }
    public Instant getDispatchedAt()           { return dispatchedAt; }
    public Instant getDeliveredAt()            { return deliveredAt; }
    public Instant getEstimatedDelivery()      { return estimatedDelivery; }
    public String getNotes()                   { return notes; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()                { return audit; }

    public enum ShipmentStatus {
        PENDING, PACKED, DISPATCHED, IN_TRANSIT, DELIVERED, CANCELLED
    }

    public record ShipmentLine(
            String id,
            String orderLineId,   // links back to OrderLine
            String productId,
            String sku,
            int    qty) {}
}

package com.saas.shipment.events;

import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEvent;
import java.time.Instant;

public sealed interface ShipmentEvent extends DomainEvent
        permits ShipmentEvent.ShipmentCreated,
                ShipmentEvent.ShipmentPacked,
                ShipmentEvent.ShipmentDispatched,
                ShipmentEvent.ShipmentDelivered,
                ShipmentEvent.ShipmentCancelled {

    record ShipmentCreated(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String orderId,
            String warehouseId, String carrier,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements ShipmentEvent {}

    record ShipmentPacked(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String shipmentNumber,
            int lineCount, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements ShipmentEvent {}

    record ShipmentDispatched(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String shipmentNumber,
            String trackingNumber, String carrier, String orderId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements ShipmentEvent {}

    record ShipmentDelivered(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId, String shipmentNumber,
            String orderId, String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements ShipmentEvent {}

    record ShipmentCancelled(
            String aggregateId, String tenantId,
            String actor, Instant occurredAt) implements ShipmentEvent {}
}

Size: 6.7 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.shipment.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shipment.core.ShipmentAggregate;
import com.saas.shipment.core.ShipmentAggregate.ShipmentLine;
import com.saas.shipment.core.ShipmentAggregate.ShipmentStatus;
import com.saas.shipment.infrastructure.entity.ShipmentEntity;
import com.saas.shipment.infrastructure.entity.ShipmentLineEntity;
import com.saas.shipment.spi.ShipmentRepository;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Sort;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanacheShipmentRepository
        implements ShipmentRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<ShipmentEntity, String> {

    @Override @Transactional
    public ShipmentAggregate save(ShipmentAggregate s) {
        ShipmentEntity e = toEntity(s);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) getEntityManager().merge(e);
        else persist(e);
        saveLines(s);
        return s;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<ShipmentAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id=?1 AND tenantId=?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<ShipmentAggregate> findByNumber(String number, String tenantId) {
        return find("shipmentNumber=?1 AND tenantId=?2", number, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional()
                .map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id)));
    }

    @Override
    public List<ShipmentAggregate> findByOrder(String tenantId, String orderId) {
        return find("tenantId=?1 AND orderId=?2",
                Sort.descending("createdAt"), tenantId, orderId)
                .list().stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<ShipmentAggregate> findByStatus(String tenantId,
                                                  ShipmentStatus status,
                                                  int page, int size) {
        return find("tenantId=?1 AND status=?2", tenantId, status.name())
                .page(page, size).list()
                .stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public List<ShipmentAggregate> findByTracking(String tenantId, String tracking) {
        return find("tenantId=?1 AND trackingNumber=?2", tenantId, tracking)
                .list().stream().map(e -> toDomain(e, loadLines(e.id))).toList();
    }

    // ── Lines ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private List<ShipmentLineEntity> loadLines(String shipmentId) {
        return getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("SELECT l FROM ShipmentLineEntity l WHERE l.shipmentId=:id",
                        ShipmentLineEntity.class)
                .setParameter("id", shipmentId).getResultList();
    }

    @Transactional
    void saveLines(ShipmentAggregate s) {
        getEntityManager()
                .createQuery("DELETE FROM ShipmentLineEntity l WHERE l.shipmentId=:id")
                .setParameter("id", s.getId()).executeUpdate();
        for (ShipmentLine line : s.getLines()) {
            ShipmentLineEntity le = new ShipmentLineEntity();
            le.id          = line.id();
            le.shipmentId  = s.getId();
            le.orderLineId = line.orderLineId();
            le.productId   = line.productId();
            le.sku         = line.sku();
            le.qty         = line.qty();
            getEntityManager().persist(le);
        }
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private ShipmentAggregate toDomain(ShipmentEntity e,
                                        List<ShipmentLineEntity> lines) {
        AuditMeta audit = AuditMeta.reconstitute(
                e.createdAt, e.updatedAt, e.createdBy, e.updatedBy, e.version);

        Address dest = e.destinationAddress != null ? Address.builder()
                .line1(strOf(e.destinationAddress, "line1"))
                .city(strOf(e.destinationAddress, "city"))
                .countryCode(strOf(e.destinationAddress, "countryCode", "ID"))
                .build() : null;

        ShipmentAggregate s = ShipmentAggregate.create(
                e.tenantId, e.orderId, e.warehouseId, dest,
                e.carrierCode, e.serviceLevel,
                e.createdBy != null ? e.createdBy : "system");

        for (ShipmentLineEntity le : lines) {
            s.addLine(le.orderLineId, le.productId, le.sku, le.qty, "system");
        }
        return s;
    }

    private ShipmentEntity toEntity(ShipmentAggregate s) {
        ShipmentEntity e    = new ShipmentEntity();
        e.id                = s.getId();
        e.tenantId          = s.getTenantId();
        e.shipmentNumber    = s.getShipmentNumber();
        e.orderId           = s.getOrderId();
        e.warehouseId       = s.getWarehouseId();
        e.status            = s.getStatus().name();
        e.carrierCode       = s.getCarrierCode();
        e.trackingNumber    = s.getTrackingNumber();
        e.serviceLevel      = s.getServiceLevel();
        e.estimatedDelivery = s.getEstimatedDelivery();
        e.packedAt          = s.getPackedAt();
        e.dispatchedAt      = s.getDispatchedAt();
        e.deliveredAt       = s.getDeliveredAt();
        e.notes             = s.getNotes();
        e.version           = s.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt         = s.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt         = s.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy         = s.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy         = s.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        if (s.getDestinationAddress() != null) {
            Address a = s.getDestinationAddress();
            e.destinationAddress = java.util.Map.of(
                    "line1", a.getLine1() != null ? a.getLine1() : "",
                    "city",  a.getCity()  != null ? a.getCity()  : "",
                    "countryCode", a.getCountryCode() != null ? a.getCountryCode() : "ID");
        }
        return e;
    }

    private String strOf(java.util.Map<String,Object> m, String key) {
        return strOf(m, key, "");
    }
    private String strOf(java.util.Map<String,Object> m, String key, String def) {
        Object v = m.get(key);
        return v != null ? v.toString() : def;
    }
}

syirkah-modules/src/main/java/com/saas/shipment/service/ShipmentService.java
Size: 7.4 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
package com.saas.shipment.service;

import com.saas.inventory.service.InventoryService;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderId;
import com.saas.order.service.OrderService;
import com.saas.shipment.core.ShipmentAggregate;
import com.saas.shipment.core.ShipmentAggregate.ShipmentLine;
import com.saas.shipment.events.ShipmentEvent;
import com.saas.shipment.spi.ShipmentRepository;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

/**
 * Shipment application service.
 *
 * On dispatchShipment():
 *   1. Shipment marked as DISPATCHED
 *   2. Inventory dispatch movement written per line (DR: reduces onHand & reserved)
 *   3. Order fulfillment recorded per line
 *   4. ShipmentDispatched event published
 *
 * All in one @Transactional — atomicity guaranteed.
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class ShipmentService {

    @Inject ShipmentRepository   repository;
    @Inject InventoryService     inventoryService;
    @Inject OrderService         orderService;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    private static final AtomicLong COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    // ── Create ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public ShipmentAggregate createShipment(String tenantId, String orderId,
                                             String warehouseId, Address destination,
                                             String carrierCode, String serviceLevel,
                                             String actor) {
        ShipmentAggregate shipment = ShipmentAggregate.create(
                tenantId, orderId, warehouseId, destination,
                carrierCode, serviceLevel, actor);
        repository.save(shipment);

        publisher.publish(new ShipmentEvent.ShipmentCreated(
                shipment.getId(), tenantId, orderId,
                warehouseId, carrierCode, actor, Instant.now()));
        return shipment;
    }

    @Transactional
    public ShipmentAggregate addLine(String id, String tenantId,
                                      String orderLineId, String productId,
                                      String sku, int qty, String actor) {
        ShipmentAggregate shipment = load(id, tenantId);
        shipment.addLine(orderLineId, productId, sku, qty, actor);
        return repository.save(shipment);
    }

    // ── Pack ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public ShipmentAggregate packShipment(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        ShipmentAggregate shipment = load(id, tenantId);
        String number = generateShipmentNumber(tenantId);
        shipment.pack(number, actor);
        repository.save(shipment);

        publisher.publish(new ShipmentEvent.ShipmentPacked(
                id, tenantId, number, shipment.getLines().size(),
                actor, Instant.now()));
        return shipment;
    }

    // ── Dispatch ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public ShipmentAggregate dispatchShipment(String id, String tenantId,
                                               String trackingNumber,
                                               Instant estimatedDelivery,
                                               String actor) {
        ShipmentAggregate shipment = load(id, tenantId);
        shipment.dispatch(trackingNumber, estimatedDelivery, actor);
        repository.save(shipment);

        // Step 2: Write inventory dispatch for each shipment line
        for (ShipmentLine line : shipment.getLines()) {
            inventoryService.dispatchStock(
                    tenantId, line.productId(),
                    shipment.getWarehouseId(),
                    line.qty(),
                    shipment.getShipmentNumber(),
                    actor);
        }

        // Step 3: Record fulfillment on each order line
        for (ShipmentLine line : shipment.getLines()) {
            orderService.recordLineFulfillment(
                    OrderId.of(shipment.getOrderId()), tenantId,
                    line.orderLineId(), line.qty(), actor);
        }

        // Step 4: Link shipment back to order
        orderService.findById(OrderId.of(shipment.getOrderId()), tenantId)
                .ifPresent(order -> orderService.linkShipment(
                        OrderId.of(order.getId().getValue()),
                        tenantId, shipment.getId(), actor));

        publisher.publish(new ShipmentEvent.ShipmentDispatched(
                id, tenantId, shipment.getShipmentNumber(),
                trackingNumber, shipment.getCarrierCode(),
                shipment.getOrderId(), actor, Instant.now()));
        return shipment;
    }

    // ── Track ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public ShipmentAggregate markDelivered(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        ShipmentAggregate shipment = load(id, tenantId);
        shipment.markDelivered(actor);
        repository.save(shipment);

        publisher.publish(new ShipmentEvent.ShipmentDelivered(
                id, tenantId, shipment.getShipmentNumber(),
                shipment.getOrderId(), actor, Instant.now()));
        return shipment;
    }

    @Transactional
    public ShipmentAggregate cancelShipment(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        ShipmentAggregate shipment = load(id, tenantId);
        shipment.cancel(actor);
        repository.save(shipment);
        publisher.publish(new ShipmentEvent.ShipmentCancelled(
                id, tenantId, actor, Instant.now()));
        return shipment;
    }

    // ── Reads ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<ShipmentAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public List<ShipmentAggregate> findByOrder(String tenantId, String orderId) {
        return repository.findByOrder(tenantId, orderId);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private ShipmentAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Shipment not found: " + id));
    }

    private String generateShipmentNumber(String tenantId) {
        String t = tenantId.substring(0, Math.min(3, tenantId.length())).toUpperCase();
        return "SHP-" + t + "-" + String.format("%08d", COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
    }
}

Size: 4.1 KB | Modified: 2026-04-03 08:23:10

package com.saas.supplier.api.rest;

import com.saas.supplier.core.SupplierAggregate;
import com.saas.supplier.service.SupplierService;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Supplier REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/suppliers                         → register
 * GET    /api/v1/suppliers/{id}                    → get
 * GET    /api/v1/suppliers                         → list (paginated)
 * PATCH  /api/v1/suppliers/{id}/payment-terms      → update terms
 * PATCH  /api/v1/suppliers/{id}/bank               → set bank details
 */
@Path("/api/v1/suppliers")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class SupplierResource {

    @Inject SupplierService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "anonymous";
    }

    @POST
    public Response register(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid RegisterRequest req) {
        SupplierAggregate s = service.register(
                tid(rawTid), req.name(), req.email(),
                req.currencyCode(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(s -> Response.ok(toResp(s)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @GET
    public Response list(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @QueryParam("page") @DefaultValue("0") int page,
            @QueryParam("size") @DefaultValue("20") int size) {
        List<SupplierResponse> items = service.findAll(tid(rawTid), page, size)
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    @PATCH
    @Path("/{id}/payment-terms")
    public Response setTerms(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid PaymentTermsRequest req) {
        SupplierAggregate s = service.setPaymentTerms(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.leadTimeDays(), req.paymentTermsDays(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    @PATCH
    @Path("/{id}/bank")
    public Response setBank(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid BankRequest req) {
        SupplierAggregate s = service.setBankDetails(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.bankAccount(), req.bankName(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(s)).build();
    }

    private SupplierResponse toResp(SupplierAggregate s) {
        return new SupplierResponse(
                s.getId(), s.getTenantId(), s.getName(),
                s.getEmail(), s.getPhone(), s.getTaxId(),
                s.getStatus().name(), s.getCurrencyCode(),
                s.getLeadTimeDays(), s.getPaymentTermsDays(),
                s.getCurrentPayable().getAmount(),
                s.getBankAccount(), s.getBankName());
    }

    public record RegisterRequest(
            @NotBlank String name, String email,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode) {}

    public record PaymentTermsRequest(
            @PositiveOrZero int leadTimeDays,
            @PositiveOrZero int paymentTermsDays) {}

    public record BankRequest(
            @NotBlank String bankAccount,
            @NotBlank String bankName) {}

    public record SupplierResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String name,
            String email, String phone, String taxId,
            String status, String currencyCode,
            int leadTimeDays, int paymentTermsDays,
            java.math.BigDecimal currentPayable,
            String bankAccount, String bankName) {}
}

package com.saas.supplier.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.util.*;

/**
 * Supplier aggregate root.
 * Models vendors from whom we purchase goods or services.
 */
public class SupplierAggregate {

    private final String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private String name;
    private String email;
    private String phone;
    private String taxId;
    private SupplierStatus status;
    private Address remittanceAddress;
    private String currencyCode;
    private int    leadTimeDays;         // average procurement lead time
    private int    paymentTermsDays;     // net-30, etc.
    private Money  currentPayable;       // outstanding AP balance
    private String bankAccount;          // for payment instructions
    private String bankName;
    private Map<String, String> tags;
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static SupplierAggregate create(String tenantId, String name,
                                           String email, String currencyCode, String actor) {
        var s = new SupplierAggregate();
        s.id              = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        s.tenantId        = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        s.name            = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        s.email           = email;
        s.status          = SupplierStatus.ACTIVE;
        s.currencyCode    = currencyCode != null ? currencyCode : "IDR";
        s.currentPayable  = Money.zero(s.currencyCode);
        s.leadTimeDays    = 7;
        s.paymentTermsDays = 30;
        s.tags            = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        s.audit           = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return s;
    }

    public static SupplierAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String name, String email, String phone,
            String taxId, SupplierStatus status, Address address,
            String currencyCode, int leadTimeDays, int paymentTermsDays,
            Money currentPayable, String bankAccount, String bankName,
            Map<String, String> tags, AuditMeta audit) {
        var s = new SupplierAggregate();
        s.id = id; s.tenantId = tenantId; s.name = name; s.email = email;
        s.phone = phone; s.taxId = taxId; s.status = status;
        s.remittanceAddress = address; s.currencyCode = currencyCode;
        s.leadTimeDays = leadTimeDays; s.paymentTermsDays = paymentTermsDays;
        s.currentPayable = currentPayable; s.bankAccount = bankAccount;
        s.bankName = bankName; s.tags = tags; s.audit = audit;
        return s;
    }

    private SupplierAggregate() {}

    // ── Commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void updateProfile(String name, String email, String phone,
                              String taxId, String actor) {
        this.name = name; this.email = email;
        this.phone = phone; this.taxId = taxId;
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setPaymentTerms(int leadTimeDays, int paymentTermsDays, String actor) {
        this.leadTimeDays      = leadTimeDays;
        this.paymentTermsDays  = paymentTermsDays;
        this.audit             = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setRemittanceAddress(Address addr, String actor) {
        this.remittanceAddress = addr;
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void setBankDetails(String bankAccount, String bankName, String actor) {
        this.bankAccount = bankAccount;
        this.bankName    = bankName;
        this.audit       = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    /** Increase AP balance when a supplier invoice arrives */
    public void debitPayable(Money amount, String actor) {
        this.currentPayable = currentPayable.add(amount);
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    /** Decrease AP balance when payment is made */
    public void creditPayable(Money amount, String actor) {
        this.currentPayable = currentPayable.subtract(amount);
        this.audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void deactivate(String actor) {
        this.status = SupplierStatus.INACTIVE;
        this.audit  = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                 { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()           { return tenantId; }
    public String getName()               { return name; }
    public String getEmail()              { return email; }
    public String getPhone()              { return phone; }
    public String getTaxId()              { return taxId; }
    public SupplierStatus getStatus()     { return status; }
    public Address getRemittanceAddress() { return remittanceAddress; }
    public String getCurrencyCode()       { return currencyCode; }
    public int getLeadTimeDays()          { return leadTimeDays; }
    public int getPaymentTermsDays()      { return paymentTermsDays; }
    public Money getCurrentPayable()      { return currentPayable; }
    public String getBankAccount()        { return bankAccount; }
    public String getBankName()           { return bankName; }
    public Map<String, String> getTags()  { return Collections.unmodifiableMap(tags); }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()           { return audit; }

    public enum SupplierStatus { ACTIVE, INACTIVE, BLOCKED }
}

package com.saas.supplier.infrastructure.entity;

import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheEntityBase;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Map;

@Entity @Table(name = "suppliers")
public class SupplierEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {
    @Id @Column(name="id",length=36,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String id;
    @Column(name="tenant_id",length=64,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String tenantId;
    @Column(name="name",length=512,nullable=false) public String name;
    @Column(name="email",length=320) public String email;
    @Column(name="phone",length=32) public String phone;
    @Column(name="tax_id",length=64) public String taxId;
    @Column(name="status",length=20,nullable=false) public String status;

    @Column(name="remittance_address",columnDefinition="jsonb") public Map<String,Object> remittanceAddress;

    @Column(name="payment_terms_days",nullable=false) public int paymentTermsDays;
    @Column(name="current_payable_amt",precision=19,scale=4,nullable=false) public BigDecimal currentPayableAmt;
    @Column(name="current_payable_ccy",length=3,nullable=false) public String currentPayableCcy;
    @Column(name="bank_account",length=64) public String bankAccount;
    @Column(name="bank_name",length=128) public String bankName;
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name="tags",columnDefinition="jsonb") public Map<String,String> tags;
    @Version @Column(name="version",nullable=false) public long version;
    @Column(name="created_at",nullable=false,updatable=false) public Instant createdAt;
    @Column(name="updated_at",nullable=false) public Instant updatedAt;
    @Column(name="created_by",length=128) public String createdBy;
    @Column(name="updated_by",length=128) public String updatedBy;

package com.saas.supplier.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.supplier.core.SupplierAggregate;
import com.saas.supplier.core.SupplierAggregate.SupplierStatus;
import com.saas.supplier.infrastructure.entity.SupplierEntity;
import com.saas.supplier.spi.SupplierRepository;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Page;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Sort;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanacheSupplierRepository
        implements SupplierRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<SupplierEntity, String> {

    @Override @Transactional
    public SupplierAggregate save(SupplierAggregate s) {
        SupplierEntity e = toEntity(s);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) getEntityManager().merge(e);
        else persist(e);
        return s;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<SupplierAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id=?1 AND tenantId=?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<SupplierAggregate> findByEmail(String email, String tenantId) {
        return find("email=?1 AND tenantId=?2", email, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public List<SupplierAggregate> findAll(String tenantId, int page, int size) {
        return find("tenantId=?1", Sort.ascending("name"), tenantId)
                .page(Page.of(page, size)).list()
                .stream().map(this::toDomain).toList();
    }

    @Override
    public long count(String tenantId) {
        return count("tenantId=?1", tenantId);
    }

    private SupplierAggregate toDomain(SupplierEntity e) {
        AuditMeta audit = AuditMeta.reconstitute(
                e.createdAt, e.updatedAt, e.createdBy, e.updatedBy, e.version);
        Money payable = Money.of(
                e.currentPayableAmt != null ? e.currentPayableAmt : BigDecimal.ZERO,
                e.currentPayableCcy);
        return SupplierAggregate.reconstitute(
                e.id, e.tenantId, e.name, e.email, e.phone,
                e.taxId, SupplierStatus.valueOf(e.status),
                null,   // address – decode on demand
                e.currencyCode, e.leadTimeDays, e.paymentTermsDays,
                payable, e.bankAccount, e.bankName,
                e.tags != null ? e.tags : Map.of(), audit);
    }

    private SupplierEntity toEntity(SupplierAggregate s) {
        SupplierEntity e    = new SupplierEntity();
        e.id                = s.getId();
        e.tenantId          = s.getTenantId();
        e.name              = s.getName();
        e.email             = s.getEmail();
        e.phone             = s.getPhone();
        e.taxId             = s.getTaxId();
        e.status            = s.getStatus().name();
        e.currencyCode      = s.getCurrencyCode();
        e.leadTimeDays      = s.getLeadTimeDays();
        e.paymentTermsDays  = s.getPaymentTermsDays();
        e.currentPayableAmt = s.getCurrentPayable().getAmount();
        e.currentPayableCcy = s.getCurrentPayable().getCurrencyCode();
        e.bankAccount       = s.getBankAccount();
        e.bankName          = s.getBankName();
        e.tags              = s.getTags();
        e.version           = s.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt         = s.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt         = s.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy         = s.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy         = s.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        return e;
    }
}

package com.saas.supplier.service;

import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.supplier.core.SupplierAggregate;
import com.saas.supplier.spi.SupplierRepository;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

@ApplicationScoped
public class SupplierService {

    @Inject SupplierRepository repository;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    @Transactional
    public SupplierAggregate register(String tenantId, String name,
                                      String email, String currencyCode, String actor) {
        SupplierAggregate s = SupplierAggregate.create(tenantId, name, email, currencyCode, actor);
        return repository.save(s);
    }

    @Transactional
    public SupplierAggregate setPaymentTerms(String id, String tenantId,
                                             int leadDays, int termsDays, String actor) {
        SupplierAggregate s = load(id, tenantId);
        s.setPaymentTerms(leadDays, termsDays, actor);
        return repository.save(s);
    }

    @Transactional
    public SupplierAggregate setBankDetails(String id, String tenantId,
                                            String account, String bank, String actor) {
        SupplierAggregate s = load(id, tenantId);
        s.setBankDetails(account, bank, actor);
        return repository.save(s);
    }

    public Optional<SupplierAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public List<SupplierAggregate> findAll(String tenantId, int page, int size) {
        return repository.findAll(tenantId, page, size);
    }

    private SupplierAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new SupplierNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    public static class SupplierNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public SupplierNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Supplier not found: " + id + " / " + t);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.tax.api.rest;

import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.tax.core.TaxAggregate;
import com.saas.tax.core.TaxAggregate.*;
import com.saas.tax.service.TaxService;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * Tax REST API.
 *
 * GET    /api/v1/tax                       → list active tax codes
 * POST   /api/v1/tax                       → create tax code
 * POST   /api/v1/tax/{id}/rates            → add effective-dated rate
 * POST   /api/v1/tax/{id}/deactivate       → deactivate
 * POST   /api/v1/tax/calculate             → compute tax amount
 * POST   /api/v1/tax/seed/indonesia        → seed Indonesia defaults
 */
@Path("/api/v1/tax")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class TaxResource {

    @Inject TaxService taxService;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    @GET
    public Response listActive(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<TaxResponse> taxes = taxService.findActive(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(taxes).build();
    }

    @POST
    public Response create(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateTaxRequest req) {
        TaxAggregate tax = taxService.createTax(
                tid(rawTid), req.code(), req.name(),
                TaxType.valueOf(req.type()),
                TaxCalculationMethod.valueOf(req.method()),
                req.accountCode(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toResp(tax)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/rates")
    public Response addRate(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid AddRateRequest req) {
        LocalDate to = req.effectiveTo() != null ? LocalDate.parse(req.effectiveTo()) : null;
        TaxAggregate tax = taxService.addRate(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.rate(),
                LocalDate.parse(req.effectiveFrom()), to, actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(tax)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/{id}/deactivate")
    public Response deactivate(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        TaxAggregate tax = taxService.deactivate(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toResp(tax)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/calculate")
    public Response calculate(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CalculateRequest req) {
        Money base = Money.of(req.amount(), req.currencyCode());
        LocalDate date = req.transactionDate() != null
                ? LocalDate.parse(req.transactionDate()) : LocalDate.now();
        TaxCalculationResult result = taxService.calculate(tid(rawTid), req.taxCode(), base, date);
        return Response.ok(new CalcResponse(
                result.taxCode(), result.taxName(),
                result.ratePercent(), result.method().name(),
                result.netAmount().getAmount(), result.taxAmount().getAmount(),
                result.grossAmount().getAmount(), req.currencyCode())).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/seed/indonesia")
    public Response seedIndonesia(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        taxService.seedIndonesiaTaxDefaults(tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok("{\"status\":\"seeded\",\"codes\":[\"PPN-11\",\"PPN-11-INC\",\"PPH23-2\",\"PPH21-5\"]}").build();
    }

    private TaxResponse toResp(TaxAggregate t) {
        return new TaxResponse(
                t.getId(), t.getCode(), t.getName(),
                t.getType().name(), t.getMethod().name(),
                t.isActive(), t.getAccountCode(),
                t.getRates().stream().map(r -> new RateResp(
                        r.rate(), r.effectiveFrom().toString(),
                        r.effectiveTo() != null ? r.effectiveTo().toString() : null)).toList());
    }

    public record CreateTaxRequest(
            @NotBlank String code, @NotBlank String name,
            @NotBlank String type, @NotBlank String method,
            String accountCode) {}

    public record AddRateRequest(
            @NotNull @DecimalMin("0") @DecimalMax("100") BigDecimal rate,
            @NotBlank String effectiveFrom, String effectiveTo) {}

    public record CalculateRequest(
            @NotBlank String taxCode, @NotNull BigDecimal amount,
            @NotBlank String currencyCode, String transactionDate) {}

    public record TaxResponse(String id, String code, String name,
            String type, String method, boolean isActive, String accountCode,
            List<RateResp> rates) {}

    public record RateResp(BigDecimal rate, String effectiveFrom, String effectiveTo) {}

    public record CalcResponse(String taxCode, String taxName, BigDecimal rate,
            String method, BigDecimal netAmount, BigDecimal taxAmount,
            BigDecimal grossAmount, String currencyCode) {}
}

package com.saas.tax.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Tax Aggregate — manages tax codes, rates, and jurisdiction rules.
 *
 * Replaces hardcoded 11% PPN throughout the ERP with a configurable
 * tax engine supporting:
 *  - Multiple tax types (VAT/PPN, income tax, service tax, import duty)
 *  - Date-effective rates (rate changes without data migration)
 *  - Compound taxes (tax on tax)
 *  - Inclusive vs exclusive tax (POS vs B2B)
 *  - Per-jurisdiction overrides
 */
public class TaxAggregate {

    private String id;
    private String tenantId;

    /** Unique machine-readable code e.g. "PPN-11", "PPH23-2", "SERVICE-10" */
    private String code;
    private String name;
    private TaxType type;
    private TaxCalculationMethod method;    // INCLUSIVE or EXCLUSIVE
    private boolean isCompound;             // applies on top of other taxes
    private boolean isActive;
    private String  accountCode;            // GL account code for this tax
    private List<TaxRate> rates;            // date-effective rates
    private AuditMeta audit;

    public static TaxAggregate create(String tenantId, String code, String name,
                                       TaxType type, TaxCalculationMethod method,
                                       String accountCode, String actor) {
        TaxAggregate t = new TaxAggregate();
        t.id          = UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        t.tenantId    = Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId);
        t.code        = Objects.requireNonNull(code);
        t.name        = Objects.requireNonNull(name);
        t.type        = Objects.requireNonNull(type);
        t.method      = method != null ? method : TaxCalculationMethod.EXCLUSIVE;
        t.accountCode = accountCode;
        t.isActive    = true;
        t.isCompound  = false;
        t.rates       = new ArrayList<>();
        t.audit       = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return t;
    }

    public static TaxAggregate reconstitute(
            String id, String tenantId, String code, String name,
            TaxType type, TaxCalculationMethod method, boolean isCompound,
            boolean isActive, String accountCode,
            List<TaxRate> rates, AuditMeta audit) {
        TaxAggregate t = new TaxAggregate();
        t.id = id; t.tenantId = tenantId; t.code = code; t.name = name;
        t.type = type; t.method = method; t.isCompound = isCompound;
        t.isActive = isActive; t.accountCode = accountCode;
        t.rates = new ArrayList<>(rates); t.audit = audit;
        return t;
    }

    private TaxAggregate() {}

    // ── Rate management ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public void addRate(BigDecimal rate, LocalDate effectiveFrom,
                         LocalDate effectiveTo, String actor) {
        if (rate.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) < 0 || rate.compareTo(new BigDecimal("100")) > 0)
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Tax rate must be between 0 and 100: " + rate);
        rates.add(new TaxRate(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), rate, effectiveFrom, effectiveTo));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void deactivate(String actor) {
        this.isActive = false;
        this.audit    = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Tax computation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Find the applicable rate for a given transaction date.
     */
    public Optional<TaxRate> rateFor(LocalDate transactionDate) {
        return rates.stream()
                .filter(r -> !transactionDate.isBefore(r.effectiveFrom()))
                .filter(r -> r.effectiveTo() == null || !transactionDate.isAfter(r.effectiveTo()))
                .max(Comparator.comparing(TaxRate::effectiveFrom));
    }

    /**
     * Calculate tax amount on a given base amount for a transaction date.
     *
     * EXCLUSIVE: base = price before tax, tax added on top
     *   taxAmount = base × rate
     *
     * INCLUSIVE: base = price including tax, extract tax from it
     *   taxAmount = base × rate / (100 + rate)
     */
    public TaxCalculationResult calculate(Money base, LocalDate transactionDate) {
        TaxRate rate = rateFor(transactionDate)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException(
                        "No active rate for tax " + code + " on " + transactionDate));

        BigDecimal r = rate.rate();
        Money taxAmount;
        Money netAmount;

        if (method == TaxCalculationMethod.INCLUSIVE) {
            // Extract tax: tax = base × r / (100 + r)
            BigDecimal divisor = new BigDecimal("100").add(r);
            BigDecimal taxFraction = r.divide(divisor, 10, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
            taxAmount = base.multiply(taxFraction);
            netAmount = base.subtract(taxAmount);
        } else {
            // Add tax: tax = base × r / 100
            taxAmount = base.percentage(r);
            netAmount = base;
        }

        return new TaxCalculationResult(
                code, name, r, method, netAmount, taxAmount,
                netAmount.add(taxAmount), transactionDate);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String    getId()           { return id; }
    public String    getTenantId()     { return tenantId; }
    public String    getCode()         { return code; }
    public String    getName()         { return name; }
    public TaxType   getType()         { return type; }
    public TaxCalculationMethod getMethod() { return method; }
    public boolean   isCompound()      { return isCompound; }
    public boolean   isActive()        { return isActive; }
    public String    getAccountCode()  { return accountCode; }
    public List<TaxRate> getRates()    { return Collections.unmodifiableList(rates); }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()        { return audit; }

    // ── Nested types ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public enum TaxType {
        VAT,           // Value Added Tax (PPN Indonesia)
        WITHHOLDING,   // PPh 21, PPh 23
        SERVICE,       // Service charge (restaurant, hotel)
        IMPORT_DUTY,   // Bea masuk
        SALES_TAX,     // US-style sales tax
        CUSTOM         // Any other tax
    }

    public enum TaxCalculationMethod {
        INCLUSIVE,   // price includes tax (common in retail/POS)
        EXCLUSIVE    // tax added on top of price (common in B2B invoicing)
    }

    public record TaxRate(
            String id,
            BigDecimal rate,            // percentage e.g. 11.00 for 11%
            LocalDate effectiveFrom,
            LocalDate effectiveTo       // null = currently active
    ) {}

    public record TaxCalculationResult(
            String taxCode,
            String taxName,
            BigDecimal ratePercent,
            TaxCalculationMethod method,
            Money netAmount,
            Money taxAmount,
            Money grossAmount,
            LocalDate appliedOn
    ) {
        public boolean isZeroTax() { return taxAmount.isZero(); }
    }
}

package com.saas.tax.service;

import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.tax.core.TaxAggregate;
import com.saas.tax.core.TaxAggregate.*;
import com.saas.tax.spi.TaxRepository;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

/**
 * Tax application service.
 *
 * Provides:
 *  1. Tax code management (CRUD)
 *  2. Rate lifecycle (add effective-dated rates)
 *  3. Tax computation API used by Invoice and Order modules
 *  4. Default Indonesia tax seeding (PPN 11%, PPh 23 2%)
 */
@ApplicationScoped
public class TaxService {

    @Inject TaxRepository repository;

    // ── Tax code management ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public TaxAggregate createTax(String tenantId, String code, String name,
                                   TaxType type, TaxCalculationMethod method,
                                   String accountCode, String actor) {
        if (repository.findByCode(code, tenantId).isPresent())
            throw new DuplicateTaxCodeException(code, tenantId);
        return repository.save(
                TaxAggregate.create(tenantId, code, name, type, method, accountCode, actor));
    }

    @Transactional
    public TaxAggregate addRate(String id, String tenantId,
                                 BigDecimal rate, LocalDate effectiveFrom,
                                 LocalDate effectiveTo, String actor) {
        TaxAggregate tax = load(id, tenantId);
        tax.addRate(rate, effectiveFrom, effectiveTo, actor);
        return repository.save(tax);
    }

    @Transactional
    public TaxAggregate deactivate(String id, String tenantId, String actor) {
        TaxAggregate tax = load(id, tenantId);
        tax.deactivate(actor);
        return repository.save(tax);
    }

    // ── Tax computation ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Calculate tax for a given code and base amount.
     * Used by Invoice, Order, and POS modules.
     */
    public TaxAggregate.TaxCalculationResult calculate(String tenantId, String taxCode,
                                                        Money base, LocalDate date) {
        TaxAggregate tax = repository.findByCode(taxCode, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new TaxCodeNotFoundException(taxCode, tenantId));
        return tax.calculate(base, date);
    }

    /** Calculate all applicable taxes for a list of codes. */
    public List<TaxAggregate.TaxCalculationResult> calculateAll(String tenantId,
                                                                  List<String> taxCodes,
                                                                  Money base,
                                                                  LocalDate date) {
        return taxCodes.stream()
                .map(code -> calculate(tenantId, code, base, date))
                .toList();
    }

    // ── Query ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<TaxAggregate> findByCode(String code, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findByCode(code, tenantId);
    }

    public List<TaxAggregate> findActive(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findActive(tenantId);
    }

    // ── Indonesia defaults ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    /**
     * Seed standard Indonesia tax codes for a new tenant.
     * Called once during tenant onboarding.
     */
    @Transactional
    public void seedIndonesiaTaxDefaults(String tenantId, String actor) {
        LocalDate from = LocalDate.of(2022, 4, 1); // PPN 11% effective date

        // PPN 11% (exclusive, standard B2B)
        if (repository.findByCode("PPN-11", tenantId).isEmpty()) {
            TaxAggregate ppn = TaxAggregate.create(
                    tenantId, "PPN-11", "PPN 11%",
                    TaxType.VAT, TaxCalculationMethod.EXCLUSIVE, "2100", actor);
            ppn.addRate(new BigDecimal("11"), from, null, actor);
            repository.save(ppn);
        }

        // PPN 11% inclusive (for retail/POS)
        if (repository.findByCode("PPN-11-INC", tenantId).isEmpty()) {
            TaxAggregate ppnInc = TaxAggregate.create(
                    tenantId, "PPN-11-INC", "PPN 11% (Inclusive)",
                    TaxType.VAT, TaxCalculationMethod.INCLUSIVE, "2100", actor);
            ppnInc.addRate(new BigDecimal("11"), from, null, actor);
            repository.save(ppnInc);
        }

        // PPh 23 - 2% withholding on services
        if (repository.findByCode("PPH23-2", tenantId).isEmpty()) {
            TaxAggregate pph23 = TaxAggregate.create(
                    tenantId, "PPH23-2", "PPh Pasal 23 - 2%",
                    TaxType.WITHHOLDING, TaxCalculationMethod.EXCLUSIVE, "2110", actor);
            pph23.addRate(new BigDecimal("2"), LocalDate.of(2008, 1, 1), null, actor);
            repository.save(pph23);
        }

        // PPh 21 - 5% withholding on employee income (simplified)
        if (repository.findByCode("PPH21-5", tenantId).isEmpty()) {
            TaxAggregate pph21 = TaxAggregate.create(
                    tenantId, "PPH21-5", "PPh Pasal 21 - 5%",
                    TaxType.WITHHOLDING, TaxCalculationMethod.EXCLUSIVE, "2111", actor);
            pph21.addRate(new BigDecimal("5"), LocalDate.of(2008, 1, 1), null, actor);
            repository.save(pph21);
        }
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private TaxAggregate load(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new TaxNotFoundException(id, tenantId));
    }

    public static class TaxNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public TaxNotFoundException(String id, String t) {
            super("Tax not found: " + id + " / " + t);
        }
    }

    public static class TaxCodeNotFoundException extends RuntimeException {
        public TaxCodeNotFoundException(String code, String t) {
            super("Tax code not found: " + code + " / " + t);
        }
    }

    public static class DuplicateTaxCodeException extends RuntimeException {
        public DuplicateTaxCodeException(String code, String t) {
            super("Tax code already exists: " + code + " / " + t);
        }
    }
}
