package com.saas.warehouse.api.rest;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.StockTransfer;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate.WarehouseType;
import com.saas.warehouse.service.WarehouseService;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.*;
import jakarta.ws.rs.core.*;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Warehouse REST API.
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/warehouses                                → create warehouse
 * GET    /api/v1/warehouses                                → list all
 * GET    /api/v1/warehouses/{id}                          → get
 * POST   /api/v1/warehouses/{id}/bins                     → add bin location
 *
 * POST   /api/v1/transfers                                 → create transfer
 * POST   /api/v1/transfers/{id}/lines                     → add line
 * POST   /api/v1/transfers/{id}/dispatch                  → dispatch (TRANSFER_OUT)
 * POST   /api/v1/transfers/{id}/lines/{lid}/receive       → receive line (TRANSFER_IN)
 * POST   /api/v1/transfers/{id}/complete                  → complete transfer
 */
@Path("/api/v1")
@Produces(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
@Consumes(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
public class WarehouseResource {

    @Inject WarehouseService service;
    @Context SecurityContext security;

    private String tid(String h) {
        if (h == null || h.isBlank()) throw new BadRequestException("X-Tenant-Id required");
        return h;
    }
    private String actor() {
        var p = security.getUserPrincipal();
        return p != null ? p.getName() : "system";
    }

    // ── Warehouses ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST
    @Path("/warehouses")
    public Response create(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateWhRequest req) {
        Address addr = req.city() != null ? Address.builder()
                .line1(req.addressLine1()).city(req.city())
                .countryCode(req.countryCode() != null ? req.countryCode() : "ID")
                .build() : null;
        WarehouseAggregate wh = service.createWarehouse(
                tid(rawTid), req.code(), req.name(),
                WarehouseType.valueOf(req.type().toUpperCase()),
                addr, actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toWhResp(wh)).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/warehouses")
    public Response list(@HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        List<WhResponse> items = service.findAll(tid(rawTid))
                .stream().map(this::toWhResp).toList();
        return Response.ok(items).build();
    }

    @GET
    @Path("/warehouses/{id}")
    public Response getById(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        return service.findById(id, tid(rawTid))
                .map(wh -> Response.ok(toWhResp(wh)).build())
                .orElse(Response.status(404).build());
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/warehouses/{id}/bins")
    public Response addBin(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid AddBinRequest req) {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = service.addBin(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.binCode(),
                req.zone(), req.capacity(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toWhResp(wh)).build();
    }

    // ── Stock Transfers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @POST
    @Path("/transfers")
    public Response createTransfer(
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid CreateTransferRequest req) {
        StockTransfer t = service.createTransfer(
                tid(rawTid), req.fromWarehouseId(), req.fromBin(),
                req.toWarehouseId(), req.toBin(), actor());
        return Response.status(201).entity(toTrResp(t)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/transfers/{id}/lines")
    public Response addTransferLine(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid TransferLineRequest req) {
        StockTransfer t = service.addTransferLine(
                id, tid(rawTid), req.productId(),
                req.sku(), req.qty(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toTrResp(t)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/transfers/{id}/dispatch")
    public Response dispatch(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        StockTransfer t = service.dispatchTransfer(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toTrResp(t)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/transfers/{id}/lines/{lineId}/receive")
    public Response receiveLine(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @PathParam("lineId") String lineId,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid,
            @Valid ReceiveLineRequest req) {
        StockTransfer t = service.receiveTransferLine(
                id, tid(rawTid), lineId, req.qty(), actor());
        return Response.ok(toTrResp(t)).build();
    }

    @POST
    @Path("/transfers/{id}/complete")
    public Response complete(
            @PathParam("id") String id,
            @HeaderParam("X-Tenant-Id") String rawTid) {
        StockTransfer t = service.completeTransfer(id, tid(rawTid), actor());
        return Response.ok(toTrResp(t)).build();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private WhResponse toWhResp(WarehouseAggregate wh) {
        return new WhResponse(
                wh.getId(), wh.getTenantId(), wh.getCode(),
                wh.getName(), wh.getType().name(), wh.isActive(),
                wh.getActiveBins().size());
    }

    private TrResponse toTrResp(StockTransfer t) {
        return new TrResponse(
                t.getId(), t.getTenantId(), t.getTransferNumber(),
                t.getFromWarehouseId(), t.getToWarehouseId(),
                t.getStatus().name(), t.getLines().size(),
                t.getDispatchedAt() != null ? t.getDispatchedAt().toString() : null,
                t.getReceivedAt() != null ? t.getReceivedAt().toString() : null);
    }

    // ── DTOs ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public record CreateWhRequest(
            @NotBlank String code, @NotBlank String name,
            @NotBlank String type,
            String addressLine1, String city, String countryCode) {}

    public record AddBinRequest(
            @NotBlank String binCode, String zone,
            @Positive int capacity) {}

    public record CreateTransferRequest(
            @NotBlank String fromWarehouseId, String fromBin,
            @NotBlank String toWarehouseId, String toBin) {}

    public record TransferLineRequest(
            @NotBlank String productId, @NotBlank String sku,
            @Positive int qty) {}

    public record ReceiveLineRequest(@Positive int qty) {}

    public record WhResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String code,
            String name, String type, boolean isActive, int binCount) {}

    public record TrResponse(
            String id, String tenantId, String transferNumber,
            String fromWarehouse, String toWarehouse, String status,
            int lineCount, String dispatchedAt, String receivedAt) {}
}

package com.saas.warehouse.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.*;

/**
 * Stock Transfer aggregate.
 *
 * Models the movement of stock between two warehouses (or bins).
 * Workflow:
 *   DRAFT → IN_TRANSIT → RECEIVED → COMPLETED
 *         ↘ CANCELLED
 *
 * On IN_TRANSIT:  source warehouse dispatches (TRANSFER_OUT movement)
 * On RECEIVED:    destination warehouse receives (TRANSFER_IN movement)
 */
public class StockTransfer {

    private String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private       String transferNumber;
    private       String fromWarehouseId;
    private       String fromBinCode;
    private       String toWarehouseId;
    private       String toBinCode;
    private final List<TransferLine> lines = new ArrayList<>();
    private       TransferStatus status;
    private       String notes;
    private       Instant dispatchedAt;
    private       Instant receivedAt;
    private       AuditMeta audit;

    public static StockTransfer create(String tenantId,
                                        String fromWarehouseId, String fromBin,
                                        String toWarehouseId, String toBin,
                                        String actor) {
        var t = new StockTransfer();
        t.id = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        t.tenantId        = tenantId;
        t.fromWarehouseId = fromWarehouseId;
        t.fromBinCode     = fromBin;
        t.toWarehouseId   = toWarehouseId;
        t.toBinCode       = toBin;
        t.status          = TransferStatus.DRAFT;
        t.audit           = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return t;
    }

    private StockTransfer() {}

    // ── Commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public TransferLine addLine(String productId, String sku, int qty, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        TransferLine line = new TransferLine(UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                productId, sku, qty, 0);
        lines.add(line);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return line;
    }

    public void dispatch(String transferNumber, String actor) {
        assertDraft();
        if (lines.isEmpty()) throw new IllegalStateException("Transfer has no lines");
        this.transferNumber = transferNumber;
        this.status         = TransferStatus.IN_TRANSIT;
        this.dispatchedAt   = Instant.now();
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public boolean receive(String lineId, int qtyReceived, String actor) {
        if (status != TransferStatus.IN_TRANSIT)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Transfer must be IN_TRANSIT to receive");
        int idx = findLineIndex(lineId);
        TransferLine line = lines.get(idx);
        int newReceived = Math.min(line.receivedQty() + qtyReceived, line.requestedQty());
        lines.set(idx, line.withReceivedQty(newReceived));

        boolean allReceived = lines.stream().allMatch(l -> l.receivedQty() >= l.requestedQty());
        if (allReceived) {
            this.status      = TransferStatus.RECEIVED;
            this.receivedAt  = Instant.now();
        }
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return allReceived;
    }

    public void complete(String actor) {
        if (status != TransferStatus.RECEIVED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Transfer must be RECEIVED before completing");
        this.status = TransferStatus.COMPLETED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void cancel(String actor) {
        if (status == TransferStatus.RECEIVED || status == TransferStatus.COMPLETED)
            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot cancel a received transfer");
        this.status = TransferStatus.CANCELLED;
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    private void assertDraft() {
        if (status != TransferStatus.DRAFT) throw new IllegalStateException("Transfer is not DRAFT");
    }

    private int findLineIndex(String lineId) {
        for (int i = 0; i < lines.size(); i++)
            if (lines.get(i).id().equals(lineId)) return i;
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Transfer line not found: " + lineId);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()                      { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()                { return tenantId; }
    public String getTransferNumber()          { return transferNumber; }
    public String getFromWarehouseId()         { return fromWarehouseId; }
    public String getFromBinCode()             { return fromBinCode; }
    public String getToWarehouseId()           { return toWarehouseId; }
    public String getToBinCode()               { return toBinCode; }
    public List<TransferLine> getLines()       { return Collections.unmodifiableList(lines); }
    public TransferStatus getStatus()          { return status; }
    public Instant getDispatchedAt()           { return dispatchedAt; }
    public Instant getReceivedAt()             { return receivedAt; }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()                { return audit; }

    public enum TransferStatus { DRAFT, IN_TRANSIT, RECEIVED, COMPLETED, CANCELLED }

    public record TransferLine(String id, String productId, String sku,
                                int requestedQty, int receivedQty) {
        public TransferLine withReceivedQty(int qty) {
            return new TransferLine(id, productId, sku, requestedQty, qty);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.warehouse.core;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;

import java.util.*;

/**
 * Warehouse aggregate root.
 *
 * A warehouse contains one or more physical bin locations.
 * Stock transfers move inventory between warehouses or between bins within one warehouse.
 */
public class WarehouseAggregate {

    private String id;
    private final String tenantId;
    private       String code;          // e.g. "WH-JKT-01"
    private       String name;
    private       WarehouseType type;   // MAIN, TRANSIT, VIRTUAL, RETURNS
    private       Address address;
    private       boolean isActive;
    private final List<BinLocation> bins = new ArrayList<>();
    private       AuditMeta audit;

    public static WarehouseAggregate create(String tenantId, String code,
                                             String name, WarehouseType type,
                                             Address address, String actor) {
        var w = new WarehouseAggregate();
        w.id = java.util.UUID.randomUUID().toString();
        w.tenantId = tenantId;
        w.code     = code;
        w.name     = name;
        w.type     = type;
        w.address  = address;
        w.isActive = true;
        w.audit    = AuditMeta.create(actor);
        return w;
    }

    private WarehouseAggregate() {}

    // ── Commands ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public BinLocation addBin(String binCode, String zone, int capacity, String actor) {
        if (bins.stream().anyMatch(b -> b.code().equals(binCode)))
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Bin already exists: " + binCode);
        BinLocation bin = new BinLocation(UUID.randomUUID().toString(), binCode, zone, capacity, true);
        bins.add(bin);
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
        return bin;
    }

    public void deactivateBin(String binCode, String actor) {
        int idx = findBinIndex(binCode);
        BinLocation bin = bins.get(idx);
        bins.set(idx, new BinLocation(bin.id(), bin.code(), bin.zone(), bin.capacity(), false));
        audit = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void deactivate(String actor) {
        this.isActive = false;
        this.audit    = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    public void updateAddress(Address address, String actor) {
        this.address = address;
        this.audit   = audit.touch(actor);
    }

    // ── Queries ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public Optional<BinLocation> findBin(String binCode) {
        return bins.stream().filter(b -> b.code().equals(binCode)).findFirst();
    }

    public List<BinLocation> getActiveBins() {
        return bins.stream().filter(BinLocation::isActive).toList();
    }

    private int findBinIndex(String binCode) {
        for (int i = 0; i < bins.size(); i++)
            if (bins.get(i).code().equals(binCode)) return i;
        throw new IllegalArgumentException("Bin not found: " + binCode);
    }

    // ── Getters ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    public String getId()              { return id; }
    public String getTenantId()        { return tenantId; }
    public String getCode()            { return code; }
    public String getName()            { return name; }
    public WarehouseType getType()     { return type; }
    public Address getAddress()        { return address; }
    public boolean isActive()          { return isActive; }
    public List<BinLocation> getBins() { return Collections.unmodifiableList(bins); }
    public AuditMeta getAudit()        { return audit; }

    public enum WarehouseType { MAIN, TRANSIT, VIRTUAL, RETURNS, CONSIGNMENT }

    public record BinLocation(
            String id,
            String code,       // e.g. "A1-B2-C3" (aisle-bay-level)
            String zone,       // e.g. "COLD", "DRY", "HAZMAT"
            int    capacity,   // max units
            boolean isActive) {}
}

package com.saas.warehouse.infrastructure.entity;

import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheEntityBase;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@Entity @Table(name = "stock_transfers")
public class StockTransferEntity extends PanacheEntityBase {
    @Id @Column(name="id",length=36,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String id;
    @Column(name="tenant_id",length=64,nullable=false,updatable=false) public String tenantId;
    @Column(name="transfer_number",length=64) public String transferNumber;
    @Column(name="from_warehouse_id",length=36,nullable=false) public String fromWarehouseId;
    @Column(name="from_bin_code",length=64) public String fromBinCode;
    @Column(name="to_warehouse_id",length=36,nullable=false) public String toWarehouseId;
    @Column(name="to_bin_code",length=64) public String toBinCode;
    @Column(name="status",length=20,nullable=false) public String status;
    @Column(name="notes",columnDefinition="TEXT") public String notes;
    @Column(name="dispatched_at") public Instant dispatchedAt;
    @Column(name="received_at") public Instant receivedAt;
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name="lines",columnDefinition="jsonb") public List<Map<String,Object>> lines;
    @Version @Column(name="version",nullable=false) public long version;
    @Column(name="created_at",nullable=false,updatable=false) public Instant createdAt;
    @Column(name="updated_at",nullable=false) public Instant updatedAt;
    @Column(name="created_by",length=128) public String createdBy;
    @Column(name="updated_by",length=128) public String updatedBy;
}

package com.saas.warehouse.infrastructure.jpa;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import com.saas.shared.model.AuditMeta;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.StockTransfer;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.StockTransfer.TransferStatus;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate.WarehouseType;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate.BinLocation;
import com.saas.warehouse.infrastructure.entity.StockTransferEntity;
import com.saas.warehouse.infrastructure.entity.WarehouseEntity;
import com.saas.warehouse.spi.WarehouseRepository;
import io.quarkus.hibernate.orm.panache.PanacheRepositoryBase;
import io.quarkus.panache.common.Sort;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.*;

@ApplicationScoped
public class PanacheWarehouseRepository
        implements WarehouseRepository,
                   PanacheRepositoryBase<WarehouseEntity, String> {

    @Inject ObjectMapper mapper;

    // ── Warehouse ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Override @Transactional
    public WarehouseAggregate save(WarehouseAggregate wh) {
        WarehouseEntity e = toWhEntity(wh);
        if (findByIdOptional(e.id).isPresent()) getEntityManager().merge(e);
        else persist(e);
        return wh;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<WarehouseAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return find("id=?1 AND tenantId=?2", id, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toWhDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<WarehouseAggregate> findByCode(String code, String tenantId) {
        return find("code=?1 AND tenantId=?2", code, tenantId)
                .firstResultOptional().map(this::toWhDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public List<WarehouseAggregate> findAll(String tenantId) {
        return find("tenantId=?1 AND isActive=true", Sort.ascending("code"), tenantId)
                .list().stream().map(this::toWhDomain).toList();
    }

    // ── Stock Transfer ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Override @Transactional
    public StockTransfer saveTransfer(StockTransfer t) {
        StockTransferEntity e = toTrEntity(t);
        Optional<StockTransferEntity> existing =
                getEntityManager().createQuery(
                        "SELECT e FROM StockTransferEntity e WHERE e.id=:id",
                        StockTransferEntity.class)
                .setParameter("id", t.getId())
                .getResultStream().findFirst();
        if (existing.isPresent()) getEntityManager().merge(e);
        else getEntityManager().persist(e);
        return t;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional<StockTransfer> findTransferById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return getEntityManager().createQuery(
                "SELECT e FROM StockTransferEntity e WHERE e.id=:id AND e.tenantId=:t",
                StockTransferEntity.class)
                .setParameter("id", id).setParameter("t", tenantId)
                .getResultStream().findFirst().map(this::toTrDomain);
    }

    @Override
    public List<StockTransfer> findOpenTransfers(String tenantId) {
        return getEntityManager().createQuery(
                "SELECT e FROM StockTransferEntity e " +
                "WHERE e.tenantId=:t AND e.status IN ('DRAFT','IN_TRANSIT')",
                StockTransferEntity.class)
                .setParameter("t", tenantId)
                .getResultList().stream().map(this::toTrDomain).toList();
    }

    // ── Mapping ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private WarehouseAggregate toWhDomain(WarehouseEntity e) {
        AuditMeta audit = AuditMeta.reconstitute(
                e.createdAt, e.updatedAt, e.createdBy, e.updatedBy, e.version);
        WarehouseAggregate wh = WarehouseAggregate.create(
                e.tenantId, e.code, e.name,
                WarehouseType.valueOf(e.type),
                null, e.createdBy != null ? e.createdBy : "system");
        // Restore bins from JSONB
        if (e.bins != null) {
            for (Map<String,Object> b : e.bins) {
                try {
                    wh.addBin(
                        (String) b.get("code"),
                        (String) b.getOrDefault("zone", "DEFAULT"),
                        ((Number) b.getOrDefault("capacity", 1000)).intValue(),
                        "system");
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        }
        return wh;
    }

    private WarehouseEntity toWhEntity(WarehouseAggregate wh) {
        WarehouseEntity e = new WarehouseEntity();
        e.id        = wh.getId();
        e.tenantId  = wh.getTenantId();
        e.code      = wh.getCode();
        e.name      = wh.getName();
        e.type      = wh.getType().name();
        e.isActive  = wh.isActive();
        e.version   = wh.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt = wh.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt = wh.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy = wh.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy = wh.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        // Serialize bins to JSON
        e.bins = wh.getBins().stream().map(b -> (Map<String,Object>) Map.of(
                "id", b.id(), "code", b.code(),
                "zone", b.zone(), "capacity", b.capacity(),
                "isActive", b.isActive())).toList();
        return e;
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private StockTransfer toTrDomain(StockTransferEntity e) {
        StockTransfer t = StockTransfer.create(
                e.tenantId, e.fromWarehouseId, e.fromBinCode,
                e.toWarehouseId, e.toBinCode,
                e.createdBy != null ? e.createdBy : "system");
        // Restore lines from JSONB
        if (e.lines != null) {
            for (Map<String,Object> l : e.lines) {
                t.addLine(
                    (String) l.get("productId"),
                    (String) l.get("sku"),
                    ((Number) l.getOrDefault("requestedQty", 1)).intValue(),
                    "system");
            }
        }
        return t;
    }

    private StockTransferEntity toTrEntity(StockTransfer t) {
        StockTransferEntity e = new StockTransferEntity();
        e.id              = t.getId();
        e.tenantId        = t.getTenantId();
        e.transferNumber  = t.getTransferNumber();
        e.fromWarehouseId = t.getFromWarehouseId();
        e.fromBinCode     = t.getFromBinCode();
        e.toWarehouseId   = t.getToWarehouseId();
        e.toBinCode       = t.getToBinCode();
        e.status          = t.getStatus().name();
        e.dispatchedAt    = t.getDispatchedAt();
        e.receivedAt      = t.getReceivedAt();
        e.version         = t.getAudit().getVersion();
        e.createdAt       = t.getAudit().getCreatedAt();
        e.updatedAt       = t.getAudit().getUpdatedAt();
        e.createdBy       = t.getAudit().getCreatedBy();
        e.updatedBy       = t.getAudit().getUpdatedBy();
        e.lines = t.getLines().stream().map(l -> (Map<String,Object>) Map.of(
                "id", l.id(), "productId", l.productId(),
                "sku", l.sku(), "requestedQty", l.requestedQty(),
                "receivedQty", l.receivedQty())).toList();
        return e;
    }
}

Size: 6.0 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:54:38

package com.saas.warehouse.service;

import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.service.InventoryService;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.StockTransfer;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate.WarehouseType;
import com.saas.warehouse.spi.WarehouseRepository;
import jakarta.enterprise.context.ApplicationScoped;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

@ApplicationScoped
public class WarehouseService {

    @Inject WarehouseRepository  repository;
    @Inject InventoryService     inventoryService;
    @Inject DomainEventPublisher publisher;

    private static final AtomicLong TRANSFER_COUNTER = new AtomicLong(1);

    // ── Warehouse management ──────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public WarehouseAggregate createWarehouse(String tenantId, String code,
                                               String name, WarehouseType type,
                                               Address address, String actor) {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = WarehouseAggregate.create(tenantId, code, name, type, address, actor);
        return repository.save(wh);
    }

    @Transactional
    public WarehouseAggregate addBin(String warehouseId, String tenantId,
                                      String binCode, String zone,
                                      int capacity, String actor) {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = loadWarehouse(warehouseId, tenantId);
        wh.addBin(binCode, zone, capacity, actor);
        return repository.save(wh);
    }

    public Optional<WarehouseAggregate> findById(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId);
    }

    public List<WarehouseAggregate> findAll(String tenantId) {
        return repository.findAll(tenantId);
    }

    // ── Stock transfers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Transactional
    public StockTransfer createTransfer(String tenantId,
                                         String fromWarehouseId, String fromBin,
                                         String toWarehouseId, String toBin,
                                         String actor) {
        StockTransfer transfer = StockTransfer.create(tenantId,
                fromWarehouseId, fromBin, toWarehouseId, toBin, actor);
        return repository.saveTransfer(transfer);
    }

    @Transactional
    public StockTransfer addTransferLine(String transferId, String tenantId,
                                          String productId, String sku,
                                          int qty, String actor) {
        StockTransfer transfer = loadTransfer(transferId, tenantId);
        transfer.addLine(productId, sku, qty, actor);
        return repository.saveTransfer(transfer);
    }

    /**
     * Dispatch: mark as IN_TRANSIT and write TRANSFER_OUT movements on source warehouse.
     */
    @Transactional
    public StockTransfer dispatchTransfer(String transferId, String tenantId, String actor) {
        StockTransfer transfer = loadTransfer(transferId, tenantId);
        String txNumber = "TRF-" + String.format("%07d", TRANSFER_COUNTER.getAndIncrement());
        transfer.dispatch(txNumber, actor);
        repository.saveTransfer(transfer);

        // Write TRANSFER_OUT movements for every line in source warehouse
        for (StockTransfer.TransferLine line : transfer.getLines()) {
            inventoryService.adjustStock(
                    tenantId, line.productId(), transfer.getFromWarehouseId(),
                    -line.requestedQty(), txNumber, actor);
        }
        return transfer;
    }

    /**
     * Receive: record received qty and write TRANSFER_IN movements on destination warehouse.
     */
    @Transactional
    public StockTransfer receiveTransferLine(String transferId, String tenantId,
                                              String lineId, int qtyReceived,
                                              String actor) {
        StockTransfer transfer = loadTransfer(transferId, tenantId);

        // Find line to get productId before mutating
        StockTransfer.TransferLine line = transfer.getLines().stream()
                .filter(l -> l.id().equals(lineId))
                .findFirst()
                .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalArgumentException("Line not found: " + lineId));

        transfer.receive(lineId, qtyReceived, actor);
        repository.saveTransfer(transfer);

        // Write TRANSFER_IN movement on destination warehouse
        inventoryService.receiveStock(
                tenantId, line.productId(), transfer.getToWarehouseId(),
                qtyReceived, StockMovement.MovementType.TRANSFER_IN,
                transfer.getTransferNumber(), actor);
        return transfer;
    }

    @Transactional
    public StockTransfer completeTransfer(String transferId, String tenantId, String actor) {
        StockTransfer transfer = loadTransfer(transferId, tenantId);
        transfer.complete(actor);
        return repository.saveTransfer(transfer);
    }

    // ── Internals ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private WarehouseAggregate loadWarehouse(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Warehouse not found: " + id));
    }

    private StockTransfer loadTransfer(String id, String tenantId) {
        return repository.findTransferById(id, tenantId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new RuntimeException("Transfer not found: " + id));
    }
}

-- V1__syirkah_schema.sql
-- Complete ERP baseline schema for PostgreSQL 15+
-- Modules: customer, supplier, order, inventory, purchase, invoice, payment, accounting, warehouse, shipment

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  CUSTOMERS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE customers (
    id                  VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id           VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    name                VARCHAR(512)  NOT NULL,
    email               VARCHAR(320),
    phone               VARCHAR(32),
    type                VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('INDIVIDUAL','COMPANY')),
    tax_id              VARCHAR(64),
    status              VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                            CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE','INACTIVE','BLOCKED')),
    billing_address     JSONB,
    shipping_address    JSONB,
    segment             VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'retail',
    credit_limit_amount NUMERIC(19,4),
    credit_limit_ccy    VARCHAR(3),
    current_balance_amt NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    current_balance_ccy VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IDR',
    payment_terms_days  INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    currency_code       VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IDR',
    tags                JSONB         NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
    version             BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by          VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by          VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_customers PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_customers_email UNIQUE (tenant_id, email)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_customers_tenant_status  ON customers (tenant_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_customers_tenant_segment ON customers (tenant_id, segment);
CREATE INDEX idx_customers_name_trgm      ON customers USING GIN (name gin_trgm_ops);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  SUPPLIERS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE suppliers (
    id                   VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id            VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    name                 VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL,
    email                VARCHAR(320),
    phone                VARCHAR(32),
    tax_id               VARCHAR(64),
    status               VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE'
                             CHECK (status IN ('ACTIVE','INACTIVE','BLOCKED')),
    remittance_address   JSONB,
    currency_code        VARCHAR(3)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IDR',
    lead_time_days       INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 7,
    payment_terms_days   INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 30,
    current_payable_amt  NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    current_payable_ccy  VARCHAR(3)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'IDR',
    bank_account         VARCHAR(64),
    bank_name            VARCHAR(128),
    tags                 JSONB        NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
    version              BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at           TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by           VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by           VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_suppliers PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_suppliers_tenant_status ON suppliers (tenant_id, status);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  ORDERS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE orders (
    id                   VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id            VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    order_number         VARCHAR(64),
    customer_id          VARCHAR(36),
    customer_name        VARCHAR(512),
    customer_tax_id      VARCHAR(64),
    channel              VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'default',
    currency_code        VARCHAR(3)   NOT NULL,
    status               VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT'
                             CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT','CONFIRMED','PROCESSING',
                                               'PARTIALLY_FULFILLED','FULFILLED','CANCELLED')),
    shipping_address     JSONB,
    billing_address      JSONB,
    payment_method       VARCHAR(32),
    payment_reference    VARCHAR(128),
    is_paid              BOOLEAN      NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    invoice_id           VARCHAR(36),
    shipment_id          VARCHAR(36),
    notes                TEXT,
    confirmed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    fulfilled_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    cancelled_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    cancellation_reason  TEXT,
    version              BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at           TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by           VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by           VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_orders PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_orders_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, order_number)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_orders_tenant_status     ON orders (tenant_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_orders_tenant_customer   ON orders (tenant_id, customer_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_orders_updated_at        ON orders (updated_at DESC);

CREATE TABLE order_lines (
    id                   VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    order_id             VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tenant_id            VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    product_id           VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    sku                  VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    product_name         VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL,
    quantity_ordered     INT          NOT NULL,
    quantity_fulfilled   INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    unit_price_amt       NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    unit_price_ccy       VARCHAR(3)   NOT NULL,
    unit_tax_amt         NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    unit_tax_ccy         VARCHAR(3),
    notes                TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT pk_order_lines PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_order_lines_order ON order_lines (order_id);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  INVENTORY LEDGER
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE stock_ledgers (
    id              VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    product_id      VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    sku             VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    warehouse_id    VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    location_id     VARCHAR(64),
    on_hand         INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    reserved        INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    incoming        INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    reorder_point   INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 5,
    reorder_qty     INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 10,
    version         BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by      VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by      VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_stock_ledgers PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_stock_product_warehouse UNIQUE (tenant_id, product_id, warehouse_id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_tenant_product   ON stock_ledgers (tenant_id, product_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_stock_low_stock        ON stock_ledgers (tenant_id, on_hand, reorder_point);

CREATE TABLE stock_movements (
    id               VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id        VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    product_id       VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    warehouse_id     VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    quantity         INT          NOT NULL,
    movement_type    VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL,
    reference        VARCHAR(128),
    snapshot_on_hand INT          NOT NULL,
    snapshot_reserved INT         NOT NULL,
    actor            VARCHAR(128),
    occurred_at      TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT pk_stock_movements PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_movements_tenant_product ON stock_movements (tenant_id, product_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_movements_reference      ON stock_movements (tenant_id, reference);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  PURCHASE ORDERS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE purchase_orders (
    id                     VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id              VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    po_number              VARCHAR(64),
    supplier_id            VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    supplier_name          VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL,
    currency_code          VARCHAR(3)   NOT NULL,
    status                 VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT'
                               CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT','SENT','PARTIALLY_RECEIVED',
                                                 'RECEIVED','CLOSED','CANCELLED')),
    expected_delivery_date DATE,
    delivery_address       JSONB,
    warehouse_id           VARCHAR(36),
    notes                  TEXT,
    invoice_ref            VARCHAR(64),
    version                BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at             TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at             TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by             VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by             VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_purchase_orders PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_po_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, po_number)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_po_tenant_status   ON purchase_orders (tenant_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_po_tenant_supplier ON purchase_orders (tenant_id, supplier_id);

CREATE TABLE purchase_order_lines (
    id              VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    po_id           VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL REFERENCES purchase_orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    product_id      VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    sku             VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    product_name    VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL,
    ordered_qty     INT          NOT NULL,
    received_qty    INT          NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    unit_cost_amt   NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    unit_cost_ccy   VARCHAR(3)   NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_po_lines PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_po_lines_po ON purchase_order_lines (po_id);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  INVOICES
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE invoices (
    id                  VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id           VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    invoice_number      VARCHAR(64),
    type                VARCHAR(12)  NOT NULL CHECK (type IN ('RECEIVABLE','PAYABLE')),
    party_id            VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    party_name          VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL,
    party_type          VARCHAR(12)  NOT NULL,
    currency_code       VARCHAR(3)   NOT NULL,
    status              VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT'
                            CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT','ISSUED','PARTIALLY_PAID',
                                              'PAID','OVERDUE','VOID')),
    issue_date          DATE,
    due_date            DATE,
    order_id            VARCHAR(36),
    po_id               VARCHAR(36),
    amount_paid_amt     NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    amount_paid_ccy     VARCHAR(3),
    notes               TEXT,
    version             BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by          VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by          VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_invoices PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_invoice_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, invoice_number)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_invoices_tenant_status  ON invoices (tenant_id, type, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_invoices_tenant_party   ON invoices (tenant_id, party_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_invoices_order_id       ON invoices (tenant_id, order_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_invoices_due_date       ON invoices (tenant_id, due_date) WHERE status NOT IN ('PAID','VOID');

CREATE TABLE invoice_lines (
    id               VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    invoice_id       VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES invoices(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    description      VARCHAR(1000) NOT NULL,
    product_id       VARCHAR(36),
    qty              INT           NOT NULL,
    unit_price_amt   NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    unit_price_ccy   VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_invoice_lines PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

CREATE TABLE invoice_tax_lines (
    id          VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    invoice_id  VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES invoices(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tax_name    VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    tax_code    VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    rate        NUMERIC(7,4)  NOT NULL,
    amount_amt  NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    amount_ccy  VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_invoice_tax_lines PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  PAYMENTS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE payments (
    id                      VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id               VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    payment_number          VARCHAR(64),
    direction               VARCHAR(12)  NOT NULL CHECK (direction IN ('INBOUND','OUTBOUND')),
    party_id                VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    party_name              VARCHAR(512) NOT NULL,
    amount_amt              NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    amount_ccy              VARCHAR(3)   NOT NULL,
    method                  VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL,
    status                  VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'
                                CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','PROCESSED','RECONCILED','FAILED','CANCELLED')),
    invoice_id              VARCHAR(36),
    reference_number        VARCHAR(128),
    gateway_transaction_id  VARCHAR(256),
    bank_account_id         VARCHAR(36),
    processed_at            TIMESTAMPTZ,
    reconciled_at           TIMESTAMPTZ,
    notes                   TEXT,
    version                 BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at              TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by              VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by              VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_payments PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_payments_tenant_status  ON payments (tenant_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_payments_invoice_id     ON payments (tenant_id, invoice_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_payments_unreconciled   ON payments (tenant_id) WHERE status = 'PROCESSED';

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  ACCOUNTING
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE chart_of_accounts (
    tenant_id          VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    code               VARCHAR(16)  NOT NULL,
    name               VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    account_type       VARCHAR(16)  NOT NULL
                           CHECK (account_type IN ('ASSET','LIABILITY','EQUITY','REVENUE','EXPENSE','COGS')),
    parent_code        VARCHAR(16),
    is_control_account BOOLEAN      NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active          BOOLEAN      NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    CONSTRAINT pk_coa PRIMARY KEY (tenant_id, code)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_coa_type ON chart_of_accounts (tenant_id, account_type);

CREATE TABLE journal_entries (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    entry_number    VARCHAR(64),
    status          VARCHAR(12)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT'
                        CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT','POSTED','REVERSED')),
    entry_date      DATE          NOT NULL,
    description     VARCHAR(1000) NOT NULL,
    reference_type  VARCHAR(32),
    reference_id    VARCHAR(36),
    currency_code   VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    reversal_of     VARCHAR(36),
    version         BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by      VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by      VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_journal_entries PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_je_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, entry_number)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_je_tenant_date      ON journal_entries (tenant_id, entry_date DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_je_reference        ON journal_entries (tenant_id, reference_type, reference_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_je_status           ON journal_entries (tenant_id, status);

CREATE TABLE journal_lines (
    id            VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    journal_id    VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES journal_entries(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tenant_id     VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    account_code  VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL,
    entry_type    VARCHAR(6)    NOT NULL CHECK (entry_type IN ('DEBIT','CREDIT')),
    amount_amt    NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    amount_ccy    VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    description   VARCHAR(500),
    CONSTRAINT pk_journal_lines PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_jl_journal      ON journal_lines (journal_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_jl_account_date ON journal_lines (tenant_id, account_code);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  WAREHOUSES
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE warehouses (
    id          VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id   VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    code        VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL,
    name        VARCHAR(256) NOT NULL,
    type        VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL
                    CHECK (type IN ('MAIN','TRANSIT','VIRTUAL','RETURNS','CONSIGNMENT')),
    address     JSONB,
    is_active   BOOLEAN      NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    bins        JSONB        NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    version     BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at  TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at  TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by  VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by  VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_warehouses PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_warehouse_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE stock_transfers (
    id                VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id         VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    transfer_number   VARCHAR(64),
    from_warehouse_id VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    from_bin_code     VARCHAR(64),
    to_warehouse_id   VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    to_bin_code       VARCHAR(64),
    status            VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT'
                          CHECK (status IN ('DRAFT','IN_TRANSIT','RECEIVED','COMPLETED','CANCELLED')),
    notes             TEXT,
    dispatched_at     TIMESTAMPTZ,
    received_at       TIMESTAMPTZ,
    lines             JSONB        NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',
    version           BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at        TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at        TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by        VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by        VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_stock_transfers PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_transfers_tenant_status ON stock_transfers (tenant_id, status);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  SHIPMENTS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE shipments (
    id                   VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    tenant_id            VARCHAR(64)  NOT NULL,
    shipment_number      VARCHAR(64),
    order_id             VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    warehouse_id         VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL,
    destination_address  JSONB,
    status               VARCHAR(20)  NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'
                             CHECK (status IN ('PENDING','PACKED','DISPATCHED',
                                               'IN_TRANSIT','DELIVERED','CANCELLED')),
    carrier_code         VARCHAR(32),
    tracking_number      VARCHAR(128),
    service_level        VARCHAR(32),
    estimated_delivery   TIMESTAMPTZ,
    packed_at            TIMESTAMPTZ,
    dispatched_at        TIMESTAMPTZ,
    delivered_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    notes                TEXT,
    version              BIGINT       NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at           TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at           TIMESTAMPTZ  NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by           VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by           VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_shipments PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_shipment_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, shipment_number)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_shipments_order    ON shipments (tenant_id, order_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_shipments_status   ON shipments (tenant_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_shipments_tracking ON shipments (tenant_id, tracking_number);

CREATE TABLE shipment_lines (
    id            VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL,
    shipment_id   VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL REFERENCES shipments(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    order_line_id VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL,
    product_id    VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL,
    sku           VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    qty           INT         NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_shipment_lines PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_shipment_lines_shipment ON shipment_lines (shipment_id);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Shared updated_at trigger
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION set_updated_at()
RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
BEGIN NEW.updated_at = now(); RETURN NEW; END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql;

DO $$
DECLARE tbl TEXT;
BEGIN
  FOREACH tbl IN ARRAY ARRAY[
    'customers','suppliers','orders','stock_ledgers',
    'purchase_orders','invoices','payments',
    'journal_entries','warehouses','stock_transfers','shipments']
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format(
      'CREATE TRIGGER trg_%I_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON %I FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at()',
      tbl, tbl);
  END LOOP;
END;
$$;

syirkah-modules/src/main/resources/db/migration/V2__syirkah_outbox_and_search.sql

-- V2__syirkah_outbox_and_search.sql
-- Transactional outbox table + full-text search improvements

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  pg_trgm for fuzzy search across all name columns
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_trgm;

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Domain Event Outbox
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS domain_event_outbox (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    aggregate_type  VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    aggregate_id    VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    event_type      VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    payload         JSONB         NOT NULL,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    published_at    TIMESTAMPTZ,
    retry_count     INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    last_error      TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT pk_domain_event_outbox PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

-- Relay query index: find unpublished events in insertion order
CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_relay ON domain_event_outbox (created_at ASC)
    WHERE published_at IS NULL AND retry_count < 5;

-- Aggregate event history: retrieve all events for one aggregate
CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_aggregate
    ON domain_event_outbox (tenant_id, aggregate_type, aggregate_id, created_at DESC);

-- Dead-letter query index
CREATE INDEX idx_outbox_deadletter ON domain_event_outbox (retry_count, published_at)
    WHERE published_at IS NULL AND retry_count >= 5;

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Trigram search indexes for name columns
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_customers_name_trgm   ON customers  USING GIN (name gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_suppliers_name_trgm   ON suppliers  USING GIN (name gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_customer_trgm  ON orders     USING GIN (customer_name gin_trgm_ops);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_invoices_party_trgm   ON invoices   USING GIN (party_name gin_trgm_ops);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Generated tsvector search columns
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

-- Orders: search by orderNumber + customerName
ALTER TABLE orders
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS search_vector TSVECTOR
        GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
            setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(order_number, '')), 'A') ||
            setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(customer_name, '')), 'B')
        ) STORED;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_search_gin ON orders USING GIN (search_vector);

-- Invoices: search by invoiceNumber + partyName
ALTER TABLE invoices
    ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS search_vector TSVECTOR
        GENERATED ALWAYS AS (
            setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(invoice_number, '')), 'A') ||
            setweight(to_tsvector('english', coalesce(party_name, '')), 'B')
        ) STORED;
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_invoices_search_gin ON invoices USING GIN (search_vector);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Audit composite indexes for common dashboard queries
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

-- "Show me all unpaid confirmed orders for this tenant"
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_orders_unpaid
    ON orders (tenant_id, status, confirmed_at DESC)
    WHERE status = 'CONFIRMED' AND is_paid = false;

-- "Show me all overdue invoices for this tenant"
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_invoices_overdue
    ON invoices (tenant_id, due_date ASC)
    WHERE status IN ('ISSUED', 'PARTIALLY_PAID');

-- "Show me unprocessed payments"
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_payments_pending
    ON payments (tenant_id, created_at DESC)
    WHERE status = 'PENDING';

-- "Stock movements in last N days"
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_movements_recent
    ON stock_movements (tenant_id, occurred_at DESC)
    WHERE occurred_at > (now() - interval '90 days');

syirkah-modules/src/main/resources/db/migration/V3__syirkah_reporting_views.sql
Size: 8.8 KB | Modified: 2026-09-22 12:53:39

-- V3__syirkah_reporting_views.sql
-- Standard ERP reporting views built on top of the base schema.
-- All views are tenant-scoped — always filter by tenant_id in queries.

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Accounts Receivable Aging Report
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_ar_aging AS
SELECT
    i.tenant_id,
    i.party_id                                               AS customer_id,
    i.party_name                                             AS customer_name,
    i.id                                                     AS invoice_id,
    i.invoice_number,
    i.issue_date,
    i.due_date,
    i.currency_code,
    -- Compute amount due: subquery for each invoice
    (
        COALESCE((
            SELECT SUM(il.qty * il.unit_price_amt)
            FROM invoice_lines il WHERE il.invoice_id = i.id
        ), 0)
        +
        COALESCE((
            SELECT SUM(tl.amount_amt)
            FROM invoice_tax_lines tl WHERE tl.invoice_id = i.id
        ), 0)
        - COALESCE(i.amount_paid_amt, 0)
    )                                                        AS amount_due,
    CURRENT_DATE - i.due_date                                AS days_overdue,
    CASE
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE <= i.due_date               THEN 'CURRENT'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - i.due_date BETWEEN 1  AND 30 THEN '1-30_DAYS'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - i.due_date BETWEEN 31 AND 60 THEN '31-60_DAYS'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - i.due_date BETWEEN 61 AND 90 THEN '61-90_DAYS'
        ELSE 'OVER_90_DAYS'
    END                                                      AS aging_bucket,
    i.status
FROM invoices i
WHERE i.type   = 'RECEIVABLE'
  AND i.status IN ('ISSUED', 'PARTIALLY_PAID', 'OVERDUE');

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Accounts Payable Aging Report
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_ap_aging AS
SELECT
    i.tenant_id,
    i.party_id                                               AS supplier_id,
    i.party_name                                             AS supplier_name,
    i.id                                                     AS invoice_id,
    i.invoice_number,
    i.issue_date,
    i.due_date,
    i.currency_code,
    (
        COALESCE((SELECT SUM(il.qty * il.unit_price_amt)
                  FROM invoice_lines il WHERE il.invoice_id = i.id), 0)
        +
        COALESCE((SELECT SUM(tl.amount_amt)
                  FROM invoice_tax_lines tl WHERE tl.invoice_id = i.id), 0)
        - COALESCE(i.amount_paid_amt, 0)
    )                                                        AS amount_due,
    CURRENT_DATE - i.due_date                                AS days_overdue,
    CASE
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE <= i.due_date               THEN 'CURRENT'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - i.due_date BETWEEN 1  AND 30 THEN '1-30_DAYS'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - i.due_date BETWEEN 31 AND 60 THEN '31-60_DAYS'
        WHEN CURRENT_DATE - i.due_date BETWEEN 61 AND 90 THEN '61-90_DAYS'
        ELSE 'OVER_90_DAYS'
    END                                                      AS aging_bucket,
    i.status
FROM invoices i
WHERE i.type   = 'PAYABLE'
  AND i.status IN ('ISSUED', 'PARTIALLY_PAID', 'OVERDUE');

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Inventory Valuation (stock on hand × last received unit cost)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_inventory_valuation AS
SELECT
    sl.tenant_id,
    sl.product_id,
    sl.sku,
    sl.warehouse_id,
    sl.on_hand,
    sl.reserved,
    (sl.on_hand - sl.reserved)                              AS available,
    sl.reorder_point,
    CASE WHEN (sl.on_hand - sl.reserved) <= 0
         THEN 'OUT_OF_STOCK'
         WHEN (sl.on_hand - sl.reserved) <= sl.reorder_point
         THEN 'LOW_STOCK'
         ELSE 'IN_STOCK'
    END                                                      AS stock_status,
    -- Last unit cost from most recent PURCHASE_RECEIPT movement
    (
        SELECT sm.quantity  -- placeholder: real impl joins to unit cost
        FROM stock_movements sm
        WHERE sm.tenant_id    = sl.tenant_id
          AND sm.product_id   = sl.product_id
          AND sm.warehouse_id = sl.warehouse_id
          AND sm.movement_type = 'PURCHASE_RECEIPT'
        ORDER BY sm.occurred_at DESC
        LIMIT 1
    )                                                        AS last_receipt_qty,
    sl.updated_at
FROM stock_ledgers sl;

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Order Pipeline (DRAFT → FULFILLED funnel)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_order_pipeline AS
SELECT
    o.tenant_id,
    o.status,
    COUNT(*)                                                 AS order_count,
    SUM(
        (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(ol.quantity_ordered * (ol.unit_price_amt + COALESCE(ol.unit_tax_amt, 0))), 0)
         FROM order_lines ol WHERE ol.order_id = o.id)
    )                                                        AS total_value,
    o.currency_code,
    MIN(o.created_at)                                        AS oldest_order_at,
    MAX(o.created_at)                                        AS newest_order_at
FROM orders o
WHERE o.status NOT IN ('CANCELLED')
GROUP BY o.tenant_id, o.status, o.currency_code;

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Trial Balance Summary (debit-normal net balances by account type)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_trial_balance_summary AS
SELECT
    jl.tenant_id,
    coa.account_type,
    SUM(CASE WHEN jl.entry_type = 'DEBIT'  THEN jl.amount_amt ELSE 0 END) AS total_debits,
    SUM(CASE WHEN jl.entry_type = 'CREDIT' THEN jl.amount_amt ELSE 0 END) AS total_credits,
    SUM(CASE WHEN jl.entry_type = 'DEBIT'  THEN jl.amount_amt ELSE 0 END)
    - SUM(CASE WHEN jl.entry_type = 'CREDIT' THEN jl.amount_amt ELSE 0 END) AS net_balance,
    jl.amount_ccy                                            AS currency_code
FROM journal_lines jl
JOIN journal_entries je ON je.id = jl.journal_id
JOIN chart_of_accounts coa
    ON coa.tenant_id = jl.tenant_id AND coa.code = jl.account_code
WHERE je.status = 'POSTED'
GROUP BY jl.tenant_id, coa.account_type, jl.amount_ccy;

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Purchase Order Status Summary
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW v_purchase_order_summary AS
SELECT
    po.tenant_id,
    po.supplier_id,
    po.supplier_name,
    po.status,
    COUNT(*)                                                 AS po_count,
    SUM(
        (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pol.ordered_qty * pol.unit_cost_amt), 0)
         FROM purchase_order_lines pol WHERE pol.po_id = po.id)
    )                                                        AS total_value,
    po.currency_code,
    SUM(
        (SELECT COALESCE(SUM(pol.ordered_qty - pol.received_qty), 0)
         FROM purchase_order_lines pol WHERE pol.po_id = po.id)
    )                                                        AS total_pending_qty
FROM purchase_orders po
WHERE po.status NOT IN ('CANCELLED', 'CLOSED')
GROUP BY po.tenant_id, po.supplier_id, po.supplier_name,
         po.status, po.currency_code;
