-- V4__new_domains.sql
-- Tables for: tax, currency, pricing, employee, payroll,
--             fixed_asset, budget, notification, audit_log

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  TAX
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE tax_codes (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    code            VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    type            VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL,
    method          VARCHAR(12)   NOT NULL CHECK (method IN ('INCLUSIVE','EXCLUSIVE')),
    is_compound     BOOLEAN       NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_active       BOOLEAN       NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    account_code    VARCHAR(16),
    version         BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by      VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by      VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_tax_codes PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_tax_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE tax_rates (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tax_code_id     VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES tax_codes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    rate            NUMERIC(7,4)  NOT NULL,
    effective_from  DATE          NOT NULL,
    effective_to    DATE,
    CONSTRAINT pk_tax_rates PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  CURRENCY / EXCHANGE RATES
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE exchange_rates (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    base_currency   VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    target_currency VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    version         BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by      VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by      VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_exchange_rates PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_currency_pair UNIQUE (tenant_id, base_currency, target_currency)
);

CREATE TABLE exchange_rate_entries (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    exchange_rate_id VARCHAR(36)  NOT NULL REFERENCES exchange_rates(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    rate            NUMERIC(20,8) NOT NULL,
    rate_type       VARCHAR(10)   NOT NULL CHECK (rate_type IN ('SPOT','BUY','SELL','AVERAGE')),
    effective_date  DATE          NOT NULL,
    source          VARCHAR(64),
    CONSTRAINT pk_rate_entries PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_rate_entries_date ON exchange_rate_entries (exchange_rate_id, effective_date DESC);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  PRICING
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE price_lists (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    code            VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    currency_code   VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    type            VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL,
    target_segment  VARCHAR(64),
    target_channel  VARCHAR(64),
    valid_from      DATE,
    valid_to        DATE,
    is_active       BOOLEAN       NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    version         BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by      VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by      VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_price_lists PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_price_list_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, code)
);

CREATE TABLE price_rules (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    price_list_id   VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES price_lists(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    rule_type       VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL,
    product_id      VARCHAR(36),
    category_id     VARCHAR(36),
    fixed_price_amt NUMERIC(19,4),
    fixed_price_ccy VARCHAR(3),
    discount_pct    NUMERIC(7,4),
    discount_amt    NUMERIC(19,4),
    min_quantity    INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    priority        INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 100,
    CONSTRAINT pk_price_rules PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_price_rules_list ON price_rules (price_list_id, priority);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  EMPLOYEES
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE employees (
    id                          VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id                   VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    employee_number             VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    first_name                  VARCHAR(128)  NOT NULL,
    last_name                   VARCHAR(128)  NOT NULL,
    email                       VARCHAR(320),
    phone                       VARCHAR(32),
    date_of_birth               DATE,
    national_id                 VARCHAR(32),
    tax_id                      VARCHAR(32),
    home_address                JSONB,
    department_id               VARCHAR(36),
    department_name             VARCHAR(128),
    position_id                 VARCHAR(36),
    position_title              VARCHAR(128),
    employment_type             VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL,
    status                      VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    hire_date                   DATE          NOT NULL,
    termination_date            DATE,
    termination_reason          TEXT,
    basic_salary_amt            NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    currency_code               VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    pay_period_days             INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 30,
    bank_account                VARCHAR(64),
    bank_name                   VARCHAR(128),
    bpjs_ketenagakerjaan_no     VARCHAR(32),
    bpjs_kesehatan_no           VARCHAR(32),
    version                     BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by                  VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by                  VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_employees PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_employee_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, employee_number)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_employees_status    ON employees (tenant_id, status);
CREATE INDEX idx_employees_dept      ON employees (tenant_id, department_id);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  PAYROLL / PAYSLIPS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE payslips (
    id                  VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id           VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    employee_id         VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    employee_number     VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    employee_name       VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    pay_period          VARCHAR(7)    NOT NULL,   -- "YYYY-MM"
    status              VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
    currency_code       VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    payment_reference   VARCHAR(128),
    version             BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at          TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by          VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by          VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_payslips PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_payslip_emp_period UNIQUE (tenant_id, employee_id, pay_period)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_payslips_period   ON payslips (tenant_id, pay_period);
CREATE INDEX idx_payslips_employee ON payslips (tenant_id, employee_id);

CREATE TABLE payslip_items (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    payslip_id      VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES payslips(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    item_type       VARCHAR(10)   NOT NULL CHECK (item_type IN ('EARNING','DEDUCTION')),
    code            VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    description     VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    amount_amt      NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    amount_ccy      VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT pk_payslip_items PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  FIXED ASSETS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE fixed_assets (
    id                          VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id                   VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    asset_number                VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    name                        VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    description                 TEXT,
    category                    VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    status                      VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
    acquisition_cost_amt        NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    acquisition_cost_ccy        VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    salvage_value_amt           NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    acquisition_date            DATE          NOT NULL,
    in_service_date             DATE,
    depreciation_method         VARCHAR(24)   NOT NULL,
    useful_life_years           INT           NOT NULL,
    declining_rate              NUMERIC(7,4),
    warehouse_id                VARCHAR(36),
    department_id               VARCHAR(36),
    assigned_to_employee_id     VARCHAR(36),
    asset_account_code          VARCHAR(16),
    depreciation_expense_code   VARCHAR(16),
    accumulated_deprec_code     VARCHAR(16),
    disposal_date               DATE,
    disposal_proceeds_amt       NUMERIC(19,4),
    disposal_reason             TEXT,
    version                     BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at                  TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by                  VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by                  VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_fixed_assets PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_asset_number UNIQUE (tenant_id, asset_number)
);

CREATE TABLE asset_depreciation_entries (
    id                  VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    asset_id            VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES fixed_assets(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    period_end          DATE          NOT NULL,
    depreciation_amt    NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    depreciation_ccy    VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    book_value_after_amt NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    actor               VARCHAR(128),
    created_at          TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT pk_depr_entries PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  BUDGETS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE budgets (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    currency_code   VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    period_type     VARCHAR(12)   NOT NULL,
    period_start    DATE          NOT NULL,
    period_end      DATE          NOT NULL,
    status          VARCHAR(12)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
    version         BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by      VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by      VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_budgets PRIMARY KEY (id)
);

CREATE TABLE budget_lines (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    budget_id       VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES budgets(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    account_code    VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL,
    account_name    VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    line_type       VARCHAR(10)   NOT NULL CHECK (line_type IN ('REVENUE','EXPENSE','COGS')),
    budgeted_amt    NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    budgeted_ccy    VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    actual_amt      NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    notes           TEXT,
    CONSTRAINT pk_budget_lines PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_budget_account UNIQUE (budget_id, account_code)
);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  NOTIFICATIONS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE notifications (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    type            VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    channel         VARCHAR(12)   NOT NULL,
    status          VARCHAR(12)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
    title           VARCHAR(512)  NOT NULL,
    message         TEXT          NOT NULL,
    recipient_id    VARCHAR(128),
    recipient_email VARCHAR(320),
    source_module   VARCHAR(32),
    source_event_id VARCHAR(36),
    reference_id    VARCHAR(36),
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    sent_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    read_at         TIMESTAMPTZ,
    failure_reason  TEXT,
    retry_count     INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    CONSTRAINT pk_notifications PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_notifications_unread
    ON notifications (tenant_id, recipient_id, status)
    WHERE status IN ('PENDING','SENT');
CREATE INDEX idx_notifications_type
    ON notifications (tenant_id, type, created_at DESC);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  AUDIT LOG (append-only)
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE audit_log (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    module          VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    entity_type     VARCHAR(128)  NOT NULL,
    entity_id       VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    action          VARCHAR(32)   NOT NULL,
    actor           VARCHAR(128)  NOT NULL,
    actor_ip        VARCHAR(45),
    description     TEXT,
    before_state    JSONB,
    after_state     JSONB,
    occurred_at     TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    CONSTRAINT pk_audit_log PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_entity   ON audit_log (tenant_id, entity_type, entity_id, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_actor    ON audit_log (tenant_id, actor, occurred_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX idx_audit_module   ON audit_log (tenant_id, module, occurred_at DESC);

-- Prevent UPDATE and DELETE on audit_log (append-only enforcement)
CREATE RULE audit_log_no_update AS ON UPDATE TO audit_log DO INSTEAD NOTHING;
CREATE RULE audit_log_no_delete AS ON DELETE TO audit_log DO INSTEAD NOTHING;

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  Updated_at triggers for new tables
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
DO $$
DECLARE tbl TEXT;
BEGIN
  FOREACH tbl IN ARRAY ARRAY[
    'tax_codes','exchange_rates','price_lists',
    'employees','payslips','fixed_assets','budgets']
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format(
      'CREATE TRIGGER trg_%I_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON %I ' ||
      'FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at()',
      tbl, tbl);
  END LOOP;
END;
$$;

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  CATALOG
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE catalog_categories (
    id                      VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id               VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    slug                    VARCHAR(128)  NOT NULL,
    name                    VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    description             TEXT,
    parent_id               VARCHAR(36)   REFERENCES catalog_categories(id),
    parent_slug             VARCHAR(128),
    display_order           INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    is_active               BOOLEAN       NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    custom_attribute_schema JSONB,
    image_url               VARCHAR(1024),
    version                 BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at              TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by              VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by              VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_catalog_categories PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_category_slug UNIQUE (tenant_id, slug)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_category_parent ON catalog_categories (tenant_id, parent_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_category_active ON catalog_categories (tenant_id, is_active, display_order);

CREATE TABLE catalog_brands (
    id              VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id       VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    slug            VARCHAR(128)  NOT NULL,
    name            VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    description     TEXT,
    logo_url        VARCHAR(1024),
    website_url     VARCHAR(1024),
    country_of_origin VARCHAR(64),
    is_active       BOOLEAN       NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    version         BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at      TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by      VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by      VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_catalog_brands PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_brand_slug UNIQUE (tenant_id, slug)
);

-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
--  PROMOTIONS
-- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
CREATE TABLE promotions (
    id                      VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    tenant_id               VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    code                    VARCHAR(64)   NOT NULL,
    name                    VARCHAR(256)  NOT NULL,
    description             TEXT,
    promotion_type          VARCHAR(20)   NOT NULL,
    discount_type           VARCHAR(16)   NOT NULL,
    discount_percent        NUMERIC(7,4),
    discount_amount_amt     NUMERIC(19,4),
    discount_amount_ccy     VARCHAR(3),
    min_order_value_amt     NUMERIC(19,4),
    min_order_value_ccy     VARCHAR(3),
    valid_from              DATE,
    valid_to                DATE,
    max_total_redemptions   INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_per_customer        INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    max_discount_cap_amt    NUMERIC(19,4),
    applicable_product_ids  JSONB,
    applicable_category_ids JSONB,
    applicable_segments     JSONB,
    status                  VARCHAR(12)   NOT NULL DEFAULT 'DRAFT',
    total_redemptions       INT           NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    version                 BIGINT        NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
    created_at              TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    updated_at              TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    created_by              VARCHAR(128),
    updated_by              VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_promotions PRIMARY KEY (id),
    CONSTRAINT uq_promotion_code UNIQUE (tenant_id, code)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_promotions_active ON promotions (tenant_id, status, valid_from, valid_to);

CREATE TABLE promotion_redemptions (
    id                  VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    promotion_id        VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL REFERENCES promotions(id),
    order_id            VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    customer_id         VARCHAR(36)   NOT NULL,
    discount_amt        NUMERIC(19,4) NOT NULL,
    discount_ccy        VARCHAR(3)    NOT NULL,
    redeemed_at         TIMESTAMPTZ   NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    actor               VARCHAR(128),
    CONSTRAINT pk_promo_redemptions PRIMARY KEY (id)
);
CREATE INDEX idx_redemptions_promo    ON promotion_redemptions (promotion_id);
CREATE INDEX idx_redemptions_customer ON promotion_redemptions (customer_id, promotion_id);

-- Add updated_at triggers for new tables
DO $$
DECLARE tbl TEXT;
BEGIN
  FOREACH tbl IN ARRAY ARRAY['catalog_categories','catalog_brands','promotions']
  LOOP
    EXECUTE format(
      'CREATE TRIGGER trg_%I_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE ON %I ' ||
      'FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION set_updated_at()',
      tbl, tbl);
  END LOOP;
END;
$$;

syirkah-modules/src/test/java/com/saas/accounting/integration/AccountingIntegrationTest.java

package com.saas.accounting.integration;

import com.saas.accounting.core.ChartOfAccounts;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry.JournalEntryStatus;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry.UnbalancedJournalEntryException;
import com.saas.accounting.service.AccountingService;
import com.saas.accounting.spi.AccountingRepository.TrialBalanceLine;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import io.quarkus.test.junit.QuarkusTest;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Integration test for the accounting general ledger:
 * CoA init → manual entries → post → trial balance
 */
@QuarkusTest
@TestMethodOrder(MethodOrderer.OrderAnnotation.class)
class AccountingIntegrationTest {

    @Inject AccountingService accountingService;

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-acc-it";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "accountant";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    // ── Test 1: CoA Initialization ────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(1)
    @DisplayName("Initialize CoA → 30+ standard accounts created")
    @Transactional
    void initCoA() {
        ChartOfAccounts coa = accountingService.initializeCoA(TENANT, ACTOR);

        assertThat(coa.getAllAccounts()).hasSizeGreaterThan(30);

        // Verify key accounts present
        assertThatNoException().isThrownBy(() -> coa.getAccount("1100")); // AR
        assertThatNoException().isThrownBy(() -> coa.getAccount("4010")); // Sales
        assertThatNoException().isThrownBy(() -> coa.getAccount("2100")); // PPN Payable
        assertThatNoException().isThrownBy(() -> coa.getAccount("1020")); // Bank

        // Second init is idempotent
        ChartOfAccounts coa2 = accountingService.initializeCoA(TENANT, ACTOR);
        assertThat(coa2.getAllAccounts()).hasSizeGreaterThan(30);
    }

    // ── Test 2: Balanced AR Invoice entry ─────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(2)
    @DisplayName("Post AR invoice journal entry — balanced")
    @Transactional
    void postArInvoiceEntry() {
        accountingService.initializeCoA(TENANT, ACTOR);

        JournalEntry je = accountingService.postArInvoice(
                TENANT, "INV-IT-001",
                LocalDate.now(),
                idr(1_000_000),   // subtotal
                idr(110_000),     // PPN 11%
                ACTOR);

        assertThat(je.getStatus()).isEqualTo(JournalEntryStatus.POSTED);
        assertThat(je.isBalanced()).isTrue();

        // DR 1100 (AR) = 1,110,000
        Money debits = je.getTotalDebits();
        assertThat(debits.getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1110000");

        // CR 4010 (Sales) = 1,000,000 + CR 2100 (PPN) = 110,000
        Money credits = je.getTotalCredits();
        assertThat(credits.getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1110000");

        // Account breakdown
        boolean hasArDebit = je.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("1100") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.DEBIT);
        boolean hasSalesCredit = je.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("4010") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.CREDIT);
        boolean hasPpnCredit = je.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("2100") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.CREDIT);

        assertThat(hasArDebit).isTrue();
        assertThat(hasSalesCredit).isTrue();
        assertThat(hasPpnCredit).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Test 3: Customer payment entry ────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(3)
    @DisplayName("Post customer payment — DR Bank / CR AR")
    @Transactional
    void postCustomerPaymentEntry() {
        accountingService.initializeCoA(TENANT, ACTOR);

        JournalEntry je = accountingService.postCustomerPayment(
                TENANT, "PAY-IT-001", LocalDate.now(), idr(1_110_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(je.isBalanced()).isTrue();
        assertThat(je.getStatus()).isEqualTo(JournalEntryStatus.POSTED);

        boolean hasBank = je.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("1020") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.DEBIT);
        boolean hasArCredit = je.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("1100") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.CREDIT);

        assertThat(hasBank).isTrue();
        assertThat(hasArCredit).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Test 4: Unbalanced entry blocked ──────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(4)
    @DisplayName("Unbalanced manual entry throws on post")
    @Transactional
    void unbalancedEntryBlocked() {
        accountingService.initializeCoA(TENANT, ACTOR);

        JournalEntry je = accountingService.createManualEntry(
                TENANT, LocalDate.now(), "Bad entry", CCY, ACTOR);

        accountingService.addDebitLine(
                je.getId(), TENANT, "1100", idr(500_000), "AR", ACTOR);
        accountingService.addCreditLine(
                je.getId(), TENANT, "4010", idr(300_000), "Revenue — WRONG", ACTOR);

        assertThatExceptionOfType(UnbalancedJournalEntryException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> accountingService.postEntry(je.getId(), TENANT, ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Test 5: Reversal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(5)
    @DisplayName("Reversal entry swaps debits/credits and auto-posts")
    @Transactional
    void reversalEntry() {
        accountingService.initializeCoA(TENANT, ACTOR);

        JournalEntry original = accountingService.postArInvoice(
                TENANT, "INV-REV-001", LocalDate.now(),
                idr(500_000), idr(55_000), ACTOR);

        JournalEntry reversal = accountingService.reverseEntry(
                original.getId(), TENANT, LocalDate.now(), ACTOR);

        assertThat(reversal.getStatus()).isEqualTo(JournalEntryStatus.POSTED);
        assertThat(reversal.getReversalOf()).isEqualTo(original.getId());
        assertThat(reversal.isBalanced()).isTrue();

        // Reversal should have CR 1100 (not DR) and DR 4010 (not CR)
        boolean hasArCredit = reversal.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("1100") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.CREDIT);
        boolean hasSalesDebit = reversal.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("4010") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.DEBIT);

        assertThat(hasArCredit).isTrue();
        assertThat(hasSalesDebit).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Test 6: Trial Balance ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(6)
    @DisplayName("Trial balance returns account rows after postings")
    @Transactional
    void trialBalance() {
        accountingService.initializeCoA(TENANT, ACTOR);

        // Post a sale
        accountingService.postArInvoice(
                TENANT, "INV-TB-001", LocalDate.now(),
                idr(2_000_000), idr(220_000), ACTOR);

        List<TrialBalanceLine> tb = accountingService.getTrialBalance(
                TENANT, LocalDate.now());

        assertThat(tb).isNotEmpty();
        assertThat(tb.stream().anyMatch(
                row -> row.accountCode().equals("1100"))).isTrue();
        assertThat(tb.stream().anyMatch(
                row -> row.accountCode().equals("4010"))).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Test 7: COGS entry ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(7)
    @DisplayName("COGS entry on shipment — DR COGS / CR Inventory")
    @Transactional
    void cogsEntry() {
        accountingService.initializeCoA(TENANT, ACTOR);

        JournalEntry je = accountingService.postCogsOnDispatch(
                TENANT, "SHP-COGS-001", LocalDate.now(), idr(300_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(je.isBalanced()).isTrue();

        boolean hasCogsDebit = je.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("6010") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.DEBIT);
        boolean hasInventoryCredit = je.getLines().stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.accountCode().equals("1200") &&
                     l.type() == JournalEntry.EntryType.CREDIT);

        assertThat(hasCogsDebit).isTrue();
        assertThat(hasInventoryCredit).isTrue();
    }
}

Size: 6.2 KB | Modified: 2026-04-02 10:03:43

package com.saas.accounting.unit;

import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry;
import com.saas.accounting.core.JournalEntry.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class JournalEntryTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-acc";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private JournalEntry entry() {
        return JournalEntry.create(TENANT, LocalDate.now(),
                "Test entry", "MANUAL", null, CCY, "admin");
    }

    private Money idr(long amount) {
        return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(amount), CCY);
    }

    // ── Balanced posting ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Balanced entry (DR == CR) posts successfully")
    void balancedEntryPosts() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        je.debit ("1100", idr(500_000), "AR");
        je.credit("4010", idr(500_000), "Sales");

        assertThatNoException().isThrownBy(() -> je.post("JE-001", "admin"));
        assertThat(je.getStatus()).isEqualTo(JournalEntryStatus.POSTED);
        assertThat(je.getEntryNumber()).isEqualTo("JE-001");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Multi-line balanced entry posts (one DR, multiple CRs)")
    void multiLineBalanced() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        je.debit ("1100", idr(555_000), "AR total");
        je.credit("4010", idr(500_000), "Revenue");
        je.credit("2100", idr(55_000),  "PPN 11%");

        assertThatNoException().isThrownBy(() -> je.post("JE-002", "admin"));
        assertThat(je.isBalanced()).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Unbalanced posting blocked ────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Unbalanced entry throws UnbalancedJournalEntryException")
    void unbalancedEntryThrows() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        je.debit ("1100", idr(500_000), "AR");
        je.credit("4010", idr(400_000), "Revenue — WRONG");

        assertThatExceptionOfType(UnbalancedJournalEntryException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> je.post("JE-003", "admin"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Entry with only one line cannot be posted")
    void singleLineThrows() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        je.debit("1100", idr(100_000), "Only debit");
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> je.post("JE-004", "admin"));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Empty entry cannot be posted")
    void emptyEntryThrows() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> je.post("JE-005", "admin"));
    }

    // ── Immutability after posting ─────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Cannot add lines to a POSTED entry")
    void noModificationAfterPosting() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        je.debit ("1020", idr(100_000), "Bank");
        je.credit("1100", idr(100_000), "AR");
        je.post("JE-006", "admin");

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> je.debit("5010", idr(10_000), "Expense"));
    }

    // ── Reversal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Reversal entry swaps debit/credit and is auto-posted")
    void reversalSwapsEntries() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        je.debit ("1100", idr(500_000), "AR");
        je.credit("4010", idr(500_000), "Sales");
        je.post("JE-007", "admin");

        JournalEntry reversal = je.createReversal(LocalDate.now(), "admin");
        reversal.post("JE-007-REV", "admin");

        assertThat(reversal.getStatus()).isEqualTo(JournalEntryStatus.POSTED);
        assertThat(reversal.getReversalOf()).isEqualTo(je.getId());

        // Original was DR 1100 / CR 4010
        // Reversal should be CR 1100 / DR 4010
        boolean hasCreditAr = reversal.getLines().stream()
                .anyMatch(l -> l.accountCode().equals("1100")
                        && l.type() == EntryType.CREDIT);
        boolean hasDebitSales = reversal.getLines().stream()
                .anyMatch(l -> l.accountCode().equals("4010")
                        && l.type() == EntryType.DEBIT);

        assertThat(hasCreditAr).isTrue();
        assertThat(hasDebitSales).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Cannot reverse a DRAFT entry")
    void cannotReverseDraft() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        je.debit ("1100", idr(100_000), "AR");
        je.credit("4010", idr(100_000), "Sales");
        // NOT posted

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> je.createReversal(LocalDate.now(), "admin"));
    }

    // ── Currency guard ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Cross-currency line throws")
    void crossCurrencyThrows() {
        JournalEntry je = entry(); // currency = IDR
        assertThatIllegalArgumentException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> je.debit("1100",
                        Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), "USD"), "USD line"));
    }

    // ── Totals ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("getTotalDebits and getTotalCredits computed correctly")
    void totalsComputed() {
        JournalEntry je = entry();
        je.debit ("1100", idr(600_000), "AR");
        je.credit("4010", idr(500_000), "Sales");
        je.credit("2100", idr(100_000), "PPN");

        assertThat(je.getTotalDebits().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("600000");
        assertThat(je.getTotalCredits().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("600000");
        assertThat(je.isBalanced()).isTrue();
    }
}

package com.saas.budget.unit;

import com.saas.budget.core.BudgetAggregate;
import com.saas.budget.core.BudgetAggregate.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class BudgetAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-budget";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "cfo";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    private BudgetAggregate newBudget() {
        return BudgetAggregate.create(TENANT, "Annual 2024", CCY,
                BudgetPeriodType.ANNUAL,
                LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), LocalDate.of(2024, 12, 31), ACTOR);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("New budget starts DRAFT with no lines")
    void newBudgetIsDraft() {
        BudgetAggregate b = newBudget();
        assertThat(b.getStatus()).isEqualTo(BudgetStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(b.getLines()).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("addLine creates budget line")
    void addLine() {
        BudgetAggregate b = newBudget();
        b.addLine("4010", "Sales Revenue", BudgetLineType.REVENUE, idr(100_000_000), null, ACTOR);
        assertThat(b.getLines()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(b.getTotalBudgetedRevenue().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("100000000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("approve transitions to APPROVED")
    void approve() {
        BudgetAggregate b = newBudget();
        b.addLine("4010", "Revenue", BudgetLineType.REVENUE, idr(50_000_000), null, ACTOR);
        b.approve(ACTOR);
        assertThat(b.getStatus()).isEqualTo(BudgetStatus.APPROVED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot approve empty budget")
    void cannotApproveEmpty() {
        BudgetAggregate b = newBudget();
        assertThatIllegalStateException().isThrownBy(() -> b.approve(ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("updateActual sets actual amount on correct line")
    void updateActual() {
        BudgetAggregate b = newBudget();
        b.addLine("4010", "Sales", BudgetLineType.REVENUE, idr(100_000_000), null, ACTOR);
        b.addLine("5010", "Salary", BudgetLineType.EXPENSE, idr(60_000_000), null, ACTOR);
        b.updateActual("4010", idr(95_000_000), ACTOR);

        var revenueLines = b.getLines().stream()
                .filter(l -> l.accountCode().equals("4010")).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(revenueLines.actual().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("95000000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Variance report — revenue actual < budget is unfavorable")
    void varianceReportRevenue() {
        BudgetAggregate b = newBudget();
        b.addLine("4010", "Sales", BudgetLineType.REVENUE, idr(100_000_000), null, ACTOR);
        b.updateActual("4010", idr(80_000_000), ACTOR); // missed target

        var variance = b.getVarianceReport();
        assertThat(variance).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(variance.get(0).isFavorable()).isFalse();
        assertThat(variance.get(0).absoluteVariance().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("20000000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Variance report — expense actual < budget is favorable")
    void varianceReportExpense() {
        BudgetAggregate b = newBudget();
        b.addLine("5010", "Salary", BudgetLineType.EXPENSE, idr(60_000_000), null, ACTOR);
        b.updateActual("5010", idr(50_000_000), ACTOR); // spent less than planned

        var variance = b.getVarianceReport();
        assertThat(variance.get(0).isFavorable()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Duplicate account code replaces existing line")
    void duplicateAccountReplaces() {
        BudgetAggregate b = newBudget();
        b.addLine("4010", "Sales v1", BudgetLineType.REVENUE, idr(50_000_000), null, ACTOR);
        b.addLine("4010", "Sales v2", BudgetLineType.REVENUE, idr(80_000_000), null, ACTOR);
        assertThat(b.getLines()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(b.getLines().get(0).budgeted().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("80000000");
    }
}

package com.saas.catalog.unit;

import com.saas.catalog.core.BrandAggregate;
import com.saas.catalog.core.CategoryAggregate;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class CatalogAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-catalog";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "catalog-manager";

    // ── Category ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("Root category has no parentId and isRoot=true")
    void rootCategoryIsRoot() {
        CategoryAggregate cat = CategoryAggregate.createRoot(
                TENANT, "electronics", "Electronics", "All electronics", ACTOR);
        assertThat(cat.isRoot()).isTrue();
        assertThat(cat.getParentId()).isNull();
        assertThat(cat.getSlug()).isEqualTo("electronics");
        assertThat(cat.isActive()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Slug is normalized to lowercase with hyphens")
    void slugNormalized() {
        CategoryAggregate cat = CategoryAggregate.createRoot(
                TENANT, "Gaming Laptops & Accessories!", "Gaming", null, ACTOR);
        // Should not contain uppercase or special chars
        assertThat(cat.getSlug()).matches("[a-z0-9-]+");
        assertThat(cat.getSlug()).doesNotContain("!").doesNotContain(" ");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Child category stores parentId and parentSlug")
    void childCategoryStoresParent() {
        CategoryAggregate child = CategoryAggregate.createChild(
                TENANT, "laptops", "Laptops", "Laptop computers",
                "parent-id-001", "electronics", 1, ACTOR);
        assertThat(child.isRoot()).isFalse();
        assertThat(child.getParentId()).isEqualTo("parent-id-001");
        assertThat(child.getParentSlug()).isEqualTo("electronics");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("rename updates name and description")
    void renameCategory() {
        CategoryAggregate cat = CategoryAggregate.createRoot(
                TENANT, "gadgets", "Gadgets", "Tech gadgets", ACTOR);
        cat.rename("Consumer Electronics", "All consumer electronics", ACTOR);
        assertThat(cat.getName()).isEqualTo("Consumer Electronics");
        assertThat(cat.getDescription()).isEqualTo("All consumer electronics");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("setAttributeSchema stores schema map")
    void setAttributeSchema() {
        CategoryAggregate cat = CategoryAggregate.createRoot(
                TENANT, "computers", "Computers", null, ACTOR);
        Map<String, Object> schema = Map.of(
                "ram_gb",    Map.of("type", "integer", "required", true),
                "storage_gb", Map.of("type", "integer", "required", true),
                "brand",     Map.of("type", "string", "required", false));
        cat.setAttributeSchema(schema, ACTOR);
        assertThat(cat.getCustomAttributeSchema()).containsKey("ram_gb");
        assertThat(cat.getCustomAttributeSchema()).containsKey("storage_gb");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("deactivate sets isActive to false")
    void deactivateCategory() {
        CategoryAggregate cat = CategoryAggregate.createRoot(
                TENANT, "old-cat", "Old Category", null, ACTOR);
        cat.deactivate(ACTOR);
        assertThat(cat.isActive()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("activate re-enables a deactivated category")
    void reactivateCategory() {
        CategoryAggregate cat = CategoryAggregate.createRoot(
                TENANT, "revived", "Revived", null, ACTOR);
        cat.deactivate(ACTOR);
        cat.activate(ACTOR);
        assertThat(cat.isActive()).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Brand ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("New brand is active with correct slug")
    void newBrand() {
        BrandAggregate brand = BrandAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "Samsung", "Samsung", "Korean electronics giant",
                "South Korea", ACTOR);
        assertThat(brand.isActive()).isTrue();
        assertThat(brand.getSlug()).isEqualTo("samsung");
        assertThat(brand.getCountryOfOrigin()).isEqualTo("South Korea");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Brand slug is lowercase normalized")
    void brandSlugNormalized() {
        BrandAggregate brand = BrandAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "Apple Inc.", "Apple", null, "USA", ACTOR);
        assertThat(brand.getSlug()).matches("[a-z0-9-]+");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("update changes brand details")
    void updateBrand() {
        BrandAggregate brand = BrandAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "dell", "Dell", null, "USA", ACTOR);
        brand.update("Dell Technologies", "Entsyirkahrise and consumer IT",
                "https://logo.dell.com/logo.png", "https://dell.com", ACTOR);
        assertThat(brand.getName()).isEqualTo("Dell Technologies");
        assertThat(brand.getWebsiteUrl()).isEqualTo("https://dell.com");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("deactivate brand sets isActive to false")
    void deactivateBrand() {
        BrandAggregate brand = BrandAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "obsolete-brand", "Obsolete", null, "XX", ACTOR);
        brand.deactivate(ACTOR);
        assertThat(brand.isActive()).isFalse();
    }
}

package com.saas.currency.unit;

import com.saas.currency.core.ExchangeRateAggregate;
import com.saas.currency.core.ExchangeRateAggregate.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class ExchangeRateAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-ccy";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "finance";

    private ExchangeRateAggregate usdToIdr() {
        ExchangeRateAggregate er = ExchangeRateAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "IDR", "USD", ACTOR);
        er.addRate(new BigDecimal("15500"), RateType.AVERAGE,
                LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), "BI", ACTOR);
        return er;
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Create rate pair stores base and target currencies")
    void createRatePair() {
        ExchangeRateAggregate er = usdToIdr();
        assertThat(er.getBaseCurrency()).isEqualTo("IDR");
        assertThat(er.getTargetCurrency()).isEqualTo("USD");
        assertThat(er.getRates()).hasSize(1);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("toBase converts USD → IDR correctly")
    void toBase() {
        ExchangeRateAggregate er = usdToIdr();
        Money usd100 = Money.of(new BigDecimal("100"), "USD");
        Money idr = er.toBase(usd100, LocalDate.now(), RateType.AVERAGE);

        // 100 USD × 15500 = 1,550,000 IDR
        assertThat(idr.getCurrencyCode()).isEqualTo("IDR");
        assertThat(idr.getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1550000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("fromBase converts IDR → USD correctly")
    void fromBase() {
        ExchangeRateAggregate er = usdToIdr();
        Money idr = Money.of(new BigDecimal("1550000"), "IDR");
        Money usd = er.fromBase(idr, LocalDate.now(), RateType.AVERAGE);

        // 1,550,000 / 15500 = 100 USD
        assertThat(usd.getCurrencyCode()).isEqualTo("USD");
        assertThat(usd.getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("100.0000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("rateFor returns closest past rate")
    void rateForDate() {
        ExchangeRateAggregate er = ExchangeRateAggregate.create(TENANT, "IDR", "USD", ACTOR);
        er.addRate(new BigDecimal("14500"), RateType.AVERAGE,
                LocalDate.of(2022, 1, 1), "BI", ACTOR);
        er.addRate(new BigDecimal("15500"), RateType.AVERAGE,
                LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), "BI", ACTOR);

        // Before 2024 → should use 14500
        var rate = er.rateFor(LocalDate.of(2023, 6, 1), RateType.AVERAGE);
        assertThat(rate).isPresent();
        assertThat(rate.get().rate()).isEqualByComparingTo("14500");

        // After 2024-01-01 → should use 15500
        var rateNew = er.rateFor(LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1), RateType.AVERAGE);
        assertThat(rateNew.get().rate()).isEqualByComparingTo("15500");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Wrong currency in toBase throws IllegalArgumentException")
    void wrongCurrencyThrows() {
        ExchangeRateAggregate er = usdToIdr();
        Money euros = Money.of(new BigDecimal("100"), "EUR");
        assertThatIllegalArgumentException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> er.toBase(euros, LocalDate.now(), RateType.AVERAGE));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("No rate for date throws NoRateException")
    void noRateThrows() {
        ExchangeRateAggregate er = ExchangeRateAggregate.create(TENANT, "IDR", "USD", ACTOR);
        er.addRate(new BigDecimal("15500"), RateType.AVERAGE,
                LocalDate.of(2025, 1, 1), "BI", ACTOR); // future rate
        Money usd = Money.of(BigDecimal.ONE, "USD");
        assertThatExceptionOfType(NoRateException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> er.toBase(usd, LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), RateType.AVERAGE));
    }
}

package com.saas.customer.integration;

import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate;
import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate.*;
import com.saas.customer.service.CustomerService;
import com.saas.customer.service.CustomerService.DuplicateEmailException;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import io.quarkus.test.junit.QuarkusTest;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

@QuarkusTest
@TestMethodOrder(MethodOrderer.OrderAnnotation.class)
class CustomerServiceIntegrationTest {

    @Inject CustomerService customerService;

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-customer-it";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "admin";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";
    private static String customerId;

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    @Test @Order(1)
    @DisplayName("Register customer → ACTIVE with defaults")
    @Transactional
    void register() {
        CustomerAggregate c = customerService.register(
                TENANT, "PT Pelanggan Setia",
                "pelanggan@example.com", CustomerType.COMPANY, CCY, ACTOR);

        assertThat(c.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(c.getStatus()).isEqualTo(CustomerStatus.ACTIVE);
        assertThat(c.getSegment()).isEqualTo("retail");
        assertThat(c.isCashOnly()).isTrue();
        customerId = c.getId();
    }

    @Test @Order(2)
    @DisplayName("Duplicate email throws DuplicateEmailException")
    @Transactional
    void duplicateEmailThrows() {
        customerService.register(TENANT, "Other Co", "dup@example.com",
                CustomerType.INDIVIDUAL, CCY, ACTOR);
        assertThatExceptionOfType(DuplicateEmailException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> customerService.register(TENANT, "Also Other",
                        "dup@example.com", CustomerType.INDIVIDUAL, CCY, ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @Order(3)
    @DisplayName("Same email different tenant is allowed")
    @Transactional
    void sameEmailDifferentTenant() {
        customerService.register(TENANT, "Co A", "shared@email.com",
                CustomerType.COMPANY, CCY, ACTOR);
        assertThatNoException().isThrownBy(() ->
                customerService.register("other-tenant", "Co B", "shared@email.com",
                        CustomerType.COMPANY, CCY, ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @Order(4)
    @DisplayName("Set credit limit persisted correctly")
    @Transactional
    void setCreditLimit() {
        assumeCreated();
        CustomerAggregate c = customerService.setCreditLimit(
                customerId, TENANT, idr(50_000_000), 30, ACTOR);

        assertThat(c.getCreditLimit().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("50000000");
        assertThat(c.getPaymentTermsDays()).isEqualTo(30);
        assertThat(c.isCashOnly()).isFalse();

        // Reload and verify
        CustomerAggregate reloaded = customerService
                .findById(customerId, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(reloaded.getCreditLimit().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("50000000");
    }

    @Test @Order(5)
    @DisplayName("Set segment persisted correctly")
    @Transactional
    void setSegment() {
        assumeCreated();
        customerService.setSegment(customerId, TENANT, "vip", ACTOR);
        CustomerAggregate c = customerService.findById(customerId, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(c.getSegment()).isEqualTo("vip");
    }

    @Test @Order(6)
    @DisplayName("Set billing address persisted correctly")
    @Transactional
    void setBillingAddress() {
        assumeCreated();
        Address addr = Address.builder()
                .line1("Jl. Pelanggan No. 1")
                .city("Jakarta").countryCode("ID").build();
        customerService.setBillingAddress(customerId, TENANT, addr, ACTOR);

        CustomerAggregate c = customerService.findById(customerId, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(c.getBillingAddress()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(c.getBillingAddress().getCity()).isEqualTo("Jakarta");
    }

    @Test @Order(7)
    @DisplayName("findByEmail returns correct customer")
    void findByEmail() {
        assumeCreated();
        var found = customerService.findByEmail("pelanggan@example.com", TENANT);
        assertThat(found).isPresent();
        assertThat(found.get().getId()).isEqualTo(customerId);
    }

    @Test @Order(8)
    @DisplayName("Deactivate → INACTIVE status")
    @Transactional
    void deactivate() {
        assumeCreated();
        customerService.deactivate(customerId, TENANT, ACTOR);
        CustomerAggregate c = customerService.findById(customerId, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(c.getStatus()).isEqualTo(CustomerStatus.INACTIVE);
    }

    @Test @Order(9)
    @DisplayName("Tenant isolation — wrong tenant returns empty")
    void tenantIsolation() {
        assumeCreated();
        assertThat(customerService.findById(customerId, "wrong-tenant")).isEmpty();
    }

    private void assumeCreated() {
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions.assumeTrue(
                customerId != null, "Skipping: customer not created");
    }
}

package com.saas.customer.unit;

import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate;
import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class CustomerAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-cust";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "admin";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private CustomerAggregate newCustomer() {
        return CustomerAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "PT Maju Jaya", "maju@example.com",
                CustomerType.COMPANY, CCY, ACTOR);
    }

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    // ── Construction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("New customer is ACTIVE with zero balance")
    void newCustomerIsActive() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        assertThat(c.getStatus()).isEqualTo(CustomerStatus.ACTIVE);
        assertThat(c.getCurrentBalance().isZero()).isTrue();
        assertThat(c.getSegment()).isEqualTo("retail");
        assertThat(c.isCashOnly()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("ID and tenantId are set on creation")
    void idSetOnCreation() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        assertThat(c.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(c.getTenantId()).isEqualTo(TENANT);
    }

    // ── Credit Limit ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("setCreditLimit sets limit and payment terms")
    void setCreditLimit() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        c.setCreditLimit(idr(10_000_000), 30, ACTOR);

        assertThat(c.getCreditLimit().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("10000000");
        assertThat(c.getPaymentTermsDays()).isEqualTo(30);
        assertThat(c.isCashOnly()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("hasAvailableCredit — false when balance + order exceeds limit")
    void hasAvailableCredit() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        c.setCreditLimit(idr(5_000_000), 30, ACTOR);

        // Outstanding balance = 3M, order = 3M → total 6M > 5M limit
        c.debitBalance(idr(3_000_000), ACTOR);
        assertThat(c.hasAvailableCredit(idr(3_000_000))).isFalse();

        // Outstanding 3M, order 1M → 4M < 5M limit
        assertThat(c.hasAvailableCredit(idr(1_000_000))).isTrue();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("hasAvailableCredit — false for cash-only customer")
    void cashOnlyCustomerHasNoCredit() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        assertThat(c.hasAvailableCredit(idr(1_000))).isFalse();
    }

    // ── Balance tracking ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("debitBalance increases currentBalance")
    void debitBalance() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        c.debitBalance(idr(500_000), ACTOR);
        c.debitBalance(idr(250_000), ACTOR);
        assertThat(c.getCurrentBalance().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("750000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("creditBalance decreases currentBalance")
    void creditBalance() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        c.debitBalance(idr(1_000_000), ACTOR);
        c.creditBalance(idr(400_000), ACTOR);
        assertThat(c.getCurrentBalance().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("600000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("creditBalance beyond balance throws")
    void creditBeyondBalanceThrows() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        c.debitBalance(idr(100_000), ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalArgumentException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> c.creditBalance(idr(200_000), ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Segment ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("setSegment changes segment")
    void setSegment() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        c.setSegment("vip", ACTOR);
        assertThat(c.getSegment()).isEqualTo("vip");
    }

    // ── Addresses ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("getShippingAddress falls back to billingAddress when not set")
    void shippingFallback() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        Address billing = Address.builder()
                .line1("Jl. Sudirman 1").city("Jakarta")
                .countryCode("ID").build();
        c.setBillingAddress(billing, ACTOR);

        assertThat(c.getShippingAddress()).isEqualTo(billing);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Separate shipping address overrides billing fallback")
    void separateShipping() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        Address billing  = Address.builder().line1("Jl. A").city("Jakarta").countryCode("ID").build();
        Address shipping = Address.builder().line1("Jl. B").city("Surabaya").countryCode("ID").build();
        c.setBillingAddress(billing, ACTOR);
        c.setShippingAddress(shipping, ACTOR);

        assertThat(c.getShippingAddress().getCity()).isEqualTo("Surabaya");
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("deactivate sets status to INACTIVE")
    void deactivate() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        c.deactivate(ACTOR);
        assertThat(c.getStatus()).isEqualTo(CustomerStatus.INACTIVE);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot update profile of inactive customer")
    void cannotUpdateInactive() {
        CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer();
        c.deactivate(ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> c.updateProfile("New Name", "new@x.com", null, null, ACTOR));
    }
}

package com.saas.employee.unit;

import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate;
import com.saas.employee.core.EmployeeAggregate.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class EmployeeAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-hr";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "hr-admin";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    private EmployeeAggregate newEmployee() {
        return EmployeeAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "EMP-TEN-00001",
                "Budi", "Santoso", "budi@company.co.id",
                "DEPT-ENG", "Engineering", "Software Engineer",
                EmploymentType.PERMANENT,
                LocalDate.of(2023, 1, 15),
                idr(12_000_000), ACTOR);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("New employee is ACTIVE with correct fields")
    void newEmployeeIsActive() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        assertThat(emp.getStatus()).isEqualTo(EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE);
        assertThat(emp.getFullName()).isEqualTo("Budi Santoso");
        assertThat(emp.getBasicSalary().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("12000000");
        assertThat(emp.isEmployed()).isTrue();
        assertThat(emp.getId()).isNotBlank();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("updatePersonalInfo changes contact details")
    void updatePersonalInfo() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        emp.updatePersonalInfo("Budi", "Pratama", "budi.p@company.co.id",
                "+6281234567890", LocalDate.of(1990, 5, 20),
                "1234567890123456", "NPWP-001", ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getEmail()).isEqualTo("budi.p@company.co.id");
        assertThat(emp.getNationalId()).isEqualTo("1234567890123456");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("reviseSalary changes basic salary")
    void reviseSalary() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        long v1 = emp.getAudit().getVersion();
        emp.reviseSalary(idr(15_000_000), ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getBasicSalary().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("15000000");
        assertThat(emp.getAudit().getVersion()).isGreaterThan(v1);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot revise salary of terminated employee")
    void cannotReviseSalaryIfTerminated() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        emp.terminate(LocalDate.now(), "Company closure", ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> emp.reviseSalary(idr(16_000_000), ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("transfer changes department and position")
    void transfer() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        emp.transfer("DEPT-PM", "Product", "POS-PM-001", "Product Manager", ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getDepartmentName()).isEqualTo("Product");
        assertThat(emp.getPositionTitle()).isEqualTo("Product Manager");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("putOnLeave → ON_LEAVE; returnFromLeave → ACTIVE")
    void leaveLifecycle() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        emp.putOnLeave(ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getStatus()).isEqualTo(EmployeeStatus.ON_LEAVE);
        assertThat(emp.isEmployed()).isTrue(); // still counts as employed

        emp.returnFromLeave(ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getStatus()).isEqualTo(EmployeeStatus.ACTIVE);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("resign sets RESIGNED status with date and reason")
    void resign() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        emp.resign(LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 30), "Better opportunity", ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getStatus()).isEqualTo(EmployeeStatus.RESIGNED);
        assertThat(emp.getTerminationDate()).isEqualTo(LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 30));
        assertThat(emp.getTerminationReason()).isEqualTo("Better opportunity");
        assertThat(emp.isEmployed()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("terminate sets TERMINATED status")
    void terminate() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        emp.terminate(LocalDate.now(), "Misconduct", ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getStatus()).isEqualTo(EmployeeStatus.TERMINATED);
        assertThat(emp.isEmployed()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("setBankDetails persists bank account")
    void setBankDetails() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        emp.setBankDetails("0987654321", "Bank Mandiri", ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getBankAccount()).isEqualTo("0987654321");
        assertThat(emp.getBankName()).isEqualTo("Bank Mandiri");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("setBpjsNumbers persists BPJS IDs")
    void setBpjsNumbers() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee();
        emp.setBpjsNumbers("KT-001", "KS-001", ACTOR);
        assertThat(emp.getBpjsKetenagakerjaanNo()).isEqualTo("KT-001");
        assertThat(emp.getBpjsKesehatanNo()).isEqualTo("KS-001");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("getLengthOfServiceDays reflects time since hireDate")
    void lengthOfService() {
        EmployeeAggregate emp = newEmployee(); // hired 2023-01-15
        assertThat(emp.getLengthOfServiceDays()).isGreaterThan(0);
    }
}

package com.saas.fixedasset.unit;

import com.saas.fixedasset.core.FixedAssetAggregate;
import com.saas.fixedasset.core.FixedAssetAggregate.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class FixedAssetAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-asset";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "finance";

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), "IDR"); }

    private FixedAssetAggregate newStraightLine() {
        return FixedAssetAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "FA-IT-00001", "Dell Laptop",
                AssetCategory.IT_EQUIPMENT,
                idr(15_000_000), idr(1_500_000),   // cost, salvage
                LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1),
                DepreciationMethod.STRAIGHT_LINE,
                5,          // useful life: 5 years
                null, "1510", "5060", "1520", ACTOR);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("New asset starts in DRAFT")
    void newAssetIsDraft() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = newStraightLine();
        assertThat(fa.getStatus()).isEqualTo(AssetStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(fa.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(fa.getDepreciationHistory()).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Book value = cost at creation (no depreciation yet)")
    void initialBookValue() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = newStraightLine();
        assertThat(fa.getBookValue().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("15000000");
        assertThat(fa.getAccumulatedDepreciation().isZero()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Put in service transitions to ACTIVE")
    void putInService() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = newStraightLine();
        fa.putInService(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 15), ACTOR);
        assertThat(fa.getStatus()).isEqualTo(AssetStatus.ACTIVE);
        assertThat(fa.getInServiceDate()).isEqualTo(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 15));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("DRAFT asset cannot be depreciated")
    void draftCannotDepreciate() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = newStraightLine();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> fa.depreciate(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 31), ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Straight-line depreciation: monthly = (cost - salvage) / (years × 12)")
    void straightLineMonthlyDepreciation() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = newStraightLine();
        fa.putInService(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), ACTOR);

        // (15,000,000 - 1,500,000) / (5 × 12) = 13,500,000 / 60 = 225,000/month
        DepreciationEntry entry = fa.depreciate(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 31), ACTOR);
        assertThat(entry.depreciationAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("225000");
        assertThat(fa.getBookValue().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("14775000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Multiple depreciation periods accumulate correctly")
    void multiplePeriodsAccumulate() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = newStraightLine();
        fa.putInService(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), ACTOR);

        for (int m = 1; m <= 3; m++) {
            fa.depreciate(LocalDate.of(2024, m, LocalDate.of(2024, m, 1).lengthOfMonth()), ACTOR);
        }

        // After 3 months: 3 × 225,000 = 675,000 accumulated
        assertThat(fa.getAccumulatedDepreciation().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("675000");
        assertThat(fa.getBookValue().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("14325000");
        assertThat(fa.getDepreciationHistory()).hasSize(3);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Declining balance depreciation uses book value × rate")
    void decliningBalance() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = FixedAssetAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "FA-VHC-00001", "Company Car",
                AssetCategory.VEHICLE, idr(200_000_000), idr(20_000_000),
                LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1),
                DepreciationMethod.DECLINING_BALANCE,
                8, new BigDecimal("0.20"),
                "1500", "5060", "1520", ACTOR);
        fa.putInService(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), ACTOR);

        // Monthly rate = 20% / 12 = 1.667%
        // First month: 200,000,000 × 1.667% ≈ 3,333,333
        DepreciationEntry entry = fa.depreciate(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 31), ACTOR);
        BigDecimal expected = new BigDecimal("200000000")
                .multiply(new BigDecimal("0.20"))
                .divide(BigDecimal.valueOf(12), 4, java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
        assertThat(entry.depreciationAmount().getAmount())
                .isCloseTo(expected, org.assertj.core.data.Offset.offset(new BigDecimal("1")));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Fully depreciated asset cannot be depreciated further")
    void fullyDepreciatedBlocks() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = FixedAssetAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "FA-IT-99999", "Old Laptop",
                AssetCategory.IT_EQUIPMENT, idr(1_000_000), idr(1_000_000), // cost == salvage
                LocalDate.of(2020, 1, 1),
                DepreciationMethod.STRAIGHT_LINE,
                1, null, "1510", "5060", "1520", ACTOR);
        fa.putInService(LocalDate.of(2020, 1, 1), ACTOR);

        assertThat(fa.isFullyDepreciated()).isTrue();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> fa.depreciate(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 31), ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Disposal computes gain when proceeds > book value")
    void disposalGain() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = newStraightLine();
        fa.putInService(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), ACTOR);
        fa.depreciate(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 31), ACTOR); // book = 14,775,000

        DisposalResult result = fa.dispose(
                LocalDate.of(2024, 2, 1), idr(16_000_000), "Sold to third party", ACTOR);

        assertThat(fa.getStatus()).isEqualTo(AssetStatus.DISPOSED);
        assertThat(result.isGain()).isTrue();
        assertThat(result.gainLoss().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1225000"); // 16M - 14.775M
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Disposal computes loss when proceeds < book value")
    void disposalLoss() {
        FixedAssetAggregate fa = newStraightLine();
        fa.putInService(LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), ACTOR);

        DisposalResult result = fa.dispose(
                LocalDate.of(2024, 2, 1), idr(10_000_000), "Written off", ACTOR);

        assertThat(result.isGain()).isFalse();
        assertThat(result.gainLoss().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("5000000"); // 15M - 10M
    }
}

syirkah-modules/src/test/java/com/saas/inventory/integration/InventoryStockIntegrationTest.java
Size: 9.4 KB | Modified: 2026-04-03 08:38:55

package com.saas.inventory.integration;

import com.saas.inventory.core.StockLedger;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement.MovementType;
import com.saas.inventory.service.InventoryService;
import io.quarkus.test.junit.QuarkusTest;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Integration test for inventory stock ledger: receive → reserve → dispatch.
 */
@QuarkusTest
@TestMethodOrder(MethodOrderer.OrderAnnotation.class)
class InventoryStockIntegrationTest {

    @Inject InventoryService inventoryService;

    private static final String TENANT    = "tenant-inv-it";
    private static final String PRODUCT   = "PROD-IT-WIDGET";
    private static final String SKU       = "IT-WIDGET-001";
    private static final String WAREHOUSE = "WH-IT-MAIN";
    private static final String ACTOR     = "warehouse-staff";

    // ── Test 1: Create ledger ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(1)
    @DisplayName("Create stock ledger → zero stock")
    @Transactional
    void createLedger() {
        StockLedger ledger = inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);

        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(0);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(0);
        assertThat(ledger.isOutOfStock()).isTrue();

        // Reload from DB
        StockLedger reloaded = inventoryService
                .getStock(TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE)
                .orElseThrow();
        assertThat(reloaded.getReorderPoint()).isEqualTo(10);
    }

    // ── Test 2: Idempotent creation ───────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(2)
    @DisplayName("Creating ledger twice returns same ledger (idempotent)")
    @Transactional
    void idempotentLedgerCreation() {
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);

        // Should still be one ledger
        StockLedger ledger = inventoryService
                .getStock(TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE)
                .orElseThrow();
        assertThat(ledger).isNotNull();
    }

    // ── Test 3: Receive stock ─────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(3)
    @DisplayName("Receive stock → onHand increases, movement recorded")
    @Transactional
    void receiveStock() {
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);

        StockMovement mv = inventoryService.receiveStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE,
                100, MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, "PO-IT-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(mv.quantity()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(mv.type()).isEqualTo(MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT);
        assertThat(mv.snapshotOnHand()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(mv.isStockIn()).isTrue();

        StockLedger ledger = inventoryService
                .getStock(TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(ledger.isOutOfStock()).isFalse();
        assertThat(ledger.needsReorder()).isFalse();
    }

    // ── Test 4: Reserve ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(4)
    @DisplayName("Reserve stock → available reduced, onHand unchanged")
    @Transactional
    void reserveStock() {
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);
        inventoryService.receiveStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE,
                100, MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, "PO-IT-002", ACTOR);

        StockMovement mv = inventoryService.reserveStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE, 40, "ORD-IT-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(mv.type()).isEqualTo(MovementType.RESERVATION);

        StockLedger ledger = inventoryService
                .getStock(TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(ledger.getReserved()).isEqualTo(40);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(60);
    }

    // ── Test 5: Insufficient stock blocked ───────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(5)
    @DisplayName("Over-reservation throws InsufficientStockException")
    @Transactional
    void insufficientStockThrows() {
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);
        inventoryService.receiveStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE,
                50, MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, "OB-IT-001", ACTOR);

        assertThatException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> inventoryService.reserveStock(
                        TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE,
                        60, "ORD-IT-OVR", ACTOR))
                .withMessageContaining("Insufficient");
    }

    // ── Test 6: Dispatch ──────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(6)
    @DisplayName("Dispatch → onHand and reserved both reduced")
    @Transactional
    void dispatchStock() {
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);
        inventoryService.receiveStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE,
                100, MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, "PO-IT-003", ACTOR);
        inventoryService.reserveStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE, 30, "ORD-IT-002", ACTOR);

        inventoryService.dispatchStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE, 30, "SHP-IT-001", ACTOR);

        StockLedger ledger = inventoryService
                .getStock(TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(70);
        assertThat(ledger.getReserved()).isEqualTo(0);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(70);
    }

    // ── Test 7: Movement history ──────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(7)
    @DisplayName("Movement history records all operations in order")
    @Transactional
    void movementHistory() {
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);
        inventoryService.receiveStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE,
                50, MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, "OB-HIST", ACTOR);

        List<StockMovement> history = inventoryService
                .getMovementHistory(TENANT, PRODUCT, 20);

        assertThat(history).isNotEmpty();
        assertThat(history.get(0).productId()).isEqualTo(PRODUCT);
    }

    // ── Test 8: Low-stock report ──────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(8)
    @DisplayName("Low stock list includes ledger at reorder point")
    @Transactional
    void lowStockReport() {
        // Create a separate ledger for this test with known state
        String lowProd = "PROD-IT-LOW";
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, lowProd, "IT-LOW-SKU", WAREHOUSE, 15, 30, ACTOR);
        // Receive exactly at reorder point
        inventoryService.receiveStock(
                TENANT, lowProd, WAREHOUSE,
                15, MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, "OB-LOW", ACTOR);

        List<StockLedger> lowStock = inventoryService.getLowStock(TENANT);
        boolean found = lowStock.stream().anyMatch(
                l -> l.getProductId().equals(lowProd));
        assertThat(found).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Test 9: Adjustment ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(9)
    @DisplayName("Positive and negative adjustments work correctly")
    @Transactional
    void stockAdjustment() {
        inventoryService.createLedger(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, SKU, WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);
        inventoryService.receiveStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE,
                50, MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, "OB-ADJ", ACTOR);

        // Stock count shows 55 actual — adjust +5
        inventoryService.adjustStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE, +5, "COUNT-001", ACTOR);

        StockLedger ledger = inventoryService
                .getStock(TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(55);

        // Write-off of 3 damaged units — adjust -3
        inventoryService.adjustStock(
                TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE, -3, "DAMAGE-001", ACTOR);

        ledger = inventoryService
                .getStock(TENANT, PRODUCT, WAREHOUSE).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(52);
    }
}
