package com.saas.inventory.unit;

import com.saas.inventory.core.InsufficientStockException;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockLedger;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement.MovementType;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class StockLedgerTest {

    private static final String TENANT    = "tenant-inv";
    private static final String PRODUCT   = "prod-001";
    private static final String WAREHOUSE = "wh-main";
    private static final String ACTOR     = "system";

    private StockLedger ledger;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        ledger = StockLedger.create(TENANT, PRODUCT, "SKU-001",
                WAREHOUSE, 10, 20, ACTOR);
    }

    // ── Receive ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Receiving stock increases onHand")
    void receiveIncreasesOnHand() {
        ledger.receive(50, "PO-001", MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ACTOR);
        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(50);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(50);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Receive returns correct StockMovement")
    void receiveReturnsMovement() {
        StockMovement mv = ledger.receive(30, "PO-002", MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ACTOR);
        assertThat(mv.quantity()).isEqualTo(30);
        assertThat(mv.type()).isEqualTo(MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT);
        assertThat(mv.snapshotOnHand()).isEqualTo(30);
        assertThat(mv.isStockIn()).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Reserve ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Reserve reduces available but not onHand")
    void reserveReducesAvailable() {
        ledger.receive(100, "PO-003", MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ACTOR);
        ledger.reserve(30, "ORD-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(ledger.getReserved()).isEqualTo(30);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(70);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Reserve beyond available throws InsufficientStockException")
    void reserveBeyondAvailableThrows() {
        ledger.receive(10, "PO-004", MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ACTOR);
        assertThatExceptionOfType(InsufficientStockException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> ledger.reserve(15, "ORD-002", ACTOR));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Multiple reservations are cumulative")
    void multipleReservationsCumulative() {
        ledger.receive(100, "PO-005", MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ACTOR);
        ledger.reserve(20, "ORD-A", ACTOR);
        ledger.reserve(30, "ORD-B", ACTOR);

        assertThat(ledger.getReserved()).isEqualTo(50);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(50);
    }

    // ── Release ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Release reservation restores available")
    void releaseReservation() {
        ledger.receive(100, "PO-006", MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ACTOR);
        ledger.reserve(40, "ORD-C", ACTOR);
        ledger.releaseReservation(40, "ORD-C", ACTOR);

        assertThat(ledger.getReserved()).isEqualTo(0);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(100);
    }

    // ── Dispatch ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Dispatch reduces both onHand and reserved")
    void dispatchReducesBoth() {
        ledger.receive(100, "PO-007", MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ACTOR);
        ledger.reserve(50, "ORD-D", ACTOR);
        ledger.dispatch(50, "SHP-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(50);
        assertThat(ledger.getReserved()).isEqualTo(0);
        assertThat(ledger.getAvailable()).isEqualTo(50);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Dispatch beyond reserved throws")
    void dispatchBeyondReservedThrows() {
        ledger.receive(100, "PO-008", MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, ACTOR);
        ledger.reserve(10, "ORD-E", ACTOR);
        assertThatExceptionOfType(InsufficientStockException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> ledger.dispatch(20, "SHP-002", ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Adjust ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Positive adjustment increases onHand")
    void positiveAdjustment() {
        ledger.receive(50, "OB-001", MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, ACTOR);
        ledger.adjust(10, "COUNT-001", ACTOR);
        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(60);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Negative adjustment decreases onHand")
    void negativeAdjustment() {
        ledger.receive(50, "OB-002", MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, ACTOR);
        ledger.adjust(-5, "COUNT-002", ACTOR);
        assertThat(ledger.getOnHand()).isEqualTo(45);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Adjustment below zero throws")
    void adjustmentBelowZeroThrows() {
        ledger.receive(10, "OB-003", MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalArgumentException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> ledger.adjust(-20, "COUNT-003", ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Alerts ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("needsReorder() true when available <= reorderPoint")
    void needsReorder() {
        ledger.receive(10, "OB-004", MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, ACTOR); // reorderPoint=10
        assertThat(ledger.needsReorder()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("isOutOfStock() true when available == 0")
    void outOfStock() {
        assertThat(ledger.isOutOfStock()).isTrue(); // starts at 0
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("isOutOfStock() false after receiving stock")
    void notOutOfStockAfterReceive() {
        ledger.receive(1, "OB-005", MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, ACTOR);
        assertThat(ledger.isOutOfStock()).isFalse();
    }
}

package com.saas.invoice.integration;

import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate.*;
import com.saas.invoice.service.InvoiceService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import io.quarkus.test.junit.QuarkusTest;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Integration test for the full AR invoice lifecycle:
 * create → lines → PPN → issue → payment → paid
 */
@QuarkusTest
@TestMethodOrder(MethodOrderer.OrderAnnotation.class)
class InvoiceLifecycleIntegrationTest {

    @Inject InvoiceService invoiceService;

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-invoice-it";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "it-admin";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private static String invoiceId;

    private Party customer() {
        return Party.customer("cust-inv-001", "PT Bumi Sejahtera", "NPWP-INV-001");
    }

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    @Test @Order(1)
    @DisplayName("Create AR invoice → DRAFT with net-30 terms")
    @Transactional
    void createDraftInvoice() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = invoiceService.createInvoice(
                TENANT, InvoiceType.RECEIVABLE,
                customer(), CCY, LocalDate.now(), 30, ACTOR);

        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(inv.getDueDate()).isEqualTo(LocalDate.now().plusDays(30));
        invoiceId = inv.getId();
    }

    @Test @Order(2)
    @DisplayName("Add lines → subtotal computed")
    @Transactional
    void addLines() {
        assumeCreated();

        invoiceService.addLine(invoiceId, TENANT,
                "Jasa Konsultasi IT", null, 5, idr(500_000), ACTOR);
        invoiceService.addLine(invoiceId, TENANT,
                "Lisensi Software", "PROD-SW-001", 2, idr(750_000), ACTOR);

        InvoiceAggregate inv = invoiceService.findById(invoiceId, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        // 5×500000 + 2×750000 = 4,000,000
        assertThat(inv.getSubtotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("4000000");
    }

    @Test @Order(3)
    @DisplayName("Add PPN 11% → total = subtotal × 1.11")
    @Transactional
    void addPpnTax() {
        assumeCreated();

        invoiceService.addPpnTax(invoiceId, TENANT, ACTOR);

        InvoiceAggregate inv = invoiceService.findById(invoiceId, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(inv.getTaxLines()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(inv.getTaxLines().get(0).taxCode()).isEqualTo("PPN-11");
        // Tax: 4,000,000 × 11% = 440,000
        assertThat(inv.getTotalTax().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("440000");
        // Total: 4,440,000
        assertThat(inv.getTotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("4440000");
    }

    @Test @Order(4)
    @DisplayName("Issue invoice → ISSUED with invoice number")
    @Transactional
    void issueInvoice() {
        assumeCreated();

        InvoiceAggregate inv = invoiceService.issueInvoice(invoiceId, TENANT, ACTOR);

        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.ISSUED);
        assertThat(inv.getInvoiceNumber()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(inv.getInvoiceNumber()).startsWith("INV-");
    }

    @Test @Order(5)
    @DisplayName("Partial payment → PARTIALLY_PAID, amountDue reduced")
    @Transactional
    void partialPayment() {
        assumeCreated();

        invoiceService.recordPayment(invoiceId, TENANT, idr(2_000_000), ACTOR);

        InvoiceAggregate inv = invoiceService.findById(invoiceId, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.PARTIALLY_PAID);
        assertThat(inv.getAmountPaid().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("2000000");
        assertThat(inv.getAmountDue().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("2440000");
    }

    @Test @Order(6)
    @DisplayName("Final payment → PAID, amountDue = 0")
    @Transactional
    void finalPayment() {
        assumeCreated();

        invoiceService.recordPayment(invoiceId, TENANT, idr(2_440_000), ACTOR);

        InvoiceAggregate inv = invoiceService.findById(invoiceId, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.PAID);
        assertThat(inv.getAmountDue().isZero()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test @Order(7)
    @DisplayName("Paid invoice cannot be voided")
    @Transactional
    void paidInvoiceCannotBeVoided() {
        assumeCreated();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> invoiceService.voidInvoice(invoiceId, TENANT, ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @Order(8)
    @DisplayName("Create AP invoice (payable) for supplier")
    @Transactional
    void createApInvoice() {
        Party supplier = Party.supplier("sup-001", "PT Teknologi Maju", "NPWP-SUP-001");
        InvoiceAggregate inv = invoiceService.createInvoice(
                TENANT, InvoiceType.PAYABLE, supplier, CCY,
                LocalDate.now(), 30, ACTOR);

        assertThat(inv.getType()).isEqualTo(InvoiceType.PAYABLE);
        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.DRAFT);
    }

    @Test @Order(9)
    @DisplayName("findByOrderId returns correct invoice")
    @Transactional
    void findByOrderId() {
        // Create invoice linked to a fake order ID
        InvoiceAggregate inv = invoiceService.createInvoice(
                TENANT, InvoiceType.RECEIVABLE, customer(), CCY,
                LocalDate.now(), 0, ACTOR);
        invoiceService.addLine(inv.getId(), TENANT, "Test", null, 1, idr(100_000), ACTOR);

        // Link to order
        inv = invoiceService.findById(inv.getId(), TENANT).orElseThrow();
        inv.linkOrder("ORDER-IT-999", ACTOR);

        // Note: full round-trip test requires saving — shown as pattern
        assertThat(inv.getOrderId()).isEqualTo("ORDER-IT-999");
    }

    private void assumeCreated() {
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions.assumeTrue(
                invoiceId != null, "Skipping: prior invoice not created");
    }
}

package com.saas.invoice.unit;

import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate;
import com.saas.invoice.core.InvoiceAggregate.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class InvoiceAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-inv";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "admin";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Party customer;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        customer = Party.customer("cust-001", "PT Maju Jaya", "987654321");
    }

    private InvoiceAggregate newDraftInvoice() {
        return InvoiceAggregate.create(TENANT, InvoiceType.RECEIVABLE,
                customer, CCY, LocalDate.now(), 30, ACTOR);
    }

    private Money idr(long amount) {
        return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(amount), CCY);
    }

    // ── Create ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("New invoice starts DRAFT with zero balance")
    void newInvoiceIsDraft() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(inv.getAmountPaid().isZero()).isTrue();
        assertThat(inv.getDueDate()).isEqualTo(LocalDate.now().plusDays(30));
    }

    // ── Totals ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Subtotal computed from lines")
    void subtotalFromLines() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        inv.addLine("Product A", "prod-1", 2, idr(500_000), ACTOR);
        inv.addLine("Product B", "prod-2", 1, idr(300_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(inv.getSubtotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1300000");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Total = subtotal + tax")
    void totalIncludesTax() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        inv.addLine("Product A", "prod-1", 1, idr(1_000_000), ACTOR);
        inv.addPpnTax(ACTOR);

        // 1_000_000 + 11% = 1_110_000
        assertThat(inv.getTotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1110000");
    }

    // ── Issue ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Issue transitions to ISSUED and sets invoice number")
    void issueTransitions() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        inv.addLine("Service", null, 1, idr(500_000), ACTOR);
        inv.issue("INV-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.ISSUED);
        assertThat(inv.getInvoiceNumber()).isEqualTo("INV-001");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Cannot issue empty invoice")
    void issueEmptyThrows() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> inv.issue("INV-002", ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Payment ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Partial payment moves to PARTIALLY_PAID")
    void partialPayment() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        inv.addLine("Service", null, 1, idr(1_000_000), ACTOR);
        inv.issue("INV-003", ACTOR);

        boolean paid = inv.recordPayment(idr(500_000), ACTOR);
        assertThat(paid).isFalse();
        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.PARTIALLY_PAID);
        assertThat(inv.getAmountDue().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("500000");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Full payment moves to PAID")
    void fullPayment() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        inv.addLine("Service", null, 1, idr(1_000_000), ACTOR);
        inv.issue("INV-004", ACTOR);

        boolean paid = inv.recordPayment(idr(1_000_000), ACTOR);
        assertThat(paid).isTrue();
        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.PAID);
        assertThat(inv.getAmountDue().isZero()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Ovsyirkahayment still marks PAID (zero amount due)")
    void ovsyirkahaymentMarksPaid() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        inv.addLine("Service", null, 1, idr(1_000_000), ACTOR);
        inv.issue("INV-005", ACTOR);

        inv.recordPayment(idr(1_200_000), ACTOR);
        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.PAID);
    }

    // ── Void ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Void transitions to VOID")
    void voidInvoice() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        inv.addLine("Service", null, 1, idr(500_000), ACTOR);
        inv.issue("INV-006", ACTOR);
        inv.voidInvoice(ACTOR);
        assertThat(inv.getStatus()).isEqualTo(InvoiceStatus.VOID);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Cannot void a PAID invoice")
    void cannotVoidPaid() {
        InvoiceAggregate inv = newDraftInvoice();
        inv.addLine("Service", null, 1, idr(500_000), ACTOR);
        inv.issue("INV-007", ACTOR);
        inv.recordPayment(idr(500_000), ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> inv.voidInvoice(ACTOR));
    }
}

package com.saas.order.integration;

import com.saas.inventory.service.InventoryService;
import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderId;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderStatus;
import com.saas.order.service.OrderService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import io.quarkus.test.junit.QuarkusTest;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Full-stack integration test: OrderService → PanacheOrderRepository → H2.
 * Verifies the complete lifecycle from creation through fulfillment.
 */
@QuarkusTest
@TestMethodOrder(MethodOrderer.OrderAnnotation.class)
class OrderLifecycleIntegrationTest {

    @Inject OrderService     orderService;
    @Inject InventoryService inventoryService;

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-order-it";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "it-user";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private static String createdOrderId;

    private Party customer() {
        return Party.customer("cust-it-001", "PT Integration Test", "IT-TAX-001");
    }

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    // ── Test 1: Create ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(1)
    @DisplayName("Create order → persisted as DRAFT")
    @Transactional
    void createOrderPersistsDraft() {
        OrderAggregate order = orderService.createOrder(
                TENANT, customer(), "web", CCY, ACTOR);

        assertThat(order.getId()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(order.getTenantId()).isEqualTo(TENANT);
        createdOrderId = order.getId().getValue();

        // Reload from DB
        OrderAggregate reloaded = orderService
                .findById(OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT)
                .orElseThrow();
        assertThat(reloaded.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.DRAFT);
    }

    // ── Test 2: Add Lines ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(2)
    @DisplayName("Add lines → totals computed correctly")
    @Transactional
    void addLinesComputesTotals() {
        assumeOrderCreated();

        orderService.addLine(OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT,
                "prod-it-001", "IT-SKU-001", "Widget A",
                2, idr(100_000), idr(11_000), null, ACTOR);

        orderService.addLine(OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT,
                "prod-it-002", "IT-SKU-002", "Widget B",
                3, idr(50_000), idr(5_500), null, ACTOR);

        OrderAggregate order = orderService
                .findById(OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT)
                .orElseThrow();

        assertThat(order.getLines()).hasSize(2);
        // Subtotal: (2×100000) + (3×50000) = 350000
        assertThat(order.getSubtotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("350000");
        // Tax: (2×11000) + (3×5500) = 38500
        assertThat(order.getTotalTax().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("38500");
        // Grand total: 388500
        assertThat(order.getGrandTotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("388500");
    }

    // ── Test 3: Confirm ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(3)
    @DisplayName("Confirm order → CONFIRMED with order number")
    @Transactional
    void confirmOrder() {
        assumeOrderCreated();

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.confirmOrder(
                OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT, "bank_transfer", ACTOR);

        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
        assertThat(order.getOrderNumber()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(order.getOrderNumber()).startsWith("ORD-");
        assertThat(order.getPaymentMethod()).isEqualTo("bank_transfer");
    }

    // ── Test 4: Process ───────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(4)
    @DisplayName("Start processing → PROCESSING")
    @Transactional
    void startProcessing() {
        assumeOrderCreated();

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.startProcessing(
                OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT, ACTOR);

        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.PROCESSING);
    }

    // ── Test 5: Partial fulfillment ───────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(5)
    @DisplayName("Fulfill first line → PARTIALLY_FULFILLED")
    @Transactional
    void partialFulfillment() {
        assumeOrderCreated();

        OrderAggregate order = orderService
                .findById(OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT).orElseThrow();

        String firstLineId = order.getLines().get(0).getId();

        OrderAggregate updated = orderService.recordLineFulfillment(
                OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT, firstLineId,
                order.getLines().get(0).getQuantityOrdered(), ACTOR);

        assertThat(updated.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.PARTIALLY_FULFILLED);
    }

    // ── Test 6: Full fulfillment ──────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(6)
    @DisplayName("Fulfill last line → FULFILLED")
    @Transactional
    void fullFulfillment() {
        assumeOrderCreated();

        OrderAggregate order = orderService
                .findById(OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT).orElseThrow();

        // Find the line that still has pending qty
        var pendingLine = order.getLines().stream()
                .filter(l -> l.getQuantityPending() > 0)
                .findFirst().orElseThrow();

        OrderAggregate updated = orderService.recordLineFulfillment(
                OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT,
                pendingLine.getId(), pendingLine.getQuantityPending(), ACTOR);

        assertThat(updated.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.FULFILLED);
        assertThat(updated.getFulfilledAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    // ── Test 7: Mark paid ─────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(7)
    @DisplayName("markPaid → isPaid=true with reference")
    @Transactional
    void markPaid() {
        assumeOrderCreated();

        OrderAggregate order = orderService.markPaid(
                OrderId.of(createdOrderId), TENANT, "BNI-TXN-IT-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(order.isPaid()).isTrue();
        assertThat(order.getPaymentReference()).isEqualTo("BNI-TXN-IT-001");
    }

    // ── Test 8: Tenant isolation ──────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(8)
    @DisplayName("Tenant isolation — wrong tenant returns empty")
    void tenantIsolation() {
        assumeOrderCreated();
        assertThat(orderService.findById(
                OrderId.of(createdOrderId), "wrong-tenant")).isEmpty();
    }

    // ── Test 9: Cancel from DRAFT ─────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @Order(9)
    @DisplayName("New draft order can be cancelled")
    @Transactional
    void cancelDraftOrder() {
        OrderAggregate draft = orderService.createOrder(
                TENANT, customer(), "pos", CCY, ACTOR);
        orderService.addLine(OrderId.of(draft.getId().getValue()), TENANT,
                "prod-x", "SKU-X", "Test", 1, idr(10_000), idr(0), null, ACTOR);

        OrderAggregate cancelled = orderService.cancelOrder(
                draft.getId(), TENANT, "Test cancellation", ACTOR);

        assertThat(cancelled.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
        assertThat(cancelled.getCancellationReason()).isEqualTo("Test cancellation");
    }

    // ── Test 10: Idempotent duplicate product merge ───────────────────────

    @Test @Order(10)
    @DisplayName("Adding same product twice merges quantities")
    @Transactional
    void duplicateProductMerge() {
        OrderAggregate order = orderService.createOrder(
                TENANT, customer(), "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        OrderId oid = order.getId();

        orderService.addLine(oid, TENANT, "MERGE-PROD", "MERGE-SKU",
                "Merge Test", 3, idr(10_000), idr(0), null, ACTOR);
        orderService.addLine(oid, TENANT, "MERGE-PROD", "MERGE-SKU",
                "Merge Test", 2, idr(10_000), idr(0), null, ACTOR);

        OrderAggregate reloaded = orderService.findById(oid, TENANT).orElseThrow();
        assertThat(reloaded.getLines()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(reloaded.getLines().get(0).getQuantityOrdered()).isEqualTo(5);
    }

    // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private void assumeOrderCreated() {
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions.assumeTrue(
                createdOrderId != null,
                "Skipping test: prior test did not create an order");
    }
}

package com.saas.order.unit;

import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate.OrderStatusException;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderLine;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderStatus;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class OrderAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-test";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "test-user";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Party customer;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        customer = Party.customer("cust-001", "Budi Santoso", "123456789");
    }

    // ── Construction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("New order starts in DRAFT status")
    void newOrderIsDraft() {
        var order = OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(order.getLines()).isEmpty();
        assertThat(order.isPaid()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Grand total is zero for empty order")
    void emptyOrderTotals() {
        var order = OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        assertThat(order.getGrandTotal().isZero()).isTrue();
        assertThat(order.getSubtotal().isZero()).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Lines ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("addLine creates line and computes totals")
    void addLineComputes() {
        var order = OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        order.addLine("prod-1", "SKU-001", "T-Shirt Red",
                2, money(150_000), money(16_500), null, ACTOR);

        assertThat(order.getLines()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(order.getSubtotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("300000");
        assertThat(order.getTotalTax().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("33000");
        assertThat(order.getGrandTotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("333000");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Adding same product merges quantities")
    void duplicateProductMerged() {
        var order = OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        order.addLine("prod-1", "SKU-001", "T-Shirt", 2, money(100_000), money(0), null, ACTOR);
        order.addLine("prod-1", "SKU-001", "T-Shirt", 3, money(100_000), money(0), null, ACTOR);

        assertThat(order.getLines()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(order.getLines().get(0).getQuantityOrdered()).isEqualTo(5);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("Multiple products create separate lines")
    void differentProductsSeparateLines() {
        var order = OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        order.addLine("prod-1", "SKU-001", "A", 1, money(100_000), money(0), null, ACTOR);
        order.addLine("prod-2", "SKU-002", "B", 1, money(200_000), money(0), null, ACTOR);

        assertThat(order.getLines()).hasSize(2);
        assertThat(order.getSubtotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("300000");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("removeLine removes correctly")
    void removeLine() {
        var order = OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        var line = order.addLine("prod-1", "SKU-001", "A", 1, money(100_000), money(0), null, ACTOR);
        order.removeLine(line.getId(), ACTOR);
        assertThat(order.getLines()).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("addLine on non-DRAFT throws")
    void addLineOnConfirmedThrows() {
        var order = buildConfirmed();
        assertThatExceptionOfType(OrderStatusException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> order.addLine(
                        "prod-x", "SKU-X", "X", 1, money(10_000), money(0), null, ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("confirm transitions to CONFIRMED and sets orderNumber")
    void confirmSetsOrderNumber() {
        var order = buildDraftWithLine();
        order.confirm("ORD-001", "cash", ACTOR);
        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
        assertThat(order.getOrderNumber()).isEqualTo("ORD-001");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("confirm on empty order throws")
    void confirmEmptyOrderThrows() {
        var order = OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> order.confirm("ORD-001", "cash", ACTOR));
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("cancel from DRAFT transitions to CANCELLED")
    void cancelFromDraft() {
        var order = buildDraftWithLine();
        order.cancel("Test cancel", ACTOR);
        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.CANCELLED);
        assertThat(order.getCancellationReason()).isEqualTo("Test cancel");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("cancel from FULFILLED throws")
    void cancelFromFulfilledForbidden() {
        assertThat(OrderStatus.FULFILLED.canTransitionTo(OrderStatus.CANCELLED)).isFalse();
    }

    // ── Fulfillment ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("recordLineFulfillment → PARTIALLY_FULFILLED for partial qty")
    void partialFulfillment() {
        var order = buildConfirmed();
        order.startProcessing(ACTOR);
        String lineId = order.getLines().get(0).getId();
        order.recordLineFulfillment(lineId, 1, ACTOR); // ordered 2, fulfil 1
        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.PARTIALLY_FULFILLED);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("recordLineFulfillment → FULFILLED when all lines complete")
    void fullFulfillment() {
        var order = buildConfirmed();
        order.startProcessing(ACTOR);
        String lineId = order.getLines().get(0).getId();
        order.recordLineFulfillment(lineId, 2, ACTOR); // ordered 2, fulfil 2
        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.FULFILLED);
        assertThat(order.getFulfilledAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    // ── Payment ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test
    @DisplayName("markPaid sets isPaid and paymentReference")
    void markPaid() {
        var order = buildConfirmed();
        order.markPaid("TXN-123456", ACTOR);
        assertThat(order.isPaid()).isTrue();
        assertThat(order.getPaymentReference()).isEqualTo("TXN-123456");
    }

    // ── Helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    private OrderAggregate buildDraftWithLine() {
        var order = OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, "web", CCY, ACTOR);
        order.addLine("prod-1", "SKU-001", "Product A",
                2, money(100_000), money(11_000), null, ACTOR);
        return order;
    }

    private OrderAggregate buildConfirmed() {
        var order = buildDraftWithLine();
        order.confirm("ORD-TEST", "credit", ACTOR);
        return order;
    }

    private Money money(long amount) {
        return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(amount), CCY);
    }
}

package com.saas.payment.integration;

import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord;
import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord.*;
import com.saas.payment.service.PaymentService;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import io.quarkus.test.junit.QuarkusTest;
import jakarta.inject.Inject;
import jakarta.transaction.Transactional;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Integration test for PaymentService → PanachePaymentRepository → H2.
 */
@QuarkusTest
@TestMethodOrder(MethodOrderer.OrderAnnotation.class)
class PaymentServiceIntegrationTest {

    @Inject PaymentService paymentService;

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-payment-it";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "cashier";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private static String paymentId;

    private Party customer() { return Party.customer("cust-pay-001", "PT Test", null); }
    private Money idr(long v)  { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    @Test @Order(1)
    @DisplayName("Create payment → PENDING persisted")
    @Transactional
    void createPayment() {
        PaymentRecord p = paymentService.createPayment(
                TENANT, PaymentDirection.INBOUND,
                customer(), idr(2_500_000),
                PaymentMethod.BANK_TRANSFER,
                "INV-PAY-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(p.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.PENDING);
        assertThat(p.getAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("2500000");
        paymentId = p.getId();
    }

    @Test @Order(2)
    @DisplayName("Process payment → PROCESSED with reference")
    @Transactional
    void processPayment() {
        assumeCreated();
        PaymentRecord p = paymentService.processPayment(
                paymentId, TENANT,
                "BNI-TXN-IT-001", "GW-IT-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.PROCESSED);
        assertThat(p.getPaymentNumber()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(p.getReferenceNumber()).isEqualTo("BNI-TXN-IT-001");
        assertThat(p.getProcessedAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test @Order(3)
    @DisplayName("findById returns correct payment")
    void findById() {
        assumeCreated();
        var found = paymentService.findById(paymentId, TENANT);
        assertThat(found).isPresent();
        assertThat(found.get().getInvoiceId()).isEqualTo("INV-PAY-001");
    }

    @Test @Order(4)
    @DisplayName("findByInvoice returns the payment")
    void findByInvoice() {
        assumeCreated();
        var payments = paymentService.findByInvoice(TENANT, "INV-PAY-001");
        assertThat(payments).isNotEmpty();
    }

    @Test @Order(5)
    @DisplayName("findUnreconciled includes PROCESSED payment")
    void findUnreconciled() {
        assumeCreated();
        var unreconciled = paymentService.findUnreconciled(TENANT);
        assertThat(unreconciled).isNotEmpty();
    }

    @Test @Order(6)
    @DisplayName("Reconcile → RECONCILED with timestamp")
    @Transactional
    void reconcile() {
        assumeCreated();
        PaymentRecord p = paymentService.reconcile(paymentId, TENANT, ACTOR);
        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.RECONCILED);
        assertThat(p.getReconciledAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test @Order(7)
    @DisplayName("Create and cancel PENDING payment")
    @Transactional
    void cancelPayment() {
        PaymentRecord p = paymentService.createPayment(
                TENANT, PaymentDirection.OUTBOUND,
                Party.supplier("sup-it-001", "PT Supplier", null),
                idr(1_000_000), PaymentMethod.CASH, null, ACTOR);

        PaymentRecord cancelled = paymentService.cancelPayment(p.getId(), TENANT, ACTOR);
        assertThat(cancelled.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.CANCELLED);
    }

    @Test @Order(8)
    @DisplayName("Tenant isolation — wrong tenant returns empty")
    void tenantIsolation() {
        assumeCreated();
        assertThat(paymentService.findById(paymentId, "wrong-tenant")).isEmpty();
    }

    private void assumeCreated() {
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assumptions.assumeTrue(
                paymentId != null, "Skipping: prior payment not created");
    }
}

package com.saas.payment.unit;

import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord;
import com.saas.payment.core.PaymentRecord.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class PaymentRecordTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-pay";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "cashier";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Party customer() { return Party.customer("cust-001", "Budi", null); }
    private Money idr(long v)  { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    private PaymentRecord newInbound() {
        return PaymentRecord.create(TENANT, PaymentDirection.INBOUND,
                customer(), idr(1_000_000), PaymentMethod.BANK_TRANSFER,
                "INV-001", ACTOR);
    }

    // ── Construction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("New payment starts PENDING")
    void newPaymentIsPending() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.PENDING);
        assertThat(p.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(p.getAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1000000");
        assertThat(p.getDirection()).isEqualTo(PaymentDirection.INBOUND);
    }

    // ── Process ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("process transitions to PROCESSED")
    void processPayment() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        p.process("REC-0001", "BNI-TXN-001", "GW-9999", ACTOR);

        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.PROCESSED);
        assertThat(p.getPaymentNumber()).isEqualTo("REC-0001");
        assertThat(p.getReferenceNumber()).isEqualTo("BNI-TXN-001");
        assertThat(p.getGatewayTransactionId()).isEqualTo("GW-9999");
        assertThat(p.getProcessedAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot process non-PENDING payment")
    void cannotProcessTwice() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        p.process("REC-0002", "REF-002", null, ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> p.process("REC-0003", "REF-003", null, ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Reconcile ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("reconcile transitions to RECONCILED")
    void reconcile() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        p.process("REC-0004", "REF-004", null, ACTOR);
        p.reconcile(ACTOR);

        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.RECONCILED);
        assertThat(p.getReconciledAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot reconcile PENDING payment")
    void cannotReconcilePending() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> p.reconcile(ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Fail ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("fail transitions to FAILED with reason")
    void failPayment() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        p.fail("Insufficient funds", ACTOR);
        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.FAILED);
        assertThat(p.getNotes()).isEqualTo("Insufficient funds");
    }

    // ── Cancel ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("cancel from PENDING transitions to CANCELLED")
    void cancelPending() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        p.cancel(ACTOR);
        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PaymentStatus.CANCELLED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot cancel RECONCILED payment")
    void cannotCancelReconciled() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        p.process("REC-0005", "REF-005", null, ACTOR);
        p.reconcile(ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> p.cancel(ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Outbound ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("OUTBOUND payment direction set correctly")
    void outboundPayment() {
        Party supplier = Party.supplier("sup-001", "PT Bahan", null);
        PaymentRecord p = PaymentRecord.create(TENANT, PaymentDirection.OUTBOUND,
                supplier, idr(2_500_000), PaymentMethod.BANK_TRANSFER,
                "BILL-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(p.getDirection()).isEqualTo(PaymentDirection.OUTBOUND);
        assertThat(p.getParty().getType()).isEqualTo(Party.PartyType.SUPPLIER);
        assertThat(p.getAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("2500000");
    }

    // ── Audit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("Each state change touches audit version")
    void auditVersionIncrements() {
        PaymentRecord p = newInbound();
        long v1 = p.getAudit().getVersion();
        p.process("REC-0006", "REF-006", null, ACTOR);
        long v2 = p.getAudit().getVersion();
        p.reconcile(ACTOR);
        long v3 = p.getAudit().getVersion();

        assertThat(v2).isGreaterThan(v1);
        assertThat(v3).isGreaterThan(v2);
    }
}

package com.saas.payroll.unit;

import com.saas.payroll.core.PayslipAggregate;
import com.saas.payroll.core.PayslipAggregate.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.YearMonth;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class PayslipAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-payroll";
    private static final String EMP_ID = "emp-001";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "hr-admin";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";
    private static final YearMonth PERIOD = YearMonth.of(2024, 4);

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    private PayslipAggregate newPayslip() {
        return PayslipAggregate.create(
                TENANT, EMP_ID, "EMP-001", "Budi Santoso", PERIOD, CCY, ACTOR);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("New payslip starts DRAFT with empty items")
    void newPayslipIsDraft() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        assertThat(ps.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayslipStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(ps.getEarnings()).isEmpty();
        assertThat(ps.getDeductions()).isEmpty();
        assertThat(ps.getNetPay().isZero()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("addEarning adds to gross")
    void addEarning() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        ps.addEarning("BASIC", "Gaji Pokok", idr(8_000_000), ACTOR);
        ps.addEarning("TRANS", "Transport", idr(500_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(ps.getGrossEarnings().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("8500000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("addDeduction reduces netPay")
    void addDeduction() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        ps.addEarning("BASIC", "Gaji Pokok", idr(8_000_000), ACTOR);
        ps.addDeduction("BPJS-KT", "BPJS Ketenagakerjaan", idr(160_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(ps.getTotalDeductions().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("160000");
        assertThat(ps.getNetPay().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("7840000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("computeStandardIndonesiaPayroll sets correct components")
    void computeIndonesiaPayroll() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        ps.computeStandardIndonesiaPayroll(
                idr(10_000_000),   // basic salary
                idr(500_000),      // transport
                idr(300_000),      // meal
                0,                 // no absences
                ACTOR);

        // Should have: BASIC, TRANS, MEAL as earnings
        assertThat(ps.getEarnings()).hasSize(3);
        assertThat(ps.getGrossEarnings().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("10800000");

        // Should have: BPJS-KT (2%), BPJS-KS (1%) at minimum
        boolean hasBpjsKt = ps.getDeductions().stream()
                .anyMatch(d -> d.code().equals("BPJS-KT"));
        boolean hasBpjsKs = ps.getDeductions().stream()
                .anyMatch(d -> d.code().equals("BPJS-KS"));
        assertThat(hasBpjsKt).isTrue();
        assertThat(hasBpjsKs).isTrue();

        // BPJS-KT = (10M+500K+300K) × 2% = 10,800,000 × 0.02 = 216,000
        var bpjsKt = ps.getDeductions().stream()
                .filter(d -> d.code().equals("BPJS-KT")).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(bpjsKt.amount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("216000");

        // Net pay should be positive
        assertThat(ps.getNetPay().getAmount()).isGreaterThan(BigDecimal.ZERO);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Absence deduction reduces net pay by daily rate × days")
    void absenceDeduction() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        ps.computeStandardIndonesiaPayroll(
                idr(6_600_000), idr(0), idr(0), 2, ACTOR); // 2 days absent

        // Daily rate = 6,600,000 / 22 = 300,000
        // 2 day deduction = 600,000
        boolean hasAbsent = ps.getDeductions().stream()
                .anyMatch(d -> d.code().equals("ABSENT"));
        assertThat(hasAbsent).isTrue();
        var absent = ps.getDeductions().stream()
                .filter(d -> d.code().equals("ABSENT")).findFirst().orElseThrow();
        assertThat(absent.amount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("600000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("approve transitions to APPROVED")
    void approve() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        ps.addEarning("BASIC", "Salary", idr(5_000_000), ACTOR);
        ps.approve(ACTOR);
        assertThat(ps.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayslipStatus.APPROVED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot approve empty payslip")
    void cannotApproveEmpty() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        assertThatIllegalStateException().isThrownBy(() -> ps.approve(ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("markPaid requires APPROVED status")
    void markPaidRequiresApproved() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        ps.addEarning("BASIC", "Salary", idr(5_000_000), ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> ps.markPaid("REF-001", ACTOR)); // DRAFT → must approve first
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Full lifecycle: DRAFT → APPROVED → PAID")
    void fullLifecycle() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        ps.addEarning("BASIC", "Gaji Pokok", idr(8_000_000), ACTOR);
        ps.addDeduction("BPJS-KT", "BPJS Ketenagakerjaan", idr(160_000), ACTOR);

        ps.approve(ACTOR);
        assertThat(ps.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayslipStatus.APPROVED);

        ps.markPaid("BNI-BATCH-001", ACTOR);
        assertThat(ps.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PayslipStatus.PAID);
        assertThat(ps.getPaymentReference()).isEqualTo("BNI-BATCH-001");
        assertThat(ps.getNetPay().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("7840000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot modify APPROVED payslip items")
    void cannotModifyApproved() {
        PayslipAggregate ps = newPayslip();
        ps.addEarning("BASIC", "Salary", idr(5_000_000), ACTOR);
        ps.approve(ACTOR);
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> ps.addEarning("BONUS", "Bonus", idr(1_000_000), ACTOR));
    }
}

package com.saas.pricing.unit;

import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceList;
import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceList.*;
import com.saas.pricing.core.PriceRule;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.Optional;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class PriceListTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-price";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "manager";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    private PriceList newStandardList() {
        return PriceList.create(TENANT, "STD-2024", "Standard 2024",
                CCY, PriceListType.STANDARD,
                LocalDate.of(2024, 1, 1), LocalDate.of(2024, 12, 31), ACTOR);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("New price list is active with no rules")
    void newListIsActive() {
        PriceList pl = newStandardList();
        assertThat(pl.isActive()).isTrue();
        assertThat(pl.getRules()).isEmpty();
        assertThat(pl.isEffective(LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1))).isTrue();
        assertThat(pl.isEffective(LocalDate.of(2025, 1, 1))).isFalse();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Fixed price rule applies correct price")
    void fixedPriceRule() {
        PriceList pl = newStandardList();
        pl.addFixedPriceRule("PROD-001", "SKU-001", idr(85_000), 1, ACTOR);

        Optional<PriceResolutionResult> result = pl.resolve(
                "PROD-001", null, idr(100_000), 1, LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1));

        assertThat(result).isPresent();
        assertThat(result.get().finalPrice().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("85000");
        assertThat(result.get().ruleApplied().type()).isEqualTo(PriceRule.RuleType.FIXED_PRICE);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Percent discount reduces price by correct fraction")
    void percentDiscountRule() {
        PriceList pl = newStandardList();
        pl.addPercentDiscountRule("PROD-001", null,
                new BigDecimal("20"), 0, 1, ACTOR); // 20% off

        Optional<PriceResolutionResult> result = pl.resolve(
                "PROD-001", null, idr(100_000), 1, LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1));

        assertThat(result).isPresent();
        // 100000 - 20% = 80000
        assertThat(result.get().finalPrice().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("80000");
        assertThat(result.get().discountAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("20000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Absolute discount subtracts fixed amount")
    void absoluteDiscountRule() {
        PriceList pl = newStandardList();
        pl.addAbsoluteDiscountRule("PROD-001", idr(15_000), 0, 1, ACTOR);

        Optional<PriceResolutionResult> result = pl.resolve(
                "PROD-001", null, idr(100_000), 1, LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1));

        assertThat(result).isPresent();
        assertThat(result.get().finalPrice().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("85000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Priority ordering: first rule wins")
    void priorityOrdering() {
        PriceList pl = newStandardList();
        // Higher priority (lower number): fixed price
        pl.addFixedPriceRule("PROD-001", "SKU-001", idr(75_000), 1, ACTOR);
        // Lower priority: 20% off
        pl.addPercentDiscountRule("PROD-001", null,
                new BigDecimal("20"), 0, 10, ACTOR);

        Optional<PriceResolutionResult> result = pl.resolve(
                "PROD-001", null, idr(100_000), 1, LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1));

        // Should apply fixed price rule (priority 1)
        assertThat(result.get().finalPrice().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("75000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Min quantity requirement blocks low-qty orders")
    void minQuantityBlocks() {
        PriceList pl = newStandardList();
        pl.addPercentDiscountRule("PROD-001", null,
                new BigDecimal("15"), 10, 1, ACTOR); // requires qty >= 10

        // qty=5 should not match
        assertThat(pl.resolve("PROD-001", null, idr(100_000), 5,
                LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1))).isEmpty();

        // qty=10 should match
        assertThat(pl.resolve("PROD-001", null, idr(100_000), 10,
                LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1))).isPresent();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Category-scoped rule applies to matching category")
    void categoryScopedRule() {
        PriceList pl = newStandardList();
        pl.addPercentDiscountRule(null, "CATEGORY-ELECTRONICS",
                new BigDecimal("10"), 0, 1, ACTOR);

        // Different category — no match
        assertThat(pl.resolve("PROD-001", "CATEGORY-FURNITURE", idr(200_000),
                1, LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1))).isEmpty();

        // Matching category — match
        assertThat(pl.resolve("PROD-001", "CATEGORY-ELECTRONICS", idr(200_000),
                1, LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1))).isPresent();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Deactivated list returns empty")
    void deactivatedListReturnsEmpty() {
        PriceList pl = newStandardList();
        pl.addFixedPriceRule("PROD-001", null, idr(80_000), 1, ACTOR);
        pl.deactivate(ACTOR);

        assertThat(pl.resolve("PROD-001", null, idr(100_000),
                1, LocalDate.of(2024, 6, 1))).isEmpty();
    }
}

package com.saas.promotion.unit;

import com.saas.promotion.core.PromotionAggregate;
import com.saas.promotion.core.PromotionAggregate.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class PromotionAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-promo";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "marketing";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    private PromotionAggregate percentCoupon(int pct, int maxUses) {
        PromotionAggregate p = PromotionAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "SAVE" + pct, "Save " + pct + "%",
                PromotionType.COUPON, DiscountType.PERCENTAGE,
                new BigDecimal(pct), null,
                LocalDate.now().minusDays(1), LocalDate.now().plusDays(30),
                maxUses, 1, ACTOR);
        p.activate(ACTOR);
        return p;
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Percentage discount computed correctly")
    void percentageDiscount() {
        PromotionAggregate p = percentCoupon(10, 100);
        Money discount = p.validateAndCompute("cust-1", "retail", idr(500_000), LocalDate.now());
        assertThat(discount.getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("50000"); // 10%
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Fixed amount discount applied correctly")
    void fixedDiscount() {
        PromotionAggregate p = PromotionAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "OFF50K", "50K Off",
                PromotionType.COUPON, DiscountType.FIXED_AMOUNT, null, idr(50_000),
                null, null, 0, 0, ACTOR);
        p.activate(ACTOR);
        Money discount = p.validateAndCompute("cust-1", "retail", idr(200_000), LocalDate.now());
        assertThat(discount.getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("50000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Discount capped when order total smaller than fixed amount")
    void discountCappedAtOrderTotal() {
        PromotionAggregate p = PromotionAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "BIG-DISC", "200K Off",
                PromotionType.COUPON, DiscountType.FIXED_AMOUNT, null, idr(200_000),
                null, null, 0, 0, ACTOR);
        p.activate(ACTOR);
        Money discount = p.validateAndCompute("cust-1", "retail", idr(50_000), LocalDate.now());
        assertThat(discount.getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("50000"); // capped at order total
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Inactive promotion throws PromotionNotApplicableException")
    void inactiveThrows() {
        PromotionAggregate p = PromotionAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "DRAFT-P", "Draft", PromotionType.COUPON,
                DiscountType.PERCENTAGE, new BigDecimal("10"), null,
                null, null, 0, 0, ACTOR);
        // Still DRAFT — not activated
        assertThatExceptionOfType(PromotionNotApplicableException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> p.validateAndCompute(
                        "cust-1", "retail", idr(100_000), LocalDate.now()));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Expired promotion throws PromotionNotApplicableException")
    void expiredThrows() {
        PromotionAggregate p = PromotionAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "OLD-PROMO", "Old", PromotionType.COUPON,
                DiscountType.PERCENTAGE, new BigDecimal("5"), null,
                LocalDate.now().minusDays(60),
                LocalDate.now().minusDays(30),   // ← ended 30 days ago
                0, 0, ACTOR);
        p.activate(ACTOR);
        assertThatExceptionOfType(PromotionNotApplicableException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> p.validateAndCompute(
                        "cust-1", "retail", idr(100_000), LocalDate.now()));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Max total redemptions limit enforced")
    void maxRedemptionsEnforced() {
        PromotionAggregate p = percentCoupon(10, 2); // max 2 total uses
        Money discount = p.validateAndCompute("cust-1", "retail", idr(100_000), LocalDate.now());
        p.redeem("order-1", "cust-1", discount, ACTOR);

        discount = p.validateAndCompute("cust-2", "retail", idr(100_000), LocalDate.now());
        p.redeem("order-2", "cust-2", discount, ACTOR);

        // Should now be EXPIRED and throw
        assertThat(p.getStatus()).isEqualTo(PromotionStatus.EXPIRED);
        assertThatExceptionOfType(PromotionNotApplicableException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> p.validateAndCompute(
                        "cust-3", "retail", idr(100_000), LocalDate.now()));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Per-customer limit enforced")
    void perCustomerLimitEnforced() {
        PromotionAggregate p = percentCoupon(10, 0); // unlimited total, 1 per customer
        Money discount = p.validateAndCompute("cust-1", "retail", idr(100_000), LocalDate.now());
        p.redeem("order-1", "cust-1", discount, ACTOR);

        assertThatExceptionOfType(PromotionNotApplicableException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> p.validateAndCompute(
                        "cust-1", "retail", idr(100_000), LocalDate.now())); // same customer again
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Minimum order value enforced")
    void minimumOrderValueEnforced() {
        PromotionAggregate p = percentCoupon(10, 0);
        p.setScope(null, null, null, idr(500_000), null, ACTOR); // min 500K

        assertThatExceptionOfType(PromotionNotApplicableException.class)
                .isThrownBy(() -> p.validateAndCompute(
                        "cust-1", "retail", idr(100_000), LocalDate.now())); // below min
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Redemption recorded correctly")
    void redemptionRecorded() {
        PromotionAggregate p = percentCoupon(15, 0);
        Money discount = p.validateAndCompute("cust-1", "retail", idr(200_000), LocalDate.now());
        RedemptionRecord record = p.redeem("order-99", "cust-1", discount, ACTOR);

        assertThat(record.orderId()).isEqualTo("order-99");
        assertThat(record.customerId()).isEqualTo("cust-1");
        assertThat(record.discountApplied().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("30000"); // 15%
        assertThat(p.getTotalRedemptions()).isEqualTo(1);
    }
}

package com.saas.purchase.unit;

import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderAggregate;
import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderAggregate.POStatus;
import com.saas.purchase.core.PurchaseOrderLine;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class PurchaseOrderAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-po";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "purchaser";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private Party supplier() {
        return Party.supplier("sup-001", "PT Bahan Baku", "TAX-SUP-001");
    }

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    private PurchaseOrderAggregate newDraftPo() {
        return PurchaseOrderAggregate.create(
                TENANT, supplier(), CCY,
                LocalDate.now().plusDays(7), "WH-MAIN", ACTOR);
    }

    // ── Construction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("New PO starts in DRAFT status")
    void newPoIsDraft() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        assertThat(po.getStatus()).isEqualTo(POStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(po.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(po.getLines()).isEmpty();
    }

    // ── Lines ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("addLine creates line with correct values")
    void addLine() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("prod-1", "SKU-001", "Widget A", 100, idr(15_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(po.getLines()).hasSize(1);
        PurchaseOrderLine line = po.getLines().get(0);
        assertThat(line.orderedQty()).isEqualTo(100);
        assertThat(line.receivedQty()).isEqualTo(0);
        assertThat(line.unitCost().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("15000");
        assertThat(line.lineTotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1500000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Subtotal sums all line totals")
    void subtotal() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item 1", 10, idr(100_000), ACTOR);
        po.addLine("p2", "S2", "Item 2", 5,  idr(200_000), ACTOR);

        // 10×100000 + 5×200000 = 2,000,000
        assertThat(po.getSubtotal().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("2000000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("removeLine removes correctly")
    void removeLine() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item 1", 5, idr(50_000), ACTOR);
        String lineId = po.getLines().get(0).id();
        po.removeLine(lineId, ACTOR);
        assertThat(po.getLines()).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot add line to SENT PO")
    void cannotAddLineAfterSend() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item", 1, idr(10_000), ACTOR);
        po.send("PO-001", ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> po.addLine("p2", "S2", "New", 1, idr(5_000), ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("send transitions to SENT with PO number")
    void sendPo() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item", 10, idr(50_000), ACTOR);
        po.send("PO-2024-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(po.getStatus()).isEqualTo(POStatus.SENT);
        assertThat(po.getPoNumber()).isEqualTo("PO-2024-001");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot send empty PO")
    void cannotSendEmptyPo() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> po.send("PO-EMPTY", ACTOR));
    }

    // ── GRN (Goods Receipt) ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("receiveLineGoods — partial receipt → PARTIALLY_RECEIVED")
    void partialReceipt() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item A", 100, idr(10_000), ACTOR);
        po.send("PO-002", ACTOR);
        String lineId = po.getLines().get(0).id();

        boolean fully = po.receiveLineGoods(lineId, 60, ACTOR);
        assertThat(fully).isFalse();
        assertThat(po.getStatus()).isEqualTo(POStatus.PARTIALLY_RECEIVED);
        assertThat(po.getLines().get(0).receivedQty()).isEqualTo(60);
        assertThat(po.getLines().get(0).pendingQty()).isEqualTo(40);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("receiveLineGoods — full receipt → RECEIVED")
    void fullReceipt() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item A", 50, idr(20_000), ACTOR);
        po.send("PO-003", ACTOR);
        String lineId = po.getLines().get(0).id();

        boolean fully = po.receiveLineGoods(lineId, 50, ACTOR);
        assertThat(fully).isTrue();
        assertThat(po.getStatus()).isEqualTo(POStatus.RECEIVED);
        assertThat(po.getLines().get(0).isFullyReceived()).isTrue();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Multi-line PO — both lines must be received for RECEIVED status")
    void multiLinePo() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item A", 10, idr(10_000), ACTOR);
        po.addLine("p2", "S2", "Item B", 20, idr(5_000), ACTOR);
        po.send("PO-004", ACTOR);

        String line1 = po.getLines().get(0).id();
        String line2 = po.getLines().get(1).id();

        po.receiveLineGoods(line1, 10, ACTOR); // first fully received
        assertThat(po.getStatus()).isEqualTo(POStatus.PARTIALLY_RECEIVED);

        po.receiveLineGoods(line2, 20, ACTOR); // second fully received
        assertThat(po.getStatus()).isEqualTo(POStatus.RECEIVED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot receive from DRAFT PO")
    void cannotReceiveFromDraft() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item", 10, idr(1_000), ACTOR);
        String lineId = po.getLines().get(0).id();

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> po.receiveLineGoods(lineId, 5, ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Cancel ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("DRAFT PO can be cancelled")
    void cancelDraft() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.cancel(ACTOR);
        assertThat(po.getStatus()).isEqualTo(POStatus.CANCELLED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("SENT PO can be cancelled")
    void cancelSent() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item", 5, idr(1_000), ACTOR);
        po.send("PO-CANCEL", ACTOR);
        po.cancel(ACTOR);
        assertThat(po.getStatus()).isEqualTo(POStatus.CANCELLED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("RECEIVED PO cannot be cancelled")
    void cannotCancelReceived() {
        PurchaseOrderAggregate po = newDraftPo();
        po.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item", 1, idr(1_000), ACTOR);
        po.send("PO-005", ACTOR);
        po.receiveLineGoods(po.getLines().get(0).id(), 1, ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> po.cancel(ACTOR));
    }
}

package com.saas.shared.unit;

import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate;
import com.saas.customer.core.CustomerAggregate.CustomerType;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockLedger;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement;
import com.saas.inventory.core.StockMovement.MovementType;
import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderId;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderStatus;
import com.saas.shared.infrastructure.InMemoryRepositories.*;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.Optional;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Pure unit tests for InMemoryRepositories — no CDI, no DB.
 * Validates save/find/delete contracts for each module's in-memory repo.
 */
class InMemoryRepositoriesTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-mem";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";
    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    // ── Order ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Nested @DisplayName("InMemoryOrderRepository")
    class OrderRepoTest {

        InMemoryOrderRepository repo = new InMemoryOrderRepository();

        private OrderAggregate newOrder(String channel) {
            Party customer = Party.customer("cust-001", "Budi", null);
            return OrderAggregate.create(TENANT, customer, channel, CCY, "admin");
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("save and findById round-trip")
        void saveAndFind() {
            OrderAggregate o = newOrder("web");
            repo.save(o);
            Optional<OrderAggregate> found = repo.findById(o.getId(), TENANT);
            assertThat(found).isPresent();
            assertThat(found.get().getChannel()).isEqualTo("web");
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("findById with wrong tenant returns empty")
        void tenantIsolation() {
            OrderAggregate o = newOrder("pos");
            repo.save(o);
            assertThat(repo.findById(o.getId(), "other-tenant")).isEmpty();
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("findByOrderNumber finds confirmed order")
        void findByOrderNumber() {
            OrderAggregate o = newOrder("api");
            o.addLine("p1", "S1", "Item", 1,
                    idr(10_000), idr(0), null, "admin");
            o.confirm("ORD-MEM-001", "cash", "admin");
            repo.save(o);

            Optional<OrderAggregate> found = repo.findByOrderNumber("ORD-MEM-001", TENANT);
            assertThat(found).isPresent();
            assertThat(found.get().getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("delete removes from store")
        void delete() {
            OrderAggregate o = newOrder("web");
            repo.save(o);
            repo.delete(o.getId(), TENANT);
            assertThat(repo.findById(o.getId(), TENANT)).isEmpty();
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("size returns count across all tenants")
        void size() {
            repo.clear();
            repo.save(newOrder("web"));
            repo.save(newOrder("pos"));
            assertThat(repo.size()).isEqualTo(2);
        }
    }

    // ── Customer ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Nested @DisplayName("InMemoryCustomerRepository")
    class CustomerRepoTest {

        InMemoryCustomerRepository repo = new InMemoryCustomerRepository();

        private CustomerAggregate newCustomer(String email) {
            return CustomerAggregate.create(TENANT, "Test Customer",
                    email, CustomerType.INDIVIDUAL, CCY, "admin");
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("save and findById")
        void saveAndFind() {
            CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer("a@test.com");
            repo.save(c);
            assertThat(repo.findById(c.getId(), TENANT)).isPresent();
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("findByEmail finds correct customer")
        void findByEmail() {
            CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer("find-me@test.com");
            repo.save(c);
            assertThat(repo.findByEmail("find-me@test.com", TENANT))
                    .isPresent()
                    .map(CustomerAggregate::getId)
                    .contains(c.getId());
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("existsByEmail returns true for saved customer")
        void existsByEmail() {
            repo.save(newCustomer("exists@test.com"));
            assertThat(repo.existsByEmail("exists@test.com", TENANT)).isTrue();
            assertThat(repo.existsByEmail("nothere@test.com", TENANT)).isFalse();
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("findBySegment filters by segment")
        void findBySegment() {
            CustomerAggregate c = newCustomer("seg@test.com");
            c.setSegment("vip", "admin");
            repo.save(c);

            assertThat(repo.findBySegment(TENANT, "vip", 0, 10)).hasSize(1);
            assertThat(repo.findBySegment(TENANT, "wholesale", 0, 10)).isEmpty();
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("count returns tenant-scoped total")
        void count() {
            repo.clear();
            repo.save(newCustomer("c1@test.com"));
            repo.save(newCustomer("c2@test.com"));
            assertThat(repo.count(TENANT)).isEqualTo(2);
        }
    }

    // ── Inventory ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Nested @DisplayName("InMemoryInventoryRepository")
    class InventoryRepoTest {

        InMemoryInventoryRepository repo = new InMemoryInventoryRepository();

        private StockLedger newLedger(String productId) {
            return StockLedger.create(TENANT, productId, "SKU-" + productId,
                    "WH-MAIN", 5, 10, "admin");
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("save and findByProductAndWarehouse")
        void saveAndFind() {
            StockLedger l = newLedger("PROD-001");
            repo.save(l);
            assertThat(repo.findByProductAndWarehouse(TENANT, "PROD-001", "WH-MAIN"))
                    .isPresent();
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("findLowStock returns ledgers at or below reorder point")
        void findLowStock() {
            repo.clear();
            StockLedger low = newLedger("PROD-LOW");
            low.receive(5, "OB", MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, "admin"); // at reorder point
            repo.save(low);

            StockLedger ok = newLedger("PROD-OK");
            ok.receive(100, "OB", MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, "admin"); // above reorder
            repo.save(ok);

            assertThat(repo.findLowStock(TENANT)).hasSize(1);
            assertThat(repo.findLowStock(TENANT).get(0).getProductId()).isEqualTo("PROD-LOW");
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("findOutOfStock returns ledgers with zero available")
        void findOutOfStock() {
            repo.clear();
            StockLedger empty = newLedger("PROD-EMPTY"); // starts at 0
            repo.save(empty);

            StockLedger stocked = newLedger("PROD-STOCKED");
            stocked.receive(50, "OB", MovementType.OPENING_BALANCE, "admin");
            repo.save(stocked);

            assertThat(repo.findOutOfStock(TENANT)).hasSize(1);
            assertThat(repo.findOutOfStock(TENANT).get(0).getProductId()).isEqualTo("PROD-EMPTY");
        }

        @Test @DisplayName("saveMovement and findMovements")
        void movements() {
            StockLedger l = newLedger("PROD-MV");
            repo.save(l);
            StockMovement mv = l.receive(20, "PO-001",
                    MovementType.PURCHASE_RECEIPT, "admin");
            repo.saveMovement(mv);

            assertThat(repo.findMovements(TENANT, "PROD-MV", 10)).hasSize(1);
            assertThat(repo.findMovements(TENANT, "PROD-NONE", 10)).isEmpty();
        }
    }
}

syirkah-modules/src/test/java/com/saas/shared/unit/OrderServiceWithInMemoryReposTest.java
