package com.saas.shared.unit;

import com.saas.order.core.OrderAggregate;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderId;
import com.saas.order.core.model.OrderStatus;
import com.saas.order.events.OrderEvent;
import com.saas.order.service.OrderService;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEvent;
import com.saas.shared.event.DomainEventPublisher;
import com.saas.shared.infrastructure.InMemoryRepositories.InMemoryOrderRepository;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.shared.model.Party;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

/**
 * Tests OrderService using InMemory repos and a capturing event publisher.
 * Demonstrates how to unit-test services without CDI or a database.
 */
class OrderServiceWithInMemoryReposTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-inmem";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private InMemoryOrderRepository orderRepo;
    private CapturingEventPublisher publisher;
    private OrderService orderService;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        orderRepo    = new InMemoryOrderRepository();
        publisher    = new CapturingEventPublisher();
        orderService = new OrderService(orderRepo, publisher);
    }

    private Party customer() { return Party.customer("c1", "Test Customer", null); }
    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    @Test @DisplayName("createOrder publishes OrderCreated event")
    void createPublishesEvent() {
        OrderAggregate order = orderService.createOrder(
                TENANT, customer(), "web", CCY, "admin");

        assertThat(order.getStatus()).isEqualTo(OrderStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(publisher.events).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(publisher.events.get(0)).isInstanceOf(OrderEvent.OrderCreated.class);

        OrderEvent.OrderCreated created = (OrderEvent.OrderCreated) publisher.events.get(0);
        assertThat(created.tenantId()).isEqualTo(TENANT);
        assertThat(created.currencyCode()).isEqualTo(CCY);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("confirmOrder publishes OrderConfirmed event with correct grand total")
    void confirmPublishesEvent() {
        OrderAggregate order = orderService.createOrder(
                TENANT, customer(), "pos", CCY, "admin");

        orderService.addLine(order.getId(), TENANT,
                "prod-1", "SKU-1", "Widget",
                3, idr(100_000), idr(11_000), null, "admin");

        publisher.events.clear(); // only care about confirm events

        orderService.confirmOrder(order.getId(), TENANT, "cash", "admin");

        assertThat(publisher.events).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(publisher.events.get(0)).isInstanceOf(OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed.class);

        OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed confirmed = (OrderEvent.OrderConfirmed) publisher.events.get(0);
        assertThat(confirmed.lineCount()).isEqualTo(1);
        // Grand total: 3 × (100000 + 11000) = 333000
        assertThat(confirmed.grandTotal()).isEqualTo("IDR 333000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("cancelOrder publishes OrderCancelled event")
    void cancelPublishesEvent() {
        OrderAggregate order = orderService.createOrder(
                TENANT, customer(), "web", CCY, "admin");
        publisher.events.clear();

        orderService.cancelOrder(order.getId(), TENANT, "Test reason", "admin");

        assertThat(publisher.events.get(0)).isInstanceOf(OrderEvent.OrderCancelled.class);
        var cancelled = (OrderEvent.OrderCancelled) publisher.events.get(0);
        assertThat(cancelled.reason()).isEqualTo("Test reason");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("findById with wrong tenant returns empty (in-memory isolation)")
    void tenantIsolation() {
        OrderAggregate order = orderService.createOrder(
                TENANT, customer(), "web", CCY, "admin");

        assertThat(orderService.findById(order.getId(), "other-tenant")).isEmpty();
        assertThat(orderService.findById(order.getId(), TENANT)).isPresent();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Full lifecycle: create → addLine → confirm → startProcessing → fulfill")
    void fullLifecycle() {
        OrderAggregate order = orderService.createOrder(
                TENANT, customer(), "web", CCY, "admin");
        OrderId id = order.getId();

        orderService.addLine(id, TENANT, "p1", "S1", "Product",
                2, idr(50_000), idr(5_500), null, "admin");

        orderService.confirmOrder(id, TENANT, "transfer", "admin");
        assertThat(orderRepo.findById(id, TENANT).map(OrderAggregate::getStatus))
                .contains(OrderStatus.CONFIRMED);

        orderService.startProcessing(id, TENANT, "admin");
        assertThat(orderRepo.findById(id, TENANT).map(OrderAggregate::getStatus))
                .contains(OrderStatus.PROCESSING);

        String lineId = orderRepo.findById(id, TENANT).orElseThrow()
                .getLines().get(0).getId();
        orderService.recordLineFulfillment(id, TENANT, lineId, 2, "admin");

        assertThat(orderRepo.findById(id, TENANT).map(OrderAggregate::getStatus))
                .contains(OrderStatus.FULFILLED);

        // 5 events: created, lineAdded, confirmed, processing, lineFulfilled + fulfilled
        assertThat(publisher.events.size()).isGreaterThanOrEqualTo(5);
    }

    // ── Test double — captures all published events ────────────────────────

    static class CapturingEventPublisher implements DomainEventPublisher {
        final List<DomainEvent> events = new ArrayList<>();

        @Override
        public void publish(DomainEvent event) {
            events.add(event);
        }
    }
}

package com.saas.shipment.unit;

import com.saas.shipment.core.ShipmentAggregate;
import com.saas.shipment.core.ShipmentAggregate.ShipmentStatus;
import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.time.Instant;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class ShipmentAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT    = "tenant-shp";
    private static final String ORDER_ID  = "order-001";
    private static final String WAREHOUSE = "WH-JKT-01";
    private static final String ACTOR     = "warehouse-staff";

    private Address destination() {
        return Address.builder()
                .line1("Jl. Pemuda 10").city("Surabaya").countryCode("ID").build();
    }

    private ShipmentAggregate newPending() {
        return ShipmentAggregate.create(
                TENANT, ORDER_ID, WAREHOUSE, destination(),
                "JNE", "REGULAR", ACTOR);
    }

    // ── Construction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("New shipment starts PENDING")
    void newShipmentIsPending() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        assertThat(s.getStatus()).isEqualTo(ShipmentStatus.PENDING);
        assertThat(s.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(s.getCarrierCode()).isEqualTo("JNE");
        assertThat(s.getLines()).isEmpty();
    }

    // ── Lines ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("addLine adds line correctly")
    void addLine() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        s.addLine("LINE-001", "prod-001", "SKU-001", 3, ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getLines()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(s.getLines().get(0).qty()).isEqualTo(3);
        assertThat(s.getLines().get(0).orderLineId()).isEqualTo("LINE-001");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot add line after packing")
    void cannotAddLineAfterPack() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        s.addLine("LINE-001", "prod-001", "SKU-001", 1, ACTOR);
        s.pack("SHP-001", ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> s.addLine("LINE-002", "prod-002", "SKU-002", 2, ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("pack → PACKED with shipment number")
    void pack() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        s.addLine("L1", "p1", "S1", 2, ACTOR);
        s.pack("SHP-JKT-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getStatus()).isEqualTo(ShipmentStatus.PACKED);
        assertThat(s.getShipmentNumber()).isEqualTo("SHP-JKT-001");
        assertThat(s.getPackedAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot pack empty shipment")
    void cannotPackEmpty() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> s.pack("SHP-EMPTY", ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("dispatch → DISPATCHED with tracking number")
    void dispatch() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        s.addLine("L1", "p1", "S1", 1, ACTOR);
        s.pack("SHP-002", ACTOR);

        Instant eta = Instant.now().plusSeconds(86400 * 3);
        s.dispatch("JNE-TRK-12345", eta, ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getStatus()).isEqualTo(ShipmentStatus.DISPATCHED);
        assertThat(s.getTrackingNumber()).isEqualTo("JNE-TRK-12345");
        assertThat(s.getDispatchedAt()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(s.getEstimatedDelivery()).isEqualTo(eta);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot dispatch from PENDING (must be PACKED first)")
    void cannotDispatchFromPending() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        s.addLine("L1", "p1", "S1", 1, ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> s.dispatch("TRK-000", Instant.now(), ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("markDelivered → DELIVERED with timestamp")
    void deliver() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        s.addLine("L1", "p1", "S1", 1, ACTOR);
        s.pack("SHP-003", ACTOR);
        s.dispatch("TRK-003", Instant.now().plusSeconds(86400), ACTOR);
        s.markDelivered(ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getStatus()).isEqualTo(ShipmentStatus.DELIVERED);
        assertThat(s.getDeliveredAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("cancel from PENDING succeeds")
    void cancelFromPending() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        s.cancel(ACTOR);
        assertThat(s.getStatus()).isEqualTo(ShipmentStatus.CANCELLED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot cancel DELIVERED shipment")
    void cannotCancelDelivered() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        s.addLine("L1", "p1", "S1", 1, ACTOR);
        s.pack("SHP-004", ACTOR);
        s.dispatch("TRK-004", Instant.now(), ACTOR);
        s.markDelivered(ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> s.cancel(ACTOR));
    }

    // ── Audit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("Version increments on each state change")
    void versionIncrements() {
        ShipmentAggregate s = newPending();
        long v1 = s.getAudit().getVersion();
        s.addLine("L1", "p1", "S1", 1, ACTOR);
        long v2 = s.getAudit().getVersion();
        s.pack("SHP-005", ACTOR);
        long v3 = s.getAudit().getVersion();

        assertThat(v2).isGreaterThan(v1);
        assertThat(v3).isGreaterThan(v2);
    }
}

package com.saas.supplier.unit;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.supplier.core.SupplierAggregate;
import com.saas.supplier.core.SupplierAggregate.SupplierStatus;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class SupplierAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-sup";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "purchaser";
    private static final String CCY    = "IDR";

    private SupplierAggregate newSupplier() {
        return SupplierAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "PT Bahan Baku Utama",
                "bbu@supplier.co.id", CCY, ACTOR);
    }

    private Money idr(long v) { return Money.of(BigDecimal.valueOf(v), CCY); }

    // ── Construction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("New supplier starts ACTIVE with zero payable")
    void newSupplierIsActive() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        assertThat(s.getStatus()).isEqualTo(SupplierStatus.ACTIVE);
        assertThat(s.getCurrentPayable().isZero()).isTrue();
        assertThat(s.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(s.getTenantId()).isEqualTo(TENANT);
        assertThat(s.getLeadTimeDays()).isEqualTo(7);
        assertThat(s.getPaymentTermsDays()).isEqualTo(30);
    }

    // ── Profile update ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("updateProfile changes name and email")
    void updateProfile() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        s.updateProfile("PT Bahan Baku Baru", "new@supplier.co.id",
                "+6221-9999", "TAX-NEW-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getName()).isEqualTo("PT Bahan Baku Baru");
        assertThat(s.getEmail()).isEqualTo("new@supplier.co.id");
        assertThat(s.getPhone()).isEqualTo("+6221-9999");
        assertThat(s.getTaxId()).isEqualTo("TAX-NEW-001");
    }

    // ── Payment terms ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("setPaymentTerms updates lead time and terms")
    void setPaymentTerms() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        s.setPaymentTerms(14, 45, ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getLeadTimeDays()).isEqualTo(14);
        assertThat(s.getPaymentTermsDays()).isEqualTo(45);
    }

    // ── Bank details ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("setBankDetails persists account and bank name")
    void setBankDetails() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        s.setBankDetails("1234567890", "Bank BNI", ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getBankAccount()).isEqualTo("1234567890");
        assertThat(s.getBankName()).isEqualTo("Bank BNI");
    }

    // ── AP balance tracking ───────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("debitPayable increases AP balance")
    void debitPayable() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        s.debitPayable(idr(5_000_000), ACTOR);
        s.debitPayable(idr(2_500_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getCurrentPayable().getAmount())
                .isEqualByComparingTo("7500000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("creditPayable decreases AP balance")
    void creditPayable() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        s.debitPayable(idr(10_000_000), ACTOR);
        s.creditPayable(idr(3_000_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getCurrentPayable().getAmount())
                .isEqualByComparingTo("7000000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("creditPayable beyond balance throws")
    void creditBeyondBalanceThrows() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        s.debitPayable(idr(1_000_000), ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalArgumentException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> s.creditPayable(idr(2_000_000), ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Full payment clears AP balance to zero")
    void fullPaymentClearsBalance() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        s.debitPayable(idr(4_500_000), ACTOR);
        s.creditPayable(idr(4_500_000), ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getCurrentPayable().isZero()).isTrue();
    }

    // ── Remittance address ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("setRemittanceAddress stores address")
    void setRemittanceAddress() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        Address addr = Address.builder()
                .line1("Jl. Industri No. 88")
                .city("Bekasi")
                .countryCode("ID")
                .build();
        s.setRemittanceAddress(addr, ACTOR);

        assertThat(s.getRemittanceAddress()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(s.getRemittanceAddress().getCity()).isEqualTo("Bekasi");
    }

    // ── Lifecycle ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("deactivate transitions to INACTIVE")
    void deactivate() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        s.deactivate(ACTOR);
        assertThat(s.getStatus()).isEqualTo(SupplierStatus.INACTIVE);
    }

    // ── Audit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("Each mutation increments audit version")
    void auditVersionIncrements() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        long v1 = s.getAudit().getVersion();

        s.updateProfile("New Name", "new@x.com", null, null, ACTOR);
        long v2 = s.getAudit().getVersion();

        s.setPaymentTerms(10, 60, ACTOR);
        long v3 = s.getAudit().getVersion();

        assertThat(v2).isGreaterThan(v1);
        assertThat(v3).isGreaterThan(v2);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Created by and updated by set correctly")
    void auditActors() {
        SupplierAggregate s = newSupplier();
        assertThat(s.getAudit().getCreatedBy()).isEqualTo(ACTOR);
        assertThat(s.getAudit().getUpdatedBy()).isEqualTo(ACTOR);

        s.setPaymentTerms(7, 30, "another-user");
        assertThat(s.getAudit().getCreatedBy()).isEqualTo(ACTOR); // unchanged
        assertThat(s.getAudit().getUpdatedBy()).isEqualTo("another-user");
    }
}

package com.saas.tax.unit;

import com.saas.shared.model.Money;
import com.saas.tax.core.TaxAggregate;
import com.saas.tax.core.TaxAggregate.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class TaxAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "t-tax";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "admin";

    private TaxAggregate newPpn() {
        TaxAggregate t = TaxAggregate.create(TENANT, "PPN-11", "PPN 11%",
                TaxType.VAT, TaxCalculationMethod.EXCLUSIVE, "2100", ACTOR);
        t.addRate(new BigDecimal("11"), LocalDate.of(2022, 4, 1), null, ACTOR);
        return t;
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("EXCLUSIVE PPN 11% — tax added on top of base")
    void exclusiveTax() {
        TaxCalculationResult result = newPpn()
                .calculate(Money.of(new BigDecimal("1000000"), "IDR"), LocalDate.now());
        assertThat(result.taxAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("110000");
        assertThat(result.grossAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1110000");
        assertThat(result.netAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1000000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("INCLUSIVE PPN 11% — tax extracted from gross")
    void inclusiveTax() {
        TaxAggregate t = TaxAggregate.create(TENANT, "PPN-11-INC", "PPN 11% Inc",
                TaxType.VAT, TaxCalculationMethod.INCLUSIVE, "2100", ACTOR);
        t.addRate(new BigDecimal("11"), LocalDate.of(2022, 4, 1), null, ACTOR);

        // Gross = 1,110,000 → extracted tax should be ~110,000
        TaxCalculationResult result = t.calculate(
                Money.of(new BigDecimal("1110000"), "IDR"), LocalDate.now());
        // tax = 1110000 × 11 / 111 = 110000
        assertThat(result.taxAmount().getAmount())
                .isEqualByComparingTo(new BigDecimal("110000.0000000000"));
        assertThat(result.grossAmount().getAmount()).isEqualByComparingTo("1110000");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Rate lookup respects effective date")
    void rateLookupByDate() {
        TaxAggregate t = TaxAggregate.create(TENANT, "VAT-TEST", "Test VAT",
                TaxType.VAT, TaxCalculationMethod.EXCLUSIVE, null, ACTOR);
        t.addRate(new BigDecimal("10"), LocalDate.of(2020, 1, 1),
                LocalDate.of(2022, 3, 31), ACTOR);
        t.addRate(new BigDecimal("11"), LocalDate.of(2022, 4, 1), null, ACTOR);

        // Date before rate change: should use 10%
        TaxCalculationResult r10 = t.calculate(
                Money.of(new BigDecimal("100000"), "IDR"), LocalDate.of(2021, 6, 1));
        assertThat(r10.ratePercent()).isEqualByComparingTo("10");

        // Date after rate change: should use 11%
        TaxCalculationResult r11 = t.calculate(
                Money.of(new BigDecimal("100000"), "IDR"), LocalDate.of(2023, 1, 1));
        assertThat(r11.ratePercent()).isEqualByComparingTo("11");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("No active rate throws IllegalStateException")
    void noRateThrows() {
        TaxAggregate t = TaxAggregate.create(TENANT, "FUTURE-TAX", "Future",
                TaxType.CUSTOM, TaxCalculationMethod.EXCLUSIVE, null, ACTOR);
        t.addRate(new BigDecimal("5"), LocalDate.of(2030, 1, 1), null, ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> t.calculate(
                        Money.of(new BigDecimal("100000"), "IDR"), LocalDate.now()));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Deactivated tax code has isActive=false")
    void deactivate() {
        TaxAggregate t = newPpn();
        t.deactivate(ACTOR);
        assertThat(t.isActive()).isFalse();
    }
}

package com.saas.warehouse.unit;

import com.saas.shared.model.Address;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.StockTransfer;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.StockTransfer.TransferStatus;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate.BinLocation;
import com.saas.warehouse.core.WarehouseAggregate.WarehouseType;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.*;

class WarehouseAggregateTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-wh";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "warehouse-admin";

    private Address jakartaAddr() {
        return Address.builder()
                .line1("Jl. Industri Raya No. 1")
                .city("Jakarta").countryCode("ID").build();
    }

    private WarehouseAggregate newWarehouse() {
        return WarehouseAggregate.create(
                TENANT, "WH-JKT-01", "Jakarta Main Warehouse",
                WarehouseType.MAIN, jakartaAddr(), ACTOR);
    }

    // ── Construction ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("New warehouse is ACTIVE with no bins")
    void newWarehouseIsActive() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        assertThat(wh.isActive()).isTrue();
        assertThat(wh.getCode()).isEqualTo("WH-JKT-01");
        assertThat(wh.getType()).isEqualTo(WarehouseType.MAIN);
        assertThat(wh.getBins()).isEmpty();
        assertThat(wh.getId()).isNotBlank();
    }

    // ── Bin management ────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("addBin creates bin with correct attributes")
    void addBin() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        BinLocation bin = wh.addBin("A1-B2-C3", "DRY", 500, ACTOR);

        assertThat(bin.code()).isEqualTo("A1-B2-C3");
        assertThat(bin.zone()).isEqualTo("DRY");
        assertThat(bin.capacity()).isEqualTo(500);
        assertThat(bin.isActive()).isTrue();
        assertThat(wh.getBins()).hasSize(1);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Multiple bins can be added with different zones")
    void multipleBins() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        wh.addBin("A1", "DRY",  1000, ACTOR);
        wh.addBin("B1", "COLD",  500, ACTOR);
        wh.addBin("C1", "HAZMAT", 200, ACTOR);

        assertThat(wh.getBins()).hasSize(3);
        assertThat(wh.getActiveBins()).hasSize(3);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Duplicate bin code throws IllegalArgumentException")
    void duplicateBinCodeThrows() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        wh.addBin("A1", "DRY", 100, ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalArgumentException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> wh.addBin("A1", "COLD", 200, ACTOR))
                .withMessageContaining("A1");
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("deactivateBin marks bin inactive")
    void deactivateBin() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        wh.addBin("A1", "DRY", 100, ACTOR);
        wh.deactivateBin("A1", ACTOR);

        assertThat(wh.getBins()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(wh.getActiveBins()).isEmpty();
        assertThat(wh.findBin("A1").map(BinLocation::isActive).orElse(true)).isFalse();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("findBin returns empty Optional for unknown bin")
    void findBinUnknown() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        assertThat(wh.findBin("UNKNOWN")).isEmpty();
    }

    // ── Warehouse lifecycle ────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("deactivate sets isActive false")
    void deactivateWarehouse() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        wh.deactivate(ACTOR);
        assertThat(wh.isActive()).isFalse();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("updateAddress changes address")
    void updateAddress() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        Address newAddr = Address.builder()
                .line1("Jl. Baru No. 99")
                .city("Surabaya").countryCode("ID").build();
        wh.updateAddress(newAddr, ACTOR);
        assertThat(wh.getAddress().getCity()).isEqualTo("Surabaya");
    }

    // ── Audit ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

    @Test @DisplayName("Version increments on each mutation")
    void versionIncrements() {
        WarehouseAggregate wh = newWarehouse();
        long v1 = wh.getAudit().getVersion();
        wh.addBin("X1", "DRY", 100, ACTOR);
        long v2 = wh.getAudit().getVersion();
        wh.deactivateBin("X1", ACTOR);
        long v3 = wh.getAudit().getVersion();

        assertThat(v2).isGreaterThan(v1);
        assertThat(v3).isGreaterThan(v2);
    }
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// StockTransfer tests in same compilation unit for brevity
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

class StockTransferTest {

    private static final String TENANT = "tenant-tr";
    private static final String FROM   = "WH-JAKARTA";
    private static final String TO     = "WH-SURABAYA";
    private static final String ACTOR  = "warehouse-staff";

    private StockTransfer newTransfer() {
        return StockTransfer.create(TENANT, FROM, "A1", TO, "B1", ACTOR);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("New transfer starts DRAFT")
    void newTransferIsDraft() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        assertThat(t.getStatus()).isEqualTo(TransferStatus.DRAFT);
        assertThat(t.getId()).isNotBlank();
        assertThat(t.getLines()).isEmpty();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("addLine creates transfer line")
    void addLine() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        t.addLine("prod-001", "SKU-001", 50, ACTOR);

        assertThat(t.getLines()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(t.getLines().get(0).requestedQty()).isEqualTo(50);
        assertThat(t.getLines().get(0).receivedQty()).isEqualTo(0);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("dispatch → IN_TRANSIT with transfer number")
    void dispatch() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        t.addLine("prod-001", "SKU-001", 30, ACTOR);
        t.dispatch("TRF-001", ACTOR);

        assertThat(t.getStatus()).isEqualTo(TransferStatus.IN_TRANSIT);
        assertThat(t.getTransferNumber()).isEqualTo("TRF-001");
        assertThat(t.getDispatchedAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot dispatch empty transfer")
    void cannotDispatchEmpty() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> t.dispatch("TRF-000", ACTOR));
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("receive partial → still IN_TRANSIT")
    void receivePartial() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        t.addLine("p1", "S1", 100, ACTOR);
        t.dispatch("TRF-002", ACTOR);

        boolean done = t.receive(t.getLines().get(0).id(), 60, ACTOR);
        assertThat(done).isFalse();
        assertThat(t.getStatus()).isEqualTo(TransferStatus.IN_TRANSIT);
        assertThat(t.getLines().get(0).receivedQty()).isEqualTo(60);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("receive all → RECEIVED status")
    void receiveAll() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        t.addLine("p1", "S1", 50, ACTOR);
        t.dispatch("TRF-003", ACTOR);

        boolean done = t.receive(t.getLines().get(0).id(), 50, ACTOR);
        assertThat(done).isTrue();
        assertThat(t.getStatus()).isEqualTo(TransferStatus.RECEIVED);
        assertThat(t.getReceivedAt()).isNotNull();
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("complete from RECEIVED → COMPLETED")
    void complete() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        t.addLine("p1", "S1", 10, ACTOR);
        t.dispatch("TRF-004", ACTOR);
        t.receive(t.getLines().get(0).id(), 10, ACTOR);
        t.complete(ACTOR);

        assertThat(t.getStatus()).isEqualTo(TransferStatus.COMPLETED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("cancel from DRAFT → CANCELLED")
    void cancel() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        t.cancel(ACTOR);
        assertThat(t.getStatus()).isEqualTo(TransferStatus.CANCELLED);
    }

    @Test @DisplayName("Cannot cancel RECEIVED transfer")
    void cannotCancelReceived() {
        StockTransfer t = newTransfer();
        t.addLine("p1", "S1", 5, ACTOR);
        t.dispatch("TRF-005", ACTOR);
        t.receive(t.getLines().get(0).id(), 5, ACTOR);

        assertThatIllegalStateException()
                .isThrownBy(() -> t.cancel(ACTOR));
    }
}

# ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

# Auto-create schema from JPA entities
